Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.20

Chapter 1901
The so-called "Transit of the Sun" was when a star appeared between the sun and the land, blocking a portion of the sun's light and casting a huge shadow.
Transit of the Sun was not uncommon in the divine territory.
However, the transit of the Sea of Time was rather special.
Every few years, a star would block the sun's light, forming a shadow that would cover the entire Sea of Time.
Every transit of the Sun would be a different star. The nine stars that appeared in front of the Blind Tribe's matriarch would rotate.
What people didn't know was that when the Sea of Time was blocked by the sun, it was also the time when the Sea of Time opened. The nine stars represented the nine different entrances to the Sea of Time.
All the large and small forbidden areas in the divine territory were constantly explored by the True Gods of the various aristocratic families.
As soon as the Shenwu coins were formed, they would be snatched away by the True Gods. In turn, the Shenwu coins became even more precious and the True Gods searched for them even more crazily. This created a vicious cycle.
However, the Sea of Time only opened on the day of the transit of the Sun.
Other times, no one would explore it. The Shenwu coins that slowly formed in this forbidden area would accumulate. To the True Gods of the aristocratic families, it was a huge treasure trove!
If possible, the aristocratic families hoped that on the day of the transit of the Sun, all the members of the floating islands would enter the Sea of Time and crazily collect Shenwu coins.
Unfortunately, the Blind Tribe set a strict rule — only the True Gods that they invited were qualified to participate in the transit of the Sun.
As for the rules of the Blind Tribe's invitation, it was still a mystery.
In general, it seemed that the stronger the person, the more likely they were to be invited. However, occasionally, some inconspicuous fellows from the floating islands would be invited.
In the beginning, the aristocratic families had continuously organized a group of True Gods to wait for the Blind Tribe's invitation on the day of the transit of the Sun. However, the Blind Tribe continued to invite them in their own way without any changes. In the end, the aristocratic families on the floating islands gave up. When the transit of the Sun arrived, if anyone was confident enough to be invited by the Blind Tribe, they would just have to wait in the Sea of Time.
The shadow cast by the Aqua Star quickly spread across the Sea of Time. It blocked the light from the Blind Tribe's island, the light from the floating islands, and the entire Sea of Time …
The entire Sea of Time was immersed in darkness.
Of course, this wasn't absolute darkness. The sun's scorching light could still penetrate the Aqua Star's dim light.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
When the sky darkened, the Blind Tribesmen lit up torches one after another. At the same time, they began to make rhythmic cries.
"Wah wah, wah wah..."
After the Celestial Water Star rose, all of the Blind Tribe members began to dance and perform their Blind Tribe's ritual.
The Blind Tribesmen were all incomparably devout. This was a festival that belonged only to them, but to outsiders, it was rather boring.
The saints and secondary saints were all resting with their eyes closed while the true gods were quietly making their preparations. The forbidden grounds of the Sea of Time were about to open up, and the amount of shenwu coins they had accumulated over the years was astonishing. They naturally had to make sufficient preparations for their cultivation arts.
"Mu Ning," Mu Xuerong suddenly called out.
Mu Ning turned her head and asked, "Sister, what's the matter?"
"After entering the forbidden area of the Sea of Time, I still have things to do. If you have time, keep an eye on that brat," Mu Xuerong said.
"Do we need to force him to take action?" Mu Ning asked.
"No need. You just need to pay attention." Mu Xuerong shook her head.
"Sister doesn't believe in Mu Ning's strength," Mu Ning said resentfully.
However, Mu Ning also knew that if that brat was really an anonymous person in the Arena of Gods, she was afraid that she would not be his opponent. After all, she had lost to him when she was still a Divine Warrior. Now, he had also advanced to become a true god.
Mu Xuerong then said seriously, "Sister doesn't need to believe in your strength. As long as I prove something, I will kill him in an instant."
"Kill?" Mu Ning was stunned.
Although she had lost to an anonymous person, there was not much enmity between them. There was no need to kill him, right?
Seeing Mu Ning's expression, Mu Xuerong smiled faintly. "There are some things that you don't need to know so clearly."
Mu Ning's face revealed a disappointed expression. She looked at Luo Zheng again, as if she felt that this fellow was not as simple as an anonymous person. After all, people who had spent so much effort to enter the Arena of Gods anonymously more or less hid some unspeakable secrets.
The Blind Tribe's dreary and boring ceremony was finally over …
Many of the True Gods present had excited expressions on their faces. Of course, there were also a small number of True Gods who did not know what the Festival of the Sun was about. They stood there stupidly, even wondering what they had come here for. For these individual True Gods, they thought that the only thing they had gained from this Festival of the Sun was that they had seen the elegance of the Saints of the various large families.
The white-clothed woman waved her hand lightly, and the "Aqua Star" in the center of the nine pagodas emitted a faint light. This light became stronger and stronger, and as it continued to shine, it emitted a dazzling blue light. Finally, it formed a strange passageway.
"Ah, isn't this the passageway to the forbidden area?"
A True God could not help but shout out in surprise.
This True God might have been the same as Luo Zheng, unintentionally invited by the Blind Tribe. He did not know what would happen next, so when he saw the entrance to the forbidden area suddenly appear, he could not help but shout out.
A True God beside him glared fiercely at him, probably thinking that he was making a fuss over nothing.
Instead, it was the Blind Tribe's matriarch who said gently, "That's right. Every Festival of the Sun will open the forbidden area. You can enter and explore on your own. You can also take away any opportunities you find."
A look of joy appeared on the True God's face. Previously, he had thought that the Festival of the Sun was rather boring. He did not know that so many people were still so fascinated by it. Now, he was naturally very happy.
After the azure passageway had completely stabilized, the Perfect True Gods present looked at each other.
"Whoosh!"
Mu Xuerong was the first to rush in.
Immediately after, the other Perfect True Gods of the large families flew in one by one, instantly entering the passageway.
Although it was not explicitly stated, the True Gods present still followed the rules and entered the passageway. Those with higher cultivation would always have priority.
The goal of these Perfect True Gods was not Shenwu Coins!
After the Perfect True Gods entered, they were followed by the upper True Gods, followed by the middle True Gods like Han Cangyan, and then the lower True Gods.
Han Liusu did not wait for Han Chuyue and the others. Although her back was facing Luo Zheng, she still quietly paid attention to Luo Zheng's movements. She kept looking at Han Chuyue, Han Biluo, and Luo Zheng, who knew what they were whispering to each other.
At this moment, she did not stop at all and rushed into the passageway alone.
"Ah! Big Sister went in alone! "Han Chuyue's mouth gaped.
"Let's go in too." Luo Zheng did not hesitate and walked into the passageway with Han Chuyue and Han Biluo.
This was Luo Zheng's first time entering the forbidden area of the Divine Realm.
According to his judgment, the forbidden area of the Divine Realm was a place that the True Gods imagined. It was a place created by the power of faith. He wondered what the forbidden area of the Sea of Time was like.

Chapter 1902
Because the Sea of Time hadn't been opened for a long time, a considerable amount of shenwu coins had accumulated within.
As soon as he stepped into the Forbidden Grounds, he was greeted by a mountain of Shenwu Coins at the entrance …
This was also due to the fact that there were nine paths leading into the forbidden grounds. The last time this path was opened was before the ninth Transit Festival. According to the Transit Festival that was held once every eight years, it had been seventy-two years since this path was opened. Naturally, the amount of Shenwu coins gathered on this path was astonishing.
Because everyone had just entered, the True Gods still maintained restraint. They only used their spatial rings to collect the treasures and did not fight over them.
Not far away, Han Liusu saw the shenwu coins and her eyes flashed. Then, a surge of 'force' surged out of her body. She waved her sleeves, "Collect!"
"Huff huff huff …"
A fierce wind swept up …
Under this gale, the shenwu coins on both sides of the path were swept up and fell into her sleeves.
Those True Gods who were picking up Shenwu Coins turned their heads and glared at him.
These True Gods had entered the Sea of Time for the shenwu coins, but there had to be some rules. If they all used their abilities like Han Liusu to collect the coins, they would probably start fighting on the spot.
"Little girl from Han Jia! I'm warning you, if you do this again, don't blame me for being rude, "a True God from Tang Family said coldly. He was an intermediate True God.
The other True Gods also glared at Han Liusu with unfriendly expressions.
Han Liusu's position in Han Jia was indeed special, but which of the True Gods who were invited were easy to deal with? Most of them were core disciples of wealthy families. Their status and birth weren't any worse than Han Liusu's.
Who knew that Han Liusu would sneer, "I'll just take these shenwu coins. What can you do about it?"
With that, Han Liusu waved her sleeves again. Gusts of wind swept up even more shenwu coins and sucked them into her sleeves …
Luo Zheng, Han Biluo, and Han Chuyue looked at each other helplessly.
Han Liusu didn't lack shenwu coins. With her background, she didn't have much need for them. She was clearly throwing a tantrum.
However, this was not Han Jia's floating island. How could these elites let her have her way?
Real God Tang's face turned cold. His body suddenly floated and he charged toward Han Liusu. At the same time, he clenched his right fist and the veins on the back of his fist instantly turned black. It was as though there was an extremely heavy weight moving around his fist.
"How dare you!"
"Stop! She is our Han Clan's eldest princess! "
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo ordered together.
"I don't care which family's princess you are. What's so great about Han Jia?" True God Tang didn't care at all. He even sped up.
This martial artist surnamed Tang was Tang Lun's new disciple. With Tang Lun's support, he did not place Han Liusu in his eyes.
Just as Martial Artist Tang was about to reach Han Liusu, a figure suddenly appeared in front of Han Liusu with unparalleled speed. This person was Leng Lin Yue.
"Tang Hu, it's just some Shenwu coins. There's a lot more along the way. There's no need to make a move here, is there?" Leng Linyue said calmly.
When Tang Hu saw that it was Leng Linyue, his expression softened slightly. "Since Brother Lin Yue said so, it's fine. She's clearly causing trouble! If she continues to cause trouble, don't blame me for being impolite! "After saying that, Tang Hu turned and left.
Leng Linyue turned around with a faint smile on his face. "Liusu, I'm afraid you've never been to the forbidden grounds before. There are many unwritten rules in the forbidden grounds. If everyone has a certain amount of Shenwu coins, they'll be divided according to the ratio. In this case, everyone should do their best to collect Shenwu coins. No one is allowed to use their god art to monopolize the coins."
The All Saints Hall was established in the Sea of Time to establish rules for the various wealthy families.
Some rules were written on the Governing Slate, but not all of the messy rules were written on it. After all, writing on the Governing Slate required a hefty price.
The disciples of the major clans were already familiar with the rules of distributing Shenwu coins. After all, it was already extremely dangerous to roam the forbidden grounds. If they were to fight over Shenwu coins, the disciples of the major clans would probably kill each other all day long. This was not something the major clans were willing to see.
Of course, such rules were usually established when the strength of the two parties was equal. Killing for Shenwu coins was not uncommon in the forbidden area.
Han Liusu had just seen a figure suddenly blocking her way. She was originally happy, but when she saw that it was Leng Linyue, she was disappointed. She looked back at Luo Zheng and saw that he was standing still. She was even more disappointed. She immediately put on a fake smile and said to Leng Linyue, "Thank you for the reminder. It's indeed my first time entering the forbidden area."
"If that's the case, just follow me. Even a level one or two Holy Land is full of danger and uncertainty. Many True Gods have died there. The Sea of Time is even more dangerous," Leng Lin Yue said gently.
"Mm." Han Liusu nodded.
Last time, Leng Linyue and Han Liusu had a huge conflict. When he returned to the Leng family, he was taught a good lesson by his father.
Leng Linyue was very curious about his father's attitude. In terms of strength, his father was no weaker than Emperor Han Qing. In terms of family background and overall strength, he left the Han family behind. Furthermore, the Han family and the Leng family were connected by marriage. Anyone with eyes could see that the Han family was sucking up to the Leng family.
Why was his father so nervous about this marriage?
With Leng Linyue's arrogant personality, he would not lower his head to a woman. However, under his father's repeated warnings, he could only go along with it.
However, at this time, Leng Linyue looked at Han Liusu's sweet smile and was very pleased with himself.
Luo Zheng stood in place, his face expressionless. Han Bi Luo and Han Liusu's jaws dropped. How much Han Liusu hated Leng Lin Yue, the two sisters knew best. Now that he had suddenly changed his attitude, the two of them couldn't adapt for a while.
"Big sister Liusu … what's wrong?" Han Biluo asked.
"It seems like he's doing this on purpose because he's angry with Luo Tianxing." Han Chuyue helplessly curled her lips.
The two sisters looked at Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng expressionlessly walked forward. At the same time, he said, "Hurry up and collect the shenwu coins. Otherwise, they'll all be taken away."
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo followed behind Luo Zheng. However, the two sisters still carefully looked at Luo Zheng's back. They covered their mouths and muttered …
"Do you think Luo Tianxing has any thoughts?"
"Maybe he's jealous …"
"… …"
Although the two girls lowered their voices, how could they avoid Luo Zheng's sharp hearing?
He could only smile helplessly.
As for Mu Ning, who was further away, she carefully watched Luo Zheng while collecting the remaining Shenwu Coins.
She was a bit angry that Leng Linyue had suddenly interfered.
Mu Xuerong had ordered her to keep a close eye on Luo Zheng. Just now, Luo Zheng had clearly wanted to fight with Tang Hu. This was an excellent opportunity to observe Luo Zheng. As long as they forced Luo Zheng to make a move, he would eventually reveal a flaw …
"It's okay. The Sea of Time is filled with danger. There's still a chance." Mu Ning put all the shenwu coins in her spatial ring and followed after.
The interior of the Sea of Time forbidden area was also a strange and colorful world.
In this forbidden area, the laws of time seemed to be everywhere. In the soil beneath their feet, on the distant mountains, and in the surrounding trees, there were always colorful lights flashing. It was as if everything in this forbidden area relied on the power of time to exist …
Shenwu coins were scattered on both sides of the road leading to the Aqua Star. Many True Gods also stopped to collect them.
Naturally, Luo Zheng would not let go of this opportunity. Along the way, he had actually picked up three thousand Shenwu Coins. Even for a disciple of a wealthy family, three thousand Shenwu Coins was a huge sum. The True God who had picked up the most Shenwu Coins had probably accumulated over ten thousand Shenwu Coins along the way.
Moreover, those Perfect True Gods like Mu Xuerong, Han Tianxiao, and the others couldn't be bothered to pick up the Divine Martial Coins in this place. After entering the forbidden ground, they hurriedly flew away. It was obvious that they wanted to search for more important opportunities. It was no wonder that the various aristocratic families placed so much importance on the Ling Ri Sheng Assembly.
When the group reached the end of the road, a True God in front suddenly said, "Oh no, our luck isn't too good. This time, there's a group of time wraiths blocking the way. I'm afraid we're in trouble."

Chapter 1903
These humanoid creatures were like ghosts. Their figures were erratic, and the aura they emitted was not particularly strong.
"Ah, so unlucky …" Seeing these time wraiths, Han Chuyue curled her lips.
"What's wrong? These things don't seem very powerful, "Luo Zheng asked, puzzled.
The aura emitted by the time wraiths was only equivalent to the strength of an ordinary evil spirit. Killing them was not difficult.
"The Time Wraiths are indeed not strong, but their main bodies are in another time and cannot be attacked. And once you are possessed by the Time Wraiths, they will drag you into a crack in time and tear you in half." Han Chuyue had prepared quite well for this trip to the Sea of Time forbidden area.
The reason why the Sea of Time was so terrifying was because of this.
The entire Sea of Time was made up of countless fragments of time. Each wave was a different space-time. If one fell into the Sea of Time, it was equivalent to one's body falling into a different time. Putting aside the scale of time, it was equivalent to one's body being cut apart by countless spatial cracks.
However, in this aspect, the laws of time were much more domineering than the spatial rifts. For example, Luo Zheng had been able to resist the cutting of the spatial rifts for a long time. Even though the spatial rifts in the divine territory were more powerful than those in the universe, they still could not be compared to the temporal rifts. This was because these so-called rifts did not truly exist as "rifts". Instead, they were separated by different times.
Fortunately, there were many experts in this group of True Gods. With so many peak True Gods and middle True Gods gathered, there would naturally be people who would think of a way. Luo Zheng was not in a hurry.
Including Han Cangyan, the group of True Gods discussed for a while. Then, there were True Gods who volunteered.
"Isn't it just a group of time wraiths? Time wraiths do not have intelligence. I will lead them away. "The one who spoke was a black-haired youth. He looked very young, but he was already an upper True God.
Upper True Gods could fly in the forbidden area, but this place was filled with strange traps. These upper True Gods did not dare to barge in like those Paragons.
Many True Gods were delighted when they heard the black-haired youth's words. It was naturally a good thing that someone could be the vanguard.
After the black-haired young man walked to the intersection, he took out a small bamboo flute from his bosom. He shook the flute lightly, and many golden objects fell out from the flute. They were actually six or seven golden humanoid puppets!
Just from the appearance of this humanoid puppet, there didn't seem to be anything special about it. However, after the black-haired young man made a mysterious gesture, an invisible force suddenly spread out. When everyone felt the powerful force, they all had the illusion that they were no longer in control of their own lives.
"Great Manipulation Technique?" Luo Zheng asked.
In the Arena of Gods, Luo Zheng had experienced a similar type of Force. However, the Force he had encountered was from a lower level True God, who was far less domineering than the black-haired youth.
Han Chuyue nodded, "He is the Fang Family's Fang Yin. He is addicted to making puppets and has already cultivated the Great Control Technique to a very deep realm. Those puppets of his are also very powerful, each puppet has the strength of a middle True God! It is said that he has a very special alchemy puppet that can even kill an upper True God. "
Some Divine Daos might seem very weak, but when cultivated to a very deep level, they would also have powerful aspects.
When those alchemy puppets were awakened by Fang Yin, they immediately blossomed with an extremely powerful aura. It was even more powerful than the aura of many middle True Gods! It was unknown whether these alchemy puppets had powerful souls hidden within them, or whether they were given such aura through other means.
"Go!"
The black-haired youth lightly pointed, and those alchemy puppets rushed towards the group of Time Wraiths.
"Clang clang clang …"
As these alchemy puppets ran, their bodies emitted the sound of metal colliding, creating a huge noise.
One of the alchemy puppets even waved its arm. As that arm rotated, it revealed a circular quiver. This quiver fiercely shot out golden arrows towards the Time Wraiths.
"Swish swish swish swish …"
These alchemy puppets quickly attracted the attention of the Time Wraiths.
"Weng …"
Everyone felt their eardrums tremble. This was the sound of the Time Wraiths shrieking. They floated and rushed towards these alchemy puppets.
Fang Yin's method of controlling these alchemy puppets with the "Great Control Technique" had already reached an unfathomable level. Those alchemy puppets were almost like Fang Yin's left and right arms. They were abnormally nimble.
Seeing that the alchemy puppets had attracted the attention of all the Time Wraiths, his eyes flashed slightly. He suddenly waved his hand, and the alchemy puppets quickly changed directions and rushed in another direction.
The nine paths of the Sea of Time's forbidden land converged at the place occupied by the Time Wraiths. Fang Yin simply led this group of Time Wraiths to another path. But during this process, one of the alchemy puppets slowed down a bit and was entangled by a Time Wraiths, causing that alchemy puppet to lose control.
That alchemy puppet circled around a few times before being cut apart from its head. It rapidly turned into uniform fragments before collapsing with a loud rumble. Fine pieces of metal scattered all over the ground.
Seeing this scene, many True Gods had unsightly expressions. Fang Yin's alchemy puppets were made of the toughest materials in the God Realm. Once they were entangled by the Time Wraiths, they wouldn't be able to withstand a single blow.
"Weng weng weng …"
Those Time Wraiths seemed to be extremely excited and kept chasing after the alchemy puppets. The path in front of everyone was unimpeded.
"Now is the time!" Fang Yin shouted, and at the same time, he also rushed out.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Apart from Fang Yin, the other True Gods also heard the news and moved at an extremely fast speed.
"Fortunately, Fang Yin is here. Otherwise, it wouldn't be easy to get rid of these Time Wraiths …" a True God at the front said with a smile.
But before he could finish his words, his face stiffened and he couldn't continue.
A large group of Time Wraiths appeared in front of the True Gods once again!
"How could there be so many!"
"The first layer of the Sea of Time can't be so dangerous …"
"Let's go back!"
Those who had the qualifications to enter the Sea of Time were almost all core disciples of the major clans. Almost all of them were the most outstanding existences on the floating islands.
Although the Sea of Time was dangerous, these dangers were still manageable. As long as they didn't enter the third layer of the Sea of Time, it would be fine.
Therefore, the Sage Emperors were willing to let their children and followers enter to gain experience. After all, other than Shenwu coins, they could also find some pretty good opportunities.
But this time, so many Time Wraiths had appeared in the forbidden area, catching the True Gods off guard.
Some True Gods chose to retreat …
However, those True Gods had just taken a few steps back when they saw another group of Time Wraiths appear behind them. These Time Wraiths that had been lured away by the alchemy puppets had already cut the alchemy puppets into pieces and were surrounding them once again. All the True Gods were trapped in a predicament with no way out.

Chapter 1904
Outside the Forbidden Grounds, the Sages noticed this scene and their expressions turned ugly.
After all, many of the True Gods in the forbidden land were the direct descendants of the Saints.
"What's going on with this forbidden area? Why are there so many Time Wraiths? "asked a Saint.
In the first level of the Sea of Time forbidden land, seven or eight Time Wraiths would be the limit. Now, a bunch of Time Wraiths had appeared.
The female leader of the Blind Tribe was wearing a mask, so no one could see her expression. She said, "This time, the difficulty of the Sea of Time has increased."
The master of the forbidden land could affect the difficulty of the forbidden land to a certain extent, but he couldn't control the entire forbidden land.
Since the birth of the Forbidden Lands in the God's Domain, the difficulty of all the Forbidden Lands had constantly increased and even expanded.
For example, the Soul Wilderness, a level six forbidden land, was located in the Godly Spirit Realm. In the beginning, the Soul Wilderness only occupied one-tenth of the Godly Spirit Realm. But every God Era, the Soul Wilderness would expand and eat a part of the Godly Spirit Realm.
As the Soul Desolation grew larger and larger, its expansion was also accelerating. Now that the Soul Desolation had already swallowed the entire Divine Soul Region, it was beginning to expand towards the surrounding Divine Dust Region and Sealed Mountain Region. The gods living in these two regions could only constantly migrate to avoid being swept into the Soul Desolation.
Aside from the Soul Wilderness, the Takeshi Spiral Forest and other forbidden lands were slowly but surely expanding. Even the Sea of Time was expanding day by day. Perhaps the Sea of Time's area was expanding by one foot per day, but sand accumulated to form a tower.
Therefore, some people in the Godly Spirit Realm predicted that the entire Godly Spirit Realm would be consumed by the forbidden lands sooner or later.
"Isn't the difficulty under your control?" asked another Saint.
The female leader shook her head. "No, it's different this time. It should be the birth of a faith treasure …"
"A faith treasure?"
"You mean a faith treasure has appeared in the Sea of Time again?"
"What level of faith supreme treasure is it?"
Hearing the Female Clan Leader's words, the Sages were shocked.
Even Dongfang Chunjun's eyes were shrouded in black and white mists. He was rather moved.
The most common weapon in the God Domain was the grandmist artifact artifact. Before the appearance of the chaos artifact artifact, the grandmist artifact artifact was the mainstream artifact artifact in the God Domain. Sabers, swords, axes, hammers, arrows, and even fans, teapots, jade pendants … the grandmist artifact was the grandmist artifact artifact.
After the appearance of Chaos Treasures, the status of Grandmist Treasures fell slightly. However, both treasures had their own advantages and disadvantages. Grandmist Treasures had the advantage of having a deep foundation, while Chaos Treasures could indirectly utilize powerful Chaos Energy.
But in front of the most precious treasures of faith, the treasures forged by these refiners were not even worth mentioning!
After Luo Zheng's inner world gave birth to the Forbidden Lands, he had deduced the origin of the Forbidden Lands of the Divine Realm on his own. The Forbidden Lands of the Divine Realm of the Divine Realm of the Divine Realm were formed from the imaginations of countless gods. In other words, they were formed from the power of faith.
It was the same with the treasures of faith. They were also weapons imagined by countless gods, and these treasures of faith often had special and powerful abilities.
However, there were also strong and weak faith treasures.
The True Gods in the Divine Realm would link their faith treasures to the level of the forbidden land.
Even the smallest and most common level 1 forbidden area could give birth to a level 1 forbidden area. However, only one level 1 forbidden area could give birth to a level 1 forbidden area. If a level 1 forbidden area gave birth to a level 1 forbidden area, the owner of this level 1 forbidden area would become the owner of this level 1 forbidden area.
It was the same for a level-2 forbidden area. It could only produce a level-2 faith true treasure, but it could also produce a level-1 faith true treasure.
Although not many people knew about the forbidden land in the Sea of Time, the concept of "time" was deeply ingrained in the hearts of almost every True God. When the concept of time appeared in their minds, it would invisibly turn into a trace of power of faith and gather in the forbidden land in the Sea of Time.
The Sea of Time Forbidden Land was on the same level as other Level 6 Forbidden Lands.
The forbidden area of the Sea of Time had already produced a level 6 faith treasure, which was the "Sword of Time of the Gods" owned by the female head of the Blind Tribe. After obtaining this sword, she also became the controller of the forbidden area of the Sea of Time.
A level 6 faith treasure was the most powerful existence. It could often affect the entire Divine Realm.
The forbidden area of the Sea of Time would not produce a second level 6 faith treasure. However, a level 4 or level 5 faith treasure might appear in a level 6 forbidden area. Perhaps these faith treasures did not have the ability to dominate all living things in the Divine Realm like the "Sword of Time of the Gods". However, they still had extremely terrifying magical powers.
These faith treasures were hard to obtain even for the Sages of the rich and powerful families.
For example, the Sage of the Qin Family had a level 1 faith treasure. It was obtained from the level 2 forbidden area "Sea of Time". That faith treasure was a blood-colored ruyi.
It was said that the Qin Family had spent a considerable amount of Shenwu coins to buy this faith treasure. The amount of Shenwu coins was enough to change two words on the Governing Slate.
However, a Level One Faith Supreme Treasure might have very powerful Divine Powers, but it was of no substantial help to the powerful existence of the Sage Realm. Therefore, the blood-colored ruyi was like an ornament that was worn by the Sage of the Qin Family. For a time, it became a joke in the Sacred Halls.
Just a level 1 faith treasure was enough to make the Sages pay so much attention to it. Naturally, it was because of the terrifying magical powers bestowed by the faith treasure.
When they heard the Matriarch's words, they naturally showed considerable interest.
The Matriarch shook her head. "I'm not sure. Maybe it's a level 3 or level 4 faith treasure."
"A level 4 faith treasure!"
"For the little guys, it's also a kind of experience. This forbidden area of the Sea of Time may be ten times more dangerous than before, but the opportunities are also a thousand times greater …"
"That's right …"
The Sages wanted to withdraw their direct descendants. The forbidden area of the Sea of Time was already quite dangerous. Now that there was a change in it, it was even more dangerous. Death might happen in the blink of an eye.
However, a truly great opportunity was always accompanied by great danger. These Sages might have a trace of spoiling their children, but they were more willing to let them seize the opportunity in front of them.
While the Sages were discussing outside, the True Gods in the forbidden area had indeed fallen into a great life and death crisis.
Under the encirclement of the densely packed Time Wraiths, there was almost no possibility of rushing out. Even Luo Zheng frowned.
If they were real living beings, they might have a chance. But these Resentful Spirits of Time were strange creatures that didn't even have a physical body. There was simply no way to fight them. The only thing they could do was run. But now, how could they run?
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo were both afraid. They couldn't help but lean against Luo Zheng. Han Biluo's soft body was even slightly trembling. She had never even been to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, so when had she ever imagined that she would one day face life and death? As for Han Chuyue, she had after all ventured into the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Compared to Han Biluo, she was a bit calmer.
The other True Gods were also abnormally ugly. If this situation wasn't handled well, none of the True Gods present would survive.
"I'll draw them away. You two take the chance to escape!" At this critical moment, an upper True God stepped forward.
An upper True God could fly. After drawing these Resentful Spirits of Time away, they had a chance to escape. But for him, it was also extremely dangerous!
This upper True God came from the War God Temple. His name was Zhan Ming.
At this moment, he stepped forward bravely. All the True Gods present revealed grateful expressions.
Even the War God Temple's Temple Master outside the forbidden area, one of the three Sages of the Xuan Yue family, Zhan Wuji, revealed a proud expression. He said, "Our War God Temple doesn't have a single scoundrel!"
At this moment, the Resentful Spirits of Time swarmed over.
At the same time, Zhan Ming roared. Surging battle power surged from his body, covering the sky and covering the earth as it rushed towards the Resentful Spirits of Time ahead!
"This battle intent … It's exactly the same as Zhan Beihai's divine ability. It's just stronger. This person also comes from the War God Temple." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. To be able to step forward at this time, this upper True God's courage was commendable.
Zhan Ming's earth-shattering battle intent successfully attracted the attention of most of the Resentful Spirits of Time ahead. Then, like a roc spreading its wings, he soared into the sky!
As the Resentful Spirits of Time chased after him, there was a path of survival in front of everyone.

Chapter 1905
"Charge out!"
Without any hesitation, the True Gods took a step forward and charged forward.
"Follow me!" Leng Lin Yue remained calm and said to Han Liusu behind him.
Han Liusu did not have much of an expression on her face as she rushed forward. However, she was worried about Han Chuyue and Han Biluo behind her so she would occasionally look back.
It turned out that Luo Zheng's group of three were originally at the back. The Time Wraiths behind them naturally targeted the True Gods at the very back!
"Weng …"
A Time Wraith floated over and its light body instantly wrapped around a middle True God.
"Ah! Ah! Save me! "
The middle True God let out a miserable shriek in an instant. His body began to sway involuntarily. Very quickly, a shallow mark appeared on the middle True God's head. His sturdy skull had been evenly sliced apart in an instant. Red and white objects shot out from the cut. It was a terrifying sight.
"Pu pu pu pu pu …"
As more and more scars appeared on the middle True God's body, more and more blood spurted out.
Seeing this scene, Han Biluo's face turned as white as paper. At this moment, she was also running with all her strength …
But at this moment …
An inferior True Divinity beside Han Bi Luo was slightly slower and was also entangled by a Time Resentment Spirit. This inferior True Divinity relied on his instincts to grab onto Han Bi Luo's hand!
"Ah!"
Han Biluo screamed and tried to break free from this True God's hand. However, she did not expect this lower True God's strength to be so great. She was unable to break free.
It was also because of this delay that three Time Wraiths charged towards Han Biluo.
"Biluo!"
"Biluo!"
Han Liusu's face was filled with anxiety as she turned and rushed towards Han Biluo.
Han Chuyue stood there in a daze, seemingly scared silly.
As for Han Biluo, her face was filled with fear as she looked at Luo Zheng. She was unable to accept the fact that she had fallen here!
"Sou!"
In the nick of time, Luo Zheng's figure flickered, and his finger gently pressed down on the arm of the lower True God. At the same time, an invisible force sliced towards the arm of the lower True God who was grabbing Han Bi Luo.
"Crack!"
The Lower True God's arm broke, and at the same time, Luo Zheng grabbed Han Bi Luo, clamping her petite body under his armpit. At the same time, he turned around and used the same method to clamp the scared silly Han Chuyue under his other armpit. Just like that, with each hand holding one, a huge force erupted from his feet.
"Whoosh …"
In order to escape for their lives, these True Divinities all burst forth with their fastest speed. But at this time, they felt a gust of wind blow past them. It was actually a lower True Divinity carrying two Proof Divinities. This speed caused these True Divinities to widen their eyes.
Han Liusu, who stayed in place, stared blankly at this scene until Luo Zheng rushed in front of her with her two sisters. She then heard Luo Zheng say with a smile, "Run, don't be in a daze!"
"None of your business!" Han Liusu subconsciously retorted. But thinking about it, she seemed to have overreacted. This fellow didn't care about her at all. He had only said a few words to remind her and then ran in front of her. An intense feeling of displeasure rose in her heart.
The speed of these wraiths was slightly faster than the True Divinities. The slower True Divinities suffered. As long as they were entangled by the Time Wraiths, there was almost no chance of survival.
Almost all the True Divinities, after hearing a mournful scream from behind, would subconsciously increase their speed …
While fleeing, Luo Zheng's speed was extremely fast. Under the continuous eruption of energy, he maintained a sprinting posture. Not long after, he rushed to the front of the group.
"We're here! Ahead is the Time Temple! These Time Wraiths are unable to enter! "
A middle True Divinity in the group shouted.
A strangely shaped temple appeared in front of the crowd, and no one knew what it was.
Even True Divinities didn't have unlimited physical strength. Under this kind of burst of energy, many True Divinities had persevered to their limits, hovering between life and death. Hearing the words of the middle True Divinity, everyone's spirits suddenly rose!
"Quickly go in!"
"We can hide in the Time Temple for a while!"
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
The True Divinities increased their speed again.
Although Luo Zheng was carrying a girl on each side, his expression didn't change at all. Instead, his speed became faster and faster. In the end, he was the first to rush into the Time Temple.
This scene even attracted the attention of the Sages outside the forbidden area.
"How can that Lower True Divinity have such endurance and speed? He's carrying two girls without blushing and can still rush to the front?" a quasi-sage from the Tang Family asked curiously.
Leng Linyue and Mu Xuerong might have guessed Luo Zheng's identity, but these Sages couldn't possibly pay attention to the matters of the floating island. Naturally, they didn't know …
The only one who knew the truth was probably Aunt Hanjiu.
She had a faint smile on her face as she gazed at Luo Zheng. In her heart, she was slightly worried.
Aunt Hanjiu didn't know that Luo Zheng would be invited by the Blind Tribe. If he revealed any flaws now, how would he escape from the Sages? Luo Zheng, this fellow, was quite daring …
As for Emperor Hanqing, he had a calm expression on his face as he stared at Luo Zheng. No one knew what he was thinking.
Many True Divinities rushed into the Time Temple. Apart from the Upper True Divinities, almost all the other True Divinities were paralyzed inside. Han Liusu was also constantly gasping for breath. At the same time, she circulated her power and tried her best to calm her state of mind.
After the last True Divinity rushed out, the Time Wraiths still rushed toward the Time Temple!
Han Biluo and Han Chuyue finally recovered. When they saw the densely packed Time Wraiths rushing toward them like a tide, their beautiful faces became nervous again. Their fingers couldn't help but pinch Luo Zheng's arm.
Buzz buzz buzz buzz buzz …
The moment these Time Wraiths rushed into the Time Temple, many True Divinities subconsciously screamed.
If they could really rush in, none of the True Divinities present would be spared. Many of these True Divinities entered the Sea of Time for the first time. Naturally, they were worried.
However, when these Time Wraiths arrived at the entrance of the Temple of Time, they were unable to take another step forward. They could only densely arrange themselves in front of the entrance, as if there was an invisible wall separating them from the outside. These things were indeed unable to enter the Temple of Time!
The True Divinities finally relaxed.
Just looking at the Time Wraiths crowded together, they looked like a bunch of insects. It looked very disgusting and made one's scalp tingle uncontrollably.
"Ah, this hand …" Han Biluo was so frightened that she almost couldn't feel anything. Now that she had recovered, she realized that Luo Zheng had cut off half of the Lower True Divinity's arm and was still holding on tightly to her.
"Don't move." Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and gently slapped the hand away from Han Biluo. Then, he kicked it to the side. At the same time, he stood up and looked around the Time Temple.
At this moment, everyone heard a shout coming from outside the Time Temple again. "Help! Chase those Time Wraiths away! I …"
Through these translucent Time Wraiths, everyone saw that not far away in mid-air, the Upper True Divinity Zhan Ming was still leading a group of Time Wraiths. At this time, there was a layer of Time Wraiths at the entrance of the Time Temple. Zhan Ming had no chance to rush in.
No matter what, Zhan Ming was willing to take a risk for everyone just now. Now, they couldn't just watch as he was dragged away by the Time Wraiths.

Chapter 1906
These elites also had their own pride. They had to repay the kindness they owed others.
However, in the face of the densely packed Time Wraiths, rushing out was suicide. Who would be willing to risk their lives?
"Fang Yin … Do you have any more puppets?" a True God asked.
Fang Yin had used the puppets in his hands to lure away a group of Time Wraiths. However, the puppets he sent out were all cut into pieces. They were all useless.
"Yes, but it's useless," Fang Yin said.
He did have a few powerful puppets. He even had a Taiyi gold puppet. This Taiyi gold puppet was much stronger than Fang Yin himself.
"That's right. These Time Wraiths are blocking the door. Even if there are puppets, it's useless," Han Cangyan said with a frown.
Fang Yin nodded.
If he drove the puppets out of the door, he would definitely be entangled by the Time Wraiths. The only result was that he would be cut into pieces and completely useless.
"Help!"
Zhan Ming was still circling in the air. The Time Wraiths behind him were like a swarm of angry wasps. They followed him tirelessly. As long as he was entangled by any of the Time Wraiths, he would definitely die. His life was hanging by a thread.
Many True Gods were very anxious when they saw this scene. However, they did not have any good ideas.
Even Zhan Wuji, who was outside the Sea of Time, frowned deeply when he saw this scene.
There were not many True Gods in the Temple of War. However, every one of them was an elite. Zhan Ming was Zhan Wuji's disciple. In the future, he would definitely have a chance to become a Perfected True God. Zhan Wuji definitely did not want to see him die in the Sea of Time!
At this moment, Luo Zheng walked toward the door of the Temple of Time. As he approached the door, there was a commotion among the Time Wraiths crowding the door.
Han Liusu saw his actions and looked worried. She wanted to stop him. However, she clenched her teeth and did not say anything.
"Luo Tianxing, don't get too close to those things," Han Biluo quickly said.
Luo Zheng was only two or three inches away from the nearest Time Wraiths. He immediately said, "These Time Wraiths seem to have only one method of attack. After entangling their target, they only use the power of the time crack to cut them …"
"The problem is that no one can block the cutting power of the time crack," Han Cangyan said, shaking his head.
"There's no need to block the cutting power. There are some things that they can't cut." Luo Zheng smiled.
The True Gods stared at Luo Zheng with puzzled expressions. Mu Ning's eyes also revealed a look of curiosity. She couldn't help but ask, "What can't be cut?"
"For example, a liquid like water," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Then, he tapped his spatial ring lightly, and a golden light appeared in front of him. It was the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Luan Statue …
Seeing this, Han Liusu, Han Biluo and the others were also surprised. This was one of the rewards from the Han Clan. It turned out that Han Liusu had taken a fancy to this Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Chariot. It was originally placed in Saint Emperor City, but it suddenly disappeared. They didn't expect it to be in Luo Zheng's hands.
They instantly understood the meaning of Luo Zheng's words.
Fang Yin's puppet was very complex, and after being cut, it would collapse.
However, the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue was different. Like the Heavenly Divination Physique, it could take on many forms. In reality, it was just a ball of flowing metal liquid!
Han Liusu and the others understood the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue, but the other True Gods were still puzzled.
Previously, Luo Zheng had used the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue as a temporary body for the Extreme Evil Old Man. Now, he could only make do with it. After taking out the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue, the Extreme Evil Old Man quietly returned to his mind.
Then, under Luo Zheng's control, the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue suddenly pounced forward!
As soon as the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue rushed out of the Temple of Time, it was caught by one of the Time Wraiths. Luo Zheng immediately lost contact with the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue. He could only watch as the Time Wraiths dragged the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue up, then used the Temporal Rift to cut it open …
"Shua, shua, shua …"
Layer by layer, the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue was cut into pieces of uniformly thick gold. They fell to the ground with tinkling sounds.
Seeing this, everyone looked disappointed. They didn't know what Luo Zheng was doing.
"Hmph, this thing is just a piece of trash. What use is it?" Leng Linyue sneered. He didn't notice Han Liusu rolling her eyes at him behind him.
Not long after, the gold pieces began to soften slowly. They stuck to each other and became a whole again. Then, the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue suddenly flipped over and rushed outside!
The Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue was too close to the Time Wraiths. It didn't have time to increase its speed. After running less than ten feet, the Time Wraiths at the door rushed over again. In the blink of an eye, it was entangled by another Time Wraiths and was cut into pieces again …
But at this time, all the True Gods had a glimmer of hope. They already understood what the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue was. Although the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue was very rare, similar magic treasures weren't particularly rare either. Using it now would naturally have a miraculous effect!
After the Time Wraiths cut the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue into pieces, they stuck to the door of the Temple of Time again.
Then, the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue quietly merged back together again …
This time, the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue pulled away from the Time Wraiths. When it flipped over again, it finally had a chance to rush out!
"Whoosh!"
The Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue turned into a golden stream of light and flew away. It immediately attracted the attention of the Time Wraiths.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Accompanied by an ear-piercing cry, the Time Wraiths that had been piling up in front of the door all followed the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue!
"Now! Quick! "
Luo Zheng, who was standing in front of the door, shouted towards the sky.
Zhan Ming had almost reached his limit. He was almost relying on his willpower to persist. His attention was also very lax.
Even when the Time Wraiths in front of the door of the Temple of Time left, he didn't notice!
With Luo Zheng's shout, Zhan Ming's spirit was suddenly lifted. His figure suddenly swayed, and then he swooped down toward the Temple of Time.
"Whoosh!"
Zhan Ming didn't fly over the last part of his journey. He almost smashed into the Temple of Time.
After rushing through the door of the Temple of Time, Luo Zheng quickly grabbed Zhan Ming and swung him up. After swinging him several times, he slowly slowed down and put him down.
At the same time, the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue in the distance was once again cut into uniform pieces. The Time Wraiths flew back again, and together with the group of Time Wraiths that followed Zhan Ming, they crowded at the door of the Temple of Time again.
Zhan Ming panted heavily, and his whole body sat on the ground as if he was weak. After a while, he squeezed out a few words from his mouth, "Thank you, brother!"
The people of the War God Temple had always been hot-tempered, and at the same time, they were also very loyal. Zhan Ming was naturally grateful to Luo Zheng from the bottom of his heart for being able to escape from death.
"It's what I should do. Besides, you were in such a dangerous situation to save everyone," Luo Zheng said lightly. With his own strength, Zhan Ming just had a chance to escape alone, but he sacrificed himself to lure a large number of Time Wraiths away. Not everyone had such a good temper.
Then Luo Zheng looked again at the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue in the distance. He didn't know if there was another back door in the Temple of Time. If there was only one door, they could use it when they wanted to go out later.
Outside the Sea of Time Forbidden Land, Endless War saw this scene and laughed loudly, "Good, good, good, which rich family does this kid come from? He has a bright future! When he comes to the War God Temple in the future, I, Endless War, will definitely reward him! "
"Indeed, this little guy has some tricks up his sleeve, but there is also a certain amount of luck involved. Otherwise, Zhan Ming would have died," another Sage said lightly.
Was this little guy's only trick up his sleeve just luck?

Chapter 1907
After bringing Zhan Ming safely into the Temple of Time, everyone was in the mood to look at the temple.
The Temple of Time was extremely magnificent. In the middle of the spacious hall stood a strange sculpture. The sculpture looked like a monster with spikes all over its body. It looked like a huge worm, but its head had a circular mouth. In its mouth were rows of sharp barbed teeth. It looked very ferocious.
"What kind of monster is this?" Luo Zheng looked at the statue curiously. He couldn't figure out why the Temple of Time would have such a strange statue.
Everything in the forbidden land was created by the power of faith, and the Temple of Time was the same. This monster in front of them had to be something that existed in legends.
Han Chu Yue stared at these strange insects and showed a disgusted expression. At the same time, she shook her head. Although she knew about the Blind Tribe, this was her first time entering the forbidden land of the Sea of Time. She was unfamiliar with this forbidden land.
When Zhan Ming heard this, he smiled and said, "This is a Time Source Worm. Legend has it that the passage of time is due to these worms devouring the past."
"If the past is swallowed by them, does that mean the past shouldn't exist?" Luo Zheng asked.
According to Han Chuyue's explanation, only the Blind Tribe's matriarch had the right to go back to the past. This meant the past was real. The matriarch could also change the past to a certain extent. She could kill the living beings of the past to affect the present.
Han Chuyue's explanation was contradictory to Luo Zheng's understanding.
In fact, when Han Chuyue told him about the Blind Tribe's matriarch's ability, he was very confused.
If the matriarch could really go back to the past and kill the Saint, then theoretically, all the clues of the Saint's past would disappear. Everything related to the Saint would change. For example, the Saint's children would disappear. The Saint's spouse wouldn't have any interaction with the Saint. They wouldn't even be able to build a floating island. This would have a profound impact on the future.
But according to Han Chuyue, after killing the Saint, only the Saint would disappear. All the traces he left behind would still exist. Logically speaking, this didn't make sense.
The mysteries of time were too deep, and even Han Chuyue was unable to explain it. She could only tell Luo Zheng what she understood.
"The Time Source Worm devouring the past is just a legend. The real Time Source Worm doesn't devour the past. On the contrary, they also live in time. They belong to the race of guardians of time. This race is quite powerful. Even Saints can't escape from them!" Zhan Ming said calmly.
"Saints will encounter these Time Source Worms?" The more Luo Zheng listened, the more confused he became. He looked at Han Chuyue and asked, "Didn't you say that because Matriarch obtained the Sword of Time, she could return to the past? Since the Genesis Bugs of this era exist in time, how could the Saints encounter them? "
Han Chuyue shrugged. She couldn't answer Luo Zheng's question. Although she had read about the Sea of Time and the forbidden grounds, she didn't know all the secrets within.
Who knew that Zhan Ming shook his head. "Time cracks are everywhere in the Sea of Time. With the Saints' ability, they have a way to find larger Time cracks. Using these Time cracks, they can also enter."
Every wave in the Sea of Time had infinite Time cracks. It was just that those Time cracks were very fragmented and naturally couldn't be passed through.
However, in such a vast sea of time, there would occasionally be relatively large and complete time rifts. Some of the time rifts were even over thirty meters wide. Although these time rifts often disappeared in the blink of an eye, with enough patience, one could still pass through them.
"I think you have mistaken the cause and effect relationship," Zhan Ming continued. "Because Matriarch has that sword, she can safely return to the past. But the other Saints can't return to the past even if they enter the Time cracks. Because the Saints can't pass through the obstruction of the race of guardians of time."
"So that's how it is."
Hearing Zhan Ming's explanation, Luo Zheng nodded. Although there were still many mysteries in his heart, he could be considered to have somewhat understood the context. It turned out that there was a race in time. The things involved were indeed much more complicated than he had imagined.
"Let's go. This Temple of Time is an opportunity that you can't miss. I've been there once and benefited a lot," Zhan Ming said with a slight smile.
Many True Gods had already bypassed this huge Time Bug statue and entered the corridor in the center of the main hall. On both sides of this corridor, there were arc-shaped arches. The arches were pitch-black. The True Gods didn't hesitate and quickly entered. At this time, different lights lit up from inside the arches.
"I know this place. This is the Time Chamber. It's said that anyone who enters it to cultivate can break through their bottleneck!" Han Chuyue rushed to say.
"It's so magical?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
Zhan Ming stepped to the side and said at the same time, "It's indeed so magical. This Time Chamber can show you any period of history in the divine territory, and the things that happened during this period of time are exactly what you need. You can go in and try. This Time Chamber only works once for anyone, so there's no need for me to go in."
When Zhan Ming first entered the Temple of Time, he was only a middle True God and had encountered the biggest bottleneck in his life.
He was still unable to comprehend the "invincible fighting spirit" in the last three stages of the War God Path. After entering the Time Chamber, he actually returned to the ancient battlefield and met the first Ancient Enlightened Warrior who cultivated the War God Path. Moreover, he witnessed the Ancient Enlightened Warrior erupt with the "invincible fighting spirit" and the imposing manner of one against ten thousand!
Later, after Zhan Ming left the Temple of Time and returned to the War God Temple, he completely grasped the "invincible fighting spirit" seven days later. At the same time, he broke through the shackles of cultivation and became an upper True God.
After listening to Zhan Ming, Luo Zheng became more and more amazed. In other words, the Time Chamber could put you in a scene you needed to cultivate and comprehend.
This kind of magical place once again exceeded Luo Zheng's ability to understand. He didn't know how it was done.
Seeing more and more True Gods enter those arched doors, he also chose an arched door and stepped inside.
He didn't know what this Time Chamber would show him …
After others entered, they seemed to quickly sink into a certain period of time. However, Luo Zheng didn't react for a long time. It was still pitch-black.
After waiting like this for a long time, Luo Zheng noticed that strands of dark brown aura began to appear in this pitch-black space.
"Primal chaos aura?"
Luo Zheng didn't come back to his senses for a moment, his face full of a strange expression …
What did this slowly rotating primal chaos aura mean?

Chapter 1908
Luo Zheng stared at this mass of chaotic energy as it slowly rotated for a long time …
As it constantly rotated, the light in the Passage of Time chamber slowly turned bright, but the surroundings were still dusky.
"Is this the beginning of the Realm of the Gods?"
Luo Zheng was very curious about the origin of the Realm of the Gods.
Many True Divinities didn't think about the origin of the Realm of the Gods because they rarely looked outside of the Realm of the Gods.
But because Luo Zheng had created a "small Realm of the Gods" that was extremely similar to the Realm of the Gods, he had a faint guess in his heart. Was the Realm of the Gods created by some almighty being? Was it even possible that it was the "leapfrog life form" that Saint Jiu spoke of?
Under Luo Zheng's gaze, the chaotic energy in this dusky space became increasingly dense. The speed of the spiral also became faster and faster. At the same time, all of the chaotic energy crazily condensed towards the center, finally forming a small "nucleus".
At this time, the faint phantom of an axe appeared.
"Axe?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
In the legends, the Good Fortune Axe was the one that split open the Realm of the Gods. But this legend appeared when the Realm of the Gods was first created. Moreover, the Good Fortune Axe shattered a few days after splitting open the Realm of the Gods. Because of this, this legend wasn't very convincing.
"Hu …"
A hand appeared in the darkness.
It was a pure white jade hand. From the fingertips to the palm, there weren't any wrinkles. The thickness of the fingers was just right.
After that hand grabbed the Good Fortune Axe, it fiercely chopped down at the "nucleus" …
"Bang!"
Accompanied by a thunderous sound, a blinding light bloomed. The light was so bright that Luo Zheng couldn't open his eyes. His eardrums buzzed and his heart thumped.
In the Passage of Time chamber, he had only seen the past, but it still gave him such a great shock. He could imagine how terrifying it was when the Good Fortune Axe split open the "nucleus".
When the light dimmed, Luo Zheng looked again. He discovered that the "nucleus" hadn't been split open. Only a small crack had appeared on its surface. The axe hadn't split open the "nucleus"!
Then, the jade hand once again waved the Good Fortune Axe, chopping down at the "nucleus".
"Bang!"
"Boom!"
It wasn't until the Good Fortune Axe chopped down a third time that there was a dull cracking sound. The 'core' had finally split open.
In an instant, lightning flashed and thunder rumbled …
All of the chaotic energy transformed into sticky raindrops that wreaked havoc in this world, turning it into an extremely vast world.
Seeing this vast world, Luo Zheng was absolutely certain that this was indeed the scene of the creation of the Divine Domain.
But what was the use of the Time Chamber showing him this scene?
When others entered the Time Chamber, they would be able to see a piece of history that could enlighten them. It was either the shackles of their techniques or the bottleneck of their cultivation. A certain piece of history would always be able to enlighten the True Divinities who entered.
But the scene in front of him didn't seem to have much effect on Luo Zheng …
After this world unfolded, it rapidly evolved in front of Luo Zheng at a dazzling speed.
After a long period of heavy rain, it turned into a sea. Continents rose from the sea and turned into mountains and rivers. Greenery appeared in the mountains and rivers and turned into forests. These evolutions were not too different from Luo Zheng's Inner World.
But after this world evolved to a certain extent, everything suddenly stopped.
At the same time, the chaotic seawater began to crazily boil …
Under the crazy boiling, all of the chaotic seawater evaporated in a few breaths of time and turned into a huge cloud.
At the same time, a golden sun appeared in the sea. After that dazzling sun appeared, it quickly rose into the sky.
Noticing the appearance of these clouds, Luo Zheng felt that they were very familiar. These clouds were somewhat similar to the Dao Accumulation clouds formed after he swallowed the mud, but the scene was far less massive.
"These aren't Dao Accumulation clouds, these are … Realm Clouds! How could they be Realm Clouds? "Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a strange expression.
The spreading clouds in front of him were very similar to the Realm Clouds formed when Luo Zheng became a Realm Lord. So when he saw this scene, he became even more confused.
When Luo Zheng became a Realm Lord in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Saint Jiu told him that the Realm Clouds he condensed contained the aura of the "Three Thousand Divine Daos General Outline". At that time, Luo Zheng didn't understand Saint Jiu's words. He didn't know what the "Three Thousand Divine Daos General Outline" was.
However, he firmly remembered the aura in the Realm Clouds. It was exactly the same as the aura in the clouds in front of him.
Just as he was feeling even more confused, a huge shadow of a pagoda was suddenly refracted from the clouds. At the top of the pagoda shadow was a human figure and a dragon figure.
"Roar!"
As the figure pulled out a sword, the dragon's shadow fused with his sword.
The human figure held the longsword and continuously danced in the clouds. Sword light shot out from the clouds in all directions of the Divine Realm.
"Primordial Sword Technique?" Luo Zheng's eyebrows slightly raised.
When Luo Zheng fought against Hua Tianming in the Illusion Battlefield, the Primordial Sword Technique left a deep impression on him. At the same time, Luo Zheng also understood the essence of it. However, the Primordial Sword Technique was a pure sword technique. It wasn't particularly compatible with the Yi God Sword because the Emotion Severing Divine Dao pursued extreme power. Usually, it only had one move.
However, the Primordial Sword Technique that the human figure used was different from Hua Tianming's. Or rather, the Primordial Sword Technique was only a small part of the sword techniques that the human figure used.
Every sword he slashed out emitted a huge amount of power. The aura within the power was still the aura of the clouds …
"Sou!"
A sword light floated out. It flew over millions of miles and slashed at the edge of the Divine Realm. The edge of the Divine Realm was cut open like a piece of cloth.
The world began to expand.
One sword …
One hundred swords …
Ten thousand swords …
One hundred thousand swords …
A total of one hundred thousand swords. Under the continuous slashes, the Divine Realm had expanded countless times. Luo Zheng observed these hundred thousand swords from beginning to end.
"Huala …"
Not long after, a torrential rain fell from the Realm Cloud. This time, the raindrops weren't brown. Instead, they were pure and transparent ordinary raindrops. It was the same. The Divine Realm didn't have the true Primal Chaos Aura. This was quite different from Luo Zheng's Inner World.
The creatures in Luo Zheng's Inner World weren't afraid of the Primal Chaos Aura. Furthermore, the Primal Chaos Sea hadn't experienced this transformation process.
The human figure slowly dissipated. Luo Zheng closed his eyes. He recalled the human figure's sword technique in his mind.
That human figure slashed out one hundred thousand swords …
However, there were only thirteen sword moves …
"Any one of these thirteen sword moves is incomparably powerful. Moreover … there shouldn't be anyone in the Divine Realm who can use them. The aura contained within his sword moves is very heterogeneous. However, I can roughly sense that it's the Dao of several hundred Divine Daos entwined together!"
"The so-called Divine Dao General Outline is the fusion of three thousand Divine Daos. So this is the reason why father bestowed me with the Immeasurable Ruler!"
"Because only the Immeasurable Ruler can help me absorb the three thousand Divine Daos. This is also the most convenient and simple method. Even so, it's not easy to collect all three thousand Divine Daos!"

Chapter 1909
After the figure in the clouds slashed out 100,000 times, everything in the Secret Room of Time disappeared in front of Luo Zheng, and the Secret Room of Time returned to darkness.
Luo Zheng wasn't in a hurry to leave the Secret Room of Time. Instead, he stood in the darkness and quietly savored it.
It wasn't possible to use the Immeasurable Ruler to absorb all 3,000 types of Divine Daos in a short period of time. Even if the figure slashed out 100,000 times, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to comprehend it properly, because he couldn't understand many of the Dao connotations.
This Secret Room of Time wasn't meant to teach Luo Zheng divine abilities, but to open up a path for him …
"This path is to fuse Divine Daos."
"Indeed, some people have fused two or even three types of Divine Daos together. However, that's only because they took the strong points of each Divine Daos and complemented each other. Many Divine Daos conflict with each other. Fusing hundreds of Divine Daos together is simply unheard of! Because there's no second person in the Divine Realm who has this ability! This Secret Room of Time is really magical. "
"Can I fuse them together?"
Luo Zheng had a total of 307 types of Divine Daos in his body, so he could give it a try.
With a slight thought, a steady stream of primal chaos energy was extracted from the sea of primal chaos and gathered on the scales of the godhead along with those rays of light. Soon, those scales began to emit a faint light, and the Dao connotations of various Divine Daos began to slowly dissipate.
Fledgling Flame Divine Dao …
Black Tortoise Divine Dao …
Purple Cloud Divine Dao …
Snow Imprint Divine Dao …
God Slaying Divine Dao …
Li Water Divine Dao …
…
…
Each scale only absorbed a small amount of primal chaos energy, so the Dao connotations produced weren't much.
The Dao connotations of these more than 300 types of Divine Daos were different. After they slowly intertwined with each other, their colors began to change constantly, and finally turned into a dark brown color similar to primal chaos energy!
"Very smooth! I wonder if it's effective … "
The process of fusing the Dao connotations was so smooth that it was beyond Luo Zheng's expectations. After fusing these Divine Daos' Dao connotations, Luo Zheng quietly extracted them.
However, the moment these mixed Taoist Accumulations were drawn out, Luo Zheng immediately felt an extremely wild energy rush out of them.
This energy was so wild that after it rushed out of his Dantian, it was like a wild horse running amok in his body.
"Puff, puff, puff …"
A few muffled sounds came from Luo Zheng's body.
Soon after, a metallic taste came from his throat and he spat out a mouthful of blood. His body went limp and he fell to the ground.
"Are you alright?" Evil Old Man asked worriedly. He had no idea what Luo Zheng had done.
Just now, the Evil Old Man had been lurking in Luo Zheng's mind. When he heard Zhan Ming's words, he roughly knew what this Secret Room of Time was.
In the Divine Domain, there was also a type of "Epiphany Stone", which could allow a cultivator to enter the best state and obtain a trace of inspiration that was most suitable for them, thus allowing them to break through their realm. It was said that someone had developed a miraculous divine ability through the use of the "Epiphany Stone" because he had seen a trace of an illusory scene.
However, the Epiphany Stone couldn't be compared to the Time Chamber. In comparison, the Time Chamber was far too grand. It could directly create the most suitable period of time for you!
Evil Old Man had been guessing what kind of scene Luo Zheng would see, but he never expected to see the birth of the Divine Domain.
He knew about Luo Zheng's background and knew that he was an extraordinary existence. However, what kind of earth-shattering technique did this guy cultivate that required the birth of the Divine Domain to comprehend?
Seeing that Luo Zheng had indeed comprehended something, Evil Old Man was rather expectant. However, he didn't expect that Luo Zheng would suffer internal injuries just as he made a move.
"I'm fine. Only my internal organs are shattered …"
Luo Zheng took out a snow-white elixir and swallowed it. He had taken this elixir from the alchemy room in case of an emergency. After swallowing it, he instantly felt a cooling sensation spread throughout his body. His internal organs began to recover rapidly.
Then, he clenched his teeth and slowly got up from the ground.
The idea given by the Time Chamber was indeed correct. However, he didn't expect the mixed Taoist Connotation to be so violent. He lost control of it before he could draw it out of his body.
"The amount of Divine Daos that figure could fuse was limited. Perhaps he avoided some of the conflicting Divine Daos. I didn't filter them and fused all of them together. The repelling force of the Dao is too strong, and the fused Dao is too violent. I can't control it!"
After failing once, Luo Zheng wasn't discouraged at all. He started experimenting again. A second time, a third time …
…
Many of the True Gods comprehended in the Time Chamber, and when they came out, they couldn't suppress the excitement on their faces.
Leng Linyue's face was full of pride. He stood at the door of the Time Chamber and waited. After a while, Han Liusu came out of the Time Chamber with a shocked expression. She seemed to have seen something unbelievable in the Time Chamber.
"Liusu, how do you feel?" Leng Linyue asked.
"Not bad," Han Liusu replied calmly.
"That's good!" Leng Linyue smiled, "I also have a great harvest. I feel like I can advance to upper True God at any time!"
"Oh," Han Liusu nodded and looked at the Time Chamber where Luo Zheng was.
Leng Linyue noticed Han Liusu's gaze and his eyes flashed with coldness.
Han Liusu had been quietly paying attention to that little brat all the way. Leng Linyue had always been paying attention to that little brat. However, how could the arrogant Leng Linyue tolerate such a thing?
After a few times, the killing intent in his heart grew heavier and heavier.
At this time, Luo Zheng also came out of the Time Chamber. Although his injuries had more or less recovered, his face was still slightly pale.
"Luo Tianxing, you came out!"
"You seem to be injured!"
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo immediately surrounded Luo Zheng after he came out.
"I'm fine. There were some problems with my cultivation," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
"How could that be? The Time Chamber only gave you some enlightenment, "Han Biluo asked curiously.
Hearing the words of the two sisters, Han Liusu's eyes showed a trace of worry. Although it only flashed for a moment, Leng Linyue still caught it. He couldn't help sneering, "You overestimated yourself and thought you had some enlightenment. Of course there will be problems with your cultivation. Sooner or later, you will die from cultivation deviation."
Luo Zheng didn't say anything, but Han Chuyue couldn't help but ask, "What nonsense are you talking about?"
"I'm just telling the truth. To be able to injure yourself in the Time Chamber, you can be considered very talented, hehe," Leng Linyue said with a hollow laugh.
Luo Zheng just glanced at Leng Linyue indifferently and walked towards the Time Temple's door without saying a word.
Being ignored by Luo Zheng in front of Han Liusu made Leng Linyue feel even more uncomfortable. If it weren't for the fact that this place wasn't suitable for fighting, he probably couldn't wait to kill this guy.
The True Gods in the temple left the Time Chamber one after another and gathered in front of the door again.
Those Time Wraiths were still densely packed in front of the door. Zhan Ming, Fang Yin, and the other True Gods were discussing their next step.
"Think of a way to lure these Time Wraiths away. We have a chance to enter the second layer of the Time Sea," a True God suggested.
"But this time, the Time Sea is so dangerous. Just the first layer is so terrifying. Isn't going to the second layer seeking death? I say we should go back the way we came and leave the Time Sea forbidden area, "another True God said.
Some True Gods cherished their lives and naturally didn't want to take the risk.
But there were still some True Gods who were very persistent. One of them said, "This time, the forbidden area's difficulty has increased greatly. It definitely didn't become like this for no reason. In the past, the Time Sea forbidden area was very stable. Perhaps a great opportunity was born in the Time Sea."
In their opinion, as the master of the Time Sea forbidden area, the female patriarch couldn't casually change the difficulty when she invited them to participate. Now that so many Time Wraiths had appeared, it was very likely that a great opportunity was born.
Soon, the True Gods were divided into two groups. Because of this, there was even a quarrel.
"Don't quarrel. If we want to leave the Time Temple, we have to rely on Luo Tianxing," Zhan Ming turned to look at Luo Zheng and said.
Now, the Time Wraiths were still blocking the door. Everyone could only rely on the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue to lure the Time Wraiths away. Otherwise, no one could leave the Time Temple.

Chapter 1910
Dongfang Yang had swallowed a Jade Blood Golden Elixir before, which was very powerful.
Not to mention a middle True God, even a Peak True God's Blood Qi would be quickly replenished.
The Jade Blood Golden Elixir was constantly working in his body, replenishing his Blood Qi and keeping his Blood Qi vigorous at all times.
Therefore, the people in the canyon saw a very shocking scene …
Dongfang Yang was held up in midair, swaying left and right. Blood kept flowing out of his body. It was like bright red ink splashing all over the canyon. Many Real Gods tried to avoid it.
"Dongfang Yang is bleeding a lot …"
"His blood is almost dry, right?"
"Not necessarily. He just ate a Golden Elixir to replenish his Blood Qi. If it wasn't for that Golden Elixir, he would have become a dry corpse."
This scene left many people dumbfounded.
Mu Ning put her hands behind her back and looked at Luo Zheng with her round eyes. She was speechless, but a faint smile appeared on her sharp face.
Although she mainly cultivated the Killing God Dao, she didn't expect the Bloody Desolation Bite to be used like this. The Bloody Desolation Bite was not a good Magic, but it was usually used to restrict the opponent's escape. Now, the anonymous person was relying on the Bloody Desolation Bite to drain Dongfang Yang's blood.
Although the Jade Blood Golden Elixir was constantly replenishing his Blood Qi, more blood flowed out of Dongfang Yang's wounds, so the consumption was faster.
Soon, Dongfang Yang's face turned from red to pale, and soon, there was no color on his face.
He wanted to cut off the invisible vines under his feet, but the vines formed by the Long Vine Sword were firmly entwined around his lower body. If he wanted to cut off these invisible vines, he would have to destroy his body as well.
With his attainments in the Thick Earth Divine Dao, as Luo Zheng said, he was almost invincible. Moreover, he still had a few Magic Skills that he hadn't used yet. Now, he was lifted up in the air by Luo Zheng, but he had lost the chance to turn the tables.
More and more blood spurted out, and Dongfang Yang gradually felt weak. He looked panicked. If this continued, he would really die …
When the Sages outside the Sea of Time saw this unbelievable scene, they didn't know whether to laugh or cry.
Dongfang Chunjun stared at Dongfang Yang for a while, then murmured, "Ning, stop him. Don't kill him. Otherwise, Dongfang Yang will really die."
Dongfang Ning had been told by Dongfang Chunjun not to participate in the fight between them.
Dongfang Ning wouldn't disobey Dongfang Chunjun's orders. Even if he saw his brother Dongfang Yang die, he wouldn't do anything.
Now that Dongfang Chunjun had spoken, Dongfang Ning nodded.
However, just as Dongfang Ning was about to make a move, Zhan Ming suddenly extended a hand and blocked his path. "You said you wouldn't make a move."
Zhan Ming looked very serious, and two blue balls of fighting spirit bloomed in his eyes. After all, Luo Zheng had saved his life. Zhan Ming couldn't watch Dongfang Ning bully the weak. If Dongfang Ning really wanted to kill Luo Zheng, he would definitely do it.
Dongfang Ning chuckled. He didn't care about Zhan Ming's attitude. "Don't worry. The old man has spoken. Both of them can't die. Our old man has the intention of taking in disciples. I wouldn't dare to kill that brat!"
After he finished speaking, his figure fluttered as he charged straight towards Luo Zheng.
