Zhan Ming's face was uncertain. He didn't know whether Dongfang Ning was telling the truth or not, so he still followed behind him.
Luo Zheng was still controlling the Long Vine Sword. The soil under Dongfang Yang's feet swayed left and right in the air, and the blood kept splashing like ink.
"Whoosh …"
At that moment, he suddenly felt a gust of cold wind and almost subconsciously dodged to the side.
The speed of the newcomer was surprisingly fast. Luo Zheng had just turned over when he felt a hand tightly gripping his arm. At the same time, an extremely cold aura came from the hand. The aura rushed into his arm!
At that moment, Luo Zheng noticed that the person in front of him was Dongfang Yang's brother, Dongfang Ning.
"Stop. If you kill someone from the Dongfang family, you'll never be able to rise again," Dongfang Ning said calmly.
Dongfang Ning held Luo Zheng's arm, which bought him a chance to breathe. However, most of the blood in Dongfang Ning's body was gone. His meridians lacked Qi and blood, so they couldn't circulate smoothly. Even if he stood there lazily, he still couldn't break free from the Long Vine Sword.
"He stole my Shenwu coins and wants to kill me. He deserves to die," Luo Zheng said without changing his expression.
"But you can't kill him," Dongfang Ning said with a faint smile. He increased the strength in his hand, and the cold aura kept pouring into Luo Zheng's arm. Half of Luo Zheng's body was immediately frozen stiff.
"Whoosh …"
At that moment, a pure white flame suddenly floated out from Luo Zheng's arm. After the Ice Soul Devil Flame appeared, it quickly burned toward Dongfang Ning's hand.
"Ice Soul Devil Flame?"
Dongfang Ning's eyes showed a hint of surprise.
The Ice Soul Devil Flame was indeed a very powerful ability. Luo Zheng used the aura of chaos to drive it, so its power was also very terrifying.
An ordinary lower true god would find it difficult to resist the Ice Soul Devil Flame. An intermediate true god would also suffer if the Ice Soul Devil Flame touched them. However, Dongfang Ning was an upper true god. His eyes flashed slightly, and bits of white ice crystals escaped from his arm.
Those ice crystals turned into countless ice worms and swarmed toward Luo Zheng's Ice Soul Devil Flame.
The moment these ice worms touched the Ice Soul Devil Flame, the two different types of cold energy had a wonderful reaction. The ice worms actually quickly absorbed the cold energy in the Ice Soul Devil Flame!
After absorbing the Ice Soul Devil Flame, the ice worms, which were originally as small as toothpicks, quickly expanded. After absorbing a certain amount, they rolled on the ground and quickly turned into small ice cocoons.
Luo Zheng kept releasing the Ice Soul Devil Flame. Dongfang Ning also kept urging the ice worms to devour the Ice Soul Devil Flame.
The ice cocoons that rolled on the ground quickly broke. Those ice cocoons instantly turned into snow-white ice butterflies. These ice butterflies' wings were translucent, and the ice crystals formed beautiful and symmetrical patterns on their wings.
All the ice butterflies fluttered and gathered behind Dongfang Ning. These ice butterflies were clearly under Dongfang Ning's control.
The more Ice Soul Devil Flame Luo Zheng released, the more ice butterflies Dongfang Ning would nurture …
"Don't waste your energy. How about I give you Shenwu coins?" Dongfang Ning said in a negotiating tone.
Luo Zheng frowned …
With his current strength, it was indeed a bit difficult for him to fight against an upper True God.
Dongfang Ning noticed the change in Luo Zheng's expression and smiled. "Perhaps we'll be a family in the future …"
"Brother! Kill that idiot! "At this moment, Dongfang Yang, who had rested for a while, finally had some strength. He suddenly opened his mouth and cursed.
Hearing this, Dongfang Ning cursed in his heart. This idiotic brother …
Luo Zheng glared fiercely at Dongfang Yang. A murderous aura flashed on his face. An extremely violent energy was brewing in his body.
Luo Zheng couldn't completely control this violent energy. Hundreds of Dao Accumulations intertwined together, emitting an extremely frightening aura!
This aura didn't spread over a large area. Dongfang Yang, who was in midair, didn't notice it. Zhan Ming, who was standing three meters behind Dongfang Ning, also didn't notice it.
However, the instant Dongfang Ning, who was close by, smelled that aura, his eyes revealed a trace of uncontrollable fear.

Chapter 1911
The True Gods quickly distanced themselves from the large group of Time Wraiths.
After bypassing the Temple of Time and traveling for dozens of miles, the group finally slowed down.
But no one let down their guard.
Except for Luo Zheng, Han Chuyue, and the others, it was not the first time the other True Gods had entered the forbidden area. They had a lot of experience.
The difficulty of a forbidden area was not constant. Instead, it fluctuated.
When they entered the forbidden area, they encountered such a big predicament. This meant that the difficulty of the entire forbidden area had increased. The deeper they went, the more dangerous it was. They might encounter an unresolvable crisis.
But the more difficult it was, the more opportunities there would be. The risk and reward were always proportional. This was why nearly half of the True Gods were willing to take the risk.
As Luo Zheng moved forward, he continued to mix the Taoist Accumulations in the time in his body.
In the Secret Chamber of Time, he tried dozens of times.
The energy obtained after mixing hundreds of Taoist Accumulations was indeed ridiculously powerful.
But he could not control it at all, let alone use it smoothly.
In the time in his body, Luo Zheng relied on his omnipotent ability to firmly bind the Taoist Accumulations together. He was barely able to support it.
Once it was out of his control, the power would run wild in his body like an unbridled wild horse.
If he used this power to defend against the enemy, he might be seriously injured before he could even make a move.
The problem must have come from the mixed Taoist Accumulations.
In the image he saw in the Secret Chamber of Time, he only knew that the figure had mixed 3,000 Taoist Accumulations together and applied them to the sword. He could not tell which hundreds of Taoist Accumulations the figure had mixed. Like Luo Zheng, it was very dangerous to mix them randomly. Now, he could only try again and again in the world in his body, constantly adjusting the types of Taoist Accumulations, hoping to get the best ratio.
"The Goddess Statue is in front. After passing through the Goddess Statue, we can reach the second level. Everyone, be careful. The Goddess Statue this time is not easy to pass," Zhan Ming reminded them.
"Goddess Statue? What is that? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"You will know soon." Zhan Ming smiled. "If you want to enter the second level of the Sea of Time forbidden area, you must pass through here."
Everyone looked worried when they heard Zhan Ming's words.
Other than Luo Zheng and a few other True Gods, the elites of the rich and powerful families had made ample preparations before participating in the Ling Sun Festival. They had a better understanding of the Sea of Time. Hence, even Han Chuyue and Han Biluo were able to answer Luo Zheng in detail.
In the past, it was already difficult to pass through the first level of the Sea of Time. From time to time, there would be True Gods dying here. This time, the difficulty of the Sea of Time forbidden ground had increased greatly. It was likely that the difficulty would be even higher. It was possible that all of them would be wiped out!
"Who has a Stargate? I won't go if there isn't a stargate, "a True God said loudly.
The other True Gods also stopped in their tracks. Some True Gods echoed, "Indeed, if there is no Astral Door, there is almost a 90% chance of survival. I will definitely not participate. Call me again after you have passed through the Goddess statue. I will not take this risk!"
Seeing that Luo Zheng did not understand at all, Han Chuyue explained in a low voice, "The Star Gate is a treasure obtained from the Abyss Demon Domain. It is very rare. According to the current price, it would cost about seventy to eighty thousand Shenwu coins."
"So expensive?" Luo Zheng was shocked.
"Yes." Han Chuyue nodded. "However, the Star Gate is very important, especially in the forbidden ground. It is often very useful. You will understand in a moment."
However, at this time, all the True Gods were silent.
Things like the Star Gate were very expensive. Even if someone had a Star Gate, they would not be willing to take the initiative to offer it. They would hope that others would take the initiative to offer it.
After waiting for a while, Zhan Ming shook his head helplessly. "Alright, I managed to survive this time. This time, I will offer the Star Gate!"
After saying that, a black scroll appeared in Zhan Ming's hand. On the scroll, there were densely packed golden Sanskrit characters.
Zhan Ming grabbed the black scroll and shook it gently. The golden Sanskrit characters on the scroll started to shine continuously. At the same time, a faint halo appeared in front of Zhan Ming. The halo kept spinning and spreading out layers of faint blue light. In the middle, there was a deep blue color. It looked like a gem that was as tall as a person was floating in mid-air.
"This is the Star Gate? It's so beautiful. "Luo Zheng exclaimed. At the same time, he noticed that the Star Gate was emitting a strong Taoist connotation. This was the Taoist connotation emitted by the Space Supreme Dao!
Feeling the strong Taoist connotation in the Star Gate, Luo Zheng's heart jumped slightly.
Although he could not understand the Taoist connotation in it, after activating the Infinite Ruler, he could understand it.
However, he still did not know what the Star Gate was used for. More importantly, once he used the power of his eyes, he was afraid that his identity as an anonymous person would be exposed. He had fought with many True Gods present and had used the power of the Infinite Ruler before.
"Let's wait and see." Luo Zheng suppressed his impulse.
After Zhan Ming used the scroll in his hand to create a Star Gate, he asked, "I have already contributed one Star Gate. Who will contribute the other one?"
The True Gods were silent for a while. Suddenly, Han Cangyan said, "Let me do it!"
After all, Han Cangyan was the most talented Middle True God in the younger generation of Han Jia. Han Jia had given him many good things. He also took out a scroll that was exactly the same as Zhan Ming's. "When we reach the Goddess statue later, I will open this Star Gate."
Zhan Ming nodded and asked, "Now that we have gathered all the Star Gates, can we go together?"
After the other True Gods saw the Star Gate, they were slightly relieved. They followed Zhan Ming and continued to move forward.
In the team, Luo Zheng's face was still full of confusion. "Chuyue, you haven't said what the Star Gate is used for."
Han Chuyue smiled and said, "This Star Gate is equivalent to a transportation channel. However, it is a more special transportation channel. Once you step into the Star Gate, you will be transported to the nearest Star Gate."
"So it is to build a temporary transportation channel?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes. However, the Star Gate has a special characteristic. It will transport you to the nearest Star Gate. Therefore, you need to build two Star Gates," Han Chuyue said. "Later, I will let my brother use the other scroll. This way, the two Star Gates will form a temporary transportation channel."
After listening to Han Chuyue's explanation, Luo Zheng still had doubts in his heart. Why did they need a Star Gate to pass through the Goddess statue? However, he didn't ask any further. He just silently followed the team forward.
After he crossed a narrow mountain pass, he saw a huge canyon. At the same time, he saw a statue that was about ten feet tall in the middle of the canyon.
When Luo Zheng saw the statue clearly, his body shook again. His whole person was stunned.
"This is the Goddess statue?" he muttered.
"Yes. What's wrong?" Han Chuyue looked at Luo Zheng's reaction. Her face revealed a strange expression.
The silent Han Biluo also looked at Luo Zheng …
The statue of the goddess was carved out of a huge gemstone. It was completely transparent and sat on a huge tree stump with her legs together. She raised her head and looked at the sky. Whether it was her expression or her clothes, even the tiniest strand of hair, they were all incomparably fine and subtle.
Luo Zheng once again saw that face. It was almost the same as Luo Yan's, but the temperament was completely different.
After Luo Zheng killed that "mud" creature in the Tie family's underground, he obtained a strange bead. From that bead, he saw this face. However, at that time, Luo Zheng only saw the head of a young girl soaking in a pot. Now, he saw the whole body.

Chapter 1912
After looking at the statue for a while, Luo Zheng asked, "Who is this statue?"
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo shook their heads at the same time.
After all, this was the first time they had entered the Sea of Time. Although they had done their homework before coming and knew that there was a statue here, they didn't know much about it.
"I have two guesses about the identity of this woman." Zhan Ming's voice suddenly sounded in front of Luo Zheng. "She may be the female patriarch of the Blind Tribe."
"Female patriarch?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
The female patriarch of the Blind Tribe never showed her true face to people.
For countless god epochs, no one had seen her true face, not even the saints.
The female patriarch was the ruler of the Sea of Time. It was possible that she had erected her statue here. But why she had erected her statue here was hard to explain.
"The second guess is that she is the leader of the Guardian of Time race." Zhan Ming continued. "But this guess is also groundless. After all, no one knows what kind of existence the Guardian of Time race is. Everyone only knows that there is this race, but no one can verify what kind of existence this race is."
"So that's how it is," Luo Zheng said in a low voice.
Zhan Ming's understanding of the Sea of Time was quite deep. Most of the true gods probably didn't know as much as he did. He couldn't give a definite answer. It would be difficult to verify the identity of this goddess statue.
After Zhan Ming finished speaking, he went with Han Cangyan to set up the star door.
Han Chuyue saw a trace of disappointment on Luo Zheng's face. She blinked and said, "Do you think this goddess statue is a bit familiar?"
Luo Zheng looked strangely at Han Chuyue. "How do you know?"
"Looking at your reaction, I guess you've seen this woman somewhere before? Or does she look like your relatives? "Han Chuyue asked with a smile.
Luo Zheng nodded noncommittally.
"Although I don't know the identity of this goddess statue, if you look carefully, don't you think she looks a bit similar to you?" Han Chuyue asked softly.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's gaze fell on the face of the goddess statue again.
Just as Han Chu Yue had said, he was indeed somewhat similar.
This was not surprising.
The appearance of this goddess statue was almost exactly the same as Luo Yan, and he and Luo Yan were blood related siblings, so there were some similarities between them.
However, Han Chuyue's words reminded Luo Zheng.
It couldn't be a coincidence that the statue of the goddess looked so similar to Luo Yan.
Could it be that the statue of the goddess was really Luo Yan and his mother?
It was just a statue in front of him, but Luo Zheng had seen this woman before. His mother only had a head left now?
For a moment, Luo Zheng found it hard to accept this conjecture...
Looking at Luo Zheng's complicated expression, Han Chuyue smiled faintly. She knew that Luo Zheng's background was complicated. This statue must have some connection with him. Looking at Luo Zheng's tangled expression, she comforted him. "Don't think too much. There are many things in this world that are not in your control. Perhaps the truth is not what you think?"
"You're right." Luo Zheng nodded silently.
It was useless to think too much about these things. Sooner or later, there would be a day when he would know the truth. Then he cast his gaze below the statue of the goddess.
Han Cangyan unfurled a scroll below the statue of the goddess and opened another Star Door.
After this azure Star Door appeared, Han Cangyan drilled into it and disappeared in an instant.
After a while, Han Cangyan could be seen flying over from the high slope. At the same time, he waved at everyone. The two Star Doors were connected to each other, and a temporary transmission array was successfully built.
After Han Cangyan returned, Zhan Ming suddenly raised his voice and said, "Now that the Star Door has been successfully built, there is a way out. This time, guarding the goddess statue may be more difficult than any other time, but the rewards we will get from it will be more than any other time, so I hope everyone will do their best!"
After saying that, Zhan Ming took the lead and walked towards the goddess statue. He stretched out his hand and patted the bottom of the goddess statue lightly. A complete palm print was left on the goddess statue.
Then, the other upper true gods also walked over and left a faint palm print on the base of the goddess statue. Soon, it was the middle true god's turn, the lower true god's turn … Finally, it was Luo Zheng, Han Liusu, Han Chuyue and the rest.
Just as Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and patted the goddess statue, outside the Sea of Time, the eyes of the Blind Tribe's matriarch flashed slightly under her mask. She revealed a look of concern.
"Pa!"
Then, Luo Zheng gently patted the bottom of the goddess statue. Just like the others, he only left a faint palm print and nothing else happened.
The matriarch stared for a long time and a trace of doubt appeared in her eyes. However, the doubt was quickly replaced by disappointment and regret …
The change in the matriarch's expression was not noticed by the other sages. These sages' gazes were focused on the Perfect True Gods. A group of Perfect True Gods led by Mu Xuerong and the other Perfect True Gods were frantically escaping from the third level of the Sea of Time. They seemed to have angered a huge monster in the Sea of Time.
At the bottom of the goddess statue, all the True Gods' palm prints were imprinted on it. This tall goddess statue suddenly emitted a faint green light. The green light was like a green liquid that slowly rose from the bottom of the goddess statue and filled the entire goddess statue.
"Everyone, pay attention. Although this is not the first time many of you have entered the Sea of Time, I still have to remind you that only by passing through here can we qualify to enter the second level of the Sea of Time. In a while, there will be a large number of ferocious spirits attacking. We have to do everything we can to protect the goddess statue. Once the goddess statue is completely destroyed, we will lose the opportunity to enter the second level! If you can't hold on any longer, you can use the stargate to teleport outside for a short rest. After that, please immediately return to participate in the battle … "Zhan Ming said in a calm voice. Suddenly, the canyon trembled. A layer of colorful light curtain appeared around them and enveloped the entire canyon. Seeing the changes in the canyon, Zhan Ming smiled faintly." It has begun! "
"So, after activating the goddess statue, the canyon became a sealed space." Luo Zheng's eyes flickered slightly. The reason why he left a stargate here was to leave an escape route for himself. Otherwise, if he was defeated, he would not be able to escape even if he wanted to.
It was no wonder that some True Gods were clamoring earlier that they were unwilling to continue without the stargate. This stargate was a way to save their lives.
"Rumble …"
The entire canyon began to tremble. At the same time, from the four passes around the canyon, a large black mass emerged.
At the same time, a strange rhythm sounded in everyone's ears. It was as if countless people were breathing heavily, and the breathing sounds were uniform.
"Black butterflies," Luo Zheng said faintly.
"This is the first wave of ferocious beasts. Lie Rhythmic Butterflies are a type of ferocious spirit in the Dark Flame Forbidden Land. Everyone, be careful. The Lie Rhythmic Butterflies themselves are not powerful, but the flames that they turn into after they die are extremely difficult to deal with. Be careful not to be surrounded by them," Zhan Ming reminded.
Four groups of Lie Rhythmic Butterflies emerged from the four passes in the canyon. They fluttered toward the goddess statue in a spiral shape. As they fluttered continuously, the rhythm of their breathing became more and more intense!
"Go!" Zhan Ming ordered. He took the lead and rushed out.
All the True Gods around the goddess statue also rushed toward the Lie Rhythmic Butterflies one after another. Luo Zheng was naturally no exception.
"Hmph, it's just Lie Rhythmic Butterflies. Look at how you guys are acting as if you are facing a great enemy. I have also wandered around the Dark Flame Forbidden Land a few times. These butterflies are not worth mentioning!" Leng Linyue laughed softly. His palm turned into a claw and he raised it high above his head. After a blood-red light bloomed from his fingertips, he waved his claw.
"Swish swish swish swish —"
A few thin lines of blood spiraled like waves and drew countless irregular arcs in the air. They weaved into a fine net and covered the group of Lie Rhythmic Butterflies in front of him.
All the Lie Rhythmic Butterflies that came into contact with the lines of blood were instantly cut into pieces. The broken black butterfly wings fluttered in all directions …
At the same time, Leng Linyue's figure retreated quickly.
"Phew, phew …"
The dead Lie Rhythmic Butterflies instantly exploded into balls of blazing white flames behind him.
Just like what Zhan Ming had said, the Lie Rhythmic Butterflies themselves were not powerful. However, after every Lie Rhythmic Butterfly died, it would explode into a ball of blazing flames called "Burning Spirit Fire." The Burning Spirit Fire could cover an area with a radius of dozens of feet. Even a True God would not be spared if he was engulfed by the Burning Spirit Fire.

Chapter 1913
Dark Flame Forbidden Land was a Grade Two Forbidden Land. Most of the Real Gods present had experience in it and they knew how powerful the Lie Yun Butterflies were.
For example, Han Chuyue, Luo Zheng and the others didn't know at first. After Zhan Ming's reminder and after seeing the other Real Gods take action, they naturally understood that the Lie Yun Butterflies were very weak. The powerful part was the Burning Spirit Fire. As long as they maintained a certain distance to kill the Lie Yun Butterflies, they wouldn't have to worry.
There was an exquisite oil paper umbrella in Han Chuyue's hand. She grabbed the handle of the oil paper umbrella and shook it gently. With a "pop" sound, the oil paper umbrella opened. Then, she turned the oil paper umbrella and the surface of the umbrella started to spin rapidly.
"Swish swish swish …"
Countless arm-length light needles shot out from the surface of the umbrella. The light needles covered the group of Lie Yun Butterflies like a rainstorm.
"Huff huff huff huff huff …"
One by one, the Blaze Butterfly was nailed to the ground. Soon after, they turned into balls of flames and exploded. The vigorous raging flames spread out and released a torrential heat wave towards Han Chuyue. Before they could get close to Han Chuyue, the terrifying heat engulfed and burned the hair in front of her forehead.
She quickly blocked the oil paper umbrella in front of her and blocked the Burning Spirit Fire outside. At the same time, she stroked the bangs in front of her forehead and revealed a gloomy expression. Then, she turned her head and looked not far away. When she saw Luo Zheng, she exclaimed, "So fast!"
Luo Zheng shuttled back and forth in the canyon. Compared to the others who tried their best to avoid the Lie Yun Butterflies, Luo Zheng was much braver. Perhaps in the eyes of some Real Gods, Luo Zheng was simply doing something that didn't want his life.
With a casual wave of his hand, countless rays of golden light emanated from his hand. These rays of golden light were derived from the Five Elements Divine Daos of the Metal Element. These rays of golden light gently flashed around him, and a large swarm of Blaze Butterflies turned into fine powder. Soon after, they erupted into flames.
These raging flames were fierce, but Luo Zheng was not afraid at all. Even if he was really caught in the flames, they would not be able to burn him, not to mention that it was only a stream of scalding heat that swept towards him.
"Swish, swish, swish …"
As the golden light covered the area, thousands of Fierce Rhythm Butterflies were destroyed. His killing of the Fierce Rhythm Butterflies was like the autumn wind sweeping the fallen leaves. His efficiency was surprisingly high!
Many True Gods thought that dealing with Lie Yundie was a piece of cake. However, when they saw Luo Zheng's killing technique, they were all surprised …
In the canyon, the only true gods who could match Luo Zheng's speed were those upper true gods.
These upper True Gods all had the ability to wander around Grade Two Forbidden Lands on their own. The Blaze Butterfly was merely a ferocious creature commonly seen in Grade Two Forbidden Lands. Why would they place it in their eyes? The upper-level true gods were shuttling through the Fierce Rhythmic Butterflies. Everywhere they went, there would be large groups of Fierce Rhythmic Butterflies falling. Large balls of flames were rising.
The number of Fierce Rhythmic Butterflies flying out of the four entrances of the canyon was shocking. In the blink of an eye, there were tens of thousands of them floating out. However, in front of the True Gods, no matter how many there were, it was meaningless.
After the time it took for an incense stick to burn, no more butterflies came out of the mountain passes.
"Let's call it a day," Zhan Ming said.
"It doesn't seem like a big deal …"
"It's too simple!"
"I've killed at least fifty thousand Fierce Rhythmic Butterflies!"
Many True Gods looked relaxed. To them, killing the Fierce Rhythmic Butterflies was too easy. They passed through it casually.
The confidence of a True God was no different from that of a mortal. It was easy for them to be inflated.
"Don't be careless. This is only the first wave. The difficulty will get higher and higher," Zhan Ming reminded them when he heard them.
Apart from Zhan Ming, a few other upper true gods and some middle true gods who had once wandered into the forbidden land of Sea of Time looked cautious. They didn't look relaxed at all. One of the upper true gods said, "Indeed, the number of Fierce Rhythmic Butterflies in the first wave is three times more than the number of Fierce Rhythmic Butterflies we've encountered before."
"Judging from the number, the difficulty this time might be three times higher," Zhan Ming said solemnly.
Hearing that, Luo Zheng creased his eyebrows.
For safety reasons, the Real Gods had to prepare two Star Doors for their escape every time. It meant that the difficulty of passing through the canyon was really high. If they were careless, they would die.
According to what Zhan Ming said, the difficulty would be three times higher. He could imagine how difficult it would be next.
After passing through the first wave, everyone took a short break. As the canyon shook again, the second wave of monsters surged over from the four entrances.
"Rustle rustle rustle …"
Everyone saw countless red scorpions the size of feet converging into a red tide and surging down. As they crawled continuously, they made a terrifying sound.
"Poisonous Wolf Scorpion!"
"Poisonous creatures!"
"Be careful. The tail of the Poisonous Wolf Scorpion can shoot poisonous needles …"
The Venomous Wolf Scorpion was a famous venomous creature in the Divine Realm. Every Venomous Wolf Scorpion had a thin poison needle on its tail. This poison needle was hidden in the cavity of its tailbone. When attacking a target, the Venomous Wolf Scorpion would inject a powerful pressure into its tail, causing the poison needle to explosively shoot out!
What was even stranger was that the venom of the Poisonous Wolf Scorpion was very special.
Ordinary Gods would only be paralyzed after being pierced, but a True God would explode and die if they were pierced by the poisonous needle.
The venom would quickly drill into the dantian, throwing the Godhead into chaos. Once the Godhead was in chaos, it would cause the entire inner world to turn upside down, eventually causing the entire world to collapse and shatter …
As for warriors and Gods below the True God level, because they did not have a Godhead, the destructive power of the venom was not great.
In the Divine Territory, there were True Gods who cultivated the "Poison God Path". They wanted to capture the Poisonous Wolf Scorpion and extract the venom from it. Naturally, this was an extremely powerful method. However, once the venom left the Poisonous Wolf Scorpion, it would rapidly lose its effect. Thus, the venom could not be used.
Many True Gods were unwilling to provoke these red scorpions. Thus, when they saw so many scorpions surging out, many True Gods revealed hesitant expressions. Although the Poisonous Wolf Scorpion was considered a low-level vicious creature and was not very powerful, touching the venom would still cost them their lives. Who would be willing to take this risk?
"Don't be afraid. The Poisonous Wolf Scorpion's poisonous needle is not difficult to dodge. Just be careful," Zhan Ming encouraged.
"Hualala …"
While those True Gods were still hesitating, Luo Zheng had already made his move expressionlessly.
With a push of his hand, a fan-shaped layer of raging flames spread along the ground. Just as it was about to burn those Poisonous Wolf Scorpions, what surprised Luo Zheng was that these Poisonous Wolf Scorpions were rather nimble. They all jumped up and actually crossed the layer of fan-shaped flames. At the same time, their tails were raised high towards him!
"Xiu xiu xiu xiu …"
Over a thousand Poisonous Wolf Scorpions simultaneously shot out black poisonous needles from their tailbones.
These poisonous needles were extremely thin and difficult to distinguish with the naked eye. They were indeed quite a threat to True Gods.
"Be careful, Luo Tianxing!" Han Chuyue cried out in alarm.
"Hmph," Luo Zheng coldly snorted. His figure floated out like a ghost.
"Ding ding ding ding ding ding …"
After those poisonous needles missed, they all nailed to the ground.
"Destroy for me!"
Luo Zheng lightly flicked his finger. A thick profound lightning bolt shot out.
"Clap clap clap …"
This profound lightning bolt, which was as thick as a water bucket, continuously split into tens of thousands of lightning bolts, each as thick as a thumb. They tightly covered a large area.
The Poisonous Wolf Scorpions' reactions were extremely nimble, exceeding Luo Zheng's expectations. However, no matter how fast they were, they would not be able to dodge these lightning bolts …
"Clap clap clap clap …"
Wherever these lightning bolts landed, the ground was instantly charred. All the Poisonous Wolf Scorpions were also turned into charcoal dust by the lightning bolts.
When the True Gods saw this, they stared fixedly at the ground. Their fear of the Poisonous Wolf Scorpions' poison had completely dissipated. In its place was greed.
As it turned out, after these Poisonous Wolf Scorpions were killed, the ground turned into Shenwu Coins!
Luo Zheng had just casually killed thousands of Poisonous Wolf Scorpions, and now there were thousands of Shenwu Coins on the ground …
No matter how terrifying the Poisonous Wolf Scorpions' poison was, for so many Shenwu Coins, it was worth it for them to risk their lives!

Chapter 1914
"Shenwu coins are already being rewarded in the second wave." Zhan Ming shook his head slightly.
"What's wrong with this forbidden area …" Another upper-level True God said.
It was not unusual for killing monsters in the canyon to be rewarded with Shenwu coins. Some warriors had even obtained faith treasures before.
However, Shenwu coins would usually appear during the seventh and eighth waves of monsters. Moreover, Shenwu coins would only appear sporadically. The amount was far less than now …
Just as they understood the rules of forbidden areas, the greater the reward, the higher the difficulty, and the greater the risk.
"Forget it. If we can get a good reward here, we can just give up on the second level of the Sea of Time." Zhan Ming shrugged. Then, a raging battle intent bloomed from his body.
At the same time, a tattoo appeared on his shoulder. It was a majestic blue lion head.
When his battle intent boiled, the lion head would quietly appear, emitting a blue light. It was vivid and lifelike.
"Whoosh …"
In the blink of an eye, Zhan Ming rushed into the group of Venomous Wolf Scorpions.
The venomous stingers of these Venomous Wolf Scorpions were indeed powerful. However, True Gods would only be hit when they were caught off guard.
The Real Gods who were present maintained a high level of vigilance. With their advantage in speed, it was not difficult for them to avoid the scorpions. Upper Real Gods like Zhan Ming seemed to have entered a place where no one could stop them. They were rampaging among the Venomous Wolf Scorpions.
He was too lazy to dodge the thin venomous stingers that shot at him. Before the stingers could even get close, they were shattered by the battle intent attached to his body.
Killing a Venomous Wolf Scorpion would produce one Shenwu coin. Faced with the endless stream of Venomous Wolf Scorpions, all the True Gods went crazy.
These True Gods ignored the power of the Venomous Wolf Scorpions and rushed to attack. Each of them used their fiercest methods to kill the Venomous Wolf Scorpions. Then, they quickly swept the thousands of Shenwu coins on the ground into their sumeru rings!
Attacking once was equivalent to exploring other small forbidden areas for a few years. Who would be willing to let go of such an opportunity?
Luo Zheng's speed in killing the Venomous Wolf Scorpions was not slow. After being the first to attack, he seized the initiative and killed his way through the Venomous Wolf Scorpions and rushed into the distance.
Those Venomous Wolf Scorpions surrounded him like a blood-red flood, and shot poisonous needles with deadly venom at him.
However, he didn't care about these poisonous needles at all. He condensed a watermelon-sized lightning ball in his hand. In the center of this lightning ball, there were four or five smaller lightning balls. When the lightning balls collided with each other, they made ear-piercing cracking sounds, as if they would explode at any time in his hand.
He grabbed the watermelon-sized lightning ball and threw it heavily onto the ground!
Boom!
A circular lightning bolt exploded under his feet. With him as the center, all the Venomous Wolf Scorpions within a radius of several hundred feet were struck by the spreading lightning. Their bright red shells were instantly charred black, and then turned into a pile of black powder …
"Pata, pata, pata …"
Hundreds and thousands of Shenwu coins rolled out from the ground.
Luo Zheng casually waved his hand, and wisps of blue wind swept out. All of the shenwu coins were swept up by the invisible wind, and when they were two feet off the ground, they rapidly spun around Luo Zheng and finally drilled into his spatial ring.
"12,000 Shenwu coins." Luo Zheng's lips curved into a smile. He flew up and rushed into another group of Venomous Wolf Scorpions.
In the canyon, only Luo Zheng, Zhan Ming, and other upper True Gods dared to play like this …
Intermediate True Gods like Han Cangyan and Leng Linyue might have this ability, but they didn't have the confidence to dodge all the Venomous Wolf Scorpions' needles. Therefore, they could only rely on the goddess statue to kill the Venomous Wolf Scorpions. Naturally, their efficiency was much lower.
Boom!
Luo Zheng jumped up and stepped into the group of Venomous Wolf Scorpions again. With this stomp, hundreds of Venomous Wolf Scorpions around him were smashed to pieces. He did the same thing. Another lightning ball condensed in his hand, and he slammed it heavily onto the ground!
The group of Venomous Wolf Scorpions disappeared before his eyes again, leaving behind only piles of Shenwu coins waiting for him to harvest.
However, before Luo Zheng could put these Shenwu coins into his spatial ring, a stocky True God suddenly rushed over and shouted coldly, "Scram!" Then, a layer of earthy yellow Dao Connotation appeared on his fist. This Dao Connotation was very condensed and thick. He slammed his fist into the ground.
"Rumble …"
The surrounding ground began to wriggle, and the Shenwu coins also gathered toward this intermediate True God. With a sudden wave of his hand, all the Shenwu coins were sucked into his spatial ring.
This series of actions was extremely fast. After the intermediate True God collected all the Shenwu coins, he tilted his head and looked at Luo Zheng indifferently. "These Venomous Wolf Scorpions were supposed to be killed by me. I don't blame you for snatching them first. Scram, hehe!"
Luo Zheng glanced at this intermediate True God indifferently.
"You don't like it? Kid? "This intermediate True God stared at Luo Zheng and sneered with a provocative look.
Even though Luo Zheng had displayed extraordinary strength in the Time Temple, it was still not worth mentioning in the eyes of this intermediate True God. He obviously did not take Luo Zheng, a Lower True God, seriously.
Not far away, Zhan Ming and the others frowned when they saw this scene. However, they did not interfere.
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly, and a faint killing intent flashed in his eyes. Then, he turned his head and leaped up, rushing into another group of Venomous Wolf Scorpions.
"Pipa!"
A huge thunder ball spread out, and a large group of Venomous Wolf Scorpions turned into charcoal powder.
However, what Luo Zheng did not expect was that the intermediate True God would rush over again and punch the ground. As the ground wriggled, he snatched the Shenwu coins again. At the same time, he said proudly, "I forgot to remind you that the Shenwu coins here are mine too!"
Luo Zheng ignored this intermediate True God and continued to rush into other groups of Venomous Wolf Scorpions. He used the Dark Thunder Divine Way to kill these groups of Venomous Wolf Scorpions. He did not even pick up the Shenwu coins. After killing the group of Venomous Wolf Scorpions, he continued to look for the next target. Then, the intermediate True God followed behind him and sucked away all the Shenwu coins.
At first, everyone was in a hurry to kill the Venomous Wolf Scorpions, so no one noticed the strange movements of these two.
As the number of Venomous Wolf Scorpions swarming out of the mountain passes decreased, many people began to notice this strange scene.
"The Venomous Wolf Scorpions that Luo Tianxing killed seemed to have been snatched away by Dongfang Yang!"
"That guy can still swallow his anger …"
"What else can he do? If he dares to resist, Dongfang Yang will kill him easily. "
The True Gods whispered among themselves.
Dongfang Yang, the genius of the Dongfang Family.
In this floating island ranking, Dongfang Yang participated in the leapfrog battles with the cultivation of an intermediate True God. He won seven battles in a row against an upper True God and lost one battle, earning a reward of 1.3 million points!
If it were not for the appearance of the anonymous person, he would probably be the most dazzling person in this floating island ranking.
Mu Ning stood with her hands behind her back, looking at Luo Zheng with a pair of heroic eyes. She did not speak. She wanted to see how Luo Zheng would respond.
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo were both depressed and worried. They were depressed because they could not help much. This Dongfang Yang came from the Dongfang Family, a rich and powerful family that the Han Family could not afford to offend. Moreover, Dongfang Yang's strength was unfathomable.
Han Liusu frowned as she stared at Luo Zheng. Although she appeared to be very cold, she had been silently observing Luo Zheng along the way. She knew Luo Zheng's temperament. If Dongfang Yang forced him, Luo Zheng would definitely make a move. However, Luo Zheng was only a lower True God. How could he gain the upper hand against an intermediate True God?
Leng Linyue was overjoyed when he saw this. He had long wanted to kill Luo Zheng, but he did not have a suitable time to make a move. Zhan Ming was the leader of this hastily put together team. Luo Zheng had saved Zhan Ming's life before. If Leng Linyue made a move, Zhan Ming would definitely intervene.
Now that Dongfang Yang had found Luo Zheng, it was enough to make him suffer!
Luo Zheng acted as if he did not see it. He continued to rush towards another group of Venomous Wolf Scorpions. He had just killed this group of Venomous Wolf Scorpions when Dongfang Yang took away all the Shenwu Coins.
Now, most of the Venomous Wolf Scorpions in the canyon had been killed. Only a few of them were slowly crawling towards the goddess statue.

Chapter 1915
Hearing this, Dongfang Yang turned his head and stared at Luo Zheng with a smile. "What did you just say?"
Why would Dongfang Yang care about a lower true god?
He had ventured into the Forbidden Land countless times and had taken the lives of the rich and powerful.
Even if they were the disciples of the Sage Emperor, or even the descendants of the Sage Emperor, he would still kill them. How could those clans not even dare to fart?
Dongfang Yang picked up Luo Zheng's Shenwu Coins and felt that it was only right.
With his strength, killing these Venomous Wolf Scorpions was also very easy. He was even more efficient than Luo Zheng.
However, he felt that Luo Zheng didn't have the right to kill the Venomous Wolf Scorpions like this!
The lower True Gods were all hiding under the goddess statue, carefully hunting the Venomous Wolf Scorpions while the middle True Gods were further away from the goddess statue. Only the upper True Gods could fearlessly dive into the Venomous Wolf Scorpions to hunt. Therefore, the upper True Gods were the ones who earned the most shenwu coins.
Why was this kid the same as an upper true god?
Dongfang Yang himself was not an Upper True God, but his abilities were enough for him to gain a foothold among the Upper True Gods. In the Arena of Gods, when he faced Upper True Gods who could fly in the sky and burrow into the ground, he was able to win seven matches in a row.
When the other upper true gods saw Luo Zheng's behavior, they were more or less displeased.
After all, Luo Zheng wasn't slow in harvesting the Venomous Wolf Scorpions. He took a share of their Shenwu Coins.
However, Luo Zheng relied on his own strength and courage. They couldn't say anything.
Dongfang Yang's actions were more straightforward. He took all of Luo Zheng's Shenwu Coins. If Luo Zheng resisted, he would definitely teach him a lesson.
He didn't expect Luo Zheng to speak up in the end. He hadn't said a word from beginning to end and had done everything for him.
"Sir, you've taken all of my Shenwu Coins and you plan to leave just like that?" Luo Zheng asked again.
The corner of Dongfang Yang's mouth twitched as he chuckled, "What a joke. I've already said that the Poisonous Wolf Scorpions you killed belonged to me. You killed my Poisonous Wolf Scorpions and I didn't intend to cause you any trouble. How dare you ask me for Shenwu coins?"
The people from the Dongfang family were famous for being overbearing. Many of the elites from the wealthy families present were also silent as they watched the situation develop with cold eyes.
Zhan Ming couldn't stand it anymore. He casually killed some scattered Poisonous Wolf Scorpions and said loudly, "Dongfang Yang, these monsters don't belong to anyone. According to the rules, whoever kills them will get them. You are breaking the rules!"
During the process of killing the Venomous Wolf Scorpions, all the True Gods were very restrained and did not fight over the loot.
Before Dongfang Yang could say anything, a sinister laugh came from behind Zhan Ming. "Rules? You want to talk about rules with my Dongfang family? Aren't you a little too naive? "
Hearing that voice, Zhan Ming's face turned cold. He turned his head and said, "Dongfang Ning, what do you mean?"
The one who spoke was the upper True God of the Dongfang family, Dongfang Ning. This person had long arms and legs and his body was like a bamboo pole. He had followed the team without saying a word. Now he said, "Zhan Ming, do you really think you are our leader?"
Because Zhan Ming had successfully lured away the Time Wraiths, it was logical for him to lead the team forward. Now, everyone was indeed listening to Zhan Ming's orders, but the upper True Gods did not necessarily care about Zhan Ming.
"I, Zhan Ming, have never thought of myself as a leader. I'm just exploring the Sea of Time. Everyone should look after each other and be more united. Naturally, it will be easier!" Zhan Ming said coldly. The other True Gods, including many upper True Gods, nodded at his words. At least Zhan Ming had selflessly contributed a Starry Door, leaving a way out for everyone.
Dongfang Ning smiled slightly and said, "I'm not targeting you, but it's better if you don't interfere in my Dongfang family's affairs. If those two little guys want to cause trouble, even I won't interfere!"
His words immediately drew a line. It was a warning to Zhan Ming. Both of them were upper True Gods, so they should not interfere in the conflict between Dongfang Yang and Luo Zheng.
Zhan Ming frowned. Since Dongfang Ning had said so, it was not good for him to interfere. It was not that he was afraid of Dongfang Ning, but in this situation, it was not a rational decision for two upper True Gods to fight.
But after all, Luo Zheng had saved his life. He knew the character of the Dongfang family. These guys would kill at the slightest disagreement. He could not just stand by and watch Dongfang Yang kill Luo Zheng.
He thought about it and could only persuade Luo Zheng. "Brother, I'll compensate you for the Shenwu coins that Dongfang Yang took away!"
Zhan Ming had obtained a lot of Shenwu coins just now. Compensating Luo Zheng was not a big deal. Besides, he still had plenty of opportunities to obtain more Shenwu coins.
The idea he proposed was already very good. Luo Zheng would probably agree.
"Thank you."
Luo Zheng cupped his hands and smiled at Zhan Ming. "I appreciate Brother Zhan Ming's kindness." Then he turned to stare at Dongfang Yang and continued, "But this dog has been following me around and picking up bones for half a day. Naturally, he has to spit it out with interest!"
Luo Zheng's words stunned Dongfang Yang. No one had ever dared to mock him like this. He then laughed wildly!
"You? Hahaha … You must be a foreign warrior. Otherwise, you wouldn't be so stupid. Do you know who I am? If you don't, let me teach you … "
Dongfang Yang was still talking, but Luo Zheng did not intend to give him a chance to continue.
A sharp light flashed in his eyes. He pushed off the ground with the tip of his foot. Under an invisible wave, he turned into a gust of wind and arrived in front of Dongfang Yang. "The only thing I need to learn from you is how to talk nonsense. But I don't want to learn."
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng's explosive speed was already very strange. It only took a very short time for him to explode from a standstill.
Moreover, he had taken Dongfang Yang by surprise. Dongfang Yang did not expect Luo Zheng to attack. Luo Zheng instantly closed in on Dongfang Yang and struck him with his elbow!
"Bang!"
The power of this fierce collision was far beyond Dongfang Yang's imagination.
The great power poured into Dongfang Yang's body. He stared at Luo Zheng with shock in his eyes. His body kept retreating until it rolled in the distance and hit the ground heavily.
Dongfang Yang had seen Luo Zheng attack several times. He knew that Luo Zheng's power was extraordinary. Besides, he had cultivated the Dark Thunder Divine Way and the Five Elements Divine Way.
In Dongfang Yang's eyes, such a foundation was excellent, but it was only excellent.
What kind of genius was Dongfang Yang?
There were only a few middle-level True Gods who could fight against higher-level True Gods and gain a foothold in the Arena of Gods.
Dongfang Yang's cultivation had not reached the middle-level True God, but he already had the battle strength of an upper-level True God.
"How dare you sneak attack me …" Dongfang Yang roared angrily. He turned over and stood up. A thick yellow halo appeared on the surface of his body. It was the Taoist Connotation of the Thick Earth Divine Way.
As soon as he steadied himself, he saw a figure flash in front of him. Luo Zheng had approached him again and punched Dongfang Yang's head from above.
Although Luo Zheng's punch was extremely powerful, the defense of the Thick Earth Divine Way was amazing. Luo Zheng's power spread along the yellow halo on the surface of Dongfang Yang's body and was immediately transmitted to the ground under Dongfang Yang's feet.
"Rumble …"
The ground around Dongfang Yang was cracked …
The ground of the whole canyon was shaking.
Luo Zheng's power spread out and was absorbed by the ground …
This ability was similar to the "power transfer" that Luo Zheng had in the universe. As long as Dongfang Yang had his back to the ground, he would have a very strong defense. This was also the characteristic of the Thick Earth Divine Way!
"You're just relying on brute force. Hahaha, it's not enough," Dongfang Yang laughed.
Luo Zheng's expression did not change. Suddenly, a blood-red light flashed in his eyes, and a fierce killing intent spread out from his body!
"God Slaying Dao?"
In the distance, Mu Ning took two steps forward and stared at Luo Zheng. Her sharp and charming eyes had a faint trace of expectation in them. If this kid really used the Killing God Dao, then there was a 100% chance that he was Anonymous.
At this moment, Luo Zheng did not have too many concerns.
"Blood Destruction Bite!"
A blood snake suddenly rushed out from Luo Zheng's hand. This blood snake ignored the Thick Earth Taoist Connotation on the surface of Dongfang Yang's body and bit a small wound on Dongfang Yang's neck.
Before Dongfang Yang could react, Luo Zheng once again threw a punch horizontally!
"Bang!"
With a muffled sound, Dongfang Yang was smashed a thousand feet away.
Under the protection of the Thick Earth Divine Way, this punch still could not hurt Dongfang Yang.

Chapter 1916
Bloody Desolation Bite!
Mu Ning's eyes widened when she saw this. She stared at Luo Zheng for a while before gradually calming down.
Although Mu Xuerong's analysis had made Mu Ning highly suspicious of Luo Zheng's identity, seeing Luo Zheng use the Bloody Desolation Bite with her own eyes was a hundred percent proof.
"It really is you …" Mu Ning murmured in a low voice.
After losing to an anonymous person in the Arena of the Gods, Mu Ning was furious. After leaving the Arena of the Gods, she locked herself in her room for a day and a night. Her strong and proud personality did not allow her to lose, let alone lose to a martial artist who had proven himself to be a Divinity.
She did not care whether Anonymous would join the Dongfang family or not.
Her only goal was to find out Anonymous's true identity and have a chance to challenge Anonymous again. This idea was like a poisonous snake in her heart. A woman with her character was like this. She was full of the desire to explore the strong people she truly admired!
Now she was finally sure that her goal was this young man in front of her!
Mu Ning thought that her elder sister Mu Xuerong's thoughts were the same as hers. What she didn't know was that Mu Xuerong had already determined that the anonymous person had another identity. Mu Xuerong's goal was to find the anonymous person and kill him.
"Huh? Bloody Desolation Bite, Mu Haiji's self-created Bloody Desolation Bite! "
"How did this guy learn it …"
"Dongfang Yang has lost so much blood. It's enough for him to suffer."
Many of the True Gods present recognized the "Desolate Blood Destruction Bite", and some of the True Gods looked at Mu Ning with strange gazes.
Even the Saints outside the Sea of Time had strange expressions on their faces. Bloody Desolation Bite was derived from the God Killing Path. It was also an Outer Path Divine Ability that the Mu family would never pass on to outsiders. How could this guy use it?
Dongfang Chunjun also looked at Luo Zheng in the canyon without saying anything. The disciples of the Dongfang family were not so easy to defeat, not to mention that Dongfang Yang's cultivation was one level higher than Luo Zheng's.
Dongfang Yang stood in the same place. The excessive loss of blood made his face pale. At the same time, he felt weak and powerless. He clapped his hand and a small red pill appeared in his hand. He flicked his finger and the pill shot into Dongfang Yang's throat like an arrow.
After the pill dissolved in an instant, a rich blood energy was released, and his originally pale face quickly regained its color.
It was the Jade Blood Golden Pill, a Sixth Revolution Golden Pill, and it could rapidly replenish the energy of blood!
Because he had replenished too much blood energy, his face even showed a trace of unhealthy redness!
Then Dongfang Yang asked sternly, "I always thought you were from the Han Family. I didn't expect you to be from the Mu Family."
The Dongfang family was the leader of the League of Wealthy Families. Generally speaking, the Dongfang family wouldn't target the other families in the League. Therefore, Dongfang Yang had a good relationship with Leng Linyue and Mu Ning. If Luo Zheng was really from the Mu family, he should have made it clear.
Moreover, the fact that Luo Zheng had absorbed the other party's Taoist Connotation under an anonymous identity had not been spread. Mu Ning had also not told anyone about this matter.
This was why Dongfang Yang had such a reaction.
"He's not from the Mu Family. He really is from the Han Family, and he's anonymous!" Mu Ning said loudly, "The Spirit Art he mastered, the Blood Destruction Desolate Bite, was something he secretly learned while fighting me!"
"Crack, crack …"
There were still a few True Gods in the canyon who couldn't be bothered with the disturbance here. They smashed the scattered Venomous Wolf Scorpions into pieces, collecting a small amount of Shenwu Coins.
Most of the True Gods looked at Luo Zheng.
Apart from Han Liusu, Han Biluo, and Han Chuyue, who already knew, the other True Gods were all surprised.
"Isn't the anonymous person a Proof of Divinity expert? This guy is a lower True God … "
"He must have broken through after the floating island ranking. No wonder he's so strong. He could defeat the number one Mu Ning when he was just a Proof of Divinity expert. With his current cultivation, he definitely has the ability to fight Dongfang Yang!"
"What's the true origin of this anonymous person? The Han Family's younger generation is full of monsters … but they can't possibly cultivate such a monster, right?"
The True Gods whispered to each other.
Outside the Sea of Time Forbidden Land, the Saints looked at Luo Zheng with a hint of interest.
"So he's the anonymous person. Haha, Emperor Han Qing, your Han Family's fortune is not small." Tang Lun laughed. "If it weren't for him, your Han Family would have fallen out of the top ten in this floating island ranking!"
When the floating island's points were calculated, Han Liusu suddenly gained nearly two million points. Because of her, the floating island's ranking had changed.
However, no one was a fool. This anonymous person didn't want to reveal his identity, so he chose to hide it. The anonymous person had to be from Han Family!
Emperor Han Qing also stared at Luo Zheng, then said indifferently, "This little guy came from our Han Family's ancestral land. He's not a direct descendant of our Han Family."
"Oh?" Tang Lun's face revealed a shocked expression, "A grass-root Real God from another tribe can grow to this level? That's even more shocking! "
Dongfang Chunjun's eyes flashed. He seemed to want to say something, but Emperor Han Qing beat him to it. "Great Sage Chunjun, I've heard that the Dongfang Family seems to be interested in the anonymous person. They want to recruit him to their floating island. I didn't know his identity before, but now I know. If the Dongfang Family is willing to accept him, it would be this child's honor!"
Many of the Saints and Quasi Sages had strange looks on their faces when they heard this. Some even looked at the emperor with disdain.
Powerful families valued their own talents the most.
A monster like the anonymous person was a genius that could only be encountered by chance.
It wasn't easy for Han Jia to produce such a monster, but Emperor Han Qing was willing to let him go. It seemed that he was using the anonymous person to curry favor with Dongfang Chunjun. For a Saint who ruled over the universe, this was far too despicable!
Aunt Hanjiu bit her lip, a trace of anger in her eyes. She felt humiliated.
Although she already knew that Emperor Han Qing would definitely hand over Luo Zheng, it was too shameful for the Han Family to fawn on Dongfang Chunjun in front of all the Saints!
Fortunately, Luo Zheng had two identities. It wouldn't be a big deal if the first one was exposed. After the Sea of Time forbidden area, Luo Zheng would go to Buzhou Spirit Mountain. No one would be able to do anything to him then!
If his second identity was exposed, it would be troublesome. If that happened, Luo Zheng would most likely die.
Dongfang Chunjun smiled and said, "You're right. I asked Dongfang Yunzhu to personally invite this little guy a few days ago, but he rejected. I was planning to send Pan 'er to Han Jia after tomorrow, but I didn't expect to see him here. I heard Yunzhu say that he's very resistant to our Dongfang Family? "
Emperor Han Qing chuckled. "Young people are always rebellious and arrogant. This child doesn't know the Dongfang Family's strength. If he does, he'll be more than happy."
As soon as he said that, many of the Saints despised him even more.
No matter how strong Dongfang Chunjun was, the Saints of the powerful families still retained their dignity. There was no need for a Saint to curry favor with another Saint.

Chapter 1917
Becoming a disciple of a Saint in the divine territory was already a great honor.
Becoming a disciple of a Saint was even more difficult. Even a Saint's own child might not have this opportunity.
Dongfang Chunjun rarely took in disciples.
Since he had spoken, it meant that Dongfang Chunjun really thought highly of Luo Zheng and really cherished his talent.
Emperor Hanqing smiled. "Great Saint Chunjun is willing to accept him as a disciple. He'll be overjoyed when he hears the news after leaving the Sea of Time!"
The other Saints, including Aunt Hanjiu, couldn't help but roll their eyes.
As a Proof of Divinity martial artist, he was able to win more than 30 matches in a row and even defeated the number one ranked Mu Ning of the Mu Family. It could be said that this anonymous martial artist was an invincible existence among the lower True Divinities. Who wouldn't want to accept such a seedling as a disciple?
It could be said that if that kid were to stand here and speak, none of the Saints would refuse to accept him.
Emperor Hanqing really didn't care about his reputation in order to please Dongfang Chunjun …
Aunt Hanjiu glanced at Emperor Hanqing and sighed in her heart. Emperor Hanqing was someone she had once respected. The once arrogant Emperor Hanqing of the Han Family was now bowing down to other Saints!
Dongfang Chunjun was indeed powerful. The Wealthy Alliance led by him had an absolute advantage in the Saints' Hall. However, the Dongfang family and the Wealthy Alliance weren't the only ones in the Sea of Time. The Xuanyue family and the Sword tribe might not be afraid of him.
"But … I'm worried that Dongfang Yang will accidentally kill this child in a fit of anger," Dongfang Chunjun said as he stared into the distance. He didn't pretend at all. He really wanted to accept Luo Zheng as a disciple.
He had an estimate of Luo Zheng's strength. Luo Zheng might be able to defeat an ordinary Middle True God, but Dongfang Yang was a top Middle True God. Even if he was hit by the God-Killing Path's Bloody Bite, if Dongfang Yang was serious, Luo Zheng might not be his opponent.
"I'll send a message to Dongfang Ning and ask him to control the situation. Don't fight too fiercely," Dongfang Chunjun said calmly.
In the eyes of the Saints, the battle between True Gods was like a fight between children …
In the canyon, the goddess statue was still looking up at the sky, but the atmosphere in the canyon was very solemn.
"That's interesting." Dongfang Yang stared at Luo Zheng for a while before saying, "I didn't expect you to be the anonymous player who participated in the leapfrog battle. Hahahaha! That day, my younger sister invited you to join my Dongfang family, but you rejected her. She threw a tantrum and almost destroyed her palace … "
Dongfang Yunzhu looked weak and lovely, but she was actually quite competitive. She was naturally angry that Luo Zheng had rejected her so harshly. After leaving the Arena of Gods, she had indeed caused quite a commotion in the Dongfang family.
"Your talent is indeed not bad, but it's a pity that you're too blind," Dongfang Yang shook his head, "Since that's the case, I'll help Yun Zhu vent her anger. Let me, Dongfang Yang, eliminate a genius like you!"
After that, the yellow Taoist Connotation on his body spread out layer by layer.
Especially on his legs, the dense Dao of the Thick Earth Divine Dao had merged him with the earth. It was as if he had always been a part of the earth and couldn't be separated from it.
At the same time, a big black axe appeared in his hand.
Not far away, Luo Zheng quietly narrowed his eyes. Since this battle was unavoidable, he had no intention of backing down.
Since his identity as the anonymous player had been exposed, he didn't need to hide anything. A faint purple mist emerged from his body. That was the Taoist Connotation of the Purple Clouds Divine Dao. Then, he spread out his hands, and wisps of invisible power spread out. These invisible power slowly condensed and soon turned into four invisible long swords …
Buddha Sword, Heavenly Tooth Sword, Long Vine Sword, Double Sun Sword!
"The rumor is true!"
"Four God-slaying Swords …"
"The key is that these four swords were summoned by power. How can he do that!"
The news that Luo Zheng used the Purple Clouds Divine Dao to turn into four virtual swords had spread.
However, most True Gods believed that Luo Zheng did know how to use the God-slaying Sword Formation, but few believed that Luo Zheng could use the four virtual swords.
This was something that only Aunt Hanjiu could do.
In other words, only quasi-sages and True Sages were qualified to use it. To do this, one must have the ability to transcend worlds to be able to draw out the power of the Nine Stars from their inner worlds.
Proving Gods couldn't do it …
Lower True Gods couldn't do it either.
Even Great Perfection couldn't do it.
However, the scene before their eyes was real. They couldn't help but believe it!
"Dongfang Ning, your younger brother may not win. Moreover, after these Venomous Wolf Scorpions are cleared out, the next wave of attacks will come. This place isn't suitable for them to fight, right?" Zhan Ming said helplessly.
Dongfang Ning looked like he was watching a show. When he heard Zhan Ming's words, he chuckled. "Well said!" Then, his figure suddenly flew to the other side. Below him, several True Gods were cleaning up the remaining Venomous Wolf Scorpions.
"Everyone, stop!" Dongfang Ning said calmly.
When the True Gods saw Dongfang Ning, they thought that he wanted to take the remaining Venomous Wolf Scorpions for himself, so they stopped.
They didn't want to fight with the upper True Gods, especially the upper True Gods of the Dongfang family …
After chasing the Real Gods away, Dongfang Ning didn't kill the scattered Viridian Wolf Scorpions. Instead, he blew a breath at the Viridian Wolf Scorpions. An extremely cold breath blew out and froze the Viridian Wolf Scorpions.
These Venomous Wolf Scorpions didn't die. They were just frozen.
According to the rules of the Goddess Statue Canyon, the next wave of monsters would only attack after the last Venomous Wolf Scorpion was cleared out.
As long as the frozen Venomous Wolf Scorpions weren't killed, the next wave of monsters wouldn't appear.
When Zhan Ming saw this, he only showed dissatisfaction. This method was usually used to deal with the last few waves of monsters in the canyon. Because the last few waves of monsters would become more and more powerful, the True Gods would become more and more tired. Everyone would think of ways to freeze one or two monsters, ushering in precious time to rest.
However, Dongfang Ning was using this method just to fight Luo Tianxing for his younger brother. This action was too selfish.
After summoning the four swords, Luo Zheng rushed toward Dongfang Yang like the wind without any hesitation.
Dongfang Yang was hit by the Bloody Desolate Bite, so naturally, he couldn't move. He stood there as steadily as Mount Tai, carrying the black axe. When he saw Luo Zheng rushing toward him, he shouted softly, "Good timing!"
Then, he swung his hands fiercely. The black axe turned into circles of black shadows, crazily circling in his hands.
The axe was as heavy as a mountain, but in Dongfang Yang's hands, it was as light as a feather!
As the heavy axe spun at high speed, it faintly showed signs of tearing the surrounding space apart!
"Dongfang Yang is borrowing the power of the earth …"
"The characteristic of the Thick Earth Divine Path is that it has extremely terrifying power. After merging with the earth, it can rely on the earth to dissolve the opponent's attack!"
"He's the best of the best!"
When the others saw Dongfang Yang using his full strength, they were secretly shocked. No wonder Dongfang Yang could defeat seven upper True Gods with the cultivation of a middle True God!
"Is his power very strong?"
As Luo Zheng approached rapidly, the battle intent in his eyes became more and more intense.
"Buddha Emperor Sword, kill him for me!"

Chapter 1918
The Buddha Emperor's Sword was extremely fast, heading straight for Dong Fang Yang's head.
However, the black axe in Dong Fang Yang's hand danced wildly as if it was an impenetrable wall!
Soon, the formless Buddha Emperor's Sword and the black axe collided!
"Peng!"
With a silent sound, the black axe in Dong Fang Yang's hand blocked in front of him. The heavy axe was still trembling slightly, and Luo Zheng's Buddha Emperor's Sword had already collapsed.
"You're not qualified," Dong Fang Yang sneered.
Luo Zheng's expression was extremely calm, and the Buddha Emperor's Sword that had collapsed actually reformed itself!
The place where the Buddha Emperor's Sword collapsed was only two feet away from Dong Fang Yang. As it reformed, the Buddha Emperor's Sword became even more solid and thicker! It slashed at Dong Fang Yang's head, so fast that Dong Fang Yang had no time to defend himself.
This was also the power of the "Void Sword" — it allowed the sword to move freely.
Now that Luo Zheng had unleashed these four swords, the power source that had been poured into them only occupied an extremely small portion of the nine Stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier. However, Luo Zheng could adjust it as he wished.
Just now, Luo Zheng had already seen the power of the black axe, so he didn't fight with Dong Fang Yang head-on. The Buddha Emperor's Sword was destroyed by Dong Fang Yang's attack, but when Dong Fang Yang relaxed, Luo Zheng suddenly released fifty percent of his power. In that instant, the Buddha Emperor's Sword became much more powerful, and its speed and power far surpassed before.
Dong Fang Yang felt the power close at hand, and his eyes narrowed slightly. There was even a hint of a smile at the corner of his mouth, and then the Buddha Emperor's Sword slashed at his head!
"Ge …"
The formless Buddha Emperor's Sword slashed at his neck, making a muffled sound.
If it were another True God, his head would have fallen to the ground. However, a yellow light appeared on the surface of Dong Fang Yang's body, and it blocked the Buddha Emperor's Sword.
At the same time, a crack appeared on the ground under Dong Fang Yang's feet.
Dongfang Yang used the Thick Earth Divine Dao to transfer the power of Luo Zheng's Buddha Emperor Sword to the ground.
The cracks on the ground continued to spread and quickly spread towards Luo Zheng. When the cracks spread to Luo Zheng's feet, an invisible force suddenly shot out and shot towards Luo Zheng!
This power was the power of the Buddha Emperor's Sword. After being absorbed by Dongfang Yang, it was transmitted through the ground and returned to Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng's reaction could not be said to be slow. The Chongyang Sword by his side had already pressed down heavily.
The Chongyang Sword focused on "protection" and was the heaviest. Under this pressure, there was a "dong" sound and the invisible force was dissolved.
Luo Zheng's eyebrows furrowed slightly …
The Thick Earth Divine Dao was similar to the power transfer he had mastered in the universe.
However, Luo Zheng had inherited the will of the entire universe and could transfer the power to the entire universe. However, Dongfang Yang seemed to be only able to transfer the power to the ground. He even transmitted the power to himself through the ground.
"Aunt Hanjiu's Virtual Sword is indeed worthy of its reputation. You can continue," Dongfang Yang said with a smile.
Luo Zheng snorted and flicked his fingers. The Buddha Emperor Sword, the Heavenly Teeth Sword, and the Long Vine Sword took turns to attack Dongfang Yang.
Dongfang Yang could indeed transfer the power to the ground. However, there was a limit. Once the limit was exceeded, the layer of yellow Taoist Connotation protecting him would eventually collapse!
"Swish, swish, swish …"
The three Virtual Swords kept dancing around Dongfang Yang like a pack of wolves on the grassland. As long as they found the right opportunity, they would bite Dongfang Yang.
At first, Dongfang Yang tried to use the black axe to defend himself. However, Luo Zheng's Phantom Sword was unpredictable. Even if he managed to defeat it, the Phantom Sword would re-form in a short period of time and slash at him. Therefore, Dongfang Yang gave up on defending himself and diverted the attacks to himself.
"Rumble …"
Cracks kept appearing on the ground under Dongfang Yang's feet. These cracks were caused by Luo Zheng's own attacks. Dongfang Yang had returned all the power to Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng controlled the three Long Swords to attack Dongfang Yang crazily. At the same time, he circled around Dongfang Yang and kept avoiding the cracks. The fight between the two was in a deadlock.
However, Dongfang Yang was much more relaxed. He only needed to stand still and return the power to Luo Zheng. He wanted to see how long Luo Zheng could hold on.
Luo Zheng was also not in a hurry …
Since he had exposed his identity as an anonymous person, he didn't need to hide his other Divine Skills except for the Emotion Severing Divine Dao.
Luo Zheng had incorporated the Deep Earth Divine Dao into the Infinite Ruler. However, the ordinary Divine Skills of the Deep Earth Divine Dao could only borrow the power of the earth. Dongfang Yang's power transfer must be an Extraterritorial Divine Skill of the Dongfang Family.
Luo Zheng had once mastered the Divine Skill of "Power Transfer", so he knew that this Divine Skill was really powerful. Taking advantage of this opportunity, Luo Zheng's eyes quietly flashed with two small golden wheels and started to analyze the Taoist Connotation emitted from Dongfang Yang's body!
"This Extraterritorial Divine Skill is really ingenious!"
Soon, Luo Zheng understood the principle of this Extraterritorial Divine Skill.
"However, it still can't be compared with the power transfer in the universe … Dongfang Yang can only transfer the power to the earth within a radius of 300 meters. I've just mastered this Divine Skill, so the range is even smaller, about 30 meters."
When Luo Zheng completely understood this Extraterritorial Divine Skill, more and more cracks appeared around Dongfang Yang.
The ground under his feet became softer and softer as if it had been plowed. Finally, it had turned into a pile of broken soil.
"It's about time!"
Luo Zheng smiled and suddenly waved his hand. The Long Vine Sword and the Heavenly Tooth Sword had already drilled into the soil layer.
Seeing Luo Zheng's smile, Dongfang Yang was slightly surprised. He didn't know what Luo Zheng was doing.
In an instant, a circular crack suddenly appeared around Dongfang Yang. Then, Dongfang Yang felt a tremor under his feet. A large piece of land under his feet suddenly rose up!
"Your Divine Skill is really powerful. As long as you're connected to the earth, you're invincible. Unfortunately, you've been bitten by the Wild Blood Desolation Bite and can't move freely. As long as the connection between you and the earth is cut off … it'll be very fun!"
Luo Zheng said indifferently as he raised his right hand high.
Under the piece of land that Dongfang Yang was standing on, the Long Vine Sword turned into countless vines and supported the whole piece of land. At the same time, some of the vines passed through the land and tightly wrapped around Dongfang Yang's legs. Dongfang Yang was lifted into the air just like that …
Because Dongfang Yang was moving from the bottom to the top, the wound on his neck caused by the Wild Blood Desolation Bite began to slowly ooze blood again.
After saying that …
Luo Zheng gently waved his right hand.

Chapter 1920
Zhan Ming had been following behind Dongfang Ning because he was afraid that Dongfang Ning would kill Luo Zheng.
Unexpectedly, all of a sudden, Dongfang Ning seemed to have seen his natural enemy. In an instant, he pulled away from Luo Zheng.
If it weren't for the canyon blocking Dongfang Ning's way, he would have been nowhere to be found …
The others were also puzzled, not knowing what Dongfang Ning was afraid of.
Luo Zheng himself couldn't control the violent energy in his Inner World. He didn't even have a way to activate it. Luo Zheng had tried many times in the Time Chamber, but he still didn't succeed …
Just now, he had no choice but to use this method under Dongfang Ning's coercion. If he could force the energy out of his body in a short time, it would have a terrible effect just by exploding it aimlessly.
Luo Zheng never expected that Dongfang Ning would have such a reaction.
Leaning against the cliff of the valley, the fear on Dongfang Ning's face gradually dissipated, but he was still full of vigilance.
His perception was sharp and powerful. Especially in the face of a life-and-death crisis, it could always stimulate a strong fighting instinct. This was one of his talents. Just now, he had this illusion between life and death.
Generally, when he had this feeling, Dongfang Ning would explode with the most powerful counterattack to kill his opponent.
But Dongfang Chunjun asked him not to hurt this guy, so Dongfang Ning's only choice was to leave as quickly as possible …
Until now, Dongfang Ning still didn't understand how the strange Taoist Connotation in Luo Zheng's body came out. Why did it give him such a strong sense of oppression?
After scaring Dongfang Ning away, Luo Zheng looked at Dongfang Yang again.
Seeing that Luo Zheng scared his brother away and then looked at him with such a gaze, Dongfang Yang suddenly shivered.
He was now powerless and was entangled by Luo Zheng's Long Vine Sword. How could he resist? His originally bloodless face was now as pale as a dead man's.
If he really killed Dongfang Yang, Dongfang Chunjun outside the Forbidden Area might not let him go …
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's right hand suddenly shook.
"Crackle …"
The layer of ice on the surface of his right hand cracked quickly. Then, he waved his hand downward, and the Long Vine Sword dragged Dongfang Yang down from the air. Dongfang Yang's blood was still dripping from the wound caused by the "Bloody Desolation Bite".
While Luo Zheng was slowly walking towards Dongfang Yang, Dongfang Ning's legs suddenly kicked against the mountain wall. Like an agile swallow, he flew twice in the air and stopped steadily on one side.
At the same time, Zhan Ming stood calmly on the other side and advised Luo Zheng, "Brother, don't be rash. If he's willing to return the Shenwu Coins to you, we'll forget about this matter!"
"Yang, return the Shenwu Coins to this brat." Dongfang Ning also had a headache dealing with Luo Zheng. He just wanted to end this matter quickly.
He knew that Dongfang Chunjun was watching him, but he couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng. Moreover, this kid was full of strange things. If he failed miserably, he would lose face, and he wouldn't be able to raise his head in front of the Holy Emperor in the future!
"Give it to me!" Luo Zheng said coldly as he extended his hand to Dongfang Yang.
Dongfang Yang's face showed his unwillingness. He looked at his brother for help. He couldn't understand why his brother was so afraid of this brat. Dongfang Ning was the top of the upper true gods. Killing this brat was as easy as snapping his fingers.
"Return the Shenwu Coins you took to Luo Zheng, and then apologize to him," Dongfang Ning ordered.
"I can return the Shenwu Coins to him, but there's no need to apologize …" Dongfang Yang's face was filled with shame and anger. When had the Dongfang family ever apologized to anyone?
"Do as I say!" Dongfang Ning said coldly.
Seeing that Dongfang Ning had turned hostile, Dongfang Yang's face was full of resentment. He struggled to take out the Shenwu Coins from his Sumeru Ring.
Luo Zheng had killed as many Venomous Wolf Scorpions as he had killed the upper true gods. Dongfang Yang took out all the Shenwu Coins, which piled up like a small mountain. There were probably more than a hundred thousand Shenwu Coins.
Luo Zheng waved his hand and an invisible energy enveloped the Shenwu Coins. With a slight shake of his hand, the energy poured into Luo Zheng's Sumeru Ring.
Luo Zheng's method of absorbing the Shenwu Coins seemed simple, but in reality, it was to turn the energy into an invisible funnel. Moreover, the funnel was a distance away from Luo Zheng. The principle behind it was the same as using the God Slaying Sword Array.
This brat could really separate the source of energy from his body at will. It was just a mystery how he had mastered the Transmigration Divine Ability …
Seeing this, Dongfang Ning and Zhan Ming sighed in their hearts.
After Luo Zheng took the Shenwu Coins, he turned and walked towards the statue of the goddess. At the same time, he said to Dongfang Ning, "I don't need an apology from this brat."
"You!"
