Dongfang Yang's face was full of anger as he stared at Luo Zheng's back.
However, Luo Zheng didn't even turn around.
"Big brother Tianxing, you're too amazing!" Han Biluo clapped her hands and smiled, her face full of admiration.
The rich and powerful playboys of the floating island always had conflicts, but very few of them could make the Dongfang family's disciples suffer.
Dongfang Yang was taught a lesson. Han Biluo felt that Luo Tianxing was really amazing!
"Amazing? Fortunately, Dongfang Ning has some reservations. Otherwise, we would be in trouble today, "Han Chuyue said worriedly.
Han Chuyue and Han Liusu knew Luo Zheng much better than Han Biluo. It wasn't that Luo Zheng had no chance of winning against Dongfang Yang, but they didn't think that Dongfang Yang would be no match for Luo Zheng.
The two sisters were most worried about Dongfang Ning. If Dongfang Ning wanted to kill Luo Zheng here, other than Zhan Ming and the other upper true gods, no one else could stop him. As for whether Zhan Ming would be willing to risk his life for Luo Zheng, that was still a question.
"It's fine. I'm just taking back my Shenwu Coins," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Not far away, Han Liusu secretly let out a sigh of relief. Beside her, Leng Linyue's gaze turned gloomy.
A few true gods noticed that the conflict here had ended and moved to kill the remaining Venomous Wolf Scorpions. After all the Venomous Wolf Scorpions were killed, another tremor came from the canyon.
"The third wave of monsters is coming. Everyone spread out. According to past experience, the third wave of monsters are usually birds," Zhan Ming reminded loudly.
As soon as he finished speaking, black things flew out from the four mountain passes around the canyon …
"Gagagaga …"
Just as Zhan Ming said, the third wave of monsters was naturally birds.
Those jet-black birds were called Dark Crows. They were a type of monster in the fourth rank forbidden area, Black Fog Forest. The Dark Crows were extremely fast, and they didn't attack the true gods in the canyon. Instead, they went straight for the goddess statue.
If the goddess statue broke, everyone would fail and they would no longer have the chance to enter the second layer of the Sea of Time.
"Quickly stop those Dark Crows!"

Chapter 1921
"Caw caw caw caw …"
The crows swarmed out, turning into black clouds that covered the sky.
The distance between the four mountain passes and the Goddess statue wasn't far. In the blink of an eye, the crows at the front had already covered one-third of the distance!
"Everyone, don't panic! Although there are many crows, they're easy to kill! "
As Zhan Ming spoke, he took a deep breath. His battle intent surged as he flew into the air. His right hand clenched into a fist, causing his veins to bulge as he punched the crows in the sky!
"Dong!"
Even though it hit empty air, it still produced a dull echo.
The fighting spirit gathered on his fist formed a fist shadow, and this fist shadow shot diagonally towards the sky!
According to Zhan Ming's estimation, this punch could at least knock down this large group of crows in front of him …
However, when the huge fist shadow smashed into the crows, a light blue ball of light appeared around the crows' bodies. These balls of light flashed and actually blocked Zhan Ming's fist shadow!
"It's the Mystic Transformation Orb! I knew it wouldn't be that simple! "Zhan Ming said through gritted teeth, his expression extremely ugly.
The Mystic Transformation Orb was also a type of faith treasure. Like the Stargate, it was a one-time consumable item. The Mystic Transformation Orb was a unique faith treasure in the Black Fog Forest, and this faith treasure was even more precious than the Stargate.
Because the Mystic Transformation Orb was extremely rare in the Black Fog Forest, and it might only appear once every ten years, the Mystic Transformation Orb would be auctioned off on the floating island, reaching a sky-high price of 400,000 Shenwu coins.
Its rarity wasn't the reason why the Mystic Transformation Orb was so valuable, but because of its miraculous effects.
After using the Mystic Transformation Orb, this faith treasure would quietly merge with the True God's body. Normally, no one would be able to detect its existence, but once someone launched a fatal attack, the Mystic Transformation Orb would display a strange power that could prevent one fatal attack!
To many True Gods, the Mystic Transformation Orb was equivalent to a second life. After all, it was something that could save a life at a critical moment, so its price was naturally high.
Zhan Ming's attack could completely wipe out a large group of crows, but all of the crows were wrapped up by the Mystic Transformation Orb!
These weak crows didn't block Zhan Ming's attack, but relied on the Mysterious Transformation Orb's ability to resist Zhan Ming's fist shadows, so when he smashed his fist, not a single crow was killed!
At the same time, the Dark Crows in other parts of the canyon also flashed with blue halos.
"Ah, so many Mysterious Transforming Magic Balls …" Han Biluo curled her lips and opened her eyes wide, "If these Mysterious Transforming Magic Balls can be stripped from the Dark Crows, wouldn't we be rich!"
A single Transforming Mystic Magic Ball was worth hundreds of thousands of Shenwu Coins, and each of the Dark Crows here had used one. This black mass of Dark Crows was equivalent to using hundreds of thousands of Transforming Mystic Magic Balls. According to Han Bi Luo's imagination, this was indeed a sky-high price.
However, the Mysterious Transformation Magic Sphere was a supreme treasure of faith. Once it was used, it was impossible for it to be restored to its original state.
"Stop dreaming, even if these Dark Crows don't hurt people, we still have to attack!" Han Chuyue said and began to attack the Dark Crows in the sky.
Zhan Ming's first fist did not have any effect, so he followed up with a second fist.
The Mysterious Transformation Magic Balls inside the Dark Crows were already broken. When the weak Dark Crows touched the fist shadows, they were instantly smashed into meat paste. Large patches of black feathers fluttered in the sky, and at the same time, large amounts of Shenwu Coins fell down!
According to previous experience, these Dark Crows relied on their extreme speed to destroy the goddess statue, but they were easy to kill. With many True Gods going all out, it was not difficult to kill them.
But this time, these Dark Crows had the Mysterious Transformation Magic Balls, so killing them would require at least two attacks. The difficulty had increased by more than double!
As the Dark Crows were killed, Shenwu Coins fell from the sky. Zhan Ming did not have time to pick them up, but continued to kill the Dark Crows in the sky.
"Four swords, open!"
Luo Zheng looked at the black mass of Dark Crows and summoned four invisible swords.
"Xiu xiu xiu xiu!"
The four swords faced upwards, spiraled up, and quickly spread out in the air, and began to swing towards the Dark Crows.
When the four swords swung past the Dark Crows, the surface of the Dark Crows' bodies flashed with azure blue light, and were immune to the Buddha Emperor Sword's attack. However, Luo Zheng split the four swords into two swords. Just as the Buddha Emperor Sword swept past, the Double Sun Sword followed closely behind.
After losing the protection of the Mysterious Transformation Magic Ball, the Double Sun Sword gently swung, and countless Dark Crows let out strange cries and fell from the sky. On the other side, the Long Vine Sword and Heavenly Fang Sword did the same. With the four swords shuttling back and forth, Luo Zheng was able to defend the area in front of him. Shenwu Coins fell like rain, mixed with the Dark Crows' black feathers.
However, as soon as he killed over a thousand Dark Crows, another black swarm of Dark Crows flew towards him.
Luo Zheng did not have time to pick up the Shenwu Coins on the ground, and could only control the four swords to charge into the sky again …
With all the True Gods working together, they were still able to control the situation at first. At least, the Dark Crows that came from all directions could only fly halfway to the goddess statue before they were killed.
But soon, there was a problem.
Because the Sea of Time forbidden zone's difficulty had increased, the rewards had also increased.
These Dark Crows were the same as the Venomous Wolf Scorpion. Every time one was killed, a Shenwu Coin would fall.
Normally, a trip to an ordinary forbidden zone would yield four to five hundred Shenwu Coins, which was already a great harvest. Now, with a single slash, a few hundred Shenwu Coins would fall. After a few attacks, the ground was covered with a thick layer of Shenwu Coins. This sea of Shenwu Coins made people feel like they were in a dream. Even the True Gods who participated in the Transit of the Sun Festival, who were the cream of the crop, could not resist the temptation.
A True God took the time to put the Shenwu Coins into his Sumeru Space while killing the Dark Crows …
When another True God saw this, he was instantly displeased. He had worked so hard to kill the Dark Crows, but this guy was secretly picking up the Shenwu Coins. Thus, the second True God also began to work hard to collect the Shenwu Coins.
Greed was easily contagious. Many True Gods felt unbalanced and began to scramble to pick up the Shenwu Coins on the ground. Because these Dark Crows were not like the Venomous Wolf Scorpion, it was difficult to tell who killed them. Some True Gods even had disputes over the Shenwu Coins, and there were even signs of fighting.
Because of this, the defensive line built around the goddess statue began to show signs of weakness.
A large flock of Dark Crows broke through the defensive line and headed straight for the goddess statue.
"Everyone, don't be in a hurry to pick up the Shenwu Coins! These Shenwu Coins can be divided equally. Everyone, work together to get through this wave of Dark Crows. The rewards later will only be better! " Zhan Ming's face was filled with anger. He secretly cursed these True Gods for being short-sighted. If the second and third waves of monsters already had such rewards, the rewards later would probably be even more amazing. It was even possible that there would be faith treasures.
However, these True Gods were destined to only see this moment. They were afraid that the Shenwu Coins beneath their feet would be snatched away by other True Gods. How could they have the time to care about the Dark Crows above them?
"Kakaka …"

Chapter 1922
True Gods who had ventured into the forbidden area of the Black Fog Forest knew that the Dark Crows were not a powerful creature in the Black Fog Forest. Even a lower True God could easily kill them.
These Dark Crows gathered densely in the shadows of the Black Fog Forest. Whenever there was a prey attack, they would fly all over the sky and attack the True God.
To kill a True God, many Dark Crows would fall. However, as long as a True God was approached by several Dark Crows, they would erupt with powerful destructive power.
The destructive power of the Dark Crows' beak was quite great. Even a middle True God would find it difficult to resist its attack. If one was surrounded by a group of Dark Crows, their skull would be pecked through in the blink of an eye.
After the group of Dark Crows rushed to the Goddess statue, they stretched out their long beaks and pecked at the huge Goddess statue.
"Duk duk duk duk duk …"
Cracks began to appear on the surface of the goddess statue, as if they were scars.
If this group of Dark Crows were allowed to peck at the Goddess statue, it would not take long for the statue to be completely destroyed.
Luo Zheng looked at the rapidly spreading cracks on the surface of the Goddess statue. His heart was moved and he rushed towards the Goddess statue.
However, before he could get close to the Goddess statue, he saw a curvaceous figure floating over. It was Mu Ning, who was holding a short sword and dressed in martial arts attire. Red light flickered under her feet as she ran around the Goddess statue!
The circle turned into a strange killing array.
Then, a strange rune appeared on the heads of all the Dark Crows lying on the Goddess statue!
"All of you, die!"
Her round eyes revealed a touch of killing intent and her figure disappeared in an instant.
"Swish swish swish swish swish …"
Mu Ning slashed at the backs of all the Dark Crows at an incredible speed.
These Dark Crows flashed with the light of the Mysterious Transformation Magic Ball and blocked Mu Ning's first slash. They were still pecking at the Goddess statue with all their might, and the cracks were rapidly expanding.
After Mu Ning slashed with her blade, she gently tapped her foot and said, "Again!"
As her voice fell, her figure disappeared again …
"Swish swish swish swish …"
This time, all the Dark Crows lying on the surface of the Goddess statue were cut in two, and black feathers fluttered down.
Throughout the entire process, Mu Ning had only made two moves, but she had cleanly beheaded all the Dark Crows.
When she turned around and saw Luo Zheng, she subconsciously revealed a provocative look. She glared at Luo Zheng, tilted her head and thought for a while before saying, "Come on!" As she spoke, she looked up into the sky and saw another large flock of crows flying over. Her figure flashed and once again formed a circle that enveloped the flock of crows …
Luo Zheng smiled, and the four swords also flew up into the sky.
Because the True Gods were fighting for the shenwu coins, the remaining True Gods who were determined to kill the Dark Crow were having a hard time. In order to protect the goddess statue, they had no choice but to shrink their encirclement and lean against the goddess statue.
Even so, a few Dark Crows were still able to break through the defensive line and would occasionally stick to the statue. Although these Dark Crows were killed in a short period of time, they still caused a certain amount of damage to the statue.
"We can't go on like this!" Zhan Ming roared, "Stop picking up Shenwu Coins! After killing these Yin Crows, we will split the Shenwu coins equally! "
Zhan Ming's words had no effect at all …
When the True Gods saw that a small portion of the goddess statue was damaged, they already had thoughts of giving up.
Since they couldn't enter the second level of the forbidden area, they might as well take the opportunity to get as much as they could. If they wasted such a great opportunity, they would definitely regret it in the future.
Aside from Zhan Ming, Dongfang Ning and the other True Gods' faces also darkened …
These True Gods were naturally more ambitious than ordinary True Gods. Shenwu Coins were tempting, but they also hoped to obtain better opportunities. However, these people in front of them didn't have any foresight. They only cared about the benefits before them!
"Let me do it." At this time, an Upper True God stepped forward.
"Mystic Moon Pavilion?" Zhan Ming was stunned, then a trace of joy appeared on his face. "That's the most suitable!"
Mystic Moon Pavilion and Zhan Ming came from the floating island of the Mystic Moon Clan. They were considered members of the same clan, but they belonged to the Mystic Moon Cult and the War God Temple respectively.
The situation in the canyon had gone out of control. It wasn't that they couldn't kill the Dark Crows. Even if they had to kill each Dark Crow twice, this was only the third wave of monsters. It wasn't that they couldn't resist. The key was that the people were distracted. Everyone was addicted to greed and wanted to snatch the Shenwu Coins.
Mystic Moon Pavilion cultivated the "Great Art of Domination"!
Mystic Moon Pavilion took a deep breath. A light brown gemstone appeared in between his eyebrows. This was a pure spirit stone that could greatly enhance the aura of one's soul!
When the light brown gemstone began to shine brightly, the True Gods in the canyon suddenly felt an extremely powerful soul pressure. It was as if a Saint had descended here!
Mystic Moon Pavilion was only an Upper True God. The strength of his soul naturally wasn't as terrifying as a Saint's. In fact, it was far from being comparable to a Great Perfection True God.
However, when the Great Art of Domination was used, it could create an irresistible aura. It gave people the illusion that a Saint had really descended. Everyone, including the True Gods who were fighting for the Shenwu Coins, felt a chill in their hearts!
"Everyone, let's get through this wave of Dark Crows first. When this wave is over, we'll divide the Shenwu Coins equally!"
"Those who don't submit, die!"
Mystic Moon Pavilion roared when he said the word "die".
The True Gods felt their hearts tremble. It was as if Mystic Moon Pavilion's order couldn't be disobeyed, and if they did, they would really die …
In truth, if these True Gods stubbornly continued to pick up the Shenwu Coins, Mystic Moon Pavilion wouldn't have joined forces with the other Upper True Gods to kill them all. After all, there were so many Saints outside the forbidden area of the Sea of Time. The True Gods and Saints here were all inextricably linked. Without a proper reason, they couldn't kill these True Gods.
However, these True Gods subconsciously chose to believe him!
This was the miraculous part of the Great Art of Domination. When the Great Art of Domination was cultivated to a very high level, one's every move would have a powerful leadership force. Anyone would have to humbly submit before it.
"Let's kill these Dark Crows first!"
"Since Mystic Moon Pavilion said we should divide the Shenwu Coins equally, we'll definitely divide them equally later. Everyone, stop fighting!"
"Another large flock of Dark Crows is coming!"
The True Gods abruptly came to a realization. In an instant, they were brimming with fighting spirit. They gave up on picking up the Shenwu Coins and began to resist the Dark Crows.
In truth, these True Gods' thoughts weren't based on their original intentions. They were just thoughts that had been bewitched by the Great Art of Domination.
No matter what, the effect was achieved.

Chapter 1923
The Dark Crows were crushed one after another, and a thick layer of Shenwu coins piled up in the canyon.
The total amount of Shenwu coins added up was not something any wealthy clan could ignore. However, under Mystic Moon Pavilion's suppression, no one dared to fight for the coins!
Everyone was doing their best to kill the Dark Crows in the sky.
If the first wave of Lieyun Butterfly and the second wave of Venomous Wolf Scorpions were easy, the third wave of Dark Crows was enough to make everyone feel pressured.
After an incense stick's worth of time …
No more Dark Crows appeared in the canyon's four entrances.
The third wave of monsters finally came to an end …
Mystic Moon Pavilion retreated to the side, and the light brown gem on his forehead dimmed before slowly disappearing …
The pressure that surged out from his body also dissipated.
With Mystic Moon Pavilion no longer suppressing the True Gods, the True Gods instantly felt relieved. Looking at the Shenwu coins, their eyes were once again filled with greed.
If they were not stopped, these True Gods would probably fall into a fight over the coins in the next moment!
"No one touch the Shenwu coins on the ground! Gather all the Shenwu coins together! "
Zhan Ming shouted. The battle intent in his body boiled, and a powerful aura emanated from him.
If they really fought over the Shenwu coins, the Lower True Gods and Middle True Gods would have no chance. In the end, all the Shenwu coins would end up in the hands of the Upper True Gods.
It was not difficult for the True Gods to understand this, so they did not act rashly.
Following that, Zhan Ming and the other Upper True Gods waved their hands, gathering the Shenwu coins on the ground into a small hill that was forty to fifty feet tall.
These upper true gods surrounded the small hill, and no one dared to come up and fight for it. Moreover, there were seven to eight hundred thousand Shen Martial Coins in the small hill, and it was quite a lot for every true god to split it evenly.
Thus, under Zhan Ming's command, all the True Gods collected the Shenwu coins in an orderly manner.
After distributing the Shenwu coins, Zhan Ming said, "The first, second, and third waves of monsters are only the most basic trials. According to past experience, the fourth wave will not have many monsters, but it will be extremely powerful. Everyone, be careful."
Many True Gods nodded their heads.
They did not know how long they could hold out here, but the longer they lasted, the more rewards they would receive. With hope in their hearts, they would naturally be more persistent.
"Rumble …"
As soon as Zhan Ming's voice fell, the canyon trembled again.
Everyone was staring at the mountain passes, and Luo Zheng was no exception.
After a while, Luo Zheng saw a flaming sword in Ya's mouth. Then he saw a huge black claw holding the flaming sword. On the back of the huge claw, there were spikes.
Boom!
Along with the sound of footsteps, a huge figure walked out of the mountain pass.
"Dong, dong, dong …"
With every step the figure took, the entire canyon shook.
"What is this?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows, his eyes full of surprise.
It was a monster that was fifty to sixty feet tall.
Compared to the huge monsters in the Takeshima Forest, this monster was not particularly tall.
However, this monster's appearance was too ferocious. Just its appearance alone gave people a strong sense of oppression. No one was willing to face it directly!
There were two huge goat horns on its head. Its face was not covered by skin, and only its bones were exposed. Two deep red eyeballs were exposed, and the outer circle of the eyeballs was burning with flames. Its body emitted a pungent smell of sulfur.
"It's a monster from the abyss!"
"Oh my god, the abyss demon is coming out!"
"Who will go first …"
Among the four forbidden areas, the most terrifying one was the abyss demon region.
The Sea of Time forbidden area was a level six forbidden area, and True Gods usually did not explore too deep into the forbidden area. After all, the Sea of Time forbidden area was special. The deeper you went, the stranger the time laws were. Some of the time laws could not be resisted by even those who had reached perfection. Therefore, ordinary True Gods would stop at the second level, and those who had reached perfection would explore the third level at most.
Very few perfection True Gods dared to enter the fourth level of the Sea of Time forbidden area.
As for the fifth and sixth levels, they were not open at all. This was the rule set by the female leader of the Blind Tribe.
The abyss demon region did not have those strange time laws.
However, almost all the terrifying legends in the entire Divine Realm came from the abyss demon region.
Over the course of countless divine epochs, trillions of divine citizens and true gods had rich imaginations of death, hell, and so on. These imaginations turned into negative faith power that continuously gathered in the Abyss Demon Domain, giving birth to the most terrifying forbidden area.
It was said that at the lowest level of the abyss demon region, some powerful demons could even kill saints!
The abyss demon was only a vicious creature that existed on the surface of the abyss demon region. However, it was not an existence that ordinary True Gods could fight against. Even Zhan Ming, Mystic Moon Xiaolou, and other Upper True Gods felt that it was very troublesome to deal with.
"Dong, dong, dong …"
What was even worse was that there was not only one abyss demon, but four of them.
After they appeared from the four mountain passes, they waved the huge flame swords in their hands and slowly walked towards the statue of the goddess. Their tails formed from black bones swayed like long whips behind them, and there was a diamond-shaped spike hanging at the back of the tail, which was also one of the sharp weapons of abyssal devil envoys.
Fortunately, the abyss demon moved very slowly. Step by step, it left a footprint on the ground and slowly walked toward the Goddess statue with a very steady pace.
"I've never been to the abyss demon region." Zhan Ming remained calm and asked, "Who has experience?"
The abyss demon region was not a place that anyone could go.
"Me." Dongfang Ning smiled faintly.
"How do we deal with it?" Zhan Ming asked.
"This thing is very powerful …" Dongfang Ning shook his head. "None of the Upper True Gods here can take a hit from it."
Hearing Dongfang Ning's words, all the True Gods' hearts sank slightly. Some True Gods subconsciously looked at the Starry Gate. Since even an Upper True God was no match for the Abyss Devil Envoy, they might as well escape as soon as possible. If they could get such a large amount of Shenwu Coins, it would be worth it.
"If that's the case, we might as well give up!"
"Even Upper True Gods are no match for it. We'll only be courting death if we go up."
"The harvest from this trip to the Sea of Time has far exceeded our expectations. It's not a pity …"
Just as all the True Gods were talking at once, Dongfang Ning stared at the abyss demon that was walking slowly in the distance and continued, "However, the abyss demon's biggest weakness is that it's extremely slow. It's actually a kind of puppet made from the bones of demons. It doesn't have much intelligence. If you want to kill it, you have to reduce its speed as much as possible. At the same time, you just have to stay away from it."

Chapter 1924
The True Gods present naturally understood Dongfang Ning's words, but they were still uncertain.
Dongfang Ning had always been a loner. Although he was powerful, he wasn't good at commanding like Zhan Ming, nor did he have the temperament of a leader like Xuanyue Xiaolou.
Seeing that everyone was silent, Dongfang Ning revealed a helpless expression. He could only say to Zhan Ming, "You command. The first step is to slow down these guys as much as possible. It's not difficult …"
Zhan Ming nodded and glanced at everyone. "Xiaolou, lead a team to deal with the Abyss Devil Envoy in the east!"
"Dongfang Yang, Fang Yin, and …"
"…"
In the blink of an eye, Zhan Ming divided the True Gods present into four teams. Then, these True Gods surrounded the four Abyss Devil Envoys.
Luo Zheng was assigned to Dongfang Ning's team.
After all, Dongfang Ning had the experience of exploring the Abyssal Demon Region. He rushed towards the Abyssal Demon Envoy and stopped when he was about six meters away from it. Then he pointed at the Abyssal Demon Envoy and said, "Go!"
Translucent ice butterflies floated toward the Abyss Devil Envoy.
"Swish swish swish swish …"
These ice butterflies kept colliding and suddenly turned into butterfly-shaped frost marks, imprinting on the Abyss Devil Envoy's body.
The Abyss Devil Envoy was originally very slow. After the butterflies imprinted on the surface of the Abyss Devil Envoy's body, its speed dropped by half. It was like a person walking in slow motion, taking one step every one or two breaths.
"You guys do it too!" Dongfang Ning shouted. Only then did he realize that the others were following behind him without saying a word. They were actually watching him move.
With Dongfang Ning's shout, the other True Gods also made their moves.
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and patted lightly. The formless Long Vine Sword shot out and turned into formless vines that kept entangling the Abyss Devil Envoy.
The Abyss Devil Envoy's strength was shocking. It could break those vines by lifting its leg. However, the Long Vine Sword also brought a considerable obstruction to the Abyss Devil Envoy. Its movement speed dropped by another 20% …
"Falling Snow Entanglement!"
A middle True God emitted the Dao of Snow Imprint Divine Dao.
Then, a white cloud appeared above the Abyss Devil Envoy. Snowflakes kept falling from the cloud, forming a constantly rotating blizzard that completely enveloped the Abyss Devil Envoy.
The other True Gods also used their own methods to restrict the movement of the Abyss Devil Envoy.
As they attacked, the speed of the Abyss Demon Envoy was constantly reduced. Every step he took was extremely difficult. He was like a puppet imitating a human.
"That's about it," Dongfang Ning said indifferently. "This Abyss Devil Envoy's body is very thick. Everyone, use your most powerful techniques!"
As soon as he finished speaking, Dongfang Ning gently flipped his palm and pulled out a translucent longsword. He condensed a little bit of extremely cold power in his arm and poured it all into the sword. This translucent longsword quickly turned blue and emitted cold air. Then, he fiercely slashed at the Abyss Devil Envoy from six meters away.
"Spirit Ice Slash!" He shouted coldly.
"Sou!"
The blue sword-light turned into a crescent shape and slashed at the Abyss Devil Envoy at an extremely fast speed.
The Abyss Devil Envoy was almost unable to move. He couldn't dodge Dongfang Ning's sword-light at all!
When the True Gods saw this sword-light, they were secretly shocked. Dongfang Ning was one of the best among the upper True Gods after all. This sword-light looked ordinary, but it contained a lot of power. If an ordinary True God was hit by this sword, how could he still be alive?
They wondered how many swords this Abyss Devil Envoy could take from Dongfang Ning.
In the blink of an eye, that icy-blue sword-light had fiercely slashed the Abyss Devil Envoy's body.
"Pa!"
When the sword-light touched the Abyss Devil Envoy, it suddenly exploded and released a dazzling blue light.
When the light dissipated, everyone focused their gazes, and they all revealed looks of astonishment.
There was only a shallow mark left on the Abyss Devil Envoy's chest!
"This Abyss Devil Envoy is so hard?"
"Dongfang Ning slashed with the cultivation of an upper True God, but it didn't move at all!"
"How do we kill it …"
Dongfang Ning didn't look surprised at all. Instead, he had a natural expression. "It's not easy to kill an Abyss Devil Envoy. There are only four of them here, so we can barely deal with them. The last time our Dongfang Family ventured into the Abyss Demon Region, we were chased by hundreds of Abyss Devil Envoys …"
One Abyss Devil Envoy was already so difficult to kill. Hundreds of Abyss Devil Envoys …
When the True Gods imagined that scene, they couldn't help but shiver.
Ordinary True Gods wouldn't explore the Abyss Demon Region. Only some elites from wealthy families would enter the forbidden land, the Abyss Demon Region, under detailed planning. However, the True Gods who entered to explore usually didn't have much luck.
"Everyone, attack." Dongfang Ning was too lazy to explain too much. He waved the longsword in his hand and slashed out sword-light continuously.
The other True Gods didn't hesitate anymore. Seeing that the Abyss Devil Envoy's movements were so slow, as long as they kept a distance, it was just a live target. There was nothing to be afraid of.
"Holy Thunder Death!"
"Pa pa pa …"
A True God released bolts of lightning that were as thick as buckets and crazily struck the Abyss Devil Envoy.
"Blood God Slash!"
"Weng …"
A giant blood-red blade slashed out horizontally, cutting the Abyss Devil Envoy's body and making a teeth-numbing sound.
Luo Zheng took out the other three longswords. After taking turns to attack, they also left traces of marks on the Abyss Devil Envoy's body …
The Abyss Devil Envoy was attacked by dozens of True Gods in turn, but it still stood tall. However, the attacks of the crowd seemed to have angered it. It waved the flaming sword in its hand and changed its direction, roaring, "Woo …"
It changed direction and rushed toward a True God.
However, various restraints were imposed on it, and even its sprint was extremely slow. The True Gods were like agile rabbits, retreating while attacking. The Abyss Devil Envoy was like a tortoise that took a beating. Although it had a thick defense, it couldn't touch any True God …
"It's very simple!" A True God said with a smile.

"I wonder what the reward for killing the Abyss Devil Envoy will be. It's so powerful, will there be a faith treasure?"
When a True God said this, all the other True Gods' faces suddenly showed signs of interest. At the same time, they looked at Dongfang Ning with great caution.
If there really was a faith treasure, how would it be distributed?
It couldn't belong to Dongfang Ning alone, right?
Many True Gods faintly had a trace of suspicion in their hearts. If they worked so hard to kill the Abyss Devil Envoy only to end up making a wedding dress for someone else, no one would be happy.
"Ka, ka …"
As the True Gods continued to attack, cracks gradually appeared on the surface of the Abyss Devil Envoy's body. The two rows of barbed ribs on its chest also began to shatter one by one.
"It looks like it won't be able to hold on for much longer!"
"Everyone, let's work harder!"
Some True Gods even quietly closed the distance between themselves and the Abyss Devil Envoy. If killing the Abyss Devil Envoy really gave them some precious faith treasure, they must be the first to snatch it.
However, a crisis quietly arrived at this moment!
"Woo, woo, woo …"
The Abyss Devil Envoy exploded with an angry aura. At the same time, it raised the flaming sword in its hand, and the red flames in its eyes also spurted out.
At the same time, its bones were plated with a layer of golden light!
Under the cover of that layer of golden light, all magical abilities lost their effect. It actually rushed toward the nearest True God at an extremely fast speed.

Chapter 1925
That middle-level True God was suddenly grabbed by the Abyss Devil Envoy, and his eyes were filled with a dazed look.
After regaining his senses, this middle-level True God also frantically struggled, wanting to break free from the Abyss Devil Envoy's grasp!
However, the Abyss Devil Envoy was like a python that had coiled around its prey. No matter how this middle-level True God struggled, he couldn't break free from its grasp. He could only watch as it raised the flaming greatsword and pierced his chest!
Whoosh...
The flaming greatsword pierced through the middle-level True God's body, and blood spurted out from his wound before being evaporated by the raging flames. As the flames spread, they also completely engulfed the middle-level True God.
"Ahhhh!"
The Abyss Devil Envoy raised the flaming greatsword high up. The middle-level True God's body was still squirming and struggling on the greatsword, and at the same time, he let out shrill screams.
Finally, the screams finally stopped, and the middle-level True God's body also turned into ashes and scattered...
Whoosh...
The Abyss Devil Envoy brandished its sword.
The flames that leaped high seemed to have burned half the sky red.

The Abyss Devil Envoy, which was covered in golden light, rushed toward the True Gods at an extremely fast speed!
"Run!"
"What's going on? This guy's speed is so fast all of a sudden!"
The Abyss Devil Envoy's defense was astonishing, and its strength was ridiculously powerful. It was far from what ordinary True Gods could contend against. Perhaps only Great Perfection True Gods could fight it.
Relying on the golden light that appeared on the surface of its body, it broke free from all the restraints on its body, and its movement speed increased explosively. All the True Gods dodged one after another!
Luo Zheng's figure slid back continuously like a shuttle. As his hands danced lightly, the Double Sun Sword and Heavenly Fang Sword shuttled back and forth, still dealing heavy blows to the Abyss Devil Envoy.
Clang! Clang!
The two invisible longswords slashed the surface of the Abyss Devil Envoy, but with a crisp sound, the two swords actually collapsed from the collision.
The Abyss Devil Envoy completely ignored Luo Zheng and chased after him at an extremely fast speed, slashing out with its sword!
Three lower-level True Gods couldn't dodge in time and were directly cut in half at the waist by the Abyss Devil Envoy!

Even if a True God was cut in half at the waist, it did not mean death.
But the flames on the greatsword came from the Abyssal Demon Domain. Just touching a wisp of it would cause the three lower True Divinities to be incinerated. Their faces twisted as they screamed and died in endless pain.
This scene struck a blow to the confidence of the gods. Except for Luo Zheng, no one else fought back against the Abyss Demon Envoy. Even Dongfang Ning and the other upper true gods circled around and looked at the Abyss Demon Envoy with vigilance. Even if these upper true gods were slashed by the Abyss Demon Envoy, they would still die!
"Wow! Wah! "
Just then, Luo Zheng heard another roar behind him.
He turned his head and saw that the other abyss devil envoy was also covered in a layer of golden light. At the same time, the abyss devil envoy brandished the blazing sword in his hand and began to chase after those true gods!
The other three teams also used the same method to deal with these Abyss Devil Envoys, but at the final stage, a layer of golden light suddenly appeared on the surface of these Abyss Devil Envoys, greatly increasing their strength!
"These Abyss Devil Envoys seem to only use this ability in times of crisis!" He looked up at Dongfang Ning and saw that Dongfang Ning's expression was also panicked. Mu Ning and Han Chuyue, who were caught in the hands of the Abyss Devil Envoy, had faces full of horror and despair. Suddenly, they saw a figure rushing over at an extremely fast speed, instantly causing a glimmer of hope to flash in the eyes of the two women.
As for the other True Divinities, they had long fled far away. When they turned back and saw this scene, they could only reveal a trace of pity. These two women were destined to fall today. At the same time, they were secretly glad that they had run quickly just now.
"Fourth sword!"
Luo Zheng, who was rushing straight towards the Abyss Devil Envoy, frantically extracted the power of the world in his body. He extracted the power of the nine stars too quickly, causing the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Nine Stars to maintain a high-speed rotation. The light emitted by them even dimmed for a while.
In the Arena of Gods, Luo Zheng didn't use all of his power against even the most powerful lower True Divinities.
He only extracted twenty to thirty percent of his power to maintain the operation of the four swords.
But at this moment, he no longer had any reason to be stingy. Almost in an instant, he extracted all of his power source!
The four formless long swords finally showed a sinister and terrifying aura in front of the world!
"Combine!"
The four long swords were originally scattered after being summoned by Luo Zheng, but with a thought, the four long swords quickly merged into one under his rotation to form an invisible giant sword!
This giant sword was almost the same as the giant sword in the hands of the Abyss Devil Envoy.
This giant sword condensed all of Luo Zheng's power source.
His power source came from Saint Jiu's gift, which was equivalent to one-third of a Saint's power.
Under Luo Zheng's constant tempering, the nine stars slowly expanded and became larger. They were able to hold more and more of the Origin of Power.
Even so, Luo Zheng didn't have the confidence to kill the Abyss Devil Envoy!
A monster that even an upper True Divinity couldn't kill with one strike, Luo Zheng couldn't kill with one strike.
Fortunately, his goal wasn't to kill the Abyss Devil Envoy, he just wanted to save Han Chuyue.
"There's a crack on the arm!"
The Abyss Devil Envoy had also been besieged by a group of True Divinities for a long time, and the surface of its body was full of potholes and cracks. Luo Zheng's gaze immediately locked onto the left arm of the Abyss Devil Envoy. There was a striking crack on the upper end of the left arm!
"It's here!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng didn't have time to think too much. He controlled the giant sword to fiercely attack the crack on the right arm.
Han Chuyue and Mu Ning only felt a huge force pass over their heads. That force contained a terrifying power, as if as long as it touched them in the slightest, they would be smashed to pieces!

"Kacha!"
Accompanied by a crisp sound.
The left arm of the Abyss Devil Envoy really broke from the crack …

Chapter 1926
Luo Zheng's all-out sword attack almost consumed 99% of his power source.
The nine stars in the inner world quickly dimmed …

What kind of power did this guy have in that attack?

The Abyssal Demon Envoy's flaming greatsword missed, followed by a vertical slash towards Han Chuyue and Mu Ning.

Some of the True Gods saw that the situation was not good and rushed to the starry door below the goddess statue.

Dong, dong, dong, dong, dong …
The four Abyss Devil Envoys waved the huge flaming sword in their hands and ran madly over the True Gods. The True Gods relied on their nimble movements to constantly dodge.

The golden light emitted by the Abyss Devil Envoys gradually dissipated, and their speed also rapidly slowed down.
"The golden light disappeared!"
"I knew that the golden light wouldn't last for long. Everyone, go!"

The True Gods who were originally frantically fleeing heaved a sigh of relief. These Abyss Devil Envoys were powerful, but once their speed slowed down, they were no threat at all.

Thousands of ice crystals smashed toward the Abyss Devil Envoys like a storm, and layers of white frost lingered around them.

All kinds of methods were used on the Abyss Devil Envoys, restricting their movements once again.

The other Abyss Devil Envoy also let out a roar of despair.

These Abyss Devil Envoys were indeed like what Dongfang Ning said. They were puppets assembled from the bones of Evil Demons, and it was very difficult to completely destroy them. However, after taking a certain amount of attacks, they completely lost their ability to move.

"What's the reward for killing these Abyss Devil Envoys?"

Hearing this, many Lower True Gods were at a loss, and those Upper True Gods quietly approached those Abyss Demons. Clearly, they had also thought of this point.
Even if it was a Level 1 or Level 2 faith treasure, it was also an incomparably precious existence.
If these four Abyss Devil Envoys were killed, four faith treasures would really appear, and they wouldn't need to protect the Goddess Statue anymore. An earth-shattering battle would probably break out here, and every True God in front of them would be their enemy!
These rich and powerful families had set many conventional rules in the forbidden area to prevent everyone from killing each other in the forbidden area.
However, in front of true temptation, these conventional rules were simply not worth mentioning …
Everyone abided by the rules because they would suffer losses if they violated the rules. Once the benefits obtained were greater than the losses, the so-called rules would become empty talk!
"Huala …"

They weren't vigilant against the Abyss Devil Envoys, but the other True Gods.
Luo Zheng also quietly stood to the side, calmly looking at the raging flames. His strength source had been completely exhausted, and he needed some time to recover, so he couldn't participate in the fight.

The bones of these Abyss Devil Envoys came from real 'Demons', and even Upper True Gods found it difficult to shatter them. Just the bones alone were extremely valuable, but it was a pity that they had been burned to ashes.
"What is that!" a True God suddenly said.
Then everyone saw an orange light flickering from the ashes of the Abyss Devil Envoys.

Chapter 1927
When the other True Gods saw this upper True God's miserable state, none of them dared to touch this orange ball of light.

At the same time, the orange balls of light in the other three piles of ashes also floated up.
Buzz buzz buzz …
After a while, the fist-sized ball of light suddenly emitted rays of light.
The speed of these rays of light was extremely fast and connected to every True God in the blink of an eye.
Some True Gods tried to dodge, but they couldn't avoid these rays of light at all.
Luo Zheng was no exception. This ray of light hit his chest. He stretched out his hand and stroked back and forth on the ray of light, but it didn't fluctuate at all.
Not long after, the four balls of light quickly disappeared, and the rays of light that connected everyone also dissipated.
However, Luo Zheng discovered that there were three more orange patterns on his chest.
These patterns weren't divine patterns or Sanskrit, but they were somewhat similar to the patterns on the abyss devil envoy's armor. He had never seen these patterns before, so he naturally couldn't judge their function.

Outside the Sea of Time forbidden area, the Sages also noticed the commotion in the canyon.

The power of faith gathered there carried the evil thoughts of all living beings over countless divine epochs, and the monsters that were born could no longer be contained.

In the canyon, everyone's eyes were still focused on the Real God who was shining with golden light.
Although they didn't know what the Promise of Absolute Beginning was, they had felt the power of the golden light before.
When the golden light appeared on the surface of the abyssal devil envoys, they immediately broke free from all restrictions. Any power driven by dao essence had almost no effect on the abyssal devil envoys. Moreover, the speed of the abyssal devil envoys had greatly increased!
After receiving Dongfang Chunjun's message, Dongfang Ning was overjoyed. "It can resist all divine dao dao essence!"
"This … is really too lucky!"
All the True Gods were overjoyed. They couldn't help but look down at the three golden lines on their chests.
These golden lines were very eye-catching at first. However, as the golden light gradually dissipated, the lines gradually disappeared into their bodies.
In the Archaion Divine Realm, no matter if it was a True God or a Saint, almost all of them used the power of faith in their inner world to drive the dao essence in their Godhead to perform divine abilities. Dao essence was the foundation. However, the Promise of Absolute Beginning could resist all divine abilities formed from dao essence. For a short period of time, they wouldn't have to be afraid of the enemy's attacks!
"These golden lines can resist all dao essence?" Luo Zheng's face was filled with astonishment. If that was the case, then the value of these three Promises of Absolute Beginning was almost immeasurable. When faced with a desperate situation, activating one Promise of Absolute Beginning was enough to turn the tables.
For example, if the Promise of Absolute Beginning was activated, then they could safely pass through those time wraiths. The only drawback was that there was a limit to the number of wraiths. They could only use it three times.
However, to be able to use such a heaven-defying divine ability three times was already very lucky.
After hearing Dongfang Ning's words, the True Gods were naturally wild with joy!
The True God who activated one Promise of Absolute Beginning was extremely depressed. Such a precious thing had actually been casually activated by him. He had a long face and repeatedly cursed himself for his cheap hands.
However, he wasn't the most depressed …
There were a few True Gods who had escaped into the Star Gate early on. Through the Star Gate, they were teleported to another Star Gate outside the canyon. Now, after coming back one after another, they heard Dongfang Ning's words and noticed that there were three faint lines on everyone's chests. Their intestines immediately turned green with regret. They had missed a great opportunity!
Luo Zheng once again returned to the Goddess statue and waited for the next wave of monsters to attack.

This arm was still tightly holding onto Han Chuyue and Mu Ning. The Mu Family and Han Family's people spent a lot of effort to rescue the two women.

Mu Ning stared at Luo Zheng for a long time before finally squeezing out the words "thank you".
Luo Zheng nodded faintly. He didn't want to deliberately save Mu Ning. If he was caught by the Abyss Devil Envoy, he probably wouldn't be able to escape death.
If it wasn't for Han Chuyue, he wouldn't have taken such a big risk to rescue them. Mu Ning was also clear about this.
"This arm, how should we divide it?" Han Cangyan suddenly said.
The four Abyss Devil Envoys had been burned by the raging fire, but this arm had been cut off by Luo Zheng and had been preserved intact.
"These demon bones are valuable, so naturally my Mu Family will get them." Mu Ning's chin was slightly raised, and her words revealed an unquestionable momentum.
"It should be so," the Mu Family's upper True God said with a nod.
Han Cangyan smiled coldly. "What kind of logic is this? This arm has your Mu Family's name written on it? "
