The Han and Mu Family weren't the only ones who coveted this arm.
Although the True Gods didn't know about the Absolute Beginning's Promise, they had more or less heard about the Abyss Demon Region. The bones of these demons had many uses, especially for refining Gold Pills. For example, the Fang family's famous "Demon Soul Gold Pill" required the bones of demons. However, the True Gods rarely ventured into the Abyss Demon Region, so the bones of demons were very scarce.
The atmosphere, which had originally calmed down, became tense again because of this severed arm.
The other True Gods originally didn't have any ideas about this severed arm, but when they saw the True Gods of the Fang Family getting involved, they naturally wanted to get a share.
"Cut off this arm and give each of us a piece!"
"This bone is very strong. How long will it take to cut it?"
"Splitting it equally is a good idea …"
Han Family was obviously the weaker party. This time, Han Family didn't have any upper True Gods invited to the Ling Sun Festival, but two Perfect True Gods were invited. However, they weren't here.
Seeing that another conflict was about to break out, Zhan Ming stood on the neutral side. "Don't fight anymore. We've already received such a reward in the fourth wave. There's bound to be an even bigger surprise in the future. It's not nice to hurt our friendship over an arm. I have a good suggestion. The arm will naturally belong to whoever cuts it off."
After Zhan Ming finished speaking, he looked at Luo Zheng meaningfully.

Chapter 1928
Luo Zheng did not intend to participate in the fight for the severed arm.
He did not have much to rely on in the Sea of Time Forbidden Land. The power of hundreds of Dao Accumulations was too violent, and he could not control it himself.
The Emotion Severing Divine Path had improved along with his cultivation, and its power was no longer the same as before. However, this Dao was a forbidden Dao, so he would not use it rashly.
In addition, the Divine Paths and Extra-Dao Divine Abilities that were unique to wealthy families could be used with the Infinite Ruler.
However, Luo Zheng's greatest reliance had always been the massive amount of power source in his body.
His top priority was to replenish the nine stars in his Inner World as soon as possible so that he could deal with the next wave of attacks from the monsters.
Now, the nine stars in his body were slowly rotating, and at the same time, they emitted a huge gravitational force, constantly drawing chaotic energy into them. In a short time, he had recovered 15% of his power source.
Now that Zhan Ming said that the severed arm belonged to him, Luo Zheng was stunned.
Zhan Ming's words were not without reason. After all, he was the one who severed the arm, so it was justified for it to belong to Luo Zheng. This way, Zhan Ming would not only resolve the dispute in front of him, but also return a favor to Luo Zheng.
Under other circumstances, no one would be willing to give the severed arm to Luo Zheng. They did not care who severed the arm. Such a precious thing, naturally, would belong to whoever was stronger.
However, the upper True Gods were quite afraid of each other, so it seemed that only Luo Zheng was qualified.
"Brother Zhan Ming's suggestion is good. I also think that the severed arm should belong to Luo Tianxing," Dongfang Ning said with a smile.
"That's right. This arm was originally severed by Luo Tianxing," Mu Ning also said. Since she spoke, she naturally represented the Mu Family's True God.
Then, Mystic Moon Xiaolou also agreed with this suggestion.
Several upper True Gods agreed with this suggestion. Perhaps the other True Gods were a little dissatisfied, but they had nothing to say.
"Since everyone thinks so, it would be impolite for me to refuse." After saying that, he reached out to put the severed arm into his Sumeru Ring.
At this moment, a middle True God from the Fang Clan stepped forward and extended his hand to stop Luo Feng. "Brother Luo, please wait a moment."
Luo Zheng sized up this middle Real God. The other Real Gods also frowned. Since everyone had reached a consensus, this guy still jumped out to stop them. He was not giving the other Real Gods any face.
Dongfang Ning even frowned. Since Dongfang Chunjun had spoken, he regarded Luo Zheng as a member of the Dongfang family.
Just as Dongfang Ning was about to flare up, the middle True God also realized that everyone was unhappy. He quickly said, "Everyone, you've misunderstood. Everyone knows that my Fang Clan is good at refining Gold Pills. This severed arm is indeed very useful to my Fang Clan. What I want to ask is if Brother Luo can sell it to me? Brother Luo, you can name a price. "
Luo Zheng faintly smiled. Then, he reached out and placed the severed arm into his spatial ring. At the same time, he said, "If the Fang Family wants to trade, then wait until we return to the floating island. I'm afraid that this place isn't suitable for trading."
Luo Zheng did not know what the Abyss Devil Envoy's severed arm was used for, but he was not willing to sell it casually. Moreover, he was not lacking in Shenwu Coins right now.
"Brother Luo is right." The middle True God from the Fang Clan cupped his hands and retreated to the side.
The fourth wave of ferocious creatures had killed eight true gods. Among them, there were six lower true gods and two middle true gods.
The moment the Abyss Devil Envoys activated the Absolute Beginning Promise, the upper True Gods quickly flew into the air. Naturally, they wouldn't die. In the process of killing the Abyss Devil Envoy, everyone had also lost a lot of energy. Now, they all sat down cross-legged around the goddess statue and began to recuperate.
Luo Zheng also took the opportunity to quickly recover his power source …
After the fourth wave of monsters attacked, the forbidden area gave them enough time to rest. After about the time it took for three incense sticks to burn, there was another tremor from the mountain passes around the canyon.
All the True Gods stood up immediately and looked into the distance. There was a hint of nervousness on their faces.
Many True Gods were somewhat conflicted. Eight people had died in the fourth wave against the Abyss Devil Envoy. The following waves of monsters would probably be more powerful than the previous one. If they were not careful, they would lose their lives here.
However, when they thought of the three Absolute Beginning Promises that they had previously received as rewards, they were also unwilling to leave just like that. What if a faith treasure really appeared?
Everyone craned their necks and looked at each other. They only saw lights flashing at the four mountain passes. However, after staring at them for a long time, they were all empty …
Everyone looked at each other. Why had they not seen any traces of monsters after such a long time?
Zhan Ming frowned. Then, he felt a faint breeze, and his expression changed drastically. "Those monsters are good at concealment techniques. Everyone, use your divine senses!" As soon as he finished speaking, he spread out his divine senses and enveloped the canyon.
Through the exploration of his divine senses, he outlined the outline of the surrounding world. He found a group of low shadows crawling forward at an extremely fast speed. The nearest shadow was already in front of him.
The shadow that was crawling on the ground suddenly jumped up, and a sharp hook suddenly appeared in its hand, grabbing towards his head!
"It's the Soul-hooking Yin Demon!" Zhan Ming shouted loudly. At the same time, he retreated quickly.
The sharp hook slashed Zhan Ming's head but did not cause any harm to him. A chill kept flowing in Zhan Ming's mind, causing his face to turn pale.
The Soul-hooking Yin Demon was a kind of soul creature. The hook would not hurt people, but it could directly drag the souls of True Gods out of their minds!
"Retreat, retreat quickly!"
"Damn it, why didn't I find it just now …"
"Ah!"
A middle True God let out a blood-curdling scream. His entire body trembled as he stood on the spot, the whites of his eyes constantly rolling upwards.
The middle-level True God's soul was hooked by the invisible hook and pulled out of his mind.
After the other Soul-hooking Yin Demons noticed his soul, they were like a pack of wolves that smelled blood. In an instant, they surrounded him and waved the hooks in their hands. They tore the middle-level True God's soul to pieces and devoured it in the blink of an eye.
As for the middle-level True God's body, it still had the whites of its eyes rolling up. It stood on the spot without moving. The body that had lost its soul had become a walking corpse.
The middle-level True God was not the only one who was affected. When Zhan Ming gave the warning, the Soul-hooking Yin Demons had already quietly surrounded them.
Some of the vicious creatures' first target was the Goddess statue in the canyon, such as the Dark Crow. Some of the vicious creatures' first target was the True Gods in the canyon, such as the Abyss Devil Envoy …
The Soul-hooking Yin Demons' first target was clearly the souls of these True Gods!
In the blink of an eye, there were seven to eight True Gods who were trembling all over. Their hands and legs kept spasming. Their souls were forcefully dragged out by the invisible hooks. After being torn apart by the Soul-hooking Yin Demons, they were quickly divided up.
Luo Zheng and Han Jia's group were not at the outermost circle. Seeing the souls of the True Gods being devoured, the three women of Han Jia were filled with fear. Under Luo Zheng's lead, they immediately chose to retreat.
As he released his divine sense, his eyes flashed with a faint green light.
His divine sense could only detect a faint shadow. However, with his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes, he could see everything more clearly.
Luo Zheng focused his gaze and saw many cloaked midgets crawling on the ground. They were using their four limbs to crawl at an extremely fast speed. Their thin, sharp hooks were the magic treasures they used to capture souls.
As he observed, he saw two Soul-hooking Yin Demons leap up and throw the hooks in their hands at him!
"Hmph!"
Luo Zheng snorted coldly. Dao Connotation flickered on his arms. Two streaks of azure lightning suddenly shot out.
"Pa, pa!"
In the blink of an eye, they hit the two Soul-hooking Yin Demons not far away.
"Squeak, squeak, squeak …"
These Soul-hooking Yin Demons did not have flesh and blood bodies. They were originally souls. They were most afraid of the evil-warding power of the lightning. After the two streaks of lightning struck, they only heard the screams of rats. The two Soul-hooking Yin Demons disappeared into thin air.
"Quick! Who's cultivating the Mystic Lightning Divine Dao? "
"Other lightning arts will do too!"
"Blast them all to death!"
The True Gods also hated these evil beings. Thinking about their souls being devoured by them, they felt a chill in their hearts.
"Rumble …"
"Crackle …"

Chapter 1929
After Luo Zheng chopped the two Soul Reaping Yin Demons into pieces, a formless soul power instantly escaped from them!
This soul power was extremely pure. It circled around on the spot and flew towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng originally wanted to dodge, but before he could do so, the soul power entered his mind and fused with his soul.
The moment it fused with his mind, Luo Zheng instantly felt his soul become slightly stronger.
"This isn't the soul body of the Soul Reaping Yin Demon that was destroyed …" Luo Zheng finally reacted. "This is the reward!"
Killing the two Soul Reaping Yin Demons did not give any shenwu coins. Instead, the reward was the soul power. This made Luo Zheng an exception.
"I can't do anything about these things. I can only use the Stargate and leave first!" a True God shouted.
Although many True Gods cultivated two or even three types of Divine Daos, not every True God cultivated the Lightning Divine Daos. To these True Gods, they were simply helpless when encountering these ghouls and other ferocious creatures.
"Pa pa pa …"
As the True Gods continuously retreated, they simultaneously shot out bolts of lightning.
White bolts, azure bolts, purple bolts …
Depending on the type of lightning they cultivated, the power would be different as well. The effects would also be as different as heaven and earth.
However, these Soul-Reaping Yin Devils weren't ordinary ghosts. They were extremely intelligent. Facing the bolts of lightning, the Soul-Reaping Yin Devils that had originally surrounded them crawled away at an extremely fast speed, rapidly dispersing …
Under the bombardment of the violent lightning, only two or three Soul Reaping Yin Demons could be killed.
Once the Soul Reaping Yin Demons found an opportunity to attack, seven or eight of them would jump out at the same time and throw the hooks in their hands!
"Be careful!" Luo Zheng warned.
Compared to other True Gods who used their divine senses to judge, Luo Zheng used his eyes to see more clearly.
"It's coming again! I, I … Absolute Beginning's Promise! "
Not far from Luo Zheng, a Lower True God also used his divine senses to detect that the Soul Reaping Yin Demons had launched a round of attacks at him. In this chaotic situation, this Lower True God became anxious and activated the Absolute Beginning's Promise on his chest.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
A golden light spiraled out and enveloped the body of this True God.
This lower True God thought that he could rest easy with the Absolute Beginning Promise, but he could only watch helplessly as the hooks circled around him and pierced into his brain. Then, this lower True God's body trembled as his soul was taken away by the Soul Reaping Yin Demons!
Luo Zheng clenched his teeth. The lightning in his hand flashed and turned into a watermelon-sized lightning ball. Then, he threw it in that direction.
However, these Soul Reaping Yin Devils reacted very quickly. After they noticed the lightning balls, they immediately scattered in all directions. Before the lightning balls landed on the ground, they jumped up again. At the same time, they didn't forget to drag the soul of the True God away!
"Explode!"
Luo Zheng spat out a word.
"Bang!"
The lightning balls exploded in advance and the lightning that shot out engulfed an area of thirty feet. However, it only engulfed three Soul Reaping Yin Devils. The other Soul Reaping Yin Devils still dragged the True God's soul away and escaped.
At the same time, a large group of Soul Reaping Yin Devils gathered together and fought to tear the True God's soul apart.
Luo Zheng could clearly see this scene. He shook his head helplessly and couldn't help but shift his gaze away.
As Dong Fangning said, the Absolute Beginning Promise was indeed a very powerful and precious life-saving ability. However, the layer of golden light on the Absolute Beginning Promise should only target the Dao Connotation in the Divine Region. In other words, the power of the three thousand Divine Daos would be immune to the Absolute Beginning Promise.
Any energy condensed from the Dao Connotation of the Divine Daos would be ineffective against that layer of golden light. However, these Soul Reaping Yin Devils were soul attacks. Hence, activating the Absolute Beginning Promise would have no effect!
For the same reason, the Promise of Absolute Beginning was ineffective against pure power.
However, cultivating the Divine Dao of Strength was also using the Dao Connotation of the Divine Dao to unleash power. It was unknown if the Absolute Beginning Promise could block it. This required further testing.
After these True Gods experienced the Abyss Devil Envoy, because of the three Promises of Absolute Beginning, they were more or less confident. They thought that activating the Promises of Absolute Beginning was equivalent to being invincible. Facing these Soul Reaping Yin Demons, many True Gods tried to activate the Promises of Absolute Beginning, but it was this action that caused them to lose their lives!
From the beginning of the Soul Reaping Yin Devils' attack until now, it had only been six to seven breaths of time. There were already more than twenty True Gods that had lost their souls. Among them, there was even an upper True God from the Liu Family.
"Hu, hu …"
Luo Zheng watched as eight to nine hooks spun in the air and came straight for him. With a twist of his body, he swiftly retreated. "Peng!" A loud crash was heard and Han Liusu's scream was heard.
"These Soul Reaping Yin Devils' target is everyone's soul. Everyone, quickly climb to the top of the Goddess statue to hide!" Luo Zheng turned around and shouted.
He saw that Han Chuyue and Han Biluo had already started climbing the tree stump below the Goddess statue. Leng Linyue had already stood on the tree stump. His expression was slightly cold. Without waiting for Han Liusu's reply, he lowered his head and grabbed Han Liusu's clothes and threw her heavily onto the tree stump.
"Ah! Be careful of those hooks! "Han Liusu screamed as she was thrown into the air to warn Luo Zheng.
Perhaps she was angry at Luo Zheng, but she was not an unreasonable woman. In this situation, how could she be angry at Luo Zheng?
Luo Zheng's Divine Sense was also spread out. He did not need to turn around to know that there were several hooks flying towards his head.
He turned around and rolled on the ground, dodging those hooks.
He did not expect that in the blink of an eye, there would be seven or eight hooks flying towards him …
Under the pressure of those hooks, Luo Zheng dodged three times in a row. As a result, he was getting further and further away from the Goddess statue!
"Destroy for me!"
As Luo Zheng continued to roll on the ground, a watermelon sized lightning ball appeared in his hand. When he had just stabilized his body, he took advantage of the momentum and smashed the lightning ball heavily onto the ground.
"Pipa!"
However, these Soul Reaping Yin Demons' reaction was too fast. When the lightning ball exploded, most of the Hook Demons had already escaped far away.
When Luo Zheng stood still, he discovered that he was surrounded by this group of Soul Reaping Yin Demons!
"Hu hu …"
Gusts of cold wind continued to revolve around Luo Zheng's body. These Soul Reaping Yin Demons carefully crawled forward. These guys looked like they were moving very slowly, but once they moved, their speed was faster than many True Gods!
"Oh no! They did it on purpose! "
Luo Zheng did not expect that after continuously dodging, he would be forced into their encirclement! Furthermore, from the looks of it, these Soul Reaping Yin Demons had clearly planned this!
"Luo Zheng, quickly run!" Han Liusu shouted from the tree stump below the Goddess statue.
"I also want to run away …"
Luo Zheng's face revealed a depressed expression. Another lightning ball condensed in his hand. The Mystic Thunder Divine Dao was indeed very effective against these Soul Reaping Yin Demons. However, these Soul Reaping Yin Demons were also very smart. After they noticed the lightning ball in Luo Zheng's hand, they did not get too close. Instead, they threw hooks at Luo Zheng from a certain distance.
The invisible hooks were accompanied by bursts of cold air as they shot towards Luo Zheng.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng directly threw the lightning ball in front of him. Taking advantage of the moment when the lightning bloomed, he rushed out of the encirclement!
However, just as Luo Zheng moved, those Soul Reaping Yin Demons also moved. More and more Soul Reaping Yin Demons joined the encirclement …
At the same time, Luo Zheng saw that not far away, an agile figure was constantly dodging those hooks. The voice seemed to have noticed Luo Zheng's side and jumped up. It turned into a red light in the air and steadily landed on Luo Zheng's side. It was Mu Ning.
Just now, she was being chased by those Soul Reaping Yin Demons. As someone who cultivated the Killing God Dao, she did not have a good way to deal with these Soul Reaping Yin Demons. Although she could use the Killing God Dao's "Devil Intent Killing Sword" to kill these Soul Reaping Yin Demons, it was too dangerous to kill them at close range. Just now, she had almost been hit by a hook!
As she was constantly escaping, she saw an open space in front of her and ran towards it. She never thought that the one standing in this open space would be Luo Zheng. Moreover, Luo Zheng was actually trapped in a encirclement.

Chapter 1930
In such a chaotic situation, most of the True Gods could only take care of themselves.
Although Han Cangyan and Zhan Ming had built the Starry Gate, there was still some distance between the Starry Gate and the Goddess statue.
When the True Gods discovered the Soul Reaping Yin Demons, it was already too late. Only a small number of True Gods who were close to the Starry Gate managed to escape. Most of the True Gods could only retreat and climb to the Goddess statue.
Mu Ning escaped into a new encirclement. Looking at the densely packed Soul Reaping Yin Demons crawling on the ground, she had an expression as if she had seen a ghost.
"Kill our way over?"
Without much time to communicate, Mu Ning simply asked.
The only way now was to kill their way out and rush to the Goddess statue.
Facing so many Soul Reaping Yin Demons, the Mu Clan's Upper True God didn't dare to save them. After all, even an Upper True God didn't have the confidence to avoid their hooks in the air.
"There's no other choice," Luo Zheng nodded and said. From his palms, lightning surged.
Mu Ning's eyes emitted traces of blood-red killing intent as if she was possessed. Her two short swords also turned blood-red, emitting a dense killing intent.
This was the "Demonic Intent Killing Sword" of the Killing God Path. It condensed her killing intent and could kill all vengeful spirits and evil spirits!
The Demonic Intent Killing Sword was often very effective against these Soul Reaping Yin Demons. However, there were too many of them. How could she deal with them all?
"Let's go!"
Luo Zheng opened his palms, and a few thunderbolts from his palms shot out!
"Pipa …"
Caught off guard, the thunderbolts in his palms instantly defeated the Soul Reaping Yin Demons in front.
"Xiu!"
Mu Ning turned into a blood-red shadow and sped forward. Her speed was even faster than Luo Zheng's. The moment she rushed towards those Soul Reaping Yin Demons, her round eyes flashed like rubies. Her thin lips spat out a word, "Die!"
"Shua shua shua!"
The pair of short swords in her hands crisscrossed, instantly killing a Soul Reaping Yin Demon in front of her. At the same time, the two short swords pulled to the sides. Two arcs of blood-red light spread out and once again cut four Soul Reaping Yin Demons in half!
The two of them killed their way out of the encirclement by taking the enemy by surprise.
But right at this moment, countless hooks shot out silently like raindrops, instantly enveloping the two's escape route!
Facing the densely packed hooks, the two of them gritted their teeth and continued to move around, trying their best to avoid them!
Although both of them were extremely agile, and some of the hooks flew past the back of Luo Zheng's head and the tip of his nose, it was almost impossible to completely avoid these hooks. Those hooks fell down like raindrops, and there was simply no space to completely dodge!
"Luo Zheng!"
Han Liusu stared fixedly at Luo Zheng and shouted loudly. At one point, she wanted to rush down from above, but was held tightly by Han Cangyan.
Most of the True Gods had already fled to the tree stump. Looking down at Luo Zheng and Mu Ning, they felt a faint sense of relief in their hearts.
"These two people can't be saved …" A True God shook his head.
Zhan Ming and Dongfang Ning's expressions were also very ugly, especially Dongfang Ning. Dongfang Chunjun had personally told him that he had to ensure the safety of "Luo Tianxing" …
If it were other ferocious creatures, Dongfang Ning would have the strength to fight. Even if they were facing the Abyss Devil Envoy, he was confident that he could save Luo Zheng. But now that he had encountered these densely packed Soul Reaping Yin Devils, he had no room to display his skills. If he rushed down now, it would be useless except for sacrificing his own life.
"Oh no!"
Mu Ning twisted her body and barely avoided one of the hooks. However, three more hooks shot out from the other side and pierced into her mind.
At the same time, Luo Zheng quickly retreated backward. He felt a cold wind spread in his mind, and a hook shot into his mind. Immediately, a sharp pain came from Luo Zheng's soul. That hook bit into his soul in an instant, and then suddenly pulled it out!
The Extreme Evil Old Man, who was hiding in Luo Zheng's mind, was also dumbfounded. There was nothing he could do in this situation.
Luo Zheng's soul was forcibly pulled out of his mind by that hook, and at the same time, several other hooks followed closely behind. Those hooks firmly bit into his soul and pulled it toward the Soul Reaping Yin Devils.
One could imagine the only outcome after being pulled over was that he would be eaten by these Soul Reaping Yin Devils.
When Luo Zheng's soul was pulled over, he saw that Mu Ning's soul was the same. Her face was full of horror, but her soul was involuntarily pulled in a direction …
Many True Gods on the tree stump saw this scene and couldn't help but sigh.
They saw that Luo Zheng and Mu Ning's bodies were standing upright, which meant that these two bodies had lost their souls …
Leng Linyue had already climbed to a higher place, a smile on his face. Luo Tianxing being able to fall here naturally made him feel very satisfied.
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo widened their eyes and kept shaking their heads. "Impossible, how could that guy die so easily?"
Han Liusu's eyes were fixed on the ground below. At the same time, she released her divine sense and searched for Luo Zheng's soul. "His soul hasn't been torn apart …" Until the last moment when Luo Zheng was torn apart, she still had a glimmer of hope, even though this glimmer of hope was very slim.
Most of the Sages outside the Sea of Time were focused on the Perfect True Gods. However, the scene in the canyon once again attracted the Sages' attention.
"That little guy actually died in the hands of the Soul Reaping Yin Devils," a Sage said softly.
Dongfang Chunjun's expression was still calm. Even if he wanted to take this person as his disciple, everything depended on fate. The female leader of the Blind Tribe didn't allow Sages to enter the Sea of Time, so he couldn't save him. He only said calmly, "It's indeed a pity …"
Aunt Han Jiu's gaze was very serious. She stared at Luo Zheng with a faintly discernible sadness in her eyes. She didn't believe that Luo Zheng would die just like that!
Luo Zheng's soul quickly fell. Then, he saw the Soul Reaping Yin Devils crawling on the ground quickly surround him. These Soul Reaping Yin Devils were originally souls. They didn't hide their greedy aura at all. After pouncing on him, they opened their big mouths and couldn't wait to tear Luo Zheng into pieces!
"Is there no other choice …" Luo Zheng sighed softly in his heart.
Then, the surface of his soul suddenly emitted rays of golden light. The golden light transformed into rays of golden sword shadows that continuously revolved around his soul.
After Luo Zheng practiced the Great Creation Technique in the universe, his soul had been greatly strengthened. With the cultivation of a Proving God, the strength of his soul was comparable to a True God's.
Now that he was promoted to a Lower True God, his soul had been greatly strengthened.
However, the Severing Emotions Divine Dao was closely related to the soul. When his soul was strengthened, he also comprehended the most crucial "Anatta Sword" of the Severing Emotions Divine Dao. It could be said that Luo Zheng's soul had also undergone the tempering of the Severing Emotions Divine Dao.
If he used it rashly, he was afraid that other True Gods would discover it. Therefore, after Luo Zheng entered the Divine Realm, he rarely used the power of his soul. In the Heaven-containing Mansion, Luo Zheng only transformed it into pure soul power. He didn't activate the golden sword shadows on the surface of his soul.
It wasn't a good idea to activate the Severing Emotions Divine Dao in the Sea of Time forbidden area. This Dao was a Forbidden Dao. It was destined to be a Divine Dao that people weren't allowed to cultivate. He didn't know if those Saints would let him go if they saw this scene.
However, in this situation, Luo Zheng didn't have a choice. The only thing he could do was to survive!
When those golden sword shadows appeared, they emitted rays of golden light that shot out in all directions.
Although these rays of golden light belonged to soul power, when they were in the universe, they were already strong enough to flatten mountains. As Luo Zheng's cultivation increased, their power was even stronger now!

Chapter 1931
These small arrows formed from soul pressure were extremely sharp.
With Luo Zheng's current cultivation, these small arrows formed from soul pressure might even be able to cut open a wound on a lower True God's body.
But the soul body was far weaker than the physical body. These Soul Reaping Yin Demons were also vengeful spirits, so how could they withstand it?
"Puff puff puff puff puff …"
In the blink of an eye, the Soul Reaping Yin Demons closest to Luo Zheng were turned into sieves by the small arrows formed from the soul pressure …
As for those soul arrows, they shot out in all directions. Some of them even shot out a distance of several thousand feet!
Because Luo Zheng was a soul body, besides the Soul Reaping Yin Demons and Mu Ning, no one else could see these small soul arrows. They could only rely on their divine sense to sense Luo Zheng's position and release an extremely sharp and powerful soul pressure.
"Hu hu …"
Some of the small soul arrows pierced through the Soul Reaping Yin Demons and shot towards the tree stump below the goddess statue!
Zhan Ming reached out his hand and lightly blocked it. From his broad hand, there was a muffled "pu" sound. Although this small soul arrow didn't injure him, the power that came from it made Zhan Ming surprised. "This is the soul pressure released by Luo Tianxing? It's so strong! "
Besides Zhan Ming, there were also a few True Gods that were hit by these small soul arrows. But after these small soul arrows flew a distance, they lost most of their power and didn't injure anyone …
But the Soul Reaping Yin Demons below were miserable!
Countless small soul arrows shot out. The Soul Reaping Yin Demons on the ground didn't have any space to dodge. They couldn't escape in time and were directly pierced by the small soul arrows.
Mu Ning's soul curled up into a ball. At this moment, her mind was completely blank. She recalled the past as if it was a conditioned reflex …
She didn't want to die here. She wanted to become the second Mu Xuerong of the Mu Family. She had always seen her sister as a role model. She also had strong self-confidence because her talent wasn't any weaker than her sister's!
Unfortunately, with her soul shattering, everything no longer existed …
An indescribable pain transmitted over. Mu Ning felt as if her soul was being torn apart. To have to endure this kind of pain before death was too tragic.
However, at this moment, she felt an extremely powerful Soul Pressure rapidly approaching her.
"Pu!"
Then, she saw that the Soul Reaping Yin Devil that was biting her soul had been pierced through!
"Poof! Puff! Puff … "
Numerous small soul arrows shot out. Holes appeared on the bodies of the Soul-catching Yin Devils one after another. They fell down one after another, turning into pure soul power and drifting behind her.
After the Soul Hooking Yin Demons fell to the ground, Mu Ning's soul struggled to crawl up. She was puzzled. Just what kind of powerhouse could have such a powerful soul force!?
When Mu Ning turned around, she saw a soul flickering with golden light!
Golden sword shadows constantly revolved around the soul. Every time the golden light flickered, small soul arrows would shoot towards her!
"Puff, puff, puff, puff …"
Even more Soul Reaping Yin Demons around her were beaten into sieves, and some were even smashed into pieces.
And she was safe and sound!
"Luo Tianxing?"
Mu Ning stared at the golden soul for a while. Her chaotic thoughts gradually cleared up, and she recognized Luo Zheng's appearance. In any case, she never thought that Luo Zheng's soul could be so powerful!
Because in Mu Ning's memory, even the soul pressure of an upper True God couldn't have such power. Otherwise, the upper True Gods in the canyon wouldn't have escaped.
How could this guy's soul pressure be so powerful …
Luo Zheng continued to release small soul arrows. At the same time, he slowly walked towards her. He reached out, picked her up from the ground, and dragged her in the direction of her body.
Her soul had been torn apart by the Soul-catching Yin Devils and had been injured, but the injury wasn't serious. After arriving near her body, Mu Ning's soul drilled into her mind.
Luo Zheng didn't want to stay in the state of a soul. Soon, he returned to his body's mind. When he opened his eyes, his eyes once again emitted a golden light.
Since the Emotion Severing Divine Dao had been exposed, he had no more scruples.
Killing these Soul-catching Yin Devils could quickly enhance his soul power, and the increase was not small. Naturally, he wouldn't let any of them go!
In fact, many warriors who fled to the tree stump also used the power of thunder and lightning to kill these Soul-catching Yin Devils. Every time they killed a Soul-catching Yin Devil, their soul power would be greatly enhanced. No one was willing to let go of this opportunity. However, they were relatively far away, so the efficiency of killing the Soul-catching Yin Devils was not high.
Luo Zheng was different. As long as they were within the range of his vision, all the Soul-catching Yin Devils were shot through and killed …
These Soul-catching Yin Devils were indeed abnormally agile. They also found that they were not Luo Zheng's opponent, so they crawled on the ground and fled. However, the canyon was only so big. Where could they escape to?
Thus, Luo Zheng launched a blatant pursuit!
The Saints outside the Sea of Time Forbidden Area focused their eyes on Luo Zheng, revealing a strange expression in their eyes.
"If I'm not wrong, this little guy has cultivated the Emotion Severing Divine Dao?" The Li Family Saint said lightly.
"Without a doubt, only the Emotion Severing Divine Dao can enhance the soul power to such an extent," another Quasi-saint nodded and said.
"The Emotion Severing Divine Dao is a forbidden Dao. Its Dao tablet was sealed more than a hundred Divine Epochs ago. The True Gods who cultivated the Emotion Severing Divine Dao were also hunted and killed. Not a single replica Dao tablet of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao remains … Where did this little guy cultivate the Emotion Severing Divine Dao?" The Fang Family Saint's face was full of suspicion.
Dongfang Chunjun also frowned. "This little guy has never used the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, and I didn't sense the Dao essence of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao from his body. Could it be that he accidentally obtained the soul cultivation method of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao?"
"The Great Sage Dongfang is right," Emperor Hanqing quickly said. "Perhaps his cultivation method was separated from the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, and he himself doesn't know …"
"Hehehe, I don't think so," Tang Lun laughed and said. "This guy didn't use the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, so he must know that it's a forbidden Dao. Just now, he was forced into a desperate situation, so he was forced to use the Emotion Severing Divine Dao to operate his own soul power. He had no choice but to survive. Who knows how far he has cultivated the Emotion Severing Divine Dao?"
Dongfang Chunjun nodded. "When he comes out, we must ask him clearly."
A mere Lower True God cultivating the Emotion Severing Divine Dao would not be a taboo for these Saints. However, the Emotion Severing Divine Dao had long been listed as a forbidden Dao. This was also the consensus of all the Saint Halls. No matter who cultivated this Divine Dao, they would not be allowed.
If Luo Tianxing really cultivated the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, it was not a big problem in Dongfang Chunjun's eyes. Back then, the Emotion Severing Divine Dao was listed as a forbidden Dao because Dongfang Chunjun pushed for the rule to be set.
If he really wanted to take this little guy as a disciple, he could completely destroy his Emotion Severing Divine Dao, and even erase his memories of it.

Chapter 1932
The Emotion Severing Divine Dao had disappeared from the Realm of the Gods for more than a hundred God Epochs.
Even if a True God had an eternal lifespan, more than a hundred God Epochs was still too long …
Although the True Gods in the canyon knew about the existence of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao and knew that it was a Forbidden Dao, they had never seen it before.
In recent years, the only time the Emotion Severing Divine Dao was when Chen Huang Yijian escaped into the Abyss Devil Domain.
However, the storm that Chen Huang Yijian caused back then wasn't too big, so many True Gods knew about it. Moreover, after Chen Huang Yijian escaped into the Abyss Devil Domain, he disappeared without a trace. No one had seen him again, so this matter slowly faded away.
Luo Zheng relied on the Divine Creation Technique to greatly increase his soul force, and relied on the Emotion Severing Divine Dao to erupt with such a powerful soul force. Those True Gods who couldn't see his origin were all stunned!
Previously, these True Gods were worried that even if they escaped to the Goddess statue, these Soul Reaping Yin Demons would eventually catch up.
Those True Gods who had grasped the Lightning Divine Dao were unable to kill these Soul Reaping Yin Demons. Perhaps, except for the upper True Gods, everyone else would be annihilated …
They never thought that this brat would rely on himself to chase a large group of Soul Reaping Yin Demons all over the canyon!
"Is this guy really just a lower True God?" A middle True God stared blankly at Luo Zheng who was madly running through the canyon. Under the perception of this middle True God, any Soul Reaping Yin Demon caught by Luo Zheng was quickly killed. This guy was simply unstoppable!
Leng Lin Yue looked at Luo Zheng in the distance, his eyelids constantly twitching. Even if he didn't want to admit it, this guy was much stronger than he had imagined …
After Mu Ning's soul returned to her body, she quickly climbed up the huge tree stump. Seeing Luo Zheng so fiercely chasing after the Soul Reaping Yin Demons, she sighed in her heart. This guy … even his soul was much stronger than she had imagined.
There was no emotion in Luo Zheng's eyes. Under the flickering golden light, those small soul arrows turned into a rain of arrows and shot out.
The Soul-reaping Yin Devil kept crawling on the ground, but in the end, it was smashed into pieces by Luo Zheng, turning into streams of pure soul power, which continuously strengthened Luo Zheng's soul.
Although many True Gods on the tree stump released lightning to kill some lone Soul Reaping Yin Demons, most of them were killed by Luo Zheng.
Because of this, Luo Zheng was able to absorb a massive amount of soul force. His soul was rapidly growing!
After killing the last Soul Hooking Yin Demon, Luo Zheng returned to the bottom of the statue.
The Real Gods who had escaped from the tree stumps then jumped down one by one. Many Real Gods still had lingering fears.
Not far away, there were a few True Gods who were either standing or lying motionless.
The souls of these True Gods had already been hollowed out. They stood motionless like statues. Because their souls had just been extracted, the life force in their bodies hadn't been cut off. They had just turned into walking corpses and had lost their consciousness. It was the same as dying.
"Second Brother!" A True God with a face full of grief called out as he held onto a statue.
Many True Gods came from the same rich and powerful family. They were all blood-related brothers. Seeing this scene, they were naturally very sad.
Zhan Ming shook his head and sighed. "Send these corpses out of the Star Door …"
"These aren't corpses!" A True God retorted.
Zhan Ming shrugged his shoulders. In fact, to determine whether a True God had died or not, the most important thing was whether their soul had been destroyed. Even if their physical body was destroyed, their soul would be perfectly preserved. There would be a chance for them to make a comeback. If their soul dissipated, then there was no saving them.
The True Gods cleaned up the scene and sent the True God corpses out of the Star Door.
The canyon was very quiet. Most of the True Gods maintained their silence.
The True Gods didn't gain any substantial benefits from defending against the Soul Hooking Yin Demons. They didn't even get a single Shenwu coin. The only ones who benefited were the True Gods who had killed the Soul Hooking Yin Demons. Their soul strength had increased greatly!
Among them, the one who benefited the most was Luo Zheng.
He had single-handedly killed more than three-quarters of the Soul Hooking Yin Demons and continuously absorbed the pure and precious soul energy. His soul strength had increased greatly!
The Evil Old Man in his mind stared blankly at Luo Zheng's soul.
Back when the Evil Old Man's physical body had perished, he had seen Luo Zheng use those soul arrows to tear apart Yin Yuanzi's soul.
At that time, Luo Zheng was merely a Proof of Divinity martial artist, but he was able to shatter the soul of an intermediate True God!
Now that Luo Zheng had absorbed so much soul energy, Luo Zheng's soul was even more full and powerful. The surface of his soul had a faint glassy glow. Even though Luo Zheng didn't release any soul suppression, the aura that was faintly exuded from his soul gave the Evil Old Man a formless pressure.
"Luo Zheng, your soul feels like it's getting closer to perfection. Are you about to become a Sun God?" the Evil Old Man couldn't help but ask.
"Sun God?" Luo Zheng replied.
The Evil Old Man nodded. "The soul of a Perfected True God is called the Sun God."
If a True God wanted to become a Perfected True God, they had to master a perfected divine ability of a Divine Dao. Even if they became a Perfected True God, the seventh stage divine ability of each Divine Dao was relatively powerful. Their combat power would be a level higher than that of an upper-level True God!
However, such a Perfected True God would still have some shortcomings — the soul and the physical body.
Some True Gods, after reaching perfection, would think of ways to make up for these two shortcomings. Cultivating nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier in the physical body would make the physical body perfect, and cultivating the soul to become a Sun God would make the soul perfect!
"It's said that after cultivating the Sun God, the soul would turn from Yin to Yang. It's like a reborn body that doesn't fear lightning or any evil-warding techniques," the Evil Old Man added.
Even if the soul of a True God still belonged to the category of "Yin God," it was essentially the same as those evil spirits and resentful spirits. They were all Yin Gods, and the soul residing in the mind was relatively weak. If lightning struck the mind, the soul would still be severely damaged.
Other than that, there were also some purifying Divine Abilities that could purify the soul.
However, once the soul became a Sun God, the soul would undergo a qualitative change. Just like the Evil Old Man said, the soul that stepped into the Sun Soul Tier was like a reborn body. It wouldn't be so afraid of the power of lightning. After the soul left the body, it could even be seen with the naked eye.
"So that's how it is." Luo Zheng nodded.
After absorbing the soul power, he indeed felt an unprecedented sense of fullness in his soul. The feeling of emptiness in his soul gradually dissipated as if his soul had turned into a substantial existence.
If he really cultivated to the Sun God Tier, his soul could really turn into a substantial body. How would that feel? Wouldn't the soul pressure unleashed by using a Sun God Tier soul to activate the Emotion Severing Divine Path be even more powerful?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng actually felt some regret. It was a pity that he'd already killed all the Soul-Reaping Yin Demons. Wouldn't it be great if another wave came?
"Hong long long …"
At this moment, the canyon began to shake again. The sixth wave of monsters was attacking.
At the same time, the True Gods who'd remained silent also became restless.
"I'm not going to defend this wave!"
"Even if there's a treasure of faith, I don't want it. If I continue to drag this out, I'm purely seeking death …"
"If everyone still wants to defend to the death, please forgive me for not accompanying you!"
Those Soul-Reaping Yin Demons had taken the lives of dozens of True Gods, but the surviving True Gods had gained nothing. It was inevitable that they would want to retreat.
These True Gods left through the Star Gate one after another in order to save their lives.
In the blink of an eye, more than half of the three hundred True Gods present had left. Only seventy or eighty True Gods remained under the statue of the Goddess …
"Are we still defending?" Dongfang Ning asked lightly.
Zhan Ming looked at the pass at the end of the canyon with a wry smile on his face. "With so few of us, we can't defend even if we want to. What's more, those things are really not something we can fight against …"
The True Gods followed Zhan Ming's gaze and looked over. From the passes in all directions, huge golden tortoises slowly crawled out.
Their thick limbs stepped on the ground, leaving pits. They crawled very slowly, but there were many of them.
"Black Armor Golden Tortoise." Dongfang Ning shook his head expressionlessly. "A fourth-level monster in Buzhou Spirit Mountain. This thing can almost reflect an attack. It's really not something we can deal with."
"Let's go." Mystic Moon Xiao Lou said lightly. Since these monsters in front of them were far from what they could deal with, leaving as soon as possible was the right choice.
Since these Upper True Gods had already made their choice, Han Jia, Mu Jia, and some True Gods who were watching from the sidelines no longer hesitated.
Zhan Ming's face showed regret. He could only shake his head and lead the way into the Star Gate.
However, at this moment, the light in the center of the Star Gate rippled slightly.

Chapter 1933
That True God came back in a panic, and his face was filled with fear.
Even the Soul Hooking Yin Demons did not make True Gods so fearful. What did he see on the other side of the Starry Door?
"What's wrong?" Zhan Ming asked curiously.
"Outside, outside …" That True God pointed at the Starry Door and stuttered. He could not even speak clearly due to his extreme fear.
Following that, the Starry Door kept flashing, and True Gods kept rushing in. These True Gods who returned from outside the canyon looked as though they had seen a ghost. It seemed like they had found something unbelievable outside the canyon.
"What happened?!" Zhan Ming asked sternly.
"Outside, there's a ferocious creature outside!" A True God said while trembling.
True Gods came back from the Starry Door one after another. Within four to five breaths, more than half of the two hundred True Gods who had left had returned.
"Ah ah ah ah …"
A burly Upper True God rushed into the Starry Door and roared at the same time, "It's here. That thing followed us in!"
This burly Upper True God came from the Xie Family. His previous performance was rather valiant. He was the kind of guy who was not afraid of anything. Even the Soul Hooking Yin Demons were killed by his Thunder God's Halberd.
But now, he looked as though he was scared out of his wits!
Just as he finished speaking, Luo Zheng saw a strange claw extending from the Starry Door.
That claw had three toes, and each toe was inlaid with a black toenail. After that claw passed through the Starry Door, it pinched the Xie Family's Upper True God as though it was pinching an ant. It dragged the Xie Family's True God toward the Starry Door.
"Chop off that claw!" Zhan Ming's face darkened, and he had already made his move!
Apart from that, the other Upper True Gods also made their moves …
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. The Buddha Emperor's Sword was like an invisible fish. It formed in the blink of an eye and slid toward that claw in the blink of an eye …
"Bang bang bang!"
"Dong dong dong …"
"Rumble …"
Big hammer, long sword, giant axe …
The True Gods made their moves and slashed at that giant claw.
Without any hesitation, Luo Zheng's Buddha Emperor's Sword struck the claw …
After this round of attacks, the giant claw that had passed through the Star Door was not damaged in the slightest!
"Help! No, kill me! Please kill me … "
The upper True Divinity of the Xie Family roared, his eyes filled with fear. His hands dug into the ground, leaving behind fingerprints. From the meaning of his words, it seemed that being dragged into the Star Gate was even more terrifying than being killed.
However, none of the True Gods present were willing to kill him. They could only watch as he disappeared into the Stargate.
"What the hell is going on? What is that? "Zhan Ming asked loudly.
A Real God who had returned from outside the canyon quickly said, "Stop talking. Han Cangyan, quickly keep the Stargate!"
Han Cangyan looked hesitant.
The Starry Door could only be opened once. Although he could indeed collect the Starry Door, the Starry Door would be rendered useless and could not be opened again.
The Mysterious Armored Golden Turtles in the canyon were slowly crawling towards them. If they kept the Stargate, wouldn't they lose their only way out? What method could they use to leave the canyon?
That claw must have come from some extremely powerful monster. Even if they escaped to the Stargate, they would still have to face that monster …
Just as Han Cangyan was hesitating, the center of the azure Stargate rippled like a lake.
"Everyone, be careful. Something else is coming!"
The True Gods present spread out and tried their best to distance themselves from the Stargate. They stared at the Stargate with wide eyes.
"Whoosh!"
Another True God dashed out from the Stargate.
That True God was wearing a blood-red robe. Her speed was extremely fast. Including Luo Zheng, Zhan Ming, and Dongfang Ning, no one could see her face clearly. This True God had already rushed to one side with a huge momentum. In order to slow down her momentum, she flipped in mid-air. The two weapons in her hands even plowed two deep trenches in the ground …
Finally, she stopped at the edge of the canyon and happened to collide with a Golden Armored Golden Turtle. Only then did she slowly stand up!
"Sister!" Mu Ning's face was full of surprise.
The newcomer was the Mu Family's Perfection True God, Mu Xuerong.
At this moment, Mu Xuerong's face was in a sorry state. Half of her fine black hair had been cut off, and there was even some dirt on her forehead. Although she was in such a sorry state, it still couldn't cover up her beautiful appearance.
Mu Xuerong nodded at Mu Ning, but the Golden Armored Golden Turtle behind her had already launched an attack.
In the end, Mu Xuerong was still a Perfection True God. When she sensed the attack behind her, she didn't even turn her head. The short scimitar in her hand turned, and a blood-red blade light howled out.
"Puchi!"
Although the Golden Armored Golden Turtle's shell was thick and heavy, under her slash, it didn't stop her at all. It was directly cut in half!
Although the Golden Armor Black Tortoise had been killed, the moment it was struck by Mu Xuerong's saber, a faint white curtain of light appeared above the tortoise's shell. A blood red saber light shot out from the curtain of light and howled towards Mu Xuerong!
"Puchi!"
The blood-red blade light slashed at Mu Xuerong's back with lightning speed.
This was the blade that Mu Xuerong had just slashed out!
As a Perfection True Divinity, a casual saber strike from Mu Xuerong was extraordinarily powerful. The blood red saber light bounced back onto her back, leaving behind a bloody wound. Even several of her ribs were broken. She bent over in pain and couldn't help but coldly snort.
At this moment, she looked back, and her face revealed an expression as if she had seen a ghost. She couldn't help but angrily say, "A Golden Armored Golden Turtle actually appeared here …"
The Golden Armored Black Tortoise's shell could bounce back all attacks. This could also be considered Mu Xuerong's carelessness. When she had rushed into the Star Door, she hadn't looked at these vicious beasts. In her impression, the strength of these vicious beasts wasn't even worth mentioning.
Seeing the other Golden Armored Golden Turtles slowly crawling towards her, she endured the pain on her back and gently leapt up. Her figure flashed and she arrived below the Goddess statue. She slumped against a tree stump below the Goddess statue and sat down. Then, her right hand gently drew a small circle in the void. A red and gold pill strangely fell out of the circle. She stretched out her finger and lightly flicked the golden pill. The golden pill drew a parabola and fell into her mouth.
After the Golden Pill entered her mouth, her originally exhausted complexion recovered a lot. Mu Ning, who was silent on the side, asked with concern, "Elder Sister, what happened outside?"
Perfection True Gods didn't care about the opportunities in the first level of the Sea of Time. They didn't even care about the opportunities in the second level.
However, many years ago, even a Perfect Real God had to follow the ordinary Real Gods to fight their way up level by level. Similarly, they had to go through waves of ferocious creatures below the Goddess statue before they were qualified to head to the second level of Time Sea.
Later on, a perfected true god accidentally discovered a space crack in the forbidden land of the Sea of Time. It was as if he used space transference to enter the second level and the third level through this crack. This was a shortcut that only perfected true gods could use.
Therefore, Perfection True Gods didn't explore with ordinary True Gods anymore. After entering the Sea of Time, all Perfection True Gods would enter the third level immediately.
Mu Xuerong and the other Perfection True Gods didn't expect that the Sea of Time forbidden land's difficulty would increase greatly this time. In the third level of the Sea of Time, they had provoked an extremely powerful vicious creature.
These Perfection True Gods were also extremely powerful. They also wanted to surround and kill this vicious creature. However, if they fought with this vicious creature in the third level of the forbidden land, they were afraid that they would alarm other powerful existences. After discussing, they lured this vicious creature to the first level of the forbidden land.

Chapter 1934
The two hundred or so True Gods who had left the canyon did not understand what had happened.
They only wanted to return to the outside of the forbidden area. If they went back directly, it was very likely that they would encounter those Time Wraiths again. They would need to discuss how to return to the entrance of the forbidden area.
They did not expect that as soon as they came out of the stargate, they would see a group of Perfected True Gods suddenly descend here!
Some True Gods were even overjoyed. With the protection of these Perfected True Gods, leaving the forbidden area of the Sea of Time was a piece of cake.
Immediately after, they realized that something was wrong. These Perfected True Gods were all injured and very tired. It looked like they had gone through a fierce battle!
Then, that huge monster appeared on the first level of the forbidden area …
Some True Gods with fast reactions immediately went through the stargate and escaped into the canyon. Therefore, the first True God who rushed in collided with Zhan Ming.
When the Xie family's Upper True God went through the stargate, he was very unlucky and was locked onto by that huge monster.
Even though the Xie family's Upper True God rushed into the stargate, he was still caught by that huge monster …
"Rumble … rumble …"
Before Mu Xuerong could answer her sister's words, violent vibrations came from outside the canyon. The sound of explosions was very uncomfortable in everyone's ears.
She looked toward the east of the canyon and said, "They are still fighting with that monster. They will probably escape in a while." Then, Mu Xuerong thought for a moment and asked, "Whose stargate is this?"
"Mine," Han Cangyan stood up and replied.
"Give me the scroll," Mu Xuerong ordered.
Han Cangyan did not like Mu Xuerong's attitude. However, under such circumstances, Mu Xuerong was a Perfected True God. He could not disobey her and could only hand over the scroll.
Then, Mu Xuerong said, "They are probably not a match for that monster. When they escape in, I will take the stargate …"
When the True Gods heard this, they understood.
These Perfected True Gods had attracted a very powerful monster from somewhere. Moreover, this group of Perfected True Gods was not a match for that monster. Instead, they had to escape into the canyon through the stargate …
All the True Gods present had gloomy expressions on their faces. Now, even their way back had been cut off. What should they do?
Luo Zheng leaned against a tree stump and looked at the tortoises that were slowly crawling over. He said, "The most important thing now is to deal with these golden tortoises, right?"
Now, the Star Gate had become a dead end. Those Golden Shell Xuan Turtles were crawling over from all directions. Although their speed was not fast, they would reach the end sooner or later.
These True Gods were depressed, but after being reminded by Luo Zheng, they suddenly woke up. Some True Gods even wanted to cry but had no tears. They still had to deal with the attacks of the monsters in the canyon!
"Boom, boom!"
Not long after Luo Zheng's voice fell, a furious roar was heard from outside the canyon. Then, a blazing flame rose up outside the eastern mountain pass.
"I'll cover you guys go in first!"
Someone shouted. Even though they were far away, Luo Zheng and the others could hear it clearly.
"Buzz …"
The center of the starry door flickered, and Great Perfection Real Gods rushed in one after another. In the blink of an eye, there were dozens of Great Perfection Real Gods in the canyon.
"Father!" Han Biluo saw a figure that was burned black and shouted loudly.
One of the Great Perfection Real Gods was the Han Family's patriarch, Han Tianxiao.
"Patriarch." Han Cangyan, Han Chuyue, and the others nodded at Han Tianxiao.
These Great Perfection Real Gods were the same as Mu Xuerong. They looked very miserable, but their auras were very stable, and their eyes were shining. They should not have suffered any serious injuries …
The Great Perfection Real Gods were either the patriarchs of the big families or leaders like Mu Xuerong. The True Gods present quickly found their backbone and gathered around the Great Perfection Real Gods of their own families.
"Mu Xuerong, you were the only one missing! You actually ran to the canyon to slack off and almost killed us! "Tang Wan said angrily.
Mu Xuerong's expression did not change. She only replied indifferently, "That guy went berserk and retreated. Isn't this common sense?"
"You really shouldn't have left early," a white-clothed man with a feather fan said. This person was the Great Perfection Real God of the Fang Family, Fang Henshao.
"Just now, if you worked hard, you might have killed that guy before he went berserk," another Great Perfection Real God said softly. The one who spoke was a Great Perfection Real God from the Sword Manor.
Seeing everyone criticizing her, Mu Xuerong rolled her eyes. She was too lazy to speak. After counting the number of people and confirming that all the Great Perfection Real Gods had entered, she took Han Cangyan's scroll and walked toward the starry door.
However, just as she walked to the side of the starry door, the center of the starry door rippled again. The black three-toed claw pierced through the starry door again!
"Hmph!"
"Blood Fiend Cut!"
Mu Xuerong coldly snorted. Her face was filled with anger. She pulled out a short sword and aimed at the three-toed claw and slashed wildly!
This three-toed claw had forcefully dragged out the Xie Family's True God. Luo Zheng and the other True Gods were unable to do anything to this claw. However, under Mu Xuerong's wild slashes, she actually forcefully cut open wounds that were so deep that the bones could be seen!
This claw could not withstand Mu Xuerong's attacks. It could only forcefully withdraw from the starry door.
After that, Mu Xuerong gently waved the scroll and erased the starry door. At the same time, the scroll turned into dust and completely dissipated …
"Roar, roar …"
"Rumble, rumble!"
At the same time, everyone heard a series of roars coming from the eastern side of the canyon. At the same time, a huge monster was hammering the layer of light that surrounded the canyon. However, this layer of light could isolate everything. The huge monster could not enter at all.
Mu Xuerong glanced indifferently and said, "That guy is still in the berserk stage. We will temporarily hide in the canyon for a while. After its berserk state disappears, we will kill our way out!"
"Temporarily hide?" A burly Perfect True God looked at the nearby Black-armored Golden Turtle and said, "Dealing with these Black-armored Golden Turtles won't be that easy, right?"
If these Perfect True Gods were to fight against the Abyss Devil Envoys or the Soul Hooking Yin Demons, it would be very simple.
Some Perfect True Gods could kill the Abyss Devil Envoys by themselves.
As for the Soul Hooking Yin Demons, it was even simpler. Some Perfect True Gods had cultivated their souls to perfection and had already become Yang Gods. If their souls were truly hooked out, they could use their soul bodies to crush the Soul Hooking Yin Demons.
However, the Black-armored Golden Turtle was very troublesome.
With its tortoise shell, this kind of vicious creature could practically reflect all attacks.
The more powerful the attack, the more powerful the rebound would be …
"It's just a little troublesome. We killed quite a few of these things in Buzhou Spirit Mountain." The Perfect True God of the Zhao Family held a golden ringed blade and walked toward the Black-armored Golden Turtle. The Black-armored Golden Turtle's innate ability was indeed a little troublesome. However, it was only a little troublesome.
With these Perfect True Gods holding the fort, Zhan Ming, Dongfang Ning, Han Cangyan, and the rest of the True Gods calmed down.
However, once they passed the trial of the goddess statue, the layer of light screen that isolated the canyon would disappear. When that time came, the situation would be completely different if the thing outside were to rush in. They had to be prepared then.
While the True Gods were still thinking, the Perfect True God of the Zhao Family had already waved his ringed blade toward the Black-armored Golden Turtles!
This Perfect True God of the Zhao Family was called Zhao Wubian. He was extremely powerful. A Perfect True God like him dealt with the Black-armored Golden Turtles like cutting vegetables.
With a swing of his blade, three Black-armored Golden Turtles were killed by him.
However, a white light screen suddenly appeared on the backs of the three Black-armored Golden Turtles. A ray of blade light shot out and reflected Zhao Wubian's attack back!
"Puff, puff, puff!"
Zhao Wubian did not dodge. He took the blade light head-on. However, the blade light did not cause any harm to him.
After Zhao Wubian killed the Black-armored Golden Turtle, three rays of blood-red fog escaped from the Black-armored Golden Turtle's body. This fog contained a strong power of blood and energy. It quickly entered Zhao Wubian's body.
The other Perfect True Gods were recuperating under the goddess statue. They did not want to fight.
At their level of cultivation, they were not very interested in ordinary rewards. However, when they saw this scene, their eyes immediately revealed their desire.

Chapter 1935
Soul, Divine Path, and even strength could all be cultivated to perfection, which was the upper limit of a "god."
After cultivating nine stars of strength, one could only continue to accumulate strength, and it would be difficult for one's soul to grow after reaching the Sun God Realm. Divine Path was the standard to determine whether one was a Perfected True God or not. If one wanted to become stronger after breaking through, one could only place their hopes on extra-dao magical powers.
However, blood energy could be absorbed limitlessly. A Perfected True God's body was very strong, and the blood energy contained in the body was far stronger than that of an ordinary True God. However, it was very difficult to rely on one's own blood energy to repair the body.
For example, Mu Xuerong had swallowed Seven Transformations Golden Pills to repair her injuries, which were extremely expensive.
If one's blood energy was strong enough, such injuries could be quickly healed, and there was no need for pills to heal.
Therefore, these Perfected True Gods were often scrambling for pills and techniques that could strengthen their blood energy. A few Perfected True Gods also stood up, planning to kill those Black-armored Golden Turtles.
The shells of these Black-armored Golden Turtles could reflect all attacks. For Perfected True Gods, it was not too much of a problem.
Luo Zheng also noticed the blood energy.
He had swallowed a Nine-Headed Snake Blood Pill in the immortal abode, which had greatly strengthened his blood energy.
However, because his body was far stronger than a True God of the same level, his blood energy could not keep up after breaking through to a lower-level True God.
In a situation where most True Gods did not plan to attack, Luo Zheng chose a direction and walked toward the Black-armored Golden Turtles!
Dong, dong, dong …
The leading Black-armored Golden Turtle noticed Luo Zheng and rushed toward him.
However, the Black-armored Golden Turtle was carrying a heavy shell on its back. Even if it was charging at full speed, its speed was very slow.
Whoosh …
Traces of strength energy condensed in front of Luo Zheng, forming the Buddha Emperor Sword and slashing toward the Black-armored Golden Turtle's head!
The Black-armored Golden Turtle saw the formless Buddha Emperor Sword coming at it menacingly and retracted its head into its shell!
"Crack!"
The Buddha Emperor Sword hit the Black-armored Golden Turtle's shell, leaving a fist-sized crack on the shell.
Luo Zheng did not use his full strength. Naturally, he could not be like those Perfected True Gods who could kill a Black-armored Golden Turtle with a raise of his hand. According to Luo Zheng's estimation, he could kill the Black-armored Golden Turtle with a few more strikes.
However, as soon as his sword slashed out, a layer of white light suddenly appeared on the shell of the Black-armored Golden Tortoise. Then, an invisible sword shadow flew out from the light and fiercely shot towards Luo Zheng!
This Buddha Emperor Sword had only condensed thirty-five percent of its power source, but it was still very threatening to Luo Zheng. If this sword hit him, he would be severely injured, if not killed.
Luo Zheng had no choice but to use the Chongyang Sword to block in front of him.
....
Accompanied by a dull sound, the two swords collided. They canceled each other out and disappeared at the same time.
"That kid … he made a huge profit in the last round of killing the Soul Reaping Yin Devils, but he's still greedy and not satisfied."
"Humph. He was lucky to kill the Soul-catching Yin Demons. I don't know what Soul Secret Skill he has that can restrain the Soul-catching Yin Demons. These Black-armored Golden Turtles are not easy to deal with!"
"Indeed. He is just asking for humiliation!"
This scene was seen by some Real Gods and they naturally treated it as a joke.
They forgot that the Soul-catching Yin Demons' first target was the True Gods, then the Goddess statue.
If Luo Zheng did not kill the Soul-catching Yin Demons, the Soul-catching Yin Demons would climb up the tree stump. By then, besides some upper-level True Gods who could fly away, the rest of the True Gods would die.
From this perspective, Luo Zheng saved the lives of most of the True Gods.
Some Perfected True Gods who did not fight stared at Luo Zheng with interest. They wanted to see if Luo Zheng could get rid of the Black-armored Golden Turtle.
"Roar!"
At this moment, the Black-armored Golden Tortoise popped its head out and bit at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's figure twisted lightly and easily avoided this bite, and then a wisp of a smile appeared on his face as he withdrew the shapeless Buddha Emperor's Sword once more!
"Swoosh!"
The Buddha Emperor Sword still could not kill the Black-armored Golden Turtle's head. It hit the turtle shell again.
"Ka!"
The crack on the huge turtle shell expanded again. At the same time, the turtle shell flashed with white light. The light screen reflected an invisible sword shadow!
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly stretched out his hand!
"Divine Li Water Mirror!"
A transparent water screen appeared in his hand.
When Luo Zheng challenged Fang Xiaoyu, who was ranked one hundred and ninety-sixth in the Arena of Gods, he absorbed Fang Xiaoyu's Divine Li Water Way. At the same time, he included Fang Xiaoyu's Divine Li Water Mirror.
The Divine Li Water Mirror was similar to the Black-armored Golden Turtle's shell. It could also reflect the opponent's attack!
However, although they were both rebounding, there was still a big difference between the two.
After the Black-armored Golden Turtle's shell absorbed the opponent's attack, it replicated the exact same attack and reflected it back. This was the Black-armored Golden Turtle's innate ability. It was very rare.
If Mu Xuerong accidentally slashed at the tortoise shell, a saber light would appear from the tortoise shell.
The Divine Li Water Mirror was different. It was purely a reflection.
Luo Zheng's Buddha Emperor Sword didn't disappear after slashing on the tortoiseshell. However, another Buddha Emperor Sword shot out from the tortoiseshell, which meant that there was an additional Buddha Emperor Sword. After the additional Buddha Emperor Sword was reflected by the Divine Li Water Mirror, Luo Zheng was actually able to control it!
This meant that Luo Zheng did not use his own power source and had an extra Buddha Emperor Sword.
"Two Buddha Emperor Swords …" Luo Zheng's eyes lit up. He realized something and controlled the two Buddha Emperor Swords to hit the Black-armored Golden Turtle again.
"Ka ka!"
Two fist-sized cracks appeared on both sides of the Black-armored Golden Turtle's shell. As the shell flashed with white light, two more Buddha Emperor Swords returned!
After Luo Zheng used the Divine Li Water Mirror to reflect the two Buddha Emperor Swords, the two replicated Buddha Emperor Swords were controlled by Luo Zheng again.
Hence, Luo Zheng repeated the same action over and over again.
After a few reflections, the Buddha Emperor Swords in front of him turned from one to two, two to four, four to eight, eight to sixteen …
Tang Wan, who was recuperating under the statue of the goddess, saw this scene unintentionally. She was confused. After a while, she was so shocked that her eyeballs almost fell out!
"Hey hey … Young Master Fang!" Tang Wan shouted. "That kid is a bit abnormal, right? Isn't the Divine Li Water Mirror an Outer Dao ability unique to your Fang family? "
With Tang Wan's reminder, Fang Henshao also turned his attention over.
At first, Fang Henshao didn't understand what was going on. He only noticed that there was a power source fluctuating around Luo Zheng's body. When he understood what was going on, he was also stunned.
"It's the Divine Li Water Mirror. Where did this guy learn it … He can actually use it like this!" Fang Henshao was also dumbfounded.
At this moment, Luo Zheng had struck the shell of the Black-armored Golden Turtle with sixteen swords at once. The shell exploded, and the Black-armored Golden Turtle died. At the same time, the Black-armored Golden Turtle emitted a trace of blood energy and entered Luo Zheng's body.
The broken shell flashed with white light, and the sixteen Buddha Emperor Swords appeared from the light screen again and were reflected back at him.
Luo Zheng used the Divine Li Water Mirror to reflect them one by one. These sixteen Buddha Emperor Swords were once again under Luo Zheng's control.
This meant that he was now controlling thirty-two Buddha Emperor Swords at once.
Looking at the densely packed Buddha Emperor Swords in front of him, Luo Zheng felt extremely shocked …
These were thirty-two Buddha Emperor Swords! If all of them hit the same target at the same time, even an upper-level True God would be blasted into pieces!
"Again!"
Luo Zheng's left hand swayed gently and controlled sixteen Buddha Emperor Swords to attack the Black-armored Golden Turtle on the other side. Then, his right hand swayed gently, and the other sixteen Buddha Emperor Swords shot out …
The two Golden Turtle were easily killed by the thirty-two Buddha Emperor Swords. At the same time, the thirty-two Buddha Emperor Swords on the broken shells of the two Golden Turtle were reflected back at Luo Zheng. After being reflected by Luo Zheng's Divine Li Water Mirror, they were used by Luo Zheng …
Now, Luo Zheng was controlling sixty-four Buddha Emperor Swords!
Sixty-four Buddha Emperor Swords gathered together and exuded a pressure unique to the Origin of Power. Even many Perfected True Gods felt quite pressured by it!

Chapter 1936
The Black Armored Golden Tortoise usually appeared in the remote areas of Buzhou Spirit Mountain. Due to its slow movement, very few True Gods would bother it.
There were not many True Gods in the Fang Family who specialized in the Li Water Divine Dao. Only three or four True Gods, including Fang Xiaoyu, had mastered the Divine Li Water Mirror, and these True Gods had never encountered the Black Armored Golden Tortoise before.
As for Fang Henshao, he had indeed killed the Black Armored Golden Tortoise when he entered Buzhou Spirit Mountain. However, he did not cultivate the Li Water Divine Dao, so he did not expect the Divine Li Water Mirror to have such an effect upon the Black Armored Golden Tortoise!
The sixty-four Buddha Emperor Swords kept swaying around Luo Zheng, and a few of them even showed signs of instability.
Luo Zheng did not have the Extreme Purple Yin Body. Although he had cultivated the Purple Cloud Divine Dao, he had not mastered the Art of Multitasking. Therefore, Aunt Hanjiu had only taught Luo Zheng the Four Sword Divine Dao.
Although Luo Zheng was now a True God, it was still difficult for him to control sixty-four Buddha Emperor Swords at once!
"Merge!"
Luo Zheng's mind moved slightly.
The sixty-four Buddha Emperor Swords quickly merged with each other, and from sixty-four swords, they quickly condensed into four giant swords!
"The power … is weakening so quickly!" Luo Zheng's eyes focused.
After the power left the body, it would quickly weaken!
After Luo Zheng's Buddha Emperor Swords left his body, he relied on the Transcendence Divine Art to continuously replenish the power source to maintain the Buddha Emperor Swords' original form. If he stopped replenishing the power source, the Buddha Emperor Swords would collapse in a short time.
The power source that formed the four giant swords did not come from Luo Zheng, but from the Black Armored Golden Tortoise.
The four giant swords were too large, and Luo Zheng's power source could not maintain their form. Therefore, the four giant swords were like melting snow, rapidly weakening …
"Go!"
Before the four giant swords completely weakened, Luo Zheng controlled the four giant swords to slash at the four Black Armored Golden Tortoises not far away!

The power of the four giant swords was astonishing, comparable to the following attack of a Great Perfection.

"This is enough," said Luo Zheng said Great.

Mu Xuerong twirled her hair as she looked at the distant Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1937

Chapter 1938

Chapter 1939

However, in the divine territory, everything was destined to be different.

The Sages were becoming more and more curious about Luo Zheng's identity …
The only one who knew the truth was Aunt Hanjiu.
Although Aunt Hanjiu maintained her composure, her heart was fluctuating wildly.
She hadn't thought that the trip to the Sea of Time would lead to the worst possible outcome. There was no turning back.
If this continued …

Chapter 1940
There weren't many Black-armored Golden Tortoises left in the canyon.
Not long after, Luo Zheng killed the last Black-armored Golden Tortoise. The four swords quickly weakened and disappeared.
Without the Black-armored Golden Tortoise's cooperation, Luo Zheng couldn't "duplicate" the swords.
Then, he returned to the Goddess statue.
"Luo … Tianxing." Han Liusu looked at Luo Zheng with a face full of guilt.
Just as Mu Xuerong said, the name wasn't important.
In the Mu Family, only Mu Jiu, Mu Tian, and Mu Han knew the name "Luo Zheng." However, Mu Haiji's three disciples had already died in the Great Evolution Universe.

Han Chuyue and Han Biluo also pursed their lips and didn't say anything.

Hearing this, Han Liusu and Han Chuyue both sighed in their hearts.
If the Sages really investigated, Luo Zheng probably wouldn't be able to hide anything …
As for the other True Gods, they didn't have much of an opinion. Most of them were concerned about the next wave of monsters.
Normally, the Goddess Protector Statue would have to go through eight waves of monsters. The most difficult ones would be the seventh and eighth waves. These last two waves gave the most rewards, but they were only Shenwu coins.

The Black-armored Golden Tortoises appeared in the sixth wave. Who knew what would come after?

"Rumble …"
Not long after, there was a rumbling sound in the canyon. Everyone thought that the seventh wave of monsters had appeared.
All of the True Gods looked at the mountain passes in all directions!
However, there were no ferocious beasts in the west, north, or south.
