All the True Gods turned their gazes to the east, and Luo Zheng was no exception.
"No, it can't be. Even if the difficulty of guarding the Goddess Statue is greatly increased, it's impossible for such a thing to appear." Even someone as steady as Zhan Ming, an upper True God like Dongfang Ning could not help but show a strong expression of fear on his face.
"Big Sister … What is that …" Mu Ning said with a trembling voice.
The three Han Jia girls also hid behind Han Tianxiao, especially Han Biluo, whose face was already deathly pale.
A ferocious grayish-brown creature appeared at the eastern mountain pass.
That monster was as tall as a small palace. It had a pair of unique fleshy wings on its back. At this moment, the wings were spread open, and one could clearly see the colorful power of time flowing through the membranes of the fleshy wings.
This ferocious creature's head was like a skinned sheep's head, but its face was even more ferocious. Its eyes were as sharp as a knife, and its gaze was as sharp as a knife.
"That claw …"
Luo Zheng's gaze focused on the monster's hand. The claw had three toes, exactly the same as the three sharp claws that had pierced through the Star Door.
"That's not a monster. The Goddess Statue's trial should have ended early." Mu Xuerong suddenly stood up, a short sword and a short knife appearing in her hands. "That's the level six monster we lured down from the third layer of the Sea of Time, the 'Shi Mi'."
In the Domain of the Gods, the levels of the monsters in the forbidden areas corresponded to the levels of the forbidden areas.
The forbidden area of the Sea of Time was classified as a level six monster. This Shi Mi was the strongest monster in the forbidden area of the Sea of Time.
In the fourth and fifth layers of the forbidden area of the Sea of Time, there might be even stronger monsters, but those places could not be entered. Naturally, it was not within the scope of consideration.
The Perfect True Gods had painstakingly lured the Shi Mi from the third layer to the first layer. Under the siege of the Perfect True Gods, the Shi Mi had entered a berserk state. They had no choice but to temporarily enter the Star Door to temporarily hide, which could be considered as having won them precious time to rest.
Originally, they had thought that they would be able to rest longer. After all, the trial of the Goddess Statue usually had eight waves of monsters attacking. Occasionally, there would be nine or even ten waves. They had not expected that it would end early this time.
After the light curtain that isolated the canyon disappeared, the Shi Mi, who had been waiting outside for a long time, naturally rushed in as soon as it could!
This Shi Mi contained a terrifying aura. Those who did not have a firm mind would not have the courage to resist in front of it. The True Gods in the canyon might be geniuses from the rich and powerful families, but because of this, many of them did not have rich experience, and their minds had not been sufficiently tempered.
When facing the Shi Mi, it was natural for them to be extremely fearful.
On the other hand, Luo Zheng's state of mind was calm, just like the other Perfect True Gods.
"See, my decision was correct. This Shi Mi's berserk state can only last for two or three incense sticks of time," Mu Xuerong said faintly. Then she turned her head to look at Tang Wan, who was not far away, and said, "What are you thinking? Why aren't you attacking? "
Tang Wan was already carrying a purple broadsword on her shoulder. The broadsword's body was wide and thick, like a flattened sledgehammer. Its aura was extraordinary.
"Got it," Tang Wan replied unwillingly. Then she shouted rudely at the True Gods, "If you don't want to die, don't stay here in a daze. Get out of here to the other side of the canyon. No one can guarantee your absolute safety. Be alert later!"
Many True Gods had been deeply suppressed by the Shi Mi's aura. When they heard Tang Wan's words, their hearts trembled. They quickly left the Goddess Statue and ran toward the west side of the canyon. The speed of their escape was extremely fast.
The aftermath of the battle between two Perfect True Gods was extremely terrifying. Ordinary True Gods would definitely die if they were caught in it, let alone dozens of Perfect True Gods fighting with the Shi Mi.
"Damn it, we're so unlucky. If we knew this would happen, we would have run away from the beginning!"
"Even if we were to face those Time Wraiths, we would still have a chance to survive. Sigh …"
"Great, now we have nowhere to run!"
Many True Gods complained as they retreated.
At first, some True Gods saw the Perfect True Gods enter the canyon and thought that their saviors had arrived.
In the end, these Perfect True Gods attracted even more terrifying existences. They could only cry without tears.
"Roar!"
The Shi Mi suddenly raised its sharp head and roared toward the sky.
The entire canyon began to shake violently. There were faint signs of space collapsing.
Even space and time were slightly disordered. However, this disordered space and time were difficult to detect.
"Whoosh!"
Tang Wan's figure flashed and disappeared from the spot. Then, she appeared in front of the Shi Mi.
Tang Wan, who was carrying a broadsword, did not show any fear. Instead, he looked indifferent. Then, he stepped into the ground.
"Dong!"
With a muffled sound, the yellow Taoist Connotation spread rapidly. Then, he became one with the ground in the canyon.
Like Dongfang Yang, he cultivated the Thick Earth Divine Dao.

Chapter 1941
Tang Wan's burly physique was nothing compared to Shi Mi's, but the dense Dao that emanated from his body made him feel extremely heavy.
He was like a thick wall that could block Shi Mi all by himself.
"Roar!"
Shi Mi suddenly let out an ear-piercing roar.
It had been fighting against these Paragons for quite some time now.
With its own strength, none of the Paragons were a match for it.
In fact, it could devour all of them in just a few breaths of time …
However, when these Great Perfection stage Sovereigns worked together, they were like a pack of wolves that constantly roamed around while it was just a helpless bull. No matter what it did, it would be completely restrained by them. It had all the strength it had, but it was practically unable to unleash it!
Shi Mi let out a roar, then flapped its thick, fleshy wings. With a powerful push of its thick, muscular legs, it slammed into Tang Wan!
At this moment, as Denglong rammed his head into the ground, the surrounding space was constantly torn apart, like the jagged teeth of a monster. However, not only was space in God's Domain stable, but it also had an extremely strong self-healing ability. The moment these small spatial cracks appeared, they quickly repaired themselves and disappeared.
"Haha, just in time!"
Tang Wan waved his hand, and a purple saber danced in the air.
Strands of earthy yellow energy swirled around the purple saber, and the aura it exuded created all kinds of illusions. It was as if he wasn't swinging a saber at all, but a mountain!
"Boom!"
The two enormous forces collided.
The surrounding space was constantly being torn apart, and the cracks in space shot out in all directions like sharp arrows …
Fortunately, the True Gods had all fled to the other side of the canyon. Otherwise, the black cracks would have been enough to slice them in half.
The Thick Earth Divine Dao wasn't an offensive divine dao. Even though Tang Wan's saber contained shocking power, it still couldn't harm Shi Mi in the slightest!
"Roar!"
Shi Mi's head pressed against Tang Wan's saber, and it began to push forward!
Even though Tang Wan was one with the earth, it still couldn't stop it!
Under his fierce push, Tang Wan began to continuously retreat. Her feet rubbed against the ground and plowed two grooves in the ground. After pushing for a distance of several hundred feet, Shi Le suddenly twisted his body and slapped Tang Wan, slapping her seventy to eighty feet away ….
Shi Mi's intelligence wasn't low.
It had wasted a lot of time on Tang Wan, but the burly man hadn't been harmed in the slightest. Instead, the other Paragons had taken advantage of it and forced it to go berserk to resolve the crisis!
"Oh, it's gotten smarter," the Zhao Family's True God said.
"It's useless. As long as it doesn't go berserk, it won't be much of a threat." Mu Xuerong gently waved the two weapons in her hands, drawing a graceful parabola in the air. In the blink of an eye, she appeared above Shi Le's head like a graceful butterfly. She stood on her tiptoes on one of Shi Le's horns while simultaneously raising the dagger and scimitar in her hands. Then, she fell backwards as if she was diving from a high altitude.
"Swoosh!"
The moment Mu Xue Yue fell, her pure eyes were smeared with blood red. Her whole body turned into a line of blood and flew around Shi Mi's body. At the same time, the blood-red sword light kept slashing at Shi Mi's body!
Every Paragons present was not weak, and they all had their own specialties.
In terms of pure strength, Mu Xue Yue Yue might not be the strongest, but in terms of killing power, Mu Xue Yue Yue, who practiced the Killing God Path, was number one.
Only she could easily cut open Shi Mi's flesh!
"Puff, puff, puff, puff …"
With every flash of the sword light, Mu Xue Yue Yue Yue Yue Yue Yue left a deep wound on Shi Mi's body.
In the blink of an eye, there were dozens of half-foot-long wounds on Shi Mi's body.
This kind of injury was nothing to Shi Le, who had a huge body.
"Roar!"
The Shi Mi roared, and its huge body became very agile at this moment. Its huge body twisted crazily, and its two claws constantly slapped and waved at its own body. However, Mu Xuerong's body was extremely agile, so how could it hit it?
He was still brandishing his two blood-red swords. Like a butcher dismembering an ox, he accurately cut off Shi Le's complicated muscles, meridians, and even some small bones.
The other Paragons in the canyon had no intention of making a move. Everything was proceeding according to the original plan. Mu Xuerong, who specialized in the Killing Gods Dao, was the most crucial step.
The Shi Mi's blood energy was extremely vigorous, and its self-healing ability was extremely powerful. Even if a Great Perfection Stage expert were to seriously injure it, its injuries would be healed in an extremely short period of time.
However, Mu Xuerong's scimitar and dagger contained the power of "Blood Breaking".
If one were to observe carefully with the naked eye, they would see traces of purple-red blood flowing from the Shi Mi's wounds. This was the power of "Blood Breaking". It was this unique power that prevented the Shi Mi's wounds from healing themselves!
Seeing the Shi Mi's injuries getting more and more serious, the True Gods gathered on the west side of the canyon all let out a long sigh of relief.
"This Shi Mi's aura was so scary, even scarier than a Saint. It turns out that it's not that powerful …"
"If this continues, Mu Xuerong can kill it by herself!"
"It can't fight back at all!"
These True Gods had been so scared that their faces turned ashen. Now, they were finally relieved.
"Killing the Shi Mi won't be that easy," Luo Zheng said, staring into the distance.
Mu Xuerong had the absolute upper hand against the Shi Mi, but the other Great Perfection Stage True Gods still looked like they were ready to attack at any time. Considering that the Great Perfection Stage True Gods had worked so hard to lure the Shi Mi into the first layer, it was obvious that things weren't that simple …
As soon as Luo Zheng finished speaking, the Shi Mi's eyes suddenly flashed with multicolored light. It raised its head and roared, and its body suddenly shook.
"That's enough. It's going to turn back time," a Great Perfection Stage True God reminded.
Mu Xuerong's body quickly stopped. She gently stepped on the Shi Mi's body and floated backward.
Then, the Shi Mi lowered its body, and a layer of multicolored light transformed into a huge cocoon shell, enveloping it inside.
"So it can turn back time!"
Seeing this scene, a True God suddenly understood.
The Shi Mi's ability to turn back time was derived from the Divine Path of Time. It was fundamentally different from the female chief of the Blind Tribe.
The only thing it could trace back to was itself.
When the Shi Mi's injuries were more serious, it would use its innate ability to turn back time. Wrapped in a thick layer of cocoon shell, its body quickly began to turn back time. The wounds that Mu Xuerong had cut open on its body quickly disappeared one by one, and it quickly recovered to its previous state.
At this moment, the other Great Perfection Stage True Gods were ready to attack.
The Shi Mi could turn back time every once in a while.
Under the effects of Time Reversal, no matter how severe its injuries were, it would be able to recover unscathed. Therefore, they had Mu Xuerong force it out of Time Reversal first, and then kill it before it could use Time Reversal again.
This was practically the only way to kill the Shi Mi …
Otherwise, once it turned back time, all their previous efforts would have been in vain.
"Ka ka ka …"
After about ten breaths, the layer of cocoon shell that enveloped the Shi Mi began to break.
The moment the Shi Mi broke out of its shell, Dongfang Taiqing calmly shouted, "Do it!"
As soon as his voice fell, all the Great Perfection Stage True Gods activated their godhoods. For a moment, the power of various Dao Essences wildly reverberated in the canyon, forming ripples that could be seen with the naked eye.
When ordinary True Gods felt these ripples, they didn't even dare to breathe loudly. Their souls were greatly suppressed.
"Great Curse Technique!"
A pale green skull appeared on top of the Shi Mi's head.
After the skull appeared, it kept shaking above the Shi Mi's head.

Chapter 1942
Every Perfected True God had cultivated a Divine Dao to the extreme.

This was the power of a Perfected True God! Luo Zheng's heart trembled.

Right at this moment, the golden halos in Luo Zheng's eyes lit up and started to absorb the Taoist Connotation in the canyon!
With the help of the Immeasurable Ruler, no matter how complicated the Taoist Connotation was, he could understand it. He lit up one of the scales on the Immeasurable Ruler. These Perfect True Gods used divine abilities of rank six, and some were even perfected divine abilities. Luo Zheng would not be able to touch these divine abilities unless he cultivated them to the level of an upper True God.
However, Outer Dao divine abilities weren't restricted by cultivation level. For example, the Snow Imprinted Divine Spear was an Outer Dao divine ability of the Snow Imprinted Divine Dao, and Luo Zheng could smoothly use it. This was the same logic as when Luo Zheng cultivated the Ice Soul Devil Flame, because the Ice Soul Devil Flame was also an Outer Dao divine ability.

Just as Luo Zheng was concentrating on reading the Taoist Connotation, he heard Zhan Ming shout, "Everyone, be careful!"
"Rumble!"
A Perfect True God summoned a huge fire dragon and blasted it on Shi Mi's body, causing the flames to roll back.
Those Perfect True Gods naturally didn't fear the remaining power of the flames, but how could some True Gods withstand it?
"Absolute Beginning Promise!"
Seeing the raging flames rolling toward them, some True Gods realized that they couldn't withstand it. They clenched their teeth and activated Absolute Beginning Promise. The surface of their bodies instantly flickered with golden light. With this layer of golden light, they could avoid all the energy attacks formed by Taoist Connotation.
"Who has a defensive magic treasure?"
"Using one Absolute Beginning Promise is too wasteful!"

Most of the True Gods were watching.

Most of the True Gods present were core members of powerful families. Some True Gods should have some powerful defensive magic treasures on them.

That True God activated his own protective magic treasure. A large golden bell appeared, instantly enveloping him. However, this bell could only protect him alone.

"Hua …"
Very quickly, the flames filled the entire canyon.
The True Gods with protective magic treasures all used their protective magic treasures to endure the flames. The True Gods with no other choice could only brace themselves and activate a precious Absolute Beginning Promise. Only Luo Zheng's body flickered with a golden light as he continuously absorbed the flames.
In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the Craftsman God had tempered his body, allowing his physical body to reach a new level. His physical body had become even more picky with flames. However, the Craftsman God had not given him an answer as to whether his physical body had become fixed because of this.
"What's up with the golden Sanskrit on this guy's body …"
"He's absorbing Wu Nantian's Demon Burning Dragon Flames!"

Although the flames were the remnants of the flames, they could not be ignored.

Luo Zheng's physical body had returned to normal. He raised his arm and sized it up. Absorbing the Demon Burning Dragon Flames did not improve his physical body at all.
The True Gods' gazes converged on the east side of the canyon.
Under the ferocious attacks of the Paragons, the Time Elk had no chance of surviving.
"Is it dead?" a True God looked over and asked.
Luo Zheng's gaze also focused on the Shi Mi. The Shi Mi was lying on the ground, motionless.
Its body was pitch-black, and the wide wings on its back were full of holes. It no longer had the ferocity it had before. It really did look like it was dead.
The Paragons also cautiously approached the Shi Mi …
However, the moment the Paragon True God got close to the Shi Mi …
The Shi Mi suddenly opened its eyes.
Its colorful eyes emitted a wild and ferocious aura.
The light quickly flowed through every piece of its flesh and meridians, and then turned into sharp spikes that shot out in all directions!
"Swish, swish, swish, swish, swish …"

Every single spike was formed from the colorful power of time.

Mu Xuerong's reaction was the fastest. In almost an instant, she had used a spatial shift. The moment her figure disappeared, those sharp spikes formed from the power of time had already pierced her chest!
The next moment, she appeared in another part of the canyon. There was a fist-sized hole in her chest, and blood was flowing out.
The other Paragons' reactions were not slow either. Except for a few who were not injured and escaped immediately, most of them were more or less injured …
The most unlucky ones were the Paragons of the Zhao Family and the Qin Family. Their heads were pierced by the spikes, and the irresistible power of time destroyed their souls. Then, their bodies were pierced by the numerous spikes, and they died on the spot!
Then, the Shi Mi staggered to its feet.
It seemed powerless to use Time Reversal again, and it let out a roar of despair …
All the spikes retreated into its body. At the same time, a colorful halo spread out from its feet.
After the halo appeared, it sucked the Shi Mi in.

Chapter 1943
A small snake was carved on the top of the jade seal. The snake raised its head and flicked its tongue, looking very lifelike.
The entire jade seal was only the size of a thumb and was very inconspicuous.
However, what kind of eyesight did the True Gods in the canyon have? Even the tiniest mosquito would not be missed.
As a result, the atmosphere in the canyon suddenly became very strange.
If it were any other forbidden ground, ordinary true gods would probably rush forward and fight for the imperial jade seal if they saw it.
However, no one acted rashly at this moment.
All the True Gods, including Mu Xuerong, knew very well that once they made a move, they would become the target of public criticism.
However, the powerful time energy emitted from the jade seal was tormenting the souls of every True Perfection True God.
Even if it was a grade one faith treasure, it was worth it for True Gods to fight for it.
Because this was the forbidden land of the Sea of Time.
Most faith treasures that were linked to the Divine Dao of Time usually had extremely heaven-defying effects.
From the powerful time energy emitted from the jade seal, it was definitely not a grade one faith treasure. It was at least a grade three or four faith treasure.
Outside the forbidden land of the Sea of Time.
Even the usually calm Sages found it hard to maintain their composure at this moment.
Any faith treasure in the forbidden land of the Sea of Time was no small matter, let alone this jade seal that looked so extraordinary.
On the surface, they did not show any fluctuations in their emotions, but secretly, they were flying with their own Great Yuan.
"Snatch that jade seal …"
"At all costs!"
"We must bring the jade seal out of the forbidden land of the Sea of Time!"
"Be careful of your methods …"
These Sages were also very conflicted.
What was certain was that if any True Perfection True God rushed into the halo, any True Perfection True God who rushed into the halo would be besieged.
Being besieged by dozens of True Gods at the same time …
Even people as strong as Tang Wan, Dongfang Taiqing, and Fang Henshao would undoubtedly die.
However, if they were a step too late, they were afraid that others would snatch it away and completely lose the chance to fight for it.
The canyon maintained a terrifying silence.
On the other hand, the True Gods were more relaxed.
"There seems to be a jade seal over there."
"It's the faith treasure of the forbidden land of the Sea of Time. Do you think they will fight for it?"
"Definitely!"
"It's fine as long as they don't get implicated."
With the cultivation of a True God, they simply did not have the qualifications to participate in the struggle. Everyone could only watch the show.
Luo Zheng's gaze crossed the canyon and landed on the Imperial Jade Seal. His eyes revealed an indifferent expression.
From the looks of it, a dispute was about to break out in the canyon. The only thing that remained to be seen was who would obtain the jade seal.
"The halo is spreading?"
After observing for a while, Luo Zheng also noticed some changes.
After Shi Mi's death, a ring of colorful light and the Imperial Jade Seal appeared under her feet. After a while, the layer of light on the ground did not disappear. Instead, it slowly expanded …
The Shi Mi's power of time was not continuous. Like the seawater in the Sea of Time, it was just irregular fragments of time condensed together and filled with irregular time cracks.
However, there were no cracks in the power of time surrounding the Imperial Jade Seal. It was a complete power of time. Who knew what would happen if it continued to spread?
…
Under the confrontation of the many Perfected True Gods, Dongfang Taiqing was the first to break the silence. "This Imperial Jade Seal is extraordinary. It must be a precious treasure. I'm sure no one here will give it up so easily."
"Of course," replied a middle-aged man in armor. He held a golden sword in his hand. A faint stream of light shuttled back and forth in the middle of the sword.
"As I said, why don't we take this treasure out of the Sea of Time and let the Sage Halls decide?" said Dongfang Taiqing.
Mu Xuerong, Tang Wan, and the other Perfected True Gods remained silent.
They belonged to the same family as the Dongfang Family. Naturally, they accepted this suggestion.
"Dongfang Taiqing, are you joking? Why don't you just ask us to take the Imperial Jade Seal out and present it to your Sage Emperor with both hands? "sneered a Perfected True God from the Sword Tribe.
"That's right. Then what are we fighting for? Will your Dongfang Chunjun cut the Imperial Jade Seal into pieces and distribute them?" said a Perfected True God from the War God Temple.
The Dongfang Family occupied a large portion of the Sage Halls. However, it did not mean that there were no wealthy families that could compete with them.
For example, the Sword Tribe and the Xuanyue Family were powers that could not be ignored …
However, if the Imperial Jade Seal was sent to the Sage Halls, it would only lead to more conflict!
Dongfang Taiqing's expression turned cold. "If it's as you said, who will take the Imperial Jade Seal?"
"Naturally, it will belong to whoever snatches it!" sneered a Perfected True God from the Xuanyue Family. Then, he disappeared in an instant.
The first Perfected True God to make a move might be in the most danger. However, he also had the highest chance of obtaining the Imperial Jade Seal!
"Dream on!"
"The Imperial Jade Seal belongs to my Tang Family!"
"…"
All the Great Perfection Real Gods moved to the location of the imperial jade seal at the first instant.
Even the most composed Perfected True Gods like Dongfang Taiqing did the same.
However, at this moment, a strange scene occurred …
All the Perfected True Gods who moved to the colorful halo were unable to touch the Imperial Jade Seal.
Their figures only flashed above the Imperial Jade Seal. They were probably still in the same position as grabbing the Imperial Jade Seal. Their faces were filled with astonishment. Then, they turned into faint figures and disappeared!
The True Gods in the canyon were astonished.
When they saw the Perfected True Gods scrambling for the Imperial Jade Seal, they knew that there would be a huge conflict.
They never expected such a thing to happen.
"Where are they?"
"They've all disappeared …"
Zhan Ming, Dongfang Ning, and the other True Gods stared with their eyes wide open.
"The Imperial Jade Seal is still there!" shouted a True God.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Some of the True Gods did not hesitate and shot toward the east of the canyon.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. Then, he rushed over as well.
In the end, all the True Gods stopped around the five-colored halo.
"The problem should be the power of time," Luo Zheng said softly.
Han Cangyan nodded. "Yes, this layer of power of time has started to fluctuate. The Perfected True Gods should have been sucked in."
Previously, the colorful halo was still spread out on the ground. Now, it had turned into a colorful whirlpool. There were even faint ripples on it.
Luo Zheng picked up a stone from the ground and held it in his hand. After aiming at the Imperial Jade Seal, he suddenly threw it!
"Whoosh!"
The stone made a sound as it pierced through the air. However, the stone disappeared as soon as it entered the range of the colorful halo.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
That was indeed the case …
Those Perfected True Gods could use space teleportation. Therefore, when they teleported above the Imperial Jade Seal, they were sucked in by the colorful halo.
In fact, as long as they touched the edge of the colorful halo, it was impossible to avoid being sucked in!
"Are the Perfected True Gods all dead?" asked a True God.
Hearing this question, Han Biluo's expression turned ugly. She said angrily, "You're the one who's dead!" After all, Han Tianxiao was also sucked in.
"No," Dongfang Ning said faintly as he stared at the light screen. "The power of time is continuous. It should be able to pass through safely. It's just that I don't know where it leads to …"
In theory, the Sea of Time in the Divine Territory could lead to any period of history.
However, the Sea of Time was formed by fragments of time. It was filled with countless time cracks. Therefore, any living thing that fell into the Sea of Time would be cut into pieces by those cracks. Then, those pieces would fall into different periods of history!
However, the power of time in front of them was a complete passage of time.
"Should we go in?" asked a True God.
"Hehe, perhaps those Perfected True Gods who entered did not return. If you all go in as well, wouldn't you all be seeking death?" mocked a True God.
Many True Gods revealed helpless expressions. The Imperial Jade Seal was so far away but right in front of them. Yet, they couldn't obtain it at all. This made them feel depressed.
Luo Zheng stared at the colorful halo. After making up his mind, he turned his head to look at Han Liusu and said, "I'll go in as well."
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Han Liusu's fine eyelashes trembled. "Why?"
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo both looked at Luo Zheng. Rashly entering was too risky.

Chapter 1944
"That brat really jumped!"
"Doesn't he care about his own life?"
"Luo Tianxing!"
The Real Gods did not expect Luo Zheng to jump just like that. Their faces were filled with astonishment.
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo covered their mouths with their hands.
After a while, Han Chuyue asked in a low voice, "Sister, what did he say to you?"
Han Liusu lowered her head and pondered. Hearing Han Chuyue's question, she smiled and said, "He told me to wait for him."
In fact, Han Liusu understood Luo Zheng's motive.
If he left the Sea of Time like this, he would be in a hopeless situation facing Luo Zheng. If he fell into Dongfang Chunjun's hands, he wouldn't be able to protect him. Although Aunt Han Jiu was a quasi saint, she wouldn't be able to resist Dongfang Chunjun …
If he did this, he might be able to find a chance to turn the situation around!
It was at this moment that another red figure jumped into the five-colored halo and disappeared from everyone's sight, leaving behind a circle of shallow ripples that slowly spread out.
"It's Mu Ning!"
"She also went in …"
"Young people these days are getting bolder and bolder!"
When Han Liusu saw that Mu Ning had also entered, a trace of interest appeared on her face.
No one knew where this five-colored halo led to. Perhaps it led to a certain period of history, or perhaps it led to an extremely dangerous secret realm.
If it was for Luo Zheng, she was willing to take this risk.
"Shua!"
Just as she was thinking this, Han Chuyue grabbed her clothes from behind.
Han Chuyue whispered, "Sister, you have to believe in him. I think he will return safely …"
Han Chuyue saw through Han Liusu's thoughts and stopped her in advance. She was afraid Han Liusu would also jump in.
"Sister, don't leave us behind," Han Chuyue begged.
Seeing Han Chuyue's pleading face, Han Liusu sighed. Luo Zheng had always been able to turn misfortunes into blessings. She hoped he would be able to get through this safely.
In the next period of time, 20 to 30 True Gods jumped in one after another.
There were also some true gods who tried to use their methods to get the jade seal out, but once anything entered the halo's range, it would disappear immediately. There were almost no exceptions.
After using all kinds of methods, these True Gods could only choose to give up and try to return to the outside of the Sea of Time.
…
…
The moment he jumped into the colorful halo, Luo Zheng felt that his whole body had become very soft, as if he had turned into an infinitely long noodle, constantly sliding downwards.
The surrounding scenery also became very strange.
The multicolored light also extended infinitely, transforming into multicolored noodles.
If he looked carefully, Luo Zheng could see some "flesh-colored" noodles. After staring at them for a while, he could tell that the other "True Gods" had also entered. Just like him, they had been stretched into noodles and were falling down along these strips of light.
"I wonder where this passage of time leads to …"
Luo Zheng was also pondering.
However, no matter where he went, it was better than returning to the Sea of Time and falling into Dongfang Chunjun's hands.
Moreover, time and space were originally two highly related concepts …
Just like the countless time fragments in the Sea of Time, there were also independent spaces within them, but those independent spaces represented the past.
"Cough, cough, cough, cough …"
At this time, Luo Zheng suddenly heard a strange sound.
He shifted his gaze to the top and saw a black noodle slithering in the middle.
"A bug?"
Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat.
At the top of the black noodle, there was a pair of scissor-like pincers, and behind the pincers, there were dark and shiny eyes. In addition, there were two rows of thin and long legs on the slender body, somewhat similar to a thousand-legged worm.
After Luo Zheng jumped in, he could only swim along these colorful light strips, unable to move at all. The other True Gods were the same.
However, the black bug waved its small legs and could swim in the light strips!
After a while, Luo Zheng saw the black bug swim above a "flesh-colored" noodle, waving its scissor-like pincers!
"This thing … wants to eat that True God?" Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat.
In such an environment, he couldn't even make a sound, let alone resist!
Soon, Luo Zheng saw traces of bright red appearing from the end of the "flesh-colored" noodle.
"It's blood!"
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
Then, he saw the flesh-colored noodle being continuously swallowed by the black bug. The length of the flesh-colored noodle kept shrinking, and the True God was swallowed inch by inch by the black bug!
"Cough, cough, cough, cough …"
After the black bug swallowed a True God, it started to swim in the light strips again.
At that moment, all the True Gods were probably hoping that the black bug wouldn't lock onto them. However, not long after, the bug locked onto a True God and slowly crawled towards that True God. Then, inch by inch, it swallowed that True God …
"I don't know which True God was swallowed. How unlucky." Luo Zheng sighed slightly. Dying in such a situation was really depressing.
While Luo Zheng was thinking about this, the black bug wandered for a while and actually swam towards him!
"Oh no!"
Luo Zheng's heart suddenly sank!
Seeing that the black bug was getting closer and closer to him, he became more and more anxious.
He didn't know how long this passage would take. If he didn't leave this passage, he was afraid that he would definitely die!
"Cough, cough, cough, cough …"
The strange sound was getting louder and louder, and the black bug was getting closer and closer to him. Luo Zheng became more and more anxious!
At this moment, he suddenly felt a flash of light coming from below.
Luo Zheng looked down and saw that his light strips had stopped abruptly. At the end of the light strips, there was a small halo. That halo was exactly the same as the one Luo Zheng saw in the canyon!
"An exit?"
Luo Zheng's heart beat wildly.
Although the speed of his descent wasn't slow, the black bug was moving faster in the light strips. He didn't know if he could hold on until then.
"Faster, faster …"
Luo Zheng urged in his heart.
Seeing that the exit was getting closer and closer to him, the black bug was already above his head. The pair of pincers were also very close to his head.
His gaze passed through the pincers that were like scissors and saw the pair of gloomy and cold eyes. He suddenly felt a chill in his heart.
"Cough, cough …"
The pincers suddenly opened and bit his head!
A heart-wrenching pain hit him!
At this moment, Luo Zheng felt his whole body suddenly relax, and then he regained his freedom!
He didn't have time to distinguish his surroundings. His body suddenly twisted in a direction. At the same time, he found that a pair of huge pincers were firmly biting him. There were also some spikes on the pincers that were embedded in his head.
The black bug rushed out of the halo with him, but half of the black bug's body was still inside the halo.
Then, Luo Zheng felt a huge force coming from the pincers. This bug wanted to drag him back into the passage of time!
"Go to hell!"
Just now, Luo Zheng couldn't move and could only be a lamb waiting to be slaughtered!
Now, the situation was naturally very different!
He grabbed the two large pincers with both hands, and in an instant, he erupted with a huge force and actually dragged the insect outwards.
At the same time, he was about to use the Buddha Emperor Sword and the Heavenly Fang Sword!
As he condensed, two balls of energy sources appeared outside his body.
He was just about to be eaten by this black bug. Now that he was angry, how could Luo Zheng let it go? He had to kill it!
He didn't expect that the moment he condensed, the two balls of energy sources would collapse with a loud bang!
"What's going on?" Luo Zheng's face was full of astonishment.
He found that he couldn't use his energy to trace the source, and he couldn't even use the Purple Cloud Divine Dao's Mind Control Technique!
However, his brain worked quickly. Since that was the case, he could only twist the two huge pincers.
With two crisp cracking sounds, the two pincers were directly broken …

Chapter 1945
Luo Zheng still had a lingering fear when he thought about what had just happened. It was really a close shave.
If he had been a little slower, he would have been devoured by the black pincers in the tunnel!
After resting for a while, Luo Zheng seemed to have thought of something. He stretched out his hand and used the Transcendence Super Technique to extract the source of power.
When he started to retrace the source of power, the ball of power suddenly collapsed and could not be retraced at all!
"What's going on?"
Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes slightly and revealed a strange expression. Logically speaking, this should not have happened.
Then, he thought of something and stretched out his hand to condense a flame!
The small square in the Godhead in his inner world that contained the Fledgling Flame Divine Dao began to flicker with a faint light. The chaotic aura was constantly being extracted and transported out of his dantian.
However, there was still no trace of Taoist Connotation in his hand!
Unwilling to accept this, Luo Zheng tried to condense other Divine Daos. He made a few hand signs in succession, but the situation was still the same.
At this moment, Luo Zheng confirmed a fact and muttered, "Is it impossible to condense Taoist Connotation in this world? I wonder if it's because I can't condense Taoist Connotation, or is it the same for all True Gods? "
"There's nothing wrong with you. It's probably because of this world." The Evil Old Man's voice sounded again.
Luo Zheng threw the Thousand Elephant Golden Chariot into the forbidden area of the Sea of Time. The Evil Old Man's soul once again returned to his mind and followed him all the way here.
He followed Luo Zheng into the Sea of Time. This journey had been an eye-opener for him.
Not only did he see Sages, but he also saw various battles between Great Perfection Stage experts and the Level Six vicious beast Shi Mi. Everything made him gasp in amazement.
Even though he was only a fragmentary soul now, he still believed that his experience with Luo Zheng had been worth it.
Luo Zheng nodded. He also felt that the Evil Old Man's words made sense. He did not encounter anything else along the way except for the long and thin pincers. It was most likely because of this world.
Then, he looked around. All he could see was a patch of gray. Other than some flickering shadows of trees, he could not see the distant scenery clearly.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
At this moment, the five-colored halo under his feet rapidly shrank. In the blink of an eye, it disappeared, revealing a barren land.
If that five-colored halo was really a time tunnel, then he should be in a certain era of the Divine Domain's past.
"The other True Gods did not come out with me …"
When Luo Zheng descended, he saw many Real Gods descending as well. However, the light strips they were in were all different. Luo Zheng couldn't determine whether they had descended in different places in the same era. Instead, they had directly escaped into a different space-time.
It was even possible that this world was just a space-time constructed by the Imperial Jade Seal.
After observing for a while, Luo Zheng chose a path and set off.
In fact, there were no roads in this place, so it should be a place where people were rarely seen.
After walking along the sparse thicket for a while, the thicket became lower and the soil under his feet became drier and drier. Soon after, he passed through the thicket. The gray fog also dissipated. What appeared in front of him was a boundless desert.
Sand dunes that were hundreds of feet high lay in front of Luo Zheng. At first glance, it was extremely majestic.
"Someone's here?"
As Luo Zheng looked around, he saw a white figure appear at the top of the sixth sand dune.
Although Luo Zheng's eyesight was amazing, the figure was very blurry. He could not distinguish the figure's appearance. He could only feel that the figure was waving at him.
"Whoosh!"
Without much hesitation, Luo Zheng's figure moved. He turned into a sharp arrow and rushed forward.
Although Luo Zheng was still unable to fly, he was still able to maintain an extremely fast speed as he traversed the desert.
The sand dunes that were hundreds of feet high and thousands of feet long could be passed in the time of a few breaths.
After ten breaths of time, Luo Zheng rushed to the top of the sixth sand dune.
His feet stepped on the hot sand. He looked around and a trace of confusion appeared on his face.
The figure had disappeared.
"Strange, it was clearly here just now." Luo Zheng frowned.
Old Man Extreme Evil was also observing the surroundings. He could not help but remind him, "This place is very strange. Luo Zheng, you have to be careful."
Luo Zheng nodded. When he turned his head to look, his expression changed several times.
"That forest disappeared?"
He had just entered the desert, but the forest behind him had disappeared.
"Luo Zheng, that figure has appeared again," Old Man Extreme Evil reminded him.
Luo Zheng looked around and saw a faint figure appear at the top of a sand dune not far from his right. That figure was waving at Luo Zheng as usual.
His eyes flashed with a trace of fierceness. "No matter what it is, I will find you!"
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng turned into a sharp arrow again and ran wildly along the side of the huge sand dune.
Every step he took on the sand dune caused a rain of sand to fall.
When he caught up to that sand dune again, the white figure disappeared in an instant and reappeared on a sand dune even further away.
Luo Zheng did not say anything and continued to chase after that figure.
He had been running for thousands of miles, but the scenery around him hadn't changed at all. It was still one sand dune after another. Luo Zheng didn't see a single living thing, not even a cactus.
"Luo Zheng, that figure is too strange. Let's not chase after it." Old Man Extreme Evil saw that Luo Zheng had not caught up to that figure after chasing for so long, and a trace of worry gradually sprouted in his heart.
Luo Zheng laughed and said rather heroically, "Even if I don't chase after it, I can't find a way back. Why worry so much?"
He had fallen into a desperate situation in the forbidden area of the Sea of Time.
In the forbidden area, he could not use the Gen token. After leaving the forbidden area, it was almost impossible to use the token in front of the Sage and escape into the immortal abode.
In a situation where there was no turning back, he was afraid that he could only choose to press forward.
As soon as he finished speaking, his eyes suddenly flashed. "That figure's movements have changed!"
Previously, the figure had been waving at Luo Zheng from the top of the sand dune. Now, it was pointing at the back of the sand dune, as if indicating that there was something there.
"Whoosh whoosh!"
Without the slightest hesitation, his figure shot out.
When he stood at the top of the huge sand dune, the white figure disappeared as usual. However, Luo Zheng soon saw the white figure appear at the bottom of the sand dune and point at a human-shaped skeleton on the ground. Then, the figure drilled into the skeleton.
"Whoosh …"
Sliding down the sand dune, Luo Zheng slowly approached the human-shaped skeleton. The doubt on his face became more and more intense.
The sand dune in the desert moved with the wind. If someone had really died here, the corpse would have been buried by the sand dune in a few days. It was impossible for it to lie on the surface of the desert forever.
Moreover, looking at the traces on the surface of the human-shaped skeleton, it was unknown how long it had been dead!
"Luo Zheng, look at the hand of the skeleton!" Old Man Extreme Evil reminded.
Luo Zheng focused his gaze and saw that there were three light black rings on the fingers of the skeleton. The rings were very exquisite and looked like a folded treasure sword.
"This is the skeleton of the Sword Race's Great Perfection True God?" Luo Zheng was instantly stunned.
In the canyon, Luo Zheng had also paid attention to the Great Perfection True Gods of the wealthy families.
The Great Perfection True God from the Sword Race did not speak at all. However, the sharp gaze that was faintly emitted from his eyes left a deep impression on Luo Zheng. The Great Perfection True God had three rings on his right hand!
Previously, when Luo Zheng saw the three rings, he was puzzled as to why the Great Perfection True God was wearing three Sumeru Rings. He had secretly asked Han Chuyue about this.
Han Chuyue explained that the rings were not Sumeru Rings that could store items.
It was a supreme honor of the Sword Race, and it was also an extremely powerful life-saving treasure.
"The Great Perfection True God from the Sword Race was indeed wearing white!"
Thinking about the constantly flickering white figure, Luo Zheng faintly felt a trace of uneasiness.
Those Great Perfection True Gods had only entered the halo 30 minutes before Luo Zheng. Not long after, a Great Perfection True God had already perished.
How could the body of a Great Perfection True God rot so easily?
Even if the corpse of an ordinary True God were to rot, according to the universe's time scale, it would probably take tens of millions of years or even hundreds of millions of years. Moreover, the jade bones that were ultimately transformed would still be sparkling.
How long would it take for a Great Perfection True God's body to rot, fester, and finally become a mottled skeleton like this?

Chapter 1946
Luo Zheng looked around cautiously. After making sure that there was no danger around, he bowed.
He bowed to the skeleton and said, "Senior, you must have a reason for bringing me here! I'm afraid I'll have to offend you. "
He didn't have any enmity with the Perfect True God from the Sword Clan.
Now that he was going through the skeleton, he naturally had to apologize. This was the least he could do to show respect to the dead. Moreover, the other party was a Perfect True God.
When the Evil Old Man saw Luo Zheng's action, he shook his head slightly. According to the Evil Old Man's style, he would have kept the skeleton immediately. Why would he be so particular about it?
Then, Luo Zheng took off the three sword-shaped rings from the skeleton and looked at them carefully.
After looking at them for a while, he still couldn't figure out what was going on.
In the end, he tried to draw out a wisp of chaotic aura and insert it into one of the rings. The moment the chaotic aura entered the ring, the ring gave off a bright white light. At the same time, the ring became unusually hot.
"Oh no!"
In a moment of desperation, Luo Zheng threw the ring out.
As the ring spun in the air, it quickly stretched out and turned into a small sword. Then, it quickly grew bigger!
In the blink of an eye, it turned into a huge silver sword that was three hundred feet long!
The huge silver sword contained an extremely sharp sword essence. It was like a huge silver ship that shot in one direction.
"Wu — —"
At the same time, there was an ear-piercing sword hum in the air. The sand that was stirred up surged to both sides like waves.
"Peng!"
With a dull sound, the huge silver sword drilled into a huge sand dune and made a huge hole in the middle of the sand dune. It pierced through and drilled into another sand dune!
"Peng peng peng peng peng …"
Dozens of sand dune were pierced through by the huge silver sword. The dull sound continued far away and finally dissipated …
Luo Zheng was half buried in the sand by the power of the huge silver sword. The pile of bones next to him was also completely buried. He coldly looked at the sand dune not far away that was quickly collapsing, his face full of astonishment.
"So powerful …" Luo Zheng was indeed shocked by the power of this huge silver sword.
"The power of this ring is so terrifying." Old Man Extreme Evil was also stunned.
Although the Extreme Evil Old Man had lived for so many eras in the God Realm, he had never had the chance to see such a powerful magic treasure.
There were so many True Gods in the Divine Realm, but how many of them had cultivated to perfection?
These three rings were perfect protective magic treasures, so it was not strange for them to have such power.
Then, Luo Zheng felt a burst of heartache. This ring had such great power, and he had wasted it just like that!
After putting on the remaining two rings on his fingers, he sank his hands into the sand, and at the same time, spread out his power source.
"Hualalala …"
Under the repeated vibrations of the power that spread out, the sand around him started to boil. With a light shout, the sand around him was pushed away by the power, and the buried bones were exposed once again.
This True God had used some unknown method to lure him here. There had to be a reason for that.
Then, Luo Zheng carefully looked through the bones again. Finally, he found a black ring on top of the skull. There was a faint soul wave coming from the ring, but this soul wave was very weak and was about to disappear!
"That white shadow should have been released by this ring. The last shadow went into this ring," Luo Zheng quickly concluded.
As soon as he touched the black ring with his hand, a faint voice came from the ring.
"This … desert … is too dangerous … go north … be careful of the black spear …"
After this True God's body died, his soul could not exist forever.
With the passing of time, the soul of this Perfect Real God, even if it had been preserved in the ring, had almost disappeared. The thing that remained in the black ring could not even be considered a remnant soul. It was merely a thread of consciousness that kept repeating itself.
Even though Luo Zheng could not hear it clearly, he still listened to it many times and memorized the voice that came from the ring.
There were many things that Luo Zheng did not understand. However, there was one thing that he was certain of. The desert was very dangerous. Even Perfected True Gods could perish in it. Luo Zheng was merely a lower True God. If he were to encounter such danger, he would probably end up in an even worse state!
"Go!"
An idea flashed in his mind.
But before he left, he took a look at the skeleton.
He stretched out his hand and hit the sand hard, creating a big pit. He buried the skeleton in it. After all, it was better to be buried in the ground.
The jet-black ring was clearly a soul treasure, but it contained the last strand of the Perfect Earthly Sovereign's consciousness. Luo Zheng did not remove it. He had already taken three rings, and the ring was the last bit of evidence the Perfect Earthly Sovereign had left in this world.
Then he turned into a sharp arrow again and shot toward the north …
However, not long after Luo Zheng left, the sand in the place where the skeleton was buried kept surging. For some reason, the skeleton buried by Luo Zheng floated out of the sand under the slow vibration and maintained its original position.
Although he had obtained two extremely powerful rings, Luo Zheng's mood became heavy. He became more vigilant of everything around him.
In the boundless desert, Luo Zheng ran north for four to six hours. The sky gradually darkened …
There was no sun in this world, and the light gradually dimmed. After another two hours, the sky was completely dark. The entire desert was immersed in absolute darkness. He could not see anything.
What Luo Zheng did not expect was that just as darkness descended, the originally empty and desolate desert suddenly changed.
The surface of the huge sand dune began to flicker with little yellow lights. From afar, it looked like a distant light. When Luo Zheng's eyes focused slightly and he activated his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes, he discovered that there were strangely shaped insects lurking on the surface of the sand dune.
Half of these strange insects' bodies were hidden in the sand dune, and only their ugly heads were exposed. They constantly danced in the air.
"This …"
Luo Zheng stopped in his tracks. His expression changed slightly, and he knew that this was not good.
At this moment, a yellow light suddenly appeared under his feet, and the head of a strange insect broke out of the ground.
"Gugugu …"
The moment this strange insect rushed out, it bit at Luo Zheng!
"Die!"
Luo Zheng kicked the body of the strange insect, and it was directly kicked into two. The two halves of the insect's body rolled down the sand dune …
After killing one strange insect, yellow lights kept shining around Luo Zheng. Countless strange insects crawled out from under the sand dune. They were like rapidly growing trees, and in the blink of an eye, they wrapped around Luo Zheng, looking like they were going to devour him completely.
"Hmph!"
Luo Zheng flipped his hand, and the Great Thousand Sword was already in his hand.
He could not use the Emotion Severing Divine Dao here, but he was not really afraid of these strange insects!
"Swish, swish, swish, swish …"
Rays of sword light spread out in all directions from Luo Zheng. Dozens of strange insects were all cut into halves by the sword light. They fell to the ground and kept wriggling. After these strange insects were killed, the light above their heads quickly dimmed.
He had just killed a group of strange insects, and more of them crawled out from the ground. There was an endless stream of them.
"There is no end to these strange insects. Hurry up and leave this place!" Old Man Extreme Evil reminded.
Luo Zheng no longer fought with these strange insects. After killing his way through a gap, he rushed out.
These strange insects were everywhere in this desert. It was simply an ocean of strange insects!
As long as he stopped, these strange insects would completely surround him. Therefore, Luo Zheng could only keep running with his head down.
In the darkness of the night, Luo Zheng could not identify his direction, and he did not know where he was going. He ran in the darkness for several hours, and he did not know how far he had run. Then, he saw a white light pillar in front of him.
At the bottom of the light pillar, there were several cloaked figures standing there!

Chapter 1947
That huge light pillar looked very close, but it took Luo Zheng an entire incense stick's worth of time to get close.
The cloaked men standing around the light pillar noticed Luo Zheng, who was not far away, and they seemed to be a little flustered. They made some noises and shouted something at Luo Zheng. At the same time, they danced toward Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng could not understand the language of these cloaked men, but judging from their tone and movements, he could feel a strong sense of warning.
Two of the cloaked men even raised two steel forks and aimed them at Luo Zheng, who was not far away!
Luo Zheng remained calm. In order to avoid angering them, he did not choose to get close. Instead, he watched silently from the side.
The only purpose of those Perfect True Gods entering this world was to control the imperial jade seal.
Luo Zheng knew too little about this world. He needed to find out useful information as soon as possible. Therefore, these cloaked men were naturally very important!
These cloaked men obviously knew the habits of those strange insects very well. There were strange insects emerging from the sand all over the desert, but within the area covered by the light pillar, there was not a single strange insect …
Luo Zheng waited for a while not far away.
Suddenly, he heard a few loud and clear chirps from the sky.
A group of dark shadows flew across the sky, circling around the huge pillar of light.
"Eagles?"
Luo Zheng raised his head and stared. Although the light was dim, he could still distinguish that those black shadows were male eagles.
These male eagles had black feathers that almost blended in with the night. Their black eyes emitted a sharp aura, and they seemed to be very aggressive.
The cloaked men below the light pillar took out a green flute and put it to their mouths to play softly. As the flute let out a pleasant and melodious sound, these male eagles rushed toward the light spot in the distance.
The male eagles flew down and stretched out their strong claws to drag out a strange insect from the sand dune. Then, they threw it gently and swallowed it.
"So these cloaked men are herding eagles," Luo Zheng understood.
The strange insects in the desert were food for these eagles, and they were almost inexhaustible.
The eagles continued to fly, and those strange insects had no resistance at all. Every time they flew, there would be one less dot of light in the desert …
This process lasted for less than an hour.
Luo Zheng kept a certain distance from those cloaked men. Those cloaked men didn't look at Luo Zheng with hostility, nor were they too wary of Luo Zheng.
However, they paid no attention to Luo Zheng. Instead, they concentrated on playing the flutes in their hands and commanding the eagles.
But at this moment, an extremely loud and clear cry suddenly came from the horizon!
"Yo!"
A streak of golden light shot over from the horizon!
The moment the golden light appeared, the eagles were also frightened. They flapped their wings and fled in all directions!
"Golden Roc!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered.
That golden light was originally a huge Golden Roc. Its body was much larger than those eagles, and its wide and thick wings were dozens of feet long. When it swooped down, it crushed the head of an eagle. Without waiting for the eagle's body to fall, it swallowed it.
"Yo!"
This Golden Roc seemed very excited. With a flap of its golden wings, it pounced on another eagle. Its huge claws suddenly squeezed and tore another eagle to shreds!
Those cloaked men who were herding eagles also panicked. They communicated with each other and then began to play the flutes in their hands. However, the originally pleasant flute sound became sharp and fierce.
The eagles were encouraged by the flute sound and turned around, turning into black spirals that shot toward the Golden Roc!
A single eagle was not a match for the Golden Roc, but when many eagles joined forces, they had the strength to fight against the Golden Roc. These eagles maintained an extremely high speed, constantly attacking the Golden Roc. For a time, golden feathers kept falling from the sky …
"Yo, yo …"
Seeing that the group of eagles had the upper hand.
Two long cries came from the distance. Unexpectedly, two more golden lights came rushing over. It was another two Golden Roc!
Seeing this scene, those cloaked men panicked.
This group of eagles already had a hard time subduing one Golden Roc. Facing three Golden Roc, they were afraid that they would be completely wiped out.
Moreover, these Golden Roc's flying speed was much faster than the eagles. In this vast desert, even if they wanted to escape, there was nowhere to run. When these eagles were killed by the Golden Roc, the cloaked men would also be pecked to death by the Golden Roc!
"Yo …"
A Golden Roc grabbed half of an eagle's wing while it was escaping. Its body tumbled in the air and fell rapidly toward the desert. With a bang, it crashed not far from Luo Zheng.
"Yo!"
The Golden Roc let out a high-pitched cry and turned into a golden light that rushed down!
The black eagle on the ground had a special appearance. Its neck had a circle of white feathers. When it was fighting with the Golden Roc, this eagle was the most courageous, but now it had the most miserable end! Blood mixed with sand wrapped around its wound. It was unable to resist at all. Its pair of eagle eyes revealed deep despair.
Seeing that pair of eagle eyes, Luo Zheng's heart moved slightly. His knees slightly bent.
The moment that Golden Roc swooped down!
"Now is the time!"
Luo Zheng turned into a ray of light and shot toward the Golden Roc!
It wasn't that the Golden Roc didn't notice Luo Zheng. These Golden Roc also had a certain level of intelligence. It realized that Luo Zheng wasn't with the cloaked men, so it didn't pay attention to Luo Zheng.
It never expected that Luo Zheng would make a move at this moment!
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng calculated the time perfectly. The Golden Roc watched as Luo Zheng rushed forward, but it had no chance to retreat.
"Dong!"
Birds' skulls were the most fragile. Even if these eagles and Golden Roc were vicious creatures, their skulls couldn't withstand a blow.
What's more, Luo Zheng's strength wasn't light. Even a True God wouldn't be able to withstand it, let alone a Golden Roc.
With a muffled sound, Luo Zheng's knee fiercely slammed into the Golden Roc's head!
The Golden Roc didn't even have time to scream before its huge body rolled to the side. After rolling a few times in the sand, it stopped moving.
"Yo, yo!"
The two Golden Roc chasing the other eagles in the sky saw this scene and let out two mournful cries. One of the Golden Roc wanted to take revenge for its companion, so it swooped down toward Luo Zheng.
The cloaked men below the pillar of light were probably worried about Luo Zheng's safety. They waved the flutes in their hands and made a strange noise at Luo Zheng. They probably wanted Luo Zheng to dodge.
Luo Zheng slightly spread his arms. The corners of his mouth turned up in a smile. He stood in place and didn't dodge.
Seeing the huge Golden Roc rushing over, Luo Zheng unexpectedly attacked in a circle!
"Boom!"
Sand and dust flew everywhere.
Under the impact of that power, the yellow sand under Luo Zheng's feet formed a clear ripple that spread out.
The cloaked men stared blankly at the flying sand and dust. After the sand and dust settled, they saw a scene that made them suddenly tremble.
The Golden Roc was lying motionless in front of Luo Zheng. Its head had completely disappeared under the bombardment …
Luo Zheng clenched his right fist and looked at the remaining Golden Roc in the sky with a smile. If this Golden Roc flew in the sky, he really wouldn't be able to do anything to it. If it rushed down, it would only be courting death!
"Yo —"
The Golden Roc circled in the sky and stared at Luo Zheng with its golden eyes. After it realized that this human in front of it wasn't a match for it, it could only reluctantly withdraw!
After the Golden Roc had gone far away, the few cloaked men under the beam of light began to yell and rush towards Luo Zheng.
Two of them raised their hands, bowed, and patted their chests. Hearing this, Luo Zheng felt a headache, but he roughly understood that they were thanking him.
The other cloaked figures surrounded the eagle with a broken wing. One of them helped the eagle bandage its wound, while the others looked very sad.
This eagle with a circle of white feathers around its neck should be quite special. Unfortunately, after its wing was broken, it would never be able to spread its wings and fly.
After the cloaked men helped the eagle clean its wound, one of them picked up a bamboo flute and blew it.
All the eagles in the sky descended and formed a circle on the ground.
One of the cloaked men patted the eagle's back and called out to Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1948
No matter what, after Luo Zheng helped these cloaked people kill the two Golden Rocs, he had won the cloaked people's favor.
For the current Luo Zheng, he needed to understand this world as soon as possible. Using these cloaked people might be his only choice.
Without much hesitation, Luo Zheng stepped onto the eagle!
The group of cloaked people also carried the broken winged eagle onto another eagle.
One of the cloaked people played a melodious flute. All the eagles immediately spread their wings and soared into the sky, flying into the depths of the darkness.
These eagles had extraordinary physical strength. After flying in the sky for four hours, they needed to rest. The places where the eagles landed were actually simple campsites.
The cloaked people were very familiar with these simple campsites. It seemed that they were the ones who built them.
"It seems that they really do know the way out of the desert," Luo Zheng thought to himself.
After resting for half an hour in the simple campsite, everyone set off again.
Along the way, Luo Zheng tried to communicate with these cloaked people. Although the languages of different races were very different, there was always a trace of a pattern. Luo Zheng didn't find it difficult to learn.
After communicating for a night, Luo Zheng also had a preliminary understanding of some simple words of this race. He was able to communicate concisely.
About twelve to fourteen hours later.
Suddenly, a yellow crack appeared in the darkness. That crack was like an infinitely long sword that stretched across the sky. That crack rapidly expanded. In the blink of an eye, the entire world turned into day!
Luo Zheng saw day and night change in such a way for the first time. Naturally, he felt very shocked.
As for these cloaked people, it wasn't strange at all.
At the same time, Luo Zheng also noticed that the surrounding scenery had changed. They had already flown out of the desert!
While sitting on the eagle's back and looking down, Luo Zheng's complexion changed several times!
From the desert area, there was a continuous stretch of yellow canyons. Those canyons were like cracks on the ground. In the middle of those canyons, there were strange black insects that were twenty to thirty thousand feet long and a hundred feet wide. They were poking their heads out and swimming around in the canyons.
These strange insects were similar to the strange insects in the desert, but they were thousands of times larger. The power they contained was also thousands of times greater.
Even if he were to pass through the desert on his own, it would be difficult for him to pass through these canyons …
"What kind of world is this?" Thinking about the Perfect Real God from Sword Clan who had died, Luo Zheng was shocked.
The cloaked man on the other eagle saw Luo Zheng looking at the giant insects below and immediately said, "Wow, Nana Slaughter!" Then he pointed at the giant insects below and said, "Nana Karu!"
Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
In the first round, the cloaked man said that the Golden Roc was very dangerous.
The second sentence was that the giant black worms were extremely dangerous!
Just as the cloaked man finished speaking, another cloaked man on the other side roared, "Huo Huo, Huo Huo!" That was a warning to everyone to be careful!
At this moment, seven to eight giant insects that were wandering in the canyons suddenly lifted their bodies. These giant insects that were hundreds of miles long actually stood upright. They opened their mouths that were big enough to swallow a mountain and rushed toward Luo Zheng and the others.
When the eagles saw this scene, they were also frightened. One by one, they spread their wings and flew to a higher altitude. Under this violent shaking, a cloaked man rolled down from the eagle's back!
Before the cloaked man could scream, Luo Zheng's hand shot out like lightning and grabbed the cloaked man. Luo Zheng swung the cloaked man in the air and pulled the cloaked man onto his eagle's back. Luo Zheng's skillful move only caused the eagle to sink down a little before it continued to rise.
The giant insects rose at an extremely fast speed. In the blink of an eye, they rushed toward everyone. They stirred up the surrounding airflow and set off a hurricane.
Fortunately, these giant insects couldn't fly. The length of their bodies was limited after all. Even if they stretched out their entire bodies, they could only reach a length of twenty to thirty thousand feet. The eagles, on the other hand, were like birds flying freely in the sky. They could increase their height infinitely!
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh swoosh swoosh …"
The group of eagles spread their wings and climbed upward at a 90 degree angle. In the end, the height of their flight still exceeded the length of these giant insects.
These giant insects couldn't swallow Luo Zheng and the others. They fell in all directions. Their huge bodies smashed into the canyons, causing violent tremors. It was as if the whole world was shaking!
"Urala!"
After getting rid of those giant insects, the cloaked man on the eagle's back suddenly began to cheer. He felt as if he had survived a disaster. The cloaked man behind Luo Zheng also expressed his gratitude to Luo Zheng. If it wasn't for Luo Zheng, he would have fallen in the giant insects' mouths.
Luo Zheng only nodded slightly. The danger just now was indeed very thrilling for these cloaked men. But for Luo Zheng, it was considered very normal.
He was still staring down. He saw that the canyons, which looked like scars on the earth, were getting wider and wider. More and more giant insects were piling up in the canyons. His expression became more and more solemn. These giant insects had to consume a terrifying amount of energy to survive in this world. They had to consume a huge amount of food.
But in that desert and this canyons, there were no other creatures besides these giant insects. How did they survive?
What he saw next made Luo Zheng feel that this world was even more unreasonable …
The canyons below continued to extend into the distance. It became wider and wider, and finally formed a huge ocean!
However, there wasn't a single drop of water in this ocean. It was densely packed with giant insects. They were constantly rolling and twisting. In addition to the black insects, there were also some purple and gray insects mixed in. This scene made one's scalp tingle!
This was a real sea of insects!
Fortunately, the eagles were flying at an extremely high altitude. They wouldn't be attacked by these giant insects.
Luo Zheng couldn't help but ask about the origin of these giant insects. However, he didn't completely understand the language of these cloaked men. The cloaked man and Luo Zheng gestured for a long time, but they still couldn't say anything.
"It seems that we can only take it slowly …"
Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of helplessness. Fortunately, learning the language of a race wasn't a difficult task. He should be able to do it in a few days.
After flying over this sea of insects, they were greeted by an even wider world. There were continuous emerald green mountains and rivers. The lakes were like jewels. It was a scene full of vitality.
It wasn't long before a lofty fort appeared before them. The fort was shaped like the head of an enormous eagle, and there were rows of eagles standing in an orderly manner on the walls of the fort. There were actually over 10,000 of them!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
The eagles carrying Luo Zheng and the others glided down and soon entered the fortress.
Unexpectedly, just as Luo Zheng jumped down from the eagle's back, he heard a burst of shouting. Then, Luo Zheng saw dozens of cloaked men carrying black forks and surrounding him!
At the same time, they kept saying "Wu Lie".
Luo Zheng already knew that it meant "enemy".
"Enemy?" Luo Zheng stood in place and glanced at these cloaked men indifferently.
In Luo Zheng's eyes, these cloaked men were not worth mentioning. If he wanted to, he could completely exterminate them in the blink of an eye!
"Yo!"
At this time, an eagle shot over like a black sword. It stood on the roof in front of them and coldly stared at Luo Zheng.
This eagle's feathers were like sharp swords. It exuded a fierce aura. Even Luo Zheng felt a faint sense of threat.
"Feathers?" Luo Zheng also noticed that this eagle had a circle of white feathers around its neck. It was exactly the same as the one with the broken wings. This kind of eagle with a circle of white feathers around its neck should be quite special.
The cloaked men surrounded Luo Zheng. However, the cloaked men who brought Luo Zheng back were vigorously arguing about something. From time to time, they would point at the broken wings of the eagle.
Luo Zheng also understood that they were probably trying to help him clarify that he was absolutely not their enemy. They even saved the eagle and so on.
After this explanation, the cloaked men's hostility towards Luo Zheng lessened a lot. They put down the forks in their hands …
The leader of the cloaked men even slightly bowed to Luo Zheng and pointed at the broken wings of the eagle to express his gratitude. It seemed that this eagle with a circle of white feathers around its neck was really important to these cloaked men.
Then, the leader of the cloaked men made an inviting gesture. It should be an invitation to come to this fort as a guest.
However, Luo Zheng did not follow the cloaked men for long before he suddenly stopped.
He turned his head and saw a red figure sitting alone in a cage not far away. Her face was buried between her knees.
"Mu Ning?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a surprised expression.

Chapter 1949
These cloaked people weren't strong, yet they were able to capture Mu Ning?
Although they couldn't use Divine Dao in this world, Mu Ning's strength wasn't something these cloaked people could compete with.
They should be relying on those eagles …
Luo Zheng Gang also noticed that some of these eagles were different from the others. Their strength was far more powerful than ordinary eagles. Perhaps, there were some extremely powerful individuals among the eagles domesticated by the cloaked man.
Looking at Mu Ning's appearance, her mood was very low.
Luo Zheng made a gesture to the cloaked man, indicating him to wait for a moment. Then, he walked towards the iron cage.
Mu Ning didn't notice Luo Zheng's arrival. She still buried her face in her legs, motionless.
"Dong dong dong!"
Luo Zheng knocked on the iron cage.
"Get lost! I won't eat your disgusting food! "Mu Ning didn't look up.
After these cloaked people locked Mu Ning up, they were afraid that she would starve to death, so they sent food every day.
How could Mu Ning eat it? Besides, she was a True God, so she didn't need to eat.
Luo Zheng crossed his hands and looked at Mu Ning with a faint smile, not saying a word.
Mu Ning seemed to feel that something was wrong. Those damn cloaked people would always say a lot of things that she couldn't understand before leaving. Why were they so quiet this time?
When she looked up, her eyes flashed with a gem-like luster, and she was stunned.
"Why are you here!"
Her surprised face was mixed with a hint of excitement and surprise.
"Why can't I be here?" Luo Zheng said lightly.
Luo Zheng didn't have a good impression of the Mu Family, even though he had saved Mu Ning twice.
But after Mu Xuerong entered the canyon, the first thing she did was to kill him. Mu Ning must have leaked information to her sister.
In his opinion, it wasn't an exaggeration to say that Mu Ning repaid kindness with enmity!
"But why are you with those cloaked people …" Mu Ning looked at the cloaked people behind her. They didn't see Luo Zheng as an enemy, but instead treated him as a guest.
Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders and didn't answer.
"Can you get me out of here?" Mu Ning asked.
Hearing Mu Ning's words, a smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face. "Why should I save my enemy?"
Although Luo Zheng knew that his biggest enemy was the Dongfang Clan, his first target was still the Mu Clan. After all, Mu Haiji was the one who wanted to destroy the Dayan Realm.
Mu Ning's excited expression condensed on her face. She never thought that Luo Zheng would suddenly say such a thing …
"It was my sister who wanted to kill you, not me …" Mu Ning gritted her teeth as she tried to defend herself.
"The result is the same," Luo Zheng said with a smile. Then he turned around and left.
"Clang!" Mu Ning pressed herself against the iron fence and continued, "I didn't know that Big Sister would kill you. She only asked me to investigate your anonymous identity. I didn't know you were Luo Xiao's son!"
"The result is still the same," Luo Zheng said without turning his head.
Since he was Luo Xiao's son and Mu Ning was Mu Haiji's daughter, there was bound to be an irreconcilable enmity between them.
When the cloaked man saw this scene, he asked in their language, "Friend?"
"No," Luo Zheng also replied in their language.
Then Luo Zheng left with the cloaked men, leaving Mu Ning in the cage.
Mu Ning was furious.
She had the same temper as Mu Xuerong. No matter when or where, when facing anyone, she was as strong as ever!
But this time, she jumped into the five-coloured halo behind Luo Zheng. After falling into this strange world, she experienced a lot of hardships and almost died at the hands of several golden rocs.
Later, she encountered those eagles. In a fit of anger, she killed three of those feathered beasts!
Who would have thought that two more eagles with white feathers around their necks would rush over. They were actually so powerful. She was no match for them and was captured by these cloaked people.
Besides her sister and father, even in front of the Mu Clan's Patriarch and other Saint Emperors, she always held her head high.
When had she ever suffered such a setback in the past?
When she thought of her sister confirming Luo Zheng's identity, she chose not to believe it and even tried to defend this guy. Under such circumstances, Luo Zheng openly admitted his identity!
An extremely tangled contradiction suddenly surged in her heart. She could no longer suppress the strong grievance and cried out in the cage.
Luo Zheng had already walked far away with the cloaked men when he suddenly heard Mu Ning's crying. His footsteps slowed, and his eyes revealed a trace of unwillingness.
Although Mu Ning's personality was very strong, she didn't have any bad intentions …
The enmity between the Luo Clan and the Mu Clan should not be shouldered by this little girl.
But what kind of enmity she had with these cloaked men that she would be imprisoned here, Luo Zheng didn't know. He could only observe the situation from now on.
Then, Luo Zheng was entertained by the cloaked men and arranged a place to stay.
These cloaked men were very kind to him and didn't restrict his freedom, allowing him to walk around the fort.
After living in the fort for a few days, he gradually became familiar with their language and could communicate smoothly. He also understood this group of cloaked men more and more.
They called themselves the Eagle Clan and were the original inhabitants of this world.
For generations, they had herded eagles for a living, or it could be said that the eagles were their weapons and friends.
As for the eagles with white feathers around their necks, they were called "King Eagles". These King Eagles were far more powerful than ordinary eagles. They were also the most powerful weapon of their clan, and also the spiritual belief of their clan.
On the fourth day, Luo Zheng saw several Eagle Clan members being escorted out of the square on the side of the fort.
The cloaks on the heads of these Eagle Clan members were removed. Luo Zheng looked from afar and recognized these Eagle Clan members at a glance. They were the ones who had brought Luo Zheng out of the desert.
Then he saw them raise their left arm and tie it to the railing.
Next to them, an Eagle Clan member carried a simple and unadorned long sword and slowly raised it. It seemed that he was going to cut off the arms of these Eagle Clan members!
