Luo Zheng frowned and jumped up. He shuttled through the roof like a dragonfly skimming the surface of the water and landed lightly on the ground. He asked in the language of the Eagle Clan, "What are you doing?"
The Eagle Clan member carrying the long sword looked at Luo Zheng indifferently and then said, "Execution."
"What did they do wrong?" Luo Zheng asked.
"They didn't protect the King Eagles well and let them cut off one of their wings," the executioner replied.
"If the King Eagles cut off their wings, they will have to cut off their arms. What if the King Eagles die? Will they be buried with the King Eagles? "Luo Zheng asked calmly.
"Yes, that's right," the executioner nodded.
"It's our fault for not protecting the King Eagles well."
"We are willing to be tortured."
"We are willing to be punished."
The Eagle Clan members said one after another.
The King Eagles were the faith of the Eagle Clan and they couldn't let anything happen to them. If the King Eagles died in the process of herding, the Eagle Clan members would have to be buried with them.
This was the rule of the Eagle Clan and Luo Zheng couldn't interfere. But he still asked, "Is there any way to exempt them from the punishment?"
The executioner shook his head, "Impossible, unless the King Eagles can spread their wings and fly again."
After their wings were broken, the King Eagles would become useless and lose their ability to fight forever. This was a great loss for the Eagle Clan, so the Eagle Clan members had to be extra careful when serving the King Eagles.
"Let the King Eagles spread their wings and fly again?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly and a smile appeared on his face.
For these Eagle Clan members, broken wings couldn't be regrown, but for Luo Zheng, it wasn't that difficult.
If the Divine Dao could be used here, Luo Zheng could use the Life Divine Dao to help the King Eagles regrow their wings. Since the Divine Dao couldn't be condensed, he still had his Jindan.
After thinking for a while, Mu Ning's figure flashed in Luo Zheng's mind. He smiled and said, "If I can help the King Eagle regrow its broken wing, will you let them go?"
"You can regrow their wings?" The executioner stared at Luo Zheng in disbelief.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly and said seriously, "I should be able to."

Chapter 1950
The Great Tribal Chief mentioned by the executioner was the highest commander of this Eagle Fort.
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.
The executioner hurriedly left, and the several Eagle clansmen waiting to be punished stared at Luo Zheng in astonishment.
"You … can really do it?"
"You can regenerate a King Eagle's broken wing?"
"How is that possible!"
The Eagle clansmen were somewhat excited, and their voices were trembling.
They were willing to be punished!
Injuring a King Eagle was an unforgivable sin, and it was even worse than injuring their lives.
So when they heard Luo Zheng suddenly make such a promise, they were naturally excited, but at the same time, they were afraid that Luo Zheng was a swindler, causing them to be excited for nothing.
"Yo —"
Not long after, there was the cry of an eagle.
A black streak of light appeared in the sky, and an eagle spread its wings and circled around, flying towards Luo Zheng.
The moment this black streak of light appeared, all the eagles in the Eagle Fort became abnormally quiet. The eagles scattered in every corner of the fort curled up their wings and stuck out their chests, motionless like soldiers being inspected.
"This eagle is so big." Luo Zheng raised his head and looked at the eagle.
This eagle's neck was actually light gold in color, and the aura it emitted made Luo Zheng feel a strong threat. This King Eagle … had the power to fight against him!
In the blink of an eye, the King Eagle landed in front of Luo Zheng, and a majestic Eagle clansman jumped down from the back of the King Eagle.
After that Eagle clansman took off that strange cloak, a middle-aged man with a broad forehead was revealed. This was the Great Tribal Chief of the Eagle Fort.
The Great Tribal Chief walked in front of Luo Zheng and asked in the Eagle language, "You can regenerate a King Eagle's broken arm?"
Unlike the other Eagle clansmen, the Great Tribal Chief didn't doubt Luo Zheng's words. After all, this kind of thing needed to be fulfilled immediately.
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.
Hearing this, the king eagle behind the chief uttered "Yo". It should have understood Luo Zheng's words as it glared at Luo Zheng with its sharp eyes.
The Great Chief took a deep look at Luo Zheng and then asked, "Do you have any other requests besides sparing their lives?"
In the Great Tribal Chief's view, sparing the lives of these people was a matter of course, and besides, these people had nothing to do with this outsider, so this was not a request.
"Of course." Luo Zheng smiled. "Release the woman you captured."
Hearing Luo Zheng's request, the Great Chief's face revealed a trace of hesitation. "She killed our eagle and is our enemy."
"I promise she won't do it again," Luo Zheng said.
The Great Chieftain considered for a moment before nodding.
Even if that woman still wanted to cause trouble after being released, she was no match for the Eagle Castle.
Under the lead of the Great Chieftain, Luo Zheng arrived at the rear tower of the Eagle Castle.
After opening the door, he saw the king eagle with the broken wing lying inside. Although its wound had been tightly bandaged, it no longer had the chance to soar in the sky. This king eagle seemed very sad.
"Please." The Great Chief made a gesture.
Luo Zheng nodded and gently patted it. A round milky-white Golden Core appeared in his hand. The Golden Core emitted a soft fragrance.
As the fragrance spread out, it immediately attracted the attention of the king eagle and the Great Chief.
This milky-white Golden Core was only a Five Revolution Golden Core. Luo Zheng had obtained it when he stayed on Han Jia's floating island. It could allow a True God's severed limb to quickly regrow.
Now, when Luo Zheng took the Golden Core and walked towards the king eagle, the king eagle with the broken wing suddenly became vigilant. Not only did it refuse to swallow the Golden Core, it even assumed an attacking posture.
Helpless, Luo Zheng turned his head to look at the Great Chief.
The Great Chief immediately took out a flute and played it. Through this flute, the Eagle Clan could communicate with the king eagles.
When the melodious sound came out, the King Eagle calmed down a lot and finally opened its beak obediently.
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and flicked the Golden Core into the king eagle's mouth. The king eagle swallowed it in one gulp.
Then, Luo Zheng retreated to the side with a smile on his face.
"Screech, screech." The king eagle with a circle of pale golden feathers behind the Great Chief cried out twice. There was a trace of nervousness in its eyes.
The Great Chief also looked at the king eagle with a nervous expression and asked, "How long … will it last?" In the Great Chief's opinion, even if Luo Zheng's Golden Core had miraculous effects, it was impossible to regrow a wing in such a short time. That would be too miraculous.
"Right away," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
Even a True God could quickly regrow a severed limb after eating this Five Revolution Golden Core. However, the new severed limb would need to be tempered again and would be far inferior to the previous limb.
But in this world, regrowing a severed wing could almost be considered a miracle.
Just as his voice fell, the king eagle's body suddenly trembled and the black feathers all over its body exploded. Then, blood spurted out from the bandaged wound and a pale green energy gathered at its severed wing.
Bones, muscles, and blood began to gather and grow at a speed visible to the naked eye!
"This, this, this fast … this is simply a miracle." The Great Chief's eyes were wide open and his face was filled with disbelief.
"Screech, screech, screech." The king eagle behind the Great Chief also cried out excitedly.
Not long after, a brand new wing grew out. Glossy black feathers also grew out!
"Huff, huff …"
The king eagle flapped its wings and set off gusts of wind. It spiraled out of the tower's window and soared into the sky.
"Thank, thank you so much …"
The Great Chief's expression was extremely excited. He placed his right hand on his chest and bowed deeply to Luo Zheng.
The growth potential of these king eagles was limitless. Losing a king eagle was a huge loss for the Eagle Clan. After all, only one king eagle could be born out of a thousand eagles!
When the king eagle flew out of the tower, it attracted the cheers of the Eagle Clan outside. They had never seen such a miracle in their lives. They were shocked but also very happy …
Those cries of surprise reached Mu Ning's ears. She raised her head and looked at the king eagle in the sky with an aggrieved expression.
Ever since she saw Luo Zheng a few days ago, that guy hadn't appeared again. Had he already left the fort of these cloaked people?
"You're so cruel, leaving me in the lurch!" She clenched her teeth and hatefully said, "Even if the Mu Family and the Luo Family are enemies, so what? I've never regarded you as an enemy …"
She didn't know how long these cloaked people would keep her here. Maybe one day she would be executed and eaten by those damned eagles …
Thinking of this, sadness welled up in her heart. Even someone as strong as her couldn't help but shed tears. She really didn't want to die in this damned place.
But at this time, a figure quietly appeared.
Mu Ning noticed something and raised her head again. She saw Luo Zheng looking at her with a faint smile on his face.
However, in her eyes, there was a faint hint of mockery in that smile.
"What are you doing here?" Mu Ning's expression quickly turned cold.
Who knew that Luo Zheng would just wave his hand and say something to the cloaked people. Two of them then walked over and opened the cage.
Mu Ning didn't immediately leave the cage. She got up and took two steps back, a vigilant look on her face. "What do you want to do?"
"I'll let you go," Luo Zheng lightly said.
"Let me go?" Mu Ning couldn't believe her ears.
Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders and no longer spoke. He turned around and left.
Mu Ning stood in the cage in a daze. What method did Luo Zheng use to make these cloaked people let her go so easily?
The moment Luo Zheng's back disappeared around the corner, she rushed out of the cage and chased after him …
… …
… …
The Eagle Tribesmen held a grand banquet to thank Luo Zheng.
Mu Ning sat on Luo Zheng's side.
From a prisoner to a guest, Mu Ning's mood fluctuated greatly. Seeing the Eagle Tribesmen treat Luo Zheng with so much respect made her even more puzzled. Moreover, this guy seemed to have learned the language of the Eagle Tribesmen in such a short time.
This guy could communicate with them freely, but she herself was in a daze at the banquet like a deaf person.
Toasting each other and toasting each other, Luo Zheng began to understand the key information of this world from the Great Tribal Chief.
His purpose in helping the Eagle Tribesmen was to understand the truth of this world as soon as possible. Now, it seemed that this world was most likely constructed by the Imperial Jade Seal. There must be a key to controlling the Imperial Jade Seal.
In addition to Luo Zheng, the other Perfection Stage powerhouses had also entered this world.
Although the Perfection Stage powerhouse from the Sword Tribe had strangely died in the desert, the other Perfection Stage powerhouses wouldn't fall so easily.

Chapter 1951
At first, these Eagle Tribesmen had some reservations about Luo Zheng.
Although Luo Zheng had rescued the King Eagle, they had only treated Luo Zheng as a guest and had not fully accepted Luo Zheng.
But now, Luo Zheng had used pills to regenerate the King Eagle's broken wings, which had a completely different meaning.
The Great Tribal Chief's heart was also burning.
A single pill was enough to save a crippled King Eagle. He was calculating how many of those miraculous pills Luo Zheng had.
He was not clear about Luo Zheng's origins, but this was not important. If the price was low enough, he even had the intention of snatching those pills!
Luo Zheng was well aware of the Great Tribal Chief's calculations.
Luo Zheng still had seven or eight Five Revolutions Golden Pills used for healing. If the Eagle Tribesmen really had the intention of snatching them, the price they would have to pay would definitely exceed their gains.
As he continued to ask the Great Tribal Chief, the outline of this world became clearer and clearer in Luo Zheng's mind.
There were three major races in this world.
The Eagle Tribe was one of them, the Golden Peng Luo Zheng had seen before was the Golden Peng Tribe, and the other major race was the Black Bird Tribe.
These three races all relied on domesticated birds to survive in this world, and the three races all lived around that vast desert.
"Is the reason for this situation because of those insects?" Luo Zheng asked.
The Great Tribal Chief nodded, "Yes, our Eagles feed on Heavenly Wheel Insects, and only those Eagles that eat Heavenly Wheel Insects can thrive!"
'So those strange insects are called Heavenly Wheel Insects', Luo Zheng thought silently.
Every once in a while, the Eagle Tribe would take their Eagles to the desert to herd them and let them prey on those Heavenly Wheel Insects. Luo Zheng just so happened to run into the Eagle Tribe's Eagle Herders in the desert …
The Golden Peng Tribe's goal was the same, to hunt Heavenly Wheel Insects. However, the three major races in this world had been fighting with each other for countless years, so when the Golden Peng Tribe saw the Eagles, they would also attack and kill them.
That desert was the center of this world. The Sword Race's Paragon had also died in that desert, and had been dead for countless years.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng asked, "Are there any special places in this world? In other words, has anyone seen something similar to the Imperial Jade Seal? "
Luo Zheng didn't know how to control the Imperial Jade Seal. He didn't even know what was going on in this world. He could only search for clues bit by bit …
"Imperial jade seal? What's that?" The Great Tribal Chief asked in confusion.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng smiled. There was no such thing as an imperial jade seal in this race.
Therefore, Luo Zheng casually broke off a table leg and used his fingers as a knife to carve it.
As his fingers danced, countless wood shavings bloomed, and a small snake was slowly carved out. Below the small snake was a square jade seal.
When Mu Ning saw this scene, her face was full of doubt. She couldn't understand the conversation between Luo Zheng and these cloaked people. She also didn't know what Luo Zheng wanted to do.
But as the piece of wood continued to take shape, Mu Ning's eyes flickered. "So it's the Imperial Jade Seal. He also wants to find a way to control this world …"
Although Mu Ning didn't know what was going on in this world, the Paragons and True Gods who entered this world had a rough inference. These inferences happened to coincide. This world was most likely a space-time constructed by the Imperial Jade Seal. The method to control the Imperial Jade Seal had to be found inside.
After Luo Zheng finished carving, he gently let out a breath. Then, he put the carved Imperial Jade Seal on his table, stared at the Great Tribal Chief, and asked, "It's this thing."
The Imperial Jade Seal that Luo Zheng had carved by hand wasn't lifelike, but its appearance wasn't far from the mark.
When the Great Tribal Chief saw the wooden sculpture on Luo Zheng's table, his pupils suddenly shrank. The Eagle Clansmen present also looked at the wooden sculpture with dignified gazes and whispered to each other.
These people's reactions made Luo Zheng somewhat happy. It seemed that he had asked the right question.
Although Mu Ning couldn't understand these people's words, she could read the expressions of these cloaked people. These people clearly recognized this wooden sculpture!
The Great Tribal Chief pondered for a moment before saying, "I recognize this snake."
"Where?" Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly rose.
But at this time, the Great Tribal Chief revealed a hesitant expression. After thinking it over, he gently sighed and said, "This is the Heavenly Wheel King Snake of the Heavenly Wheel Temple. In the desert, we have to worship the Heavenly Wheel King Snake every once in a while. We send young boys and girls to the temple to pray that it won't punish us …"
Speaking of this, the other people's faces revealed sadness.
Worship was a very solemn thing for a race. At the same time, they were willing to do it.
But for the Eagle Clan, they weren't willing. In this world, the Peng Clan and the Profound Bird Clan had the same feeling …
In the depths of the desert, there was a temple. The Heavenly Wheel King Snake controlled all the Heavenly Wheel Insects in the desert. The three clans would send young boys and girls to the temple every once in a while.
The fate of these young boys and girls was self-evident. For countless years, not a single young boy or girl had left the Heavenly Wheel Temple alive.
None of the clans were willing to send their descendants in, but the three clans would do their best to send in boys and girls. If the Golden Roc Clan sent in ten boys and girls, the Eagle Clan would send in a few more.
If a race didn't send enough young boys and girls, or sent too little, the Heavenly Wheel King Snake would punish them …
There were countless huge Heavenly Wheel Insects in the sea of insects. If over a hundred of them were released, the Eagle Stronghold would be destroyed in the blink of an eye! As long as the Heavenly Wheel King Snake was willing, it could release all the Heavenly Wheel Insects in the sea of insects and easily destroy the world.
"So that's how it is …" Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
The humans of this world relied on the Golden Peng Clan, the Eagle Clan, and the Profound Bird Clan to hunt and survive. The birds hunted the Heavenly Wheel Insects to grow, but the humans had to offer their children to the Heavenly Wheel King Snake. Otherwise, the humans would be destroyed, forming a cycle. In other words, the humans of this world lived to provide for the Heavenly Wheel King Snake!
Then this Heavenly Wheel King Snake was the key to controlling the Imperial Jade Seal.
"How often do you worship?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Not at a fixed time … Sometimes once half a year, sometimes once a month," the Great Tribal Chief said with a sad expression.
At the same time, the other people in the temple fell silent. Some of the Eagle Clan members also seemed very sad.
Over the years, many of their children had been sent to the temple. They were all their own flesh and blood. Luo Zheng's question reminded them of the sad past.
"I'm sorry," Luo Zheng said in a low voice.
The Great Tribal Chief sighed. "After so many years, we have become accustomed to it," he said, pointing to the carving on Luo Zheng's table. "The Heavenly Wheel King Snake is a god that cannot be disobeyed. Worshipping it is something we must do to survive! My poor Shasha, in half a month, she will also … "
Halfway through his words, the Great Tribal Chief's face showed a trace of sadness. His daughter had also been chosen as a sacrifice.
The girl named Shasha was only six years old and was not fully sensible. She did not understand what the adults were talking about. She sat next to the Great Tribal Chief, quietly and attentively eating her dinner.
Hearing her father mention her name, Shasha smiled and cast a bright smile at him.
"In half a month, we will worship it?" Luo Zheng's eyes showed surprise.
The Great Tribal Chief nodded. "Yes."
"Can I participate?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
The Great Tribal Chief stared at Luo Zheng in surprise, a trace of vigilance appearing on his face.
Although Luo Zheng had helped them cure a King Eagle, it did not mean that the Eagle Clan had complete trust in Luo Zheng. After all, they did not know Luo Zheng's origins, including the woman beside Luo Zheng. Whether it was her dress or aura, she did not seem to belong to this world. As for where she came from, these people could not guess.
The narrow world created a narrow worldview. Whether it was the Eagle Clan or the Peng Clan, they did not care about the truth of this world. They only wanted their clansmen to be able to pass it down from generation to generation.

Chapter 1952
After these Five Revolutions Golden Pills were taken out, their fragrance filled the entire hall.
The other Eagle clansmen hadn't seen the magic of these pills, but the Great Tribal Chief had.
When the Great Tribal Chief saw the five milky white Golden Pills, his breathing suddenly became heavy.
The King Eagles of their Eagle Race had fought for so many years, and many of them had died. There were even more King Eagles that were injured and unable to fly, including the most powerful Golden Feather and Blood Feather King Eagles!
The five pills that Luo Zheng took out could restore their original strength, which could greatly increase the strength of the Eagle Race.
"Take me there, these are yours," Luo Zheng said lightly.
The Great Tribal Chief's face showed some interest.
Recently, the friction between the Eagle Race and the Peng Race had been growing. It was very likely that a fierce war would break out between the two races. They needed these magical pills.
"I'm willing, but the other Great Tribal Chiefs may not be willing," the Great Tribal Chief said.
It turned out that the Eagle Race was divided into five tribes. Besides the Eagle Stronghold, there were four other tribes.
Even if the Great Tribal Chief agreed, the other Great Tribal Chiefs might not agree.
Luo Zheng frowned.
From the clues he had obtained, the Heavenly Wheel Temple was probably the only place to control the Imperial Jade Seal. He had to go!
But he had walked through the desert for so long. If he didn't have the guidance of the Eagle Race, it would be impossible for him to find the temple …
Thinking of this, he turned to look at Mu Ning.
Mu Ning held a wine cup in her hand, tilted her head with a smile, and said, "So you used these pills to please these cloaked people."
Mu Ning couldn't understand the conversation between Luo Zheng and these Eagle Race clansmen, but she could understand their expressions and movements.
These Eagle Race clansmen seemed to be very interested in these healing pills, and Luo Zheng probably wanted to use these pills to exchange with the Eagle Race clansmen.
Looking at Luo Zheng's expression, the Eagle Race clansmen didn't agree to Luo Zheng's request.
"Do you have any more healing pills? If you want these Reconstruction Pills, you can use any number of Revolutions Golden Pills, "Luo Zheng said.
Even First or Second Revolution Golden Pills could heal the disabled King Eagles. He only hoped that there would be more of them.
"Yes, and I have a lot." Mu Ning smiled.
"Give it to me." Luo Zheng stretched out his hand.
"Why?" Mu Ning asked.
Luo Zheng's eyes slightly darkened, "Because I've saved you three times!"
In the Sea of Time Forbidden Land, Luo Zheng had already saved Mu Ning twice. In this world, Luo Zheng had just allowed her to escape from the cage. Otherwise, who knew what kind of fate she would face!
"That's because you were willing to save me." Mu Ning smiled.
Luo Zheng stared at her beautiful, lively, and slightly proud face and threatened, "Don't force me."
Even in the Realm of the Gods, Mu Ning was far from being Luo Zheng's opponent.
Moreover, in this world, Mu Ning was unable to use her Divine Dao. Although they were both lower True Divinities, there was a considerable disparity between Mu Ning and Luo Zheng's strength!
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, Mu Ning's face revealed a resentful expression. After thinking for a moment, she firmly said, "I can give it to you … but no matter where you go, you have to bring me along!"
"Impossible." Luo Zheng shook his head.
He didn't trust Mu Ning, not to mention that Mu Xuerong was also in this world.
Bringing Mu Ning with him would always be a hidden danger.
"Then there's nothing to talk about." Mu Ning sighed softly and said, "Maybe I'm not your opponent, but I can destroy those pills in an instant." Saying this, she actually smiled and looked at Luo Zheng with a provocative gaze.
"You!" Luo Zheng's gaze trembled slightly.
At this time, Mu Ning suddenly said with an incomparably serious expression, "I swear, I won't cause trouble for you. Moreover, my sister wants to kill you. If you meet my sister, I might … be able to persuade her. Even if you are the son of Luo Xiao, you don't have to be enemies with my Mu Family …"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face revealed a strange expression.
Although he hadn't known Mu Ning for long, this woman had always been very intelligent. Why did she say these words without thinking? Had she become so naive?
A woman like Mu Xuerong couldn't be persuaded.
The crisis of the two families could be resolved with just a few words from her?
Not to mention Mu Xuerong, even Luo Zheng couldn't be persuaded …
Mu Ning's expression was incomparably serious.
"Promise me," Mu Ning stared at Luo Zheng and said.
Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment, then nodded and said, "But you must listen to all my arrangements."
Mu Ning suddenly smiled, and her serious face immediately transformed into a victorious expression. Then, with a gentle flick of her slender finger, a Golden Core jumped out from her Sumeru Ring.
Luo Zheng hadn't been in the Realm of the Gods for long, so he couldn't bring the pills from the Great Evolution Universe into the Realm of the Gods. Thus, he hadn't collected many Golden Cores in the Realm of the Gods. Those Golden Cores had only been obtained from the exchanges between the rich and powerful families.
But Mu Ning was different. She was born in the Realm of the Gods and grew up in a rich and powerful family. Her Sumeru Mustard reserves were unimaginably rich!
"These are all pills that can be used to heal injuries, but their medicinal properties are different, so their effects are different," Mu Ning said.
She took out hundreds of Golden Pills from her spatial ring. Among them, there were Second Revolution Blood Paste Restoration Pills and Sixth Revolution Body Restoration Pills. Some of them exuded a pungent smell, while others exuded a sweet medicinal fragrance.
These Golden Cores might have some differences in their medicinal effects, but they were all able to repair the bodies of the eagles.
After receiving these Golden Cores, Luo Zheng turned to the Great Tribal Chief and said, "Will these pills be enough to convince the other Great Tribal Chiefs?"
The Great Tribal Chief firmly stared at the Golden Cores and nodded cautiously, "Yes."
To be on the safe side, Luo Zheng only gave 15 of the Golden Cores to the Great Tribal Chief. Luo Zheng would keep the rest of the pills. After arriving at the shrine in the desert, he would give the rest of the pills to the Eagle Tribe Chiefs.
During this time, Luo Zheng could only wait. During this time, Luo Zheng gained a deeper understanding of this world.
This world wasn't large. The endless desert in the center was completely ruled by the Sky Wheel Insects. The only suitable place to live was the surrounding area. After reaching the edge, one couldn't go any further.
From this point of analysis, Luo Zheng's judgment deepened. This world wasn't a space-time in the past of the Divine Realm. It was just inside the Imperial Jade Seal.
If the other Perfection Sovereigns had the same judgment as him, then they might also be heading towards the Sky Wheel Temple!
Of course, there was another possibility. Those Perfection Sovereigns had already discovered the Sky Wheel Temple and entered it ahead of time.
This Imperial Jade Seal had appeared after killing Shi Mi. The Imperial Jade Seal's ability was naturally related to time. That Perfection Sovereign of the Sword Tribe might have been affected by the Sky Wheel Temple and died in the desert …
Whoosh!
A purple light shot towards Luo Zheng.
Sitting on the roof of the Eagle Fort, Luo Zheng didn't even look at it. He just waved his hand and accurately caught the purple light in his hand.
It was a palm-sized purple fruit with a transparent shell on the surface. The transparent flesh inside was very sweet and exuded a faint fruity fragrance.
"Hurry up and eat!"
At the same time, a voice came from above.
Luo Zheng looked up. Mu Ning was standing on the back of a King Eagle, looking down at him from above.
Her originally white skin was as flawless as snow under the light. Coupled with her black hair, she exuded an almost infinite vitality. She was so beautiful that it made people's hearts beat faster.
After Mu Ning discovered Luo Zheng's method of making friends with the Eagle Tribe, she took out many good things to bribe them!
As a result, the Eagle Tribe found that Mu Ning was a hundred times more generous than Luo Zheng.
From her small spatial ring, she took out powerful divine runes, golden cores, and magic treasures with various uses …
Soon, Mu Ning received treatment that surpassed Luo Zheng. The Eagle Tribe even drove a King Eagle for Mu Ning to ride.
After some simple learning, Mu Ning could already control the King Eagle under her feet with the bamboo flute in her hand! During this period of time, she had been riding the King Eagle all around Eagle Castle. Every time, she would bring back some strange things and food.
The first to taste it was Luo Zheng. Fortunately, Luo Zheng didn't fear poison. Otherwise, he would have been poisoned to death.
"Wu wu wu …"
At this time, there was a burst of sad crying from not far away.
A woman from the Eagle Tribe was crying while hugging a boy.
Tomorrow, the Eagle Tribe would gather and head to the Heavenly Wheel Temple. At that time, these boys and girls would be brought along. This meant that the boy would be separated from his mother forever …
Whoosh!
Mu Ning jumped down from the King Eagle. Her toes tapped lightly. She circled around and sat beside Luo Zheng. Looking down at the mother and son, her pink lips were pursed. There was a sad expression in her eyes.

Chapter 1955
Since Luo Zheng had chosen to fight back against a Perfection True Divinity, he naturally had the mentality of cutting off all means of retreat.
In this heavy slash, Luo Zheng had used all of his strength, revolving the source of his strength to the limit that his body could withstand!
In this world, Luo Zheng was unable to use his Dao, so he naturally couldn't use his Emotion Severing Divine Dao. But in terms of pure sword technique, he wasn't weaker than Mu Xuerong. Moreover, Mu Xuerong was also unable to use her Divine Dao.
The power contained within this sword was extremely terrifying. The heavy sword swept through the air, forming a heavy pressure that slashed towards Mu Xuerong!
Mu Xuerong coldly snorted, a look of contempt on her face.
She almost reflexively flipped her left arm and raised her right arm.
Clang!
Her short sword and scimitar had already blocked the Great Thousand Sword in midair!
But this only lasted for half a breath of time before Mu Xuerong's expression suddenly changed. She felt as if she couldn't resist the power transmitted from the heavy sword. Her arms instantly became incomparably heavy, and her entire body rapidly sank. Her feet were buried in the yellow sand.
With no other choice, she could only withdraw her scimitar and short sword.
But in the instant that she withdrew her sword, she was wide open.
Luo Zheng seized this opportunity to close in. The Great Thousand Sword slashed vertically from top to bottom. Then, he took advantage of the momentum and thrust it towards Mu Xuerong!
This heavy sword leaned towards clumsiness and was good at slashing. It wasn't good at thrusting. But in this situation, Mu Xuerong found it difficult to block this attack. She could only choose to retreat.
How could Luo Zheng let Mu Xuerong off so easily?
Mu Xuerong rapidly retreated, drawing a thin line in the desert.
But Luo Zheng's heel suddenly erupted with a tremendous power.
Sand and dust flew everywhere, and a large crater appeared beneath his feet. Borrowing this tremendous power, he transformed into a sharp arrow and shot out. At this moment, his speed wasn't any slower than Mu Xuerong's.
"Give him an inch, and he wants a mile!"
Not to mention a lower True Divinity, Mu Xuerong didn't even care about some weaker Perfection True Divinities.
Within the rich and powerful families, there were only five or six Perfection True Divinities that could contend with her!
She was such a proud woman, yet she was being forced into a corner by a lower True Divinity. What kind of humiliation was this?
Even if she couldn't use her Divine Dao, she could still subdue this brat with just her moves and strength!
After retreating for about one hundred feet, she suddenly lowered her body, coiled her short sword around the heavy sword, and lightly flicked it. She was planning to use her mysterious and nimble sword technique to deflect Luo Zheng's heavy sword, then use her curved blade to cut off Luo Zheng's head.
In a battle between Gods, martial arts and movement techniques could not determine victory or defeat, so the True Gods did not pay much attention to them.
Even so, the body movement skills and martial arts techniques practiced by the Real Gods of the big families were still top-notch existences. At certain times, they could often exhibit great effects, such as the current situation!
Luo Zheng's strength was formidable, but he had been easily drawn away by Mu Xuerong. Mu Xuerong felt a trace of satisfaction in her heart.
But her complacency only lasted for a moment.
What Mu Xuerong didn't know was that Luo Zheng's weakness lay in his Divine Dao. He had never dared to use his Emotion Severing Divine Dao, and in this world, he was unable to use it.
But in terms of sword moves, he did not lose to anyone, including Mu Xuerong.
The heavy sword had just been drawn away by Mu Xuerong, but with a swing of the heavy sword, it returned to its original position with incomparable precision. The heavy sword was still pointed at Mu Xuerong's head.
If Mu Xuerong insisted on rushing forwards, before her curved blade could even touch Luo Zheng, the sword would have already pierced through her head!
Mu Xuerong's original attack had been easily dispelled by Luo Zheng's Great Thousand Heavy Sword. After she came to a sudden stop, she still had to dodge Luo Zheng's attack. She felt an indescribable discomfort.
She was extremely unwilling to accept this. After dodging Luo Zheng's attack, she turned around once more, changing her moves once more.
She never thought that Luo Zheng would still hold the Great Thousand Heavy Sword and casually point at her.
With this one point, he had completely seen through Mu Xuerong, placing her in a dilemma!
Once, twice, thrice …
After several times, Luo Zheng was like a calm powerhouse playing with the flustered and exasperated Mu Xuerong.
At this moment, Mu Xuerong felt as if she were about to go crazy.
Fang Henshao, Tang Wan, and the others crossed their arms. They stood on the hot sand and didn't attack. They were just watching the show.
"This boy surnamed Luo can rely on his own sword moves to contend with Mu Xuerong?" Tang Wan's face was filled with surprise.
Perhaps a Perfection True Divinity didn't rely on 'moves', but how many of those that could cultivate to a Perfection True Divinity could be lacking? Even if they were lacking, it was impossible for them to be worse than a lower True Divinity!
"What a mysterious sword skill. To use one sword to break ten thousand swords, Mu Xuerong was right. That boy most likely came from the universe," Fang Henshao lightly said.
Martial artists that could struggle free from the universe generally had decent talent. It could be said that these fellows that had relied on their own strength to become 'equal level life forms' would be highly sought after in any rich and powerful family.
But Fang Henshao and the others had a trace of disdain for these 'second level life forms' that came from the universe.
These second level life forms had a common problem. They placed too much emphasis on 'techniques'.
For example, sword moves, martial skills, movement techniques, and so on. These martial skills could only be considered 'techniques'.
But in reality, 'Dao' was far more important and powerful than 'techniques'.
The 'Dao' in the Realm of the Gods naturally referred to the 'Three Thousand Divine Daos'. In the universe, these second level life forms often focused on 'techniques'. Of the second level life forms that entered the Realm of the Gods, many of them were still immersed in 'techniques'. This was what it meant to neglect the fundamentals and focus on the details.
The Realm of the Gods' citizens had far more opportunities to come into contact with the Divine Dao than the second level life forms. Naturally, they didn't place too much emphasis on martial skills. Thus, when Luo Zheng had just entered the Realm of the Gods, the martial skills that the Realm of the Gods cultivated were too horrible to look at …
As Mu Xuerong and Luo Zheng were locked in a stalemate, the Black Bird Clan, Eagle Clan, and Roc Clan Chiefs suddenly became anxious.
These three clans had accompanied the Sky Wheel King Snake for many years and were familiar with its habits. That beast's temperament was extremely unstable. If they showed even the slightest bit of disrespect, it would be angered. Not long after, it would release one or two Sky Wheel Giant Worms to wreak havoc everywhere.
These three clans hated this Sky Wheel King Snake to the bone. But this Sky Wheel King Snake controlled the lives of almost everyone in this world. In front of the Sky Wheel Temple, they could only maintain absolute respect.
One of the Roc Clan members couldn't bear it anymore. He ran over to Fang Henshao and spoke in the Roc Clan's language.
Among this group of Perfect True Gods, only Fang Henshao was proficient in the Roc Clan's language.
After hearing the Roc Clan member's words, Fang Henshao frowned slightly. This Roc Clan member said that the Sky Wheel King Snake was naturally suspicious and extremely cautious. If it was startled, it probably wouldn't appear to collect the tribute.
Because of the dispute between Mu Xuerong and Luo Zheng, if things were to be ruined, the gains would not make up for the losses!
Thinking of this, Fang Henshao quickly walked towards Mu Xuerong.
Mu Xuerong's figure was like a blood-red shuttle. She constantly circled Luo Zheng, searching for Luo Zheng's weakness.
Her speed still had an absolute advantage, but every time she launched an attack, Luo Zheng's erratic heavy sword would always be able to wait for her to strike. She could only grit her teeth and retreat.
This time, just as Mu Xuerong wanted to rush forward, Fang Henshao grabbed her wrist with an even faster speed and pulled her back!
"What are you doing?" Mu Xuerong's face was full of anger as she angrily rebuked Fang Henshao.
"If you fight with that brat here, I'm afraid you'll startle that guy." Fang Henshao pointed at the huge statue of the Sky Wheel King Snake.
Mu Xuerong was determined to kill Luo Zheng. She coldly said, "If that's the case, why aren't you helping? Are you afraid that Dongfang Taiqing will make trouble for you? "
Hearing this, Fang Henshao glanced at Luo Zheng and said, "That's true."
Fang Henshao, Tang Wan, and the others did not care about Luo Zheng's life or death. As long as Mu Xuerong did not go crazy and cause a ruckus, it was fine. Although Luo Zheng relied on his own advantages to display decent strength in this world, with several Perfection Sovereigns attacking at the same time, Luo Zheng's strength was not worth mentioning.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was startled. His middle and ring fingers curled up slightly. Those two fingers were wearing the ring of the Sword Race's Perfection Sovereigns. If these Perfection Sovereigns really wanted to attack together, he would have no choice but to fight with his life on the line.
"Let's quickly help you deal with this brat. When we return to the floating island, you have to take out some benefits." Tang Wan slowly walked over with her large blade.

Chapter 1956
Not far away, Mu Ning crawled up from the ground.
Seeing this scene, her heart was incomparably tangled.
She was the one who understood Mu Xuerong the most. Once her sister made a decision, it could not be changed!
In the past, she would not have dared to try to persuade her sister.
But at this moment, she didn't know where the courage came from, but she rushed forward. This time, she stood in front of Luo Zheng.
"Sister, please … stop!" Mu Ning said through clenched teeth.
Mu Xuerong, Tang Wan, and Fang Henshao were already prepared to act, but they did not expect Mu Ning to run out again.
Previously, Mu Ning had blocked Mu Xuerong once, and now she stood in front of Luo Zheng. They could not understand.
"Did the little girl from the Mu Family eat the wrong medicine today?" Tang Wan laughed, not knowing whether to laugh or cry.
Fang Henshao did not speak, only looking at Mu Xuerong. Mu Ning was her sister, so it was up to Mu Xuerong to deal with her.
"Mu Ning! What's wrong with you? Why is there no end to today? "
Mu Xuerong's eyebrows suddenly rose.
In front of her, Mu Ning had always been very obedient and had never disobeyed her.
But now, this was the second time!
"I, I …" Mu Ning's face was filled with nervousness. After thinking for a while, she said in a trembling voice, "I think we can have a good talk!"
"Talk?" Mu Xuerong was stunned, and her face darkened. "Are you joking?"
"Hahahaha, so funny." Tang Wan laughed, and Mu Xuerong fiercely glared at her.
Luo Zheng looked at Mu Xuerong's slender back, his face full of surprise.
He didn't think that Mu Ning would stand up again and again to block his path.
Mu Ning gritted her teeth and said, "Even if he really is from the Luo Family, and is the son of Luo Xiao, he is not necessarily an enemy of our Mu Family. Why must we fight to the death?"
Hearing Mu Ning's words, Mu Xuerong's eyebrows furrowed even more. She stared at Mu Ning as if she were looking at a stranger. After a while, she revealed a helpless smile. "Mu Ning, when did you become so naive?"
"I'm not naive." Mu Ning shook her head and said with determination, "I just want to give each other a chance."
"Haha." Mu Xuerong was truly angered to the point of laughing. If Mu Ning wasn't her little sister, she would have already used her dagger to cut her into countless pieces. She sneered and said, "I'll give you a chance. Ask that brat if he agrees."
Mu Ning immediately turned her head to look at Luo Zheng, her eyes revealing a trace of pleading.
"Impossible," Luo Zheng answered decisively and neatly. His face was very calm.
The enmity between the Luo Clan and the Mu Clan could not be resolved. There was no possibility of peace talks between him and the Mu Clan.
"You …"
Mu Ning's eyes flashed with disappointment.
In fact, with Mu Ning's intelligence, she already knew what kind of reaction her elder sister would have. She also knew that the enmity between the Mu Family and the Luo Family would not be so easy to resolve. The conflict involved the disputes within the Saint Halls, and perhaps there were even greater secrets hidden in the depths.
But she still couldn't help but try again …
The smile on Mu Xuerong's face grew colder and colder. Her gaze passed over Mu Ning and fell on Luo Zheng. "How interesting. I don't know what you did to my little sister. The Mu Ning of the past was not so naive."
"Your little sister has taken a fancy to that brat from the Luo Clan, right?" Tang Wan ridiculed.
As soon as these words came out, Mu Ning's brows furrowed again. Mu Ning was like a child whose thoughts had been guessed, and a trace of panic appeared on her face.
Luo Zheng looked at Mu Ning's black waterfall-like hair, and his heart trembled slightly.
Then he let out a long sigh and said, "Three Perfect True Divinities besieging me. It's really an honor. But you seem to have picked the wrong place." As he spoke, Luo Zheng raised his arm, and his thumb hooked onto the silver ring on his middle finger.
At the same time, his finger pointed at the temple not far away, and a silver light refracted, appearing very dazzling.
Just now, Luo Zheng had heard the conversation between Mu Xuerong and Tang Wan.
Tang Wan and Fang Henshao had joined the battle because they were afraid that his fight with Mu Xuerong would alarm the Skywheel King Snake.
"The three of you, do you still want to fight?" Luo Zheng said indifferently.
When they noticed the ring on Luo Zheng's hand, their expressions changed.
"Strike Ring? Where did you get it?"
Mu Xuerong's expression became ugly again, and that strong premonition surged up again.
When Dongfang Taiqing had stopped him in the canyon, she felt that this brat's life was very good. Now, it seemed that her premonition was very accurate.
"Hey, Mu Xuerong! Is taking this brat's life really that important? "
"That's right! This brat left the forbidden area of the Sea of Time. How could he escape from the Sage Emperor's eyes? "
"If you want to fight, go further away! Don't alarm that snake! "
When the other Perfect True Divinities saw the silver sword-shaped ring on Luo Zheng's hand, they all spoke up to stop Mu Xuerong.
These Perfect True Divinities naturally recognized the two "Strike Rings" and understood the power of these "Strike Rings" very well.
If Luo Zheng really activated one of the Strike Rings and shot it towards the temple, then he would be finished. Who knew when he would be able to enter the Heavenly Wheel Temple again!
Mu Xuerong's chest heaved up and down. Her gaze was like a viper's as she stared at Luo Zheng. The edge of her short sword trembled slightly.
Even so, she still wanted to join hands with Tang Wan and Fang Henshao to take one less risk and capture Luo Zheng. She, Mu Xuerong, would never be threatened by anyone!
At this moment …
Fang Henshao reached out and gently pressed down on Mu Xuerong's short sword.
"What are you doing …" Mu Xuerong was just about to speak when Fang Henshao made a shushing gesture and pointed towards the temple.
Two dots of light flashed from the depths of the Heavenly Wheel Temple, and then a huge monster slowly crawled out.
It was a huge black snake, and the scales on its body reflected a gorgeous luster. It was the Skywheel King Snake they had been waiting for!
The moment the Skywheel King Snake appeared, the area in front of the altar fell silent.
Everyone's bodies stiffened.
The Mystic Birds, who had been making a racket, also quieted down.
The crying children were also frightened. Each of them opened their small mouths and stared blankly at the huge black snake.
The Eagle Clan, the Roc Clan, and the Mystic Bird Clan all knelt down and kowtowed to the Skywheel King Snake.
But how could these Perfect True Gods be willing to kowtow to this snake?
If it weren't for the fact that they couldn't use Divine Dao here, they would have already rushed into the Heavenly Wheel Temple and chopped this snake to pieces.
Moreover, these Perfect True Gods were also True Gods!
In their inner worlds, it was always others who kowtowed to them. When had they ever kowtowed to others?
"Ku Lulu!"
A person from the Roc Clan lowered his voice as much as possible and reminded Mu Xuerong and the other Perfect True Gods.
The Black Bird Tribe and the Eagle Tribe also signaled to the other Great Perfection Earthly Sovereigns and Luo Zheng's group. Their faces were filled with fear, afraid that the actions of this group of outsiders would anger the Skywheel Serpent Monarch.
These Perfect True Gods hesitated for a moment, but in the end, they still bent their knees.
This world was the home of the Skywheel King Snake. In order to enter the Heavenly Wheel Temple, they had no choice but to lower themselves.
Luo Zheng also knelt on one knee. He raised his head slightly and carefully sized up the slowly wriggling Skywheel King Snake.
Just like what the Eagle Tribesmen had said, this Sky Wheel King Snake was extremely vigilant. It carefully extended its huge body out of the divine palace, looking left and right all the way, as if it would shrink back into the divine palace as long as there was any movement.
Just as Luo Zheng was observing the Skywheel King Snake, he suddenly felt a gaze from the side. He turned his head slightly and met Mu Ning's eyes.

Chapter 1957
In just a few days, Mu Ning's personality had changed a lot, which surprised Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng agreed with Mu Xuerong's words. How could the enmity between the Mu and Luo families be so easily resolved? Mu Ning's thoughts were too naive.
"Weng, weng …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng heard a series of noises in the distance.
Luo Zheng looked up and saw a curtain of light flickering in front of the Light Wheel Temple. On the surface of this curtain of light, there were pitch-black spears. These spears contained a strange power, as if they had been condensed over the years.
"Black spears!"
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. He instantly recalled the words left behind by the Great Perfection Stage Sword Race's consciousness, "Be careful of those black spears." It seemed that the Great Perfection Stage Sword Race's Great Perfection Stage Sword Race's Great Perfection Stage Sword Race's Great Perfection Stage Sword Race's Great Perfection Stage Sword Race's Great Perfection Stage Sword Race's Great Perfection Stage Sword Race's Great Perfection Stage Sky Wheel Temple had perished because of these black spears.
But what kind of power did these black spears contain that could make a Perfect Circle sword cultivator die in such a short time?
With the strength of Mu Xuerong and the other Perfection Sovereigns, if they could directly enter the Heavenly Wheel Temple, they wouldn't have wasted time infiltrating the Roc Clan and the Black Bird Clan. It should be because they encountered a huge setback in their first attempt at the Heavenly Wheel Temple and had no other choice.
"So, Dongfang Taiqing also died to these black spears?" Luo Zheng thought, then he denied his own judgment. After all, Mu Xuerong had just mentioned that Dongfang Taiqing was looking for trouble with Fang Henshao and the others.
Today, Dongfang Taiqing didn't come with them. He was most likely injured by these black spears. Luo Zheng secretly looked at the snake as he pondered.
When the Sky Wheel King Snake walked in front of the light, it suddenly spat out its long tongue and made a hissing sound. Then, the curtain of light that enveloped the Sky Wheel Temple slowly opened.
Many of the Great Perfection Stage beings raised their heads as soon as the light barrier opened, and faint killing intent flickered in their eyes.
"Hiss ~"
The moment they released their killing intent, the Sky Wheel King Snake paused for a moment, and its huge body retreated back into the light curtain. At the same time, it raised its head high and scanned its surroundings vigilantly.
Seeing this, Fang Henshao waved his hand lightly, indicating that everyone should not be anxious.
Although these Paragons could not use their Divine Dao, they did not place the Radiant Wheel Monarch Snake in their eyes.
The only thing they couldn't break through was the light curtain around the temple and the black spears. Therefore, when they saw a crack in the light curtain, they couldn't suppress their killing intent.
None of the Paragons had expected the snake to be so vigilant. Their hearts sank slightly, and they lowered their heads again.
The Sky Wheel King Snake glanced around and hissed. It hesitated for a while before dispelling the doubts in its heart. Once again, it crawled out of the light curtain and crawled toward the altar.
The length of the Sky Wheel King Snake was unknown. From the Sky Wheel Holy Palace to the sacrificial altar, it was impossible to see its entire body. More than half of its body was hidden in the Sky Wheel Holy Palace.
"Hiss!"
When it reached the top of the altar, it flicked its tongue at the Eagle Race, Roc Race, and Black Bird Race below.
The chiefs of the three clans understood and carried the children up the altar, placing the frightened children on the altar. Then, they walked down the altar and prostrated themselves on the ground again.
"Wow!"
"I'm scared …"
"Wu wu wu …"
Most of the children were so frightened that they couldn't even cry.
There were also some children who, after seeing the snake, cried with all their might.
"Hiss!"
The Sky Wheel King Snake looked at the children, and its pitch-black eyes revealed a greedy expression. It suddenly spat out its long and thin tongue. This tongue was like a rope, coiling around the children.
Most living beings would be most focused when attacking or eating, and the Sky Wheel Snake King was no exception.
By the time the Sky Wheel King Snake swallowed the children and began to retreat, it would be too late …
Luo Zheng understood this principle. The Paragons also understood this principle.
At this moment, Mu Xuerong, who had her head lowered, suddenly shouted, "Go!"
She then transformed into a streak of blood-red light and charged towards the Heavenly Wheel King Snake. The dagger and scimitar in her hands quickly unfurled. As she leapt into the air, the two weapons were like a pair of wings, following her and drawing two beautiful arcs in the air.
Besides Mu Xuerong, Fang Henshao, Tang Wan, and all the other Paragons … all charged forward.
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate at all. With a surge of power under his feet, he created a huge sandpit and followed closely behind!
The Mystic Bird clan, Eagle clan, and Roc clan's clansmen all stood up, their expressions complicated and excited.
The three clans had been oppressed by the Heavenly Wheel King Snake for so many years. Every month to half a year, they would have to sacrifice their children to the Heavenly Wheel King Snake, becoming its food. In their hearts, they hated the Heavenly Wheel King Snake to the bone.
But their fear of the Sky Wheel King Snake had been passed down from generation to generation. They had no motivation or ability to resist.
However, these outsiders who had suddenly appeared possessed powers they couldn't understand. If they could really get rid of the Sky Wheel King Snake, it might be a good thing for them!
Among the Paragons, Mu Xuerong's movements were the fastest. Everyone only saw a streak of blood red light flash past, landing on the Sky Wheel King Snake's head!
"Pu!"
The short sword in her hand was like a nail, firmly piercing through the snake's head and fixing herself on it.
"Hiss! Hiss — "
Feeling the pain, the Sky Wheel King Snake raised its head high up in the air. It used its tongue to bind the children and dragged them high up into the air. Now that it was in pain, it threw the children away!
"Catch them!"
"Catch our children …"
The Mystic Bird clan, Eagle clan, and Roc clan's clansmen were shocked. Although the ground was soft desert, falling from such a height, even if they didn't die, they would be seriously injured!
"Chirp chirp …"
"Screech —"
At this time, the Mystic Bird, Golden Roc, and Eagle clan's clansmen all flew high into the sky, catching the children who were falling rapidly.
Only then did the three clan chiefs relax. They turned to look at the Sky Wheel King Snake again, their mouths wide open!
The invincible Skywheel Serpent King was rolling around in an attempt to shake off the intruders. However, the intruders were extremely agile. They circled the serpent up and down, launching attacks at it non-stop!
The scimitar in Mu Xuerong's hand suddenly pulled at the serpent's head, and her entire body plummeted downwards. The scimitar had created a 200 foot long wound on the serpent's head, and dark red blood flowed out from the wound like a waterfall. After smelling the thick scent of blood, her eyes became extremely excited. Just as her toes touched the ground, she jumped up again.
Luo Zheng kept rolling on the snake's thick neck. The Boundless Thousand Sword slashed out circular sword lights around him. Wherever the sword lights passed, the skin and flesh would be torn open.
The other Paragons also used their own divine abilities to severely injure the Sky Wheel King Snake.
If they could use their divine path, killing the Sky Wheel King Snake would be a piece of cake. Now, they could only rely on their own strength to kill it, which was still quite strenuous …
"Huff, huff, huff …"
Suddenly, the Sky Wheel King Snake raised its head high and slammed it on the altar.
"Boom!"
With a violent tremor, many Paragons were shaken off the snake's head.
Only Luo Zheng, Mu Xuerong, and Fang Henshao pierced their weapons into the snake's head at the critical moment, firmly fixing themselves on it!
After that, the Sky Wheel Serpent King dragged the few of them back to the Sky Wheel Palace.
After the Sky Wheel King Snake retreated into the temple, the gap protecting the Sky Wheel Temple began to slowly close.
"Chase!"
"Let's rush in before the light curtain closes!"
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
The other Paragons followed closely behind, charging through the barrier of light and into the temple.
The difference in strength between Mu Ning and those Paragons was too great. She did not have the terrifying strength and body of Luo Zheng, so she did not rush in immediately. Looking at the closing light curtain, she took out the bamboo flute in her hand and summoned the king eagle of the Eagle Tribe!
When the Great Tribal Chief of the Eagle Tribe saw this, he wanted to stop her. However, on second thought, he was afraid that they had already thoroughly offended the Sky Wheel King Snake. What was done could not be undone. Now, he could only pin his hopes on this group of outsiders. He hoped that they could really kill the Sky Wheel King Snake.
The moment the king eagle rushed towards Mu Ning, Mu Ning turned and jumped on its back. She grabbed the feathers of the king eagle's back with one hand and stared at the gap in the light curtain.

Chapter 1958
Luo Zheng gripped the Da Qian Heavy Sword tightly and firmly fixed himself to the snake's head.
Along the way, he was constantly bumped up and down. He was also constantly knocked against the beams at the top of the temple. Sometimes, he was even pressed against the ceiling …
However, with the strength of his body, these collisions did not cause him any harm. On the contrary, the roof of the Sky Wheel Temple was full of bumps and hollows from his collisions!
"Peng peng peng peng …"
Like Luo Zheng, Mu Xuerong, who was firmly fixed to the other side, was also in a similar situation.
Her fixed position was higher than Luo Zheng's. As the Sky Wheel King Snake constantly retreated, her head also smashed countless beams along the way.
Although Mu Xuerong was a Perfect True God and her body was much stronger than that of an ordinary True God, she still did not have a body as strong as Luo Zheng's. Moreover, Luo Zheng's body was protected by a layer of concentric clothing. Under the repeated collisions, she was also dizzy.
The flying dust and rubble made her face covered in dust and dirt. She looked battered and exhausted.
Luo Zheng, who was hanging on the side of the snake's head, glanced at her and revealed a faint smile. Mu Xuerong noticed it and angrily said, "What are you smiling at!"
Luo Zheng shrugged and the smile on his face became even wider.
She was just about to open her mouth and curse when, with a "peng", her head heavily smashed into another beam …
In comparison, Young Master Hen, who was hanging below, was in a much better situation. His Azure Edge Sword was nailed to the side of the snake. He stood on his tiptoes on the sword's hilt. Although the snake's head was constantly shaking, he was still as steady as Mount Tai, maintaining his balance.
Seeing Mu Xuerong's battered appearance, he could not help but laugh.
Mu Xuerong was immediately angry. According to her temper, she would probably flip over and climb to the other side of the snake's head and kick Luo Zheng down.
However, the speed at which the snake's head was retreating was too fast. If she was not careful and was thrown down, it would be difficult to catch up!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
The Sky Wheel King Snake continued to retreat into the depths of the Sky Wheel Temple.
The Perfect True Gods behind it also madly ran. The person at the very back was Mu Ning. Compared to the other Perfect True Gods, there was a large gap.
An entire incense stick of time passed …
It was unknown how long the Heavenly Wheel King Snake had retreated into the depths of the ground, but the surroundings were completely immersed in darkness.
Luo Zheng gripped the hilt of the Great Thousand Heavy Sword tightly and activated his Clear Eyes Spiritual Pupils to scan his surroundings. He discovered that he was still inside the Heavenly Wheel Holy Palace. He did not expect the Heavenly Wheel Holy Palace to be so huge. According to the distance that the Heavenly Wheel King Snake had retreated, the Heavenly Wheel Holy Palace that was hidden under the desert was probably hundreds of thousands of feet deep.
He wondered how such a huge building had been built in the desert.
About 30 minutes later, Luo Zheng sensed a wisp of light in front of him. With the help of the light, Luo Zheng saw that they had reached the end of the hall. At the exit at the bottom of the hall, there was a thick round pillar extending out.
Around the circular pillar, there was another world …
Rays of different colors were like strands of noodle, circling above, below, left, and right like a spider web.
"This is … the passage of time?" Luo Zheng was slightly shocked.
When Mu Xuerong and Fang Henshao saw this scene, their faces flashed with a strange look. Obviously, they were shocked by this scene.
They had entered the world of the imperial jade seal through the passage of time, so they obviously recognized the rays of light.
"Those worms …" Luo Zheng's eyes focused slightly.
Black worms were crawling on the strands of noodle like rays of light.
When Luo Zheng had entered the passage of time, he had watched as the two True Gods were swallowed by the worms. The black worm had even drilled out of the passage of time, wanting to drag Luo Zheng back. In the end, Luo Zheng had cut off the pair of pincers, and the black worm had no choice but to retreat.
"What is the relationship between these worms and the huge light rotifers?" Luo Zheng's eyebrows knitted slightly.
To Luo Zheng and these Perfect True Gods, creating a space was not a rare thing. Their dantian had a complete space, and the world within it was probably more complete than the world in the imperial jade seal.
However, in a normal space, time moved forward in a straight line. Even the controller of this world could only allow time to move forward, and could not reverse the world back to the past.
Now, it seemed that the world created by the imperial jade seal had a great connection with this world. That was why it was able to open the passage of time …
After the Heavenly Wheel King Snake's body coiled around the huge pillar, it finally stopped!
"Hiss —"
It stuck out its tongue, raised its huge snake head, and started to circle around the pillar. It actually wanted to throw Luo Zheng and the others out.
The pillar was surrounded by a void, and on the noodle like light rays, there were many black worms. If Luo Zheng and the others really fell into it, they would probably be stretched like noodles and eaten by those worms!
Luo Zheng, Fang Henshao, and Mu Xuerong all realized the danger. They did not lower their guard, and tightly held onto the hilts of their swords …
However, under the constant swinging, Luo Zheng gradually felt that something was wrong. The Da Qian Heavy Sword, which was originally deeply in the snake's head, was now slipping out inch by inch. If it slid to a certain limit, he was afraid that he would be thrown out along with his sword!
The Heavenly Wheel King Snake probably also realized this, and it swung its head even faster.
After swinging its head six or seven times, the snake's head suddenly swung downwards!
"Hu …"
Luo Zheng's Da Qian Heavy Sword slid out.
Almost at the same time, Mu Xuerong's short sword was also thrown out. She and Luo Zheng were thrown down together!
However, Mu Xuerong seemed to have been prepared for this. While she was in the air, she took out a chain from her spatial ring. The end of the chain was connected to the scimitar in her hand. Then, she suddenly threw the chain upwards. After circling around the huge pillar, the chain once more deeply embedded itself into the Heavenly Wheel King Snake's body, and her entire body swung upwards.
As Mu Xuerong swung back and forth, she passed by Luo Zheng. Her beautiful eyes stared at Luo Zheng not too far away, and her face revealed a mocking expression. "Go die …" She faintly smiled.
Looking at Luo Zheng, who was undoubtedly going to die, Mu Xuerong felt an inexplicable sense of security in her heart.
But then, she saw Luo Zheng fall 30 or 40 feet. His hand suddenly flashed with a silver light, and then a great silver sword appeared beneath his feet!
In a moment of desperation, Luo Zheng also remembered the "Strike Air Ring" in his hand.
His feet suddenly stepped on the hilt of the great silver sword, and an extremely powerful force erupted. His entire body shot upwards, and with a leap, he jumped onto the back of the Heavenly Wheel King Snake.
At the same time, the great silver sword transformed from the "Strike Air Ring" turned into a silver stream of light. It flew towards the void, and quickly shot into the noodle like light.
The moment the great silver sword shot into the light, it also turned into a silver light, and quickly flowed along with the light.
There was a black insect resting on one of the light lines. Unable to dodge in time, it was directly pushed by the silver light, and the insect was directly obliterated!
"What a tough life!"
Mu Xuerong had never imagined that Luo Zheng would use this method to return to the back of the Heavenly Wheel King Snake.
Without waiting for Luo Zheng to completely stabilize himself, she retracted the chain that was wrapped around the beam and the snake's body. Then, she stepped on the scimitar that was connected to the chain into the Heavenly Wheel King Snake's body. This way, she could ensure that she would not fall down. Then, she pulled the chain and turned into a red light, and rushed towards Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng was not to be outdone. The Great Thousand Heavy Sword in his hand lightly flipped, and the two began to fight again on the back of the Heavenly Wheel King Snake.

Chapter 1959
"Hold on for a while!"
Mu Xuerong said without even turning her head. She pulled the chain towards Luo Zheng, step by step.
However, the Sky Wheel King Snake's head was shaking violently. Luo Zheng and Mu Xuerong's back were relatively stable.
Luo Zheng kept sliding backwards on the Sky Wheel King Snake's smooth back, the Great Chiliocosm Heavy Sword in front of his chest. Facing Mu Xuerong's fierce attacks, he kept retreating. It was obvious that Mu Xuerong would not be able to take him down in a short time.
However, Fang Henshao, who was clinging to the snake's head, was in imminent danger. He was hanging from the long sword and could be thrown down at any time …
Just then, a few Great Perfection Real Gods appeared at the entrance of the pillar.
These Perfection True Gods were also aiming for the imperial jade seal. Now that they had entered the Sky Wheel Temple with great difficulty, how could they give up?
However, the Sky Wheel King Snake's retreating speed was too fast. Naturally, they could not keep up.
Fortunately, although the temple was huge, there was only one path. They only needed to chase it down.
"Young Master Fang!"
Tang Wan had just entered and saw this scene. Her bell-like eyes opened wide. "Don't worry, I'll save you!"
These Perfect Real Gods might not be able to use Divine Way, but they naturally had many magic treasures with them. Tang Wan patted her wrist lightly and a dark red crossbow appeared next to her. Behind the crossbow, there was a huge capstan. In the capstan, there were circles of pure gold ropes.
He grabbed a section of the rope and threw the dark-red crossbow towards the bottom of the pillar. After the red crossbow fell, it was pulled by the red-gold rope and swung back up from the other side of the pillar like a pendulum.
Tang Wan jumped up and caught the crossbow. She tied a dead knot on the golden rope and knelt on the ground with one knee, aiming at the Sky Wheel King Snake's head.
"Pu!"
A crossbow arrow flashing with red light shot towards the Sky Wheel King Snake, dragging the golden rope.
In the Divine Realm, Great Perfection Stage cultivators were rarely equipped with weapons like crossbows and arrows. However, the Thick Earth Divine Dao that Tang Wan cultivated was rather special. Although this Divine Dao was extraordinarily powerful, it was not flexible enough. Therefore, Tang Wan always prepared various kinds of magic weapons.
This "Fury of the Fire God" was an extremely powerful primordial treasure. The crossbow arrow it shot was enough to injure a Perfection True God.
"Weng …"
The thick crossbow string was still vibrating. The crossbow arrow, which was as thick as an arm, had pierced through the Sky Wheel King Snake's jaw and came out!
"Hiss!"
The Sky Wheel King Snake lifted its huge head and hissed. But the crossbow arrow had pierced its mouth.. The Sky Wheel king snake was unable to tear the rope, so it could not continue retreating..........
Fang Henshao had been hanging on the rope for a long time, and his heart was already palpitating with fear. When he saw the taut golden rope, he leaped and grabbed it, sliding down the golden rope.
.. "......................
However, his cultivation relied on the Divine Dao that he cultivated. After entering this damnable place, he had become the weakest person.
"Damn woman, you're just watching me die." Fang Henshao's face was ashen.
As the most powerful Perfection True God in the Fang family, he had always thought highly of himself. Why would he want to lose his life here?
"Hasn't that woman always been like this?" Tang Wan grinned. "Come and help. Fix this snake here. This is the only path in the Radiance Wheel Shrine. The core that controls the imperial jade seal should be at the end of this huge pillar!"
After saying that, Tang Wan turned her head to look at the other Perfect True Gods.
Not all Perfection True Gods were on the same side as them. Some Perfection True Gods came from outside the alliance.
"If you want to improve, come and help. Whoever can control the imperial jade seal will depend on their own abilities!" Tang Wan said loudly.
Hearing Tang Wan's words, the Perfection True Gods' eyes flashed sharply. Without hesitation, they all attacked!
At the same time, Mu Ning, who entered the light screen last, rushed over.
Compared to the other Paragons, her speed was much slower. As she ran with all her might, she gasped for breath. Her gaze instantly swept past the giant snake and landed on Luo Zheng and Mu Xuerong.
"They're still fighting …"
Seeing this scene, Mu Ning was helpless.
She looked around and saw that there were black insects crawling on the five-colored light, and her expression changed several times. She had also entered this world through the time passage. Naturally, she knew what those black worms meant. Unable to resist, even a Perfection True God would be swallowed up by those black worms.
Luo Zheng and Mu Xuerong did not notice the movements on the snake's head. The battle between them was getting more and more intense.
Mu Xuerong relied on the chain to stabilize herself. She seemed to be fearless.
Luo Zheng cruised on the snake's smooth back. On one hand, he had to ensure that he did not slip down. On the other hand, he was tired of dealing with Mu Xuerong's attacks. Naturally, he was tired …
"Ding ding ding ding …"
Mu Xuerong's body circled, and the short sword slashed out more than a dozen times.
When the short sword and the Great Thousand Sword collided, sparks flew. More than a dozen holes appeared on the Great Thousand Sword, and a crack spread along the sword's body.
Luo Zheng's expression became more and more gloomy.
The Great Thousand Sword was very heavy, but Shenzi Shenzi's ability was limited. It could not be compared to a real grandmist treasure.
Mu Xuerong's smile grew wider. Her face was like a delicate flower. "Luo Xiao must have spent a lot of effort to cultivate you. I wonder if the other stowaway is as good as you? Where did that chess piece go? "
"Dong!"
Luo Zheng pressed the Great Thousand Sword with the back of the sword against the short sword, forcing Mu Xuerong back a few steps. He coldly said, "None of your business!"
"It's a pity that everything is a foregone conclusion. Let alone a mere lower True Divinity like you, even if you were to become a Saint, you wouldn't be able to reverse the situation." As she spoke, the sword in her hand trembled, and the short sword once again slashed at the middle of the Great Thousand Sword.
"Kacha!"
The cracks on the Great Thousand Sword spread again.
"Saint?" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed, and a mocking smile appeared on his face. He shook his head and said, "Your vision is too small. There are some thoughts that you will never be able to guess!"
"What do you mean?" Mu Xuerong saw that Luo Zheng's expression didn't seem to be fake, and a puzzled look appeared on her face.
"You don't need to know." As soon as Luo Zheng's voice fell, the Great Thousand Sword swung out again. It floated in a strange arc and went straight for Mu Xuerong's neck.
Mu Xuerong sensed the direction of Luo Zheng's sword, and her figure gently floated, easily dodging Luo Zheng's attack.
But Luo Zheng faintly smiled. Suddenly, the sword turned and slashed at the chain under Mu Xuerong's feet!
"Clang!" The chain was directly cut off by Luo Zheng.
At the same time, the Skywheel King Snake's head was finally fixed to the pillar under the efforts of Tang Wan and the others.
After the snake was unable to move, it began to coil around its body!
Mu Xuerong couldn't stand steadily, and she slid down the snake's back …
"Puff!"
In a moment of desperation, she once again stabbed the short sword into the snake's body and hung it on the other side. As for Luo Zheng, he stood on the snake's back and pointed the Great Thousand Sword at Mu Xuerong. He lightly said, "There are some chess pieces that aren't so easy to remove."
As he said this, a dignified sword intent constantly emitted from the Great Thousand Sword.

Chapter 1960
After Tang Wan and the other Perfect Earthly Sovereigns secured the snake's head, Mu Ning was the first to climb up.
She never imagined that as soon as she climbed up, she would see her sister's life hanging by a thread. She could only throw herself at Luo Zheng and beg him to show mercy.
Luo Zheng stood on the snake's back, not a single ripple in his emotions.
This woman had tried to kill him time and time again. He had no reason to show mercy!
But Mu Ning throwing herself at him made Luo Zheng hesitate for a moment.
Just as he was hesitating …
Mu Xuerong pressed down on the short sword's hilt and flipped over. Her flexible body flipped in the air, and a blood-red whip swept towards Luo Zheng!
"Shua!"
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng was wrapped in the whip and pulled down!
"Ah!"
Mu Ning's face paled as she cried out in alarm. She subconsciously pulled Luo Zheng away.
The two of them slid down, and Mu Xuerong took advantage of the momentum to pull herself up onto the snake's back.
As Mu Xuerong and Mu Ning passed each other, the two sisters' gazes met …
Mu Ning's eyes were filled with shock and fear, but Mu Xuerong's face was calm without a single ripple.
After Mu Xuerong steadied herself on the snake's back, the blood-red whip in her hand swept down once more. The whip's tip was extremely fast, and it wrapped around Mu Ning's right arm. Mu Ning tightly held onto Luo Zheng, and the two of them hung in the air.
Luo Zheng looked down at the black bugs crawling on the light, and his face was incomparably gloomy.
"Mu Ning, let go!" Mu Xuerong calmly said.
Mu Ning tightly held onto Luo Zheng. She looked up at Mu Xuerong on the snake's back and shook her head. She stubbornly said, "No!"
"Then you'll die with him," Mu Xuerong threatened, her face expressionless.
This little sister of hers had once been obedient to her, but now she had repeatedly disobeyed her. This made Mu Xuerong furious.
However, in her heart, she still loved her younger sister dearly. She had only said those words to frighten Mu Ning. It was impossible for her to let Mu Ning and Luo Zheng be buried together.
Mu Ning's round eyes blinked and she lowered her head to look at Luo Zheng. Her heart was filled with regret.
She thought that she must resolve the conflict between her sister and Luo Zheng.
She also knew that her actions were incredibly naive, but she was determined to do the same thing as Mu Xuerong. She had to do it.
He had never thought that not only would he fail to resolve the conflict, but he would also cause Luo Zheng's death. The guilt in his heart could be imagined.
At this time, a carefree smile suddenly appeared on her face. She then said to Mu Xuerong, "Okay!"
With a light flick of her right arm, she actually managed to break free from Mu Xuerong's whip!
"Mu Ning!" Mu Xuerong's expression changed greatly. She raised her blood-red whip again, but it was too late.
"Hey, hey, hey …"
At this time, it was Luo Zheng's turn to be depressed. For better or worse, you should have convinced your sister. Why did you let go without saying anything?
From this angle, he was afraid that he would no longer be able to use the "Strike Sky Ring" to borrow strength. He and Mu Ning fell towards the light!
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng and Mu Ning were about to fall into the light. Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and he activated the last Strike Sky Ring!
"Sou!"
The Strike Sky Ring once again transformed into a giant silver sword. It drilled into the light, turning into a silver-white light that shot into the depths of the light.
At the same time, Luo Zheng and Mu Ning also fell into the beam of light. Their bodies were stretched by the power of time and transformed into another form. They turned into slender strands of noodles, and followed the silver beam of light as it shot forward.
In this passage of time, the most troublesome things were those black insects. If one encountered those black insects, there was no chance of survival.
However, the Strike Sky Ring's might was extraordinary. The Strike Sky Ring that Luo Zheng had shot out had turned into a silver light in this passage, directly crushing those black insects. Now that Luo Zheng and Mu Ning were following behind, the insects in front of them would also be crushed!
"Hu hu hu …"
In the blink of an eye, the two of them flowed into the depths of the cave.
Mu Xuerong stood on the snake's back, watching this scene in a daze.
"Pa!" A sound was heard.
The long whip in her hand fell, but she didn't seem to notice.
The Perfection Sovereigns had rushed over …
Fang Henshao was angry that Mu Xuerong didn't save them. He originally wanted to reprimand Mu Xuerong, but when he saw this scene, he only moved his mouth and didn't say anything.
Tang Wan and the others just shook their heads slightly. They were familiar with Mu Xuerong's character. She was too stubborn and didn't know how to be flexible.
