In fact, they all knew in their hearts that even if they left Luo Zheng alone, he wouldn't be able to survive after leaving the Sea of Time. Now that they had finally gotten rid of Luo Zheng, they had lost their sister. This was a big loss.
"Maybe he won't die." Mu Xuerong was stunned for a while before saying, "That kid is very cunning. He shot out a Strike Sky Ring at the end. With the Strike Sky Ring opening the way, those insects will all be crushed …"
"Maybe." Tang Wan pointed ahead. A few Perfection Sovereigns had already run away on the snake's back.
After the Sky Wheel King Snake's head was fixed, its body was still squirming. However, if they paid a little attention, it was no longer a big threat.
Mu Xuerong looked at the Perfection Sovereigns who had rushed to the front and snorted coldly. She grabbed the chain that Luo Zheng had cut off and pulled out the curved sword from the snake's body. Then, she rushed into the depths of the cave …
Luo Zheng and Mu Ning shuttled back and forth in the light at an even faster speed.
Although they couldn't move in the light, Luo Zheng and Mu Ning could see their surroundings. Naturally, they could see countless black insects living in the other light. Fortunately, these black insects were not in the same light as them. Even if they noticed Mu Ning and Luo Zheng, they didn't crawl over from the light they belonged to to to chase after them.
"I just hope that the Strike Sky Ring's silver light doesn't disappear too early," Luo Zheng thought in his heart.
This Strike Air Ring was a one-time use Magic Weapon. If the silver light dissipated after traveling for a while, he and Mu Ning would have to directly face those black insects. At that time, it would be inevitable that they would be swallowed whole.
However, Luo Zheng had activated the Strike Sky Ring in the desert. The power of the Strike Sky Ring gave Luo Zheng some confidence.
The Strike Sky Ring's silver light continued to open up a path in front of them, constantly crushing those black insects. After about the time it took to drink a cup of tea, Luo Zheng heard a "bang!"
The Strike Sky Ring's silver light turned back into a huge silver sword and smashed into the opposite wall, making an explosive sound!
Then, Luo Zheng felt his body become light. His elongated body began to shrink rapidly and recover. He was suddenly happy. He had gotten out so quickly!
"Bang!"
Then, Luo Zheng hit an incomparably solid wall.
"Ah!"
Accompanied by a shriek, Mu Ning also drilled out of the light, heavily smashing into Luo Zheng's chest.
Then, she tidied up her hair and took a few steps back with a red face. Then, she whispered, "I'm sorry. I didn't think that sister would …"
Luo Zheng just waved his hand. He didn't care about what had just happened. Then, he looked to the side and his eyebrows suddenly jumped.

Chapter 1961
Looking at the imperial jade seal, Luo Zheng's heart was beating rapidly. However, he did not take the imperial jade seal immediately. Instead, he observed it carefully.
The huge pillar in the middle of the cave extended all the way down and connected to the copper wall. It was the only way to get the imperial jade seal.
The lines on the copper wall radiated with worms carved on them.
"These lines are the space-time passageways in the surrounding void of the cave …"
Luo Zheng's gaze swept across the lines one by one. Very quickly, he discovered that one of the lines did not have any worms lying on it.
He turned around and looked at the surrounding void of the cave. He found a corresponding blue line!
"I see … these lines should be the only shortcut to enter the bottom of the cave," Luo Zheng thought to himself.
If this was the case, there should be some hints in this world that would allow the people who were trying to get the imperial jade seal to find the shortcut. Using the shortcut to enter the bottom of the cave was the correct way!
However, no matter if it was those True Perfections or Luo Zheng, they did not have the patience to search. Instead, they directly barged into the Sky Wheel Temple.
As for Luo Zheng, he used the "Striking Air Ring" to forcefully take a shortcut!
"It seems that it will not be easy for Mu Xuerong and the rest to pass through this huge pillar to reach the bottom." Luo Zheng glanced at the pillar.
Just as Luo Zheng had guessed …
A group of True Perfections fixed the Sky Wheel King Snake on the pillar. They stepped on the huge snake's body, which stretched for tens of miles, and advanced.
When it came to the tail of the serpent, a Great Perfection Stage cultivator was accidentally swept off the pillar by the serpent's tail. He fell into a beam of light, where he was shredded and devoured by the black bugs.
As they continued to advance on the pillar, they encountered continuous attacks. Even though they were True Perfections, they would die if they were not careful. Hence, their advancing speed was unusually slow …
After Luo Zheng made this deduction, he confirmed that the path he was taking should be the necessary step to get the imperial jade seal. At this point, there should not be any other dangers.
Then, he carefully approached the imperial jade seal and extended his hand toward it.
Mu Ning sat behind Luo Zheng and stared blankly at Luo Zheng's back.
According to Mu Ning's position, according to her previous personality, in the face of such an important Imperial Jade Seal, she would definitely snatch it away.
However, her experience in the Imperial Jade Seal caused her to have strong doubts about her own value.
Especially his sister's behavior just now …
Mu Xuerong was a genius of the Mu family. Her every action was for the sake of the Mu family. As long as she could achieve her goal, she would do anything.
Even if she was her elder sister's biological younger sister, she could still sacrifice herself!
If nothing unexpected happened, she would also walk the same path as her sister.
From the "True Killing Hall" of the Mu family's floating island, he received the teachings of the most outstanding True God of the Mu family and stood out from the other children of the Mu family. Every word and every action he took had strict requirements.
She would become a True God and complete her cultivation. Then, she would fight for a huge amount of points for the Mu Family, allowing the entire Wealthy Family to reach a higher level …
Her elder sister had already walked this path. Did she have to walk it again?
Mu Ning suddenly felt that all of this was dull and dull. Once she saw through this mechanical and repetitive pursuit, it rapidly devalued in her eyes. Her once firm values were like a collapsing mountain, collapsing with a loud bang. What replaced it was a relaxed feeling of doing as she pleased.
At this moment, her state of mind was incomparably free and easy. Seeing Luo Zheng slowly approach the Imperial Jade Seal, she warned him, "Be careful …"
Hearing Mu Ning's warning, Luo Zheng nodded his head.
The moment Luo Zheng's finger touched the Imperial Jade Seal, it was as if he had been electrocuted. He retracted his hand!
"What's wrong?" Mu Ning asked nervously.
Luo Zheng stared blankly at the Imperial Jade Seal. His face was deathly pale. He was so nervous that he couldn't help but gasp for breath. After a while, he said, "How strange, this Imperial Jade Seal …"
The moment Luo Zheng touched the Imperial Jade Seal, he saw countless giant insects!
The reason he used the word 'countless' to describe it was because the number of giant insects was so great that it made Luo Zheng feel fear!
Luo Zheng had ridden the eagle over the sea of insects in this world twice. The giant insects in the sea of insects had left a deep impression on him and Mu Ning. They had never been able to figure out how so many giant insects had survived. What did they feed on …
The number of giant insects in the sea of insects was unimaginably large. They were tightly packed together, and each one was over a hundred miles long. They were entangled together in an incomprehensible manner. Just looking at them would make one's scalp tingle.
But the moment Luo Zheng touched the Imperial Jade Seal, he felt as if he had entered the mind of every giant insect.
Moreover, he discovered that the number of giant insects far exceeded his expectations.
In the center of the sea of insects, there was a circular abyss. On the other side of the abyss was an even wider space.
In Luo Zheng's mind, that space bloomed with various colored lines. The range of these lines was so large that Luo Zheng found it hard to imagine. Perhaps the entire Realm of the Gods wasn't as large as this …
And on top of these colorful lines, there were countless giant insects crawling.
Giant insects with black bodies, over a hundred miles long …
Giant insects with purplish black bodies, over a thousand miles long …
And giant insects with purplish gold bodies were tens of thousands of miles long, or even millions of miles long.
What was even more frightening was that even the largest purplish gold giant insects were counted in the billions. These insects were practically all over the light!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng was frightened, so he decisively let go of the Imperial Jade Seal.
"Rumble … rumble … rumble!"
At this time, several violent rumbling sounds were transmitted. The violent vibrations traveled along the pillar to Luo Zheng and Mu Ning's feet.
Mu Ning's face revealed a panicked expression. "My sister and the others … are rushing over."
Luo Zheng glanced into the distance. Some black shadows were fiercely fighting with those Perfect Sovereigns. Luo Zheng could faintly see that in the hands of those black shadows, there seemed to be colorful sabers condensed from time fragments …
Luo Zheng nodded, his face flashing with unswerving determination. He had no choice but to fuse with the Imperial Jade Seal as soon as possible.
Clenching his teeth, Luo Zheng once again grabbed the Imperial Jade Seal, then sat down cross-legged with his eyes closed.
"Boom!"
In an instant, Luo Zheng felt his consciousness being sucked in.
At this moment, he could feel the consciousness of those giant insects.
The countless giant insects only transmitted one thought to Luo Zheng, and that was "hunger" …
Perhaps affected by the consciousness of those giant insects, even Luo Zheng had a feeling of hunger. In fact, Luo Zheng didn't need to eat now.
Luo Zheng's consciousness constantly drifted between the giant insects' consciousnesses. Soon, he noticed that in the vast world of light that terrified Luo Zheng, in the center of the world of light, Luo Zheng found a slightly smaller giant insect.
This giant insect was only a few dozen kilometers in size. Its brown body was inlaid with faint golden scales. The place where it was located, under the constant interweaving of the light, formed a circular light wheel. It was lying in the center of this light wheel.
After noticing that this giant golden insect was different, Luo Zheng's consciousness invaded it in an instant.
After passing through the Imperial Jade Seal, Luo Zheng's consciousness was almost unhindered. The giant insect with golden scales accepted Luo Zheng, and Luo Zheng immediately understood the information of this giant insect.
In addition to hunger, Luo Zheng finally obtained other information and instantly understood what these giant insects ate to survive …
… …
… …
"Rumble, rumble …"
"The weakness of these shadows is the eyeball in the center of their bodies!"
Several wounds appeared on Mu Xuerong's snow-white thighs. The blades in the hands of these shadows were condensed from fragments of time. The time cracks contained within the fragments were nearly unstoppable. When these Perfection Paragons were slashed by these blades, they bled profusely.
It wasn't just her. Tang Wan and Young Master Fang Hen were also in a very sorry state. They had forced their way here, so they were more or less injured.
"We're almost at the bottom of this pillar. Everyone, hold on!" Mu Xuerong loudly said. As her voice sounded, she gently waved her slender finger, and a small blood bird condensed in her hand. Immediately, it turned into a thin ray of blood light, and rushed towards one of the figures.
Along the way, the Perfection Paragons had taken out many magic treasures in order to kill their way here.

Chapter 1962
Kacha!
A black pearl in the center of the shadow was pierced through by the little blood-colored bird. After the pearl shattered, the shadow also quickly dissipated.
Whoosh!
The little blood-colored bird circled around and returned to Mu Xuerong's hand.
She smiled, but as several shadows flashed past, Mu Xuerong's expression changed. "Sneaking away?"
It turned out that the number of shadows was decreasing. Some of the Perfection True Gods couldn't wait any longer and rushed ahead!
With a tap of her foot, Mu Xuerong also turned into a beam of red light and shot towards the pillar!
As she went deeper and deeper, a bronze wall appeared in front of her. At the same time, Mu Xuerong saw Mu Ning and Luo Zheng below the bronze wall!
"Mu Ning!"
Mu Xuerong's face revealed a trace of joy.
After all, Mu Ning was her sister. Seeing Mu Ning safe and sound, she was naturally happy.
But as she looked around, her gaze fell on Luo Zheng. At the same time, she noticed the imperial jade seal in Luo Zheng's hand, and her pupils shrank.
"Imperial jade seal!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng was sitting on the ground with his eyes closed. Clearly, he was refining the imperial jade seal.
The other Perfection True Gods had the same expression as Mu Ning. After seeing the imperial jade seal, their eyes revealed a rich greed.
For this imperial jade seal, they had been busy in the Sea of Time for so long and had even entered the space of the imperial jade seal to search for it. Now that the imperial jade seal was right in front of them, who wouldn't be moved?
Whoosh!
Mu Ning pulled out two short swords and blocked in front of Luo Zheng. Her round eyes were full of sharpness. Facing several Perfection True Gods, she didn't retreat at all!
"Yo, this chick from the Mu family is actually helping an outsider. How rare." A Perfect Real God's face revealed a mocking smile as he looked at Mu Xuerong, who was beside him.
Mu Xuerong's expression was very ugly. Since encountering Luo Zheng, her sister's behavior had been very unnatural. She didn't know what had happened to her, but now, she was blocking in front of Luo Zheng. This made her feel that Mu Ning was very unfamiliar.
"What do you want?" Mu Xuerong shouted.
One of Mu Ning's short swords was held forward while the other was held backward. She was like a praying mantis that was ready to attack at any moment.
The sword techniques she cultivated, the martial skills she cultivated, and the Divine Dao she cultivated were all inherited from Mu Xuerong.
When Mu Xuerong saw her younger sister's actions, she only felt that they were incomparably childish. She shook her head and said, "Mu Ning, step aside. You shouldn't be involved in these matters."
"I …"
Mu Ning's voice trembled slightly.
She wasn't afraid of these Great Perfection Stage beings. It was just that she wasn't used to disobeying this domineering older sister of hers. Thus, she was so agitated that she couldn't control herself.
Mu Ning forced herself to calm down and said with difficulty, "I don't need elder sister to tell me what to do!"
"You!" Mu Xuerong's eyes flashed with a trace of anger. It was hard for her to imagine that Mu Ning would say such words to her face. For a moment, she found it hard to accept.
"No matter what your sister does, don't blame me for being impolite if you don't get out of the way," one of the Great Perfection stage cultivators said with a strange smile. The silver hook in his hand flashed with a silver light, and he was about to pounce on Mu Ning.
These Great Perfection Stage beings' goal was the imperial jade seal. Whoever stood in their way would be eliminated.
Not to mention Mu Ning was a mere lower True God, even if Mu Ning was a Great Perfection Stage cultivator, they wouldn't show any mercy.
"No!"
"Dang!"
Mu Xuerong waved her short sword and blocked the Great Perfection Stage cultivator.
"Mu Xuerong, control your younger sister. Don't find trouble with me." The Great Perfection Stage True God was obviously very dissatisfied with being entangled by Mu Xuerong. He waved the silver hook in his hand and said.
Mu Xuerong couldn't watch her younger sister be killed without doing anything. How could she allow this Great Perfection Stage cultivator to attack?
But she could only entangle one Great Perfection Stage cultivator. The other Great Perfection Stage cultivators took the opportunity to rush over!
"Whoosh!"
"Huff, huff …"
"Swish!"
A long sword, a golden hammer, and a large blade. All of them were top-grade grandmist artifacts.
These three Great Perfection Stage True Gods attacked Mu Ning at the same time and tore her to shreds. There was no suspense!
Mu Ning raised the two swords in her hands, wanting to block the attacks of the three Great Perfection Stage cultivators. Although she understood that it was all futile, an inexplicable surge of courage welled up in her heart. It was this courage that supported her in front of Luo Zheng.
"Ding, ding, ding, ding …"
Mu Xuerong unleashed a series of ferocious attacks, forcing back the great perfection cultivator who used the silver hook. As he turned his head and saw what was happening, his complexion changed. But, it was too late for him to save his little sister. He could only clench his teeth and threaten, "You …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng opened his eyes.
He saw that Mu Ning had helped him block the attacks of the three Great Perfection Stage True Gods, but his expression didn't change at all.
"Pu! Pu! Pu! "
These three Great Perfection Stage cultivators had all nine stars in their bodies. Even if they couldn't use their divine dao, their attacks couldn't be ignored!
Mu Ning's resistance was like an egg hitting a rock!
"Ding, ding …"
With two crisp sounds, the two short swords in Mu Ning's hands broke in an instant. At the same time, the broadsword and long sword cut into Mu Ning's body. Then, a heavy hammer smashed into Mu Ning's body.
Mu Ning's body, which was full of vitality, was in a tragic state in an instant.
"Sister!"
Not far away, Mu Xuerong stretched out her hand in vain, her eyes wide open.
Although she had cultivated to the Great Perfection Stage, she still had feelings for her younger sister, Mu Ning.
If it was for the greater good, she could choose to sacrifice Mu Ning's life, or even her own life. But seeing Mu Ning being killed in front of her eyes was hard to accept. Her eyes were full of sorrow.
When Luo Zheng opened his eyes and saw this scene, he was very calm.
He held the jade seal in his hand and gently turned it. A thought escaped into the jade seal …
Under his command, all the giant insects in the desert, the canyon, and the sea of insects began to stir. They began to frantically search for food.
But there was no food in this world that they needed, so these giant insects could only be anxious.
But following the cave at the bottom of the sea of insects, all the giant insects in the vast space, which were originally motionless like statues, began to surge. They began to crazily devour the light in the entire world!
"Devour for a breath of time, enough …" Luo Zheng's thought was transmitted to the minds of countless giant insects.
When this thought was transmitted …
Strange things were discovered in various places in the Divine Realm.
A True Divinity had just killed a vicious creature, but in the blink of an eye, that vicious creature had recovered to a state where it was unscathed.
In an arena, a Proof of Divinity martial artist had used his strongest move. He had clearly beaten his opponent to the point of vomiting blood, but in the blink of an eye, all the blood had disappeared!
In a restaurant, someone had swallowed a dish in one breath, leaving a lingering fragrance in his mouth. But in the blink of an eye, that dish had not been touched …
…
…
Such strange occurrences were happening in almost every aspect of the God's Domain. Even Saints were not spared from such occurrences.
The premise was that these things had happened in the instant Mu Ning was killed.
And Mu Ning, who had been killed, appeared in her original place, untouched. There was not the slightest wound on her body …
The three Paragons gripped their weapons and looked at each other.
Mu Xuerong's eyes widened. This scene was completely beyond her scope of understanding.
Mu Ning looked at herself in a daze. She did not know what had happened. Just now, she could feel those cold weapons cutting into her body. Why had she recovered to her original state in the blink of an eye?
Could it be time reversal?
Mu Ning quickly rejected this idea.

Chapter 1963
The moment Luo Zheng controlled those giant worms to erase that bit of time …
Outside the forbidden area of the Sea of Time, Dongfang Chunjun's figure turned sideways. His figure blurred slightly in front of him …
The female patriarch of the Blind Tribe also bowed slightly, as if she had avoided something.
The expressions of the other Sages and Quasi-Sages changed greatly.
"There seems to be something wrong with the time in the Realm of the Gods?" Leng Yao asked with a frown.
Tang Lun nodded. For the first time, he was somewhat silent …
The flow of time in the imperial jade seal was different from the outside world. Although nearly 20 days had passed since the Great Perfections and Luo Zheng had entered the imperial jade seal, the transit in the Sea of Time had not ended.
"A breath's time was stolen." Dongfang Chunjun's eyes were full of caution. His gaze was still focused on the imperial jade seal through the forbidden area of the Sea of Time. Fei …
"It seems that someone has obtained the imperial jade seal. Is this the ability of the imperial jade seal?" a Sage asked.
The female patriarch remained silent and did not speak.
They could not see through the interior of the imperial jade seal. Naturally, they did not know the situation inside.
Since someone had controlled the imperial jade seal, the result would be revealed soon …
…
At the bottom of the cave …
Luo Zheng stood in place. A hint of surprise appeared on his indifferent face.
Through the imperial jade seal, he felt that time had a new definition for him.
If the time in the Realm of the Gods was described as a book, then every breath's time was a page of the book.
Everyone felt the passage of time as if an invisible hand was constantly flipping through the pages of the book. Page after page, breath after breath, incense after incense, day after day, month after month, year after year, divine epoch after divine epoch. The pages were constantly flipping at a constant speed towards the future.
Those giant worms were the worms lying on the pages of the book.
From the scale of time, every page of the book was a complete but static Realm of the Gods.
Just now, Luo Zheng had used those worms to eat up this static Realm of the Gods.
They were like a group of greedy silkworms. In the blink of an eye, they had devoured a mulberry leaf cleanly. In other words, they had torn off a page of the book …
It was the page where Mu Ning had been killed!
This page contained the "reason" for Mu Ning's death, which was that she had been torn apart by the weapons of the three Paragons.
After this' cause 'was born, Mu Ning would die. This was the' effect '.
However, after Luo Zheng tore off that page, he also tore off the 'cause' that was recorded on that page. Therefore, the 'effect' no longer existed. Therefore, everyone could see that Mu Ning was not dead!
"You … what did you just do?" Mu Xuerong stared at Luo Zheng.
She naturally realized that the reason for the other effects must be because of the Imperial Jade Seal.
But Mu Xuerong was no saint.
The Sages were more sensitive to time. After they constructed the universe, they would compare the flow of time at different times. Therefore, Luo Zheng only used the giant worm to tear apart the time of a breath, but the Sages quickly noticed that a small piece of time had disappeared.
Mu Xuerong and the other Paragons could not sense this. The scene in front of them was beyond common sense. It made them feel incredulous and beyond common sense …
"It's nothing. I just tore off a page of the book," Luo Zheng said to Mu Xuerong with a smile.
Mu Xuerong said with a sullen face, "What nonsense are you talking about!"
"Acting all mysterious. I don't believe that I can't kill you!"
"It's just the ability of the Imperial Jade Seal. Kill him and snatch it!"
The Paragons became restless again.
Luo Zheng held the Imperial Jade Seal and smiled. "I forgot to say. With this Imperial Jade Seal, you can control this space-time. You … can you really kill me?" After saying this, Luo Zheng reached out and grabbed the back of Mu Ning's collar, gently pulling her back.
Mu Ning was still in a daze when she was pulled into the copper wall by Luo Zheng.
The complex patterns on the surface of the copper wall flashed, and the two disappeared in front of the Paragons' eyes, leaving Mu Xuerong and the other Paragons looking at each other.
"Damn, something strange just happened. I killed that ghost shadow, but he inexplicably recovered and even slashed me!" Tang Wan rushed over with a big knife and said with a grimace.
There was a deep wound on his chest, so deep that his bones could be seen, and blood was flowing out.
Seeing the blank expressions of Mu Xuerong and the others, Tang Wan was even more baffled. "Where is he? That kid surnamed Luo, wasn't he blocked by you … "
…
"Huff, huff, huff …"
The moment Luo Zheng and Mu Ning disappeared, they appeared outside the Temple of Sky Wheel. He grabbed Mu Ning and floated high in the sky, overlooking the world.
The space-time in the Imperial Jade Seal could be considered an independent space.
It was different from the universe. The universe was built by the saints, and it was integrated with their own efforts and understanding. It was also attached to the Divine Territory, so the Divine Path could be used in the universe.
But it could not be used here.
If one had to describe it, this was an independent time entrance, and the entrance was in the middle of the insect sea.
In the blink of an eye, Mu Ning was ten thousand feet high in the sky. The Temple of Sky Wheel below had become a small dot. She was shocked, and her waist subconsciously twisted and slid down. In a hurry, she grabbed Luo Zheng's hand.
Luo Zheng took her into his arms and looked down. The natives were still kneeling in the same place …
These natives knew that they had offended the Sky Wheel Serpent King, and they were afraid that they would attract the wrath of the Sky Wheel Serpent King. If the Sky Wheel Insects in the insect sea were released, whether it was the Eagle Clan, the Black Bird Clan, or the Roc Clan, they would be in danger of being exterminated.
However, they had no choice but to kneel in the same place and pray for the forgiveness of the Sky Wheel Serpent King. After all, these outsiders were not on the same side as them.
"Let's go down," Luo Zheng said faintly.
Mu Ning did not respond to Luo Zheng. Being held by Luo Zheng like this, all the muscles in her body stiffened, and a faint blush appeared on her face. Her eyes drifted away, and her cheeks and ears were already boiling hot.
"Hu hu …"
The rapid descent set off a burst of cool wind, which calmed her down a little. It was not until she stood on the desert that she struggled out of Luo Zheng's arms. Her heart was still beating like a deer, and it could not be calmed down for a long time.
When the natives saw Luo Zheng descending from the sky, their eyes widened, and their faces were full of disbelief.
"Big sister!"
When the little boy saw Mu Ning, he trotted over and threw himself into her arms.
These children were almost eaten by the Sky Wheel Serpent King, but because the Perfect True God intervened in time, they were spared.
However, it was precisely because of this failed offering that all the natives were rather uneasy.
A Great Tribal Chief of the Eagle Tribe came forward and asked anxiously, "What happened to the Sky Wheel Serpent King …"
If the Sky Wheel Serpent King had been killed by this group of outsiders, then it would have been fine. But now, only Luo Zheng and Mu Ning had slipped out, so they thought that the Sky Wheel Serpent King had eaten all the Perfect True Gods. If this was true, then the fate of their three tribes would be incomparably miserable.
Of course, the Sky Wheel Serpent King would not kill all their tribesmen. If it did, it would lose the food that the three tribes worshipped.
"In the future, you don't need to worship that serpent anymore," Luo Zheng replied.
"What!" The Great Tribal Chief was shocked.
The others also looked at each other in dismay. They found it difficult to understand Luo Zheng's words.
The Sky Wheel Serpent King had been subdued by the Perfect True Gods. Luo Zheng did not know whether they had killed it or not …
But Luo Zheng also understood that the Sky Wheel Serpent King was relying on the imperial jade seal in his hand to drive those strange insects out of the sea of insects to cause trouble. The Sky Wheel Serpent King was only guarding the imperial jade seal in this temple.
Now that the imperial jade seal had fallen into Luo Zheng's hands, the Sky Wheel Serpent King's existence had lost its meaning. Naturally, there was no need to worship that big serpent.
"I, I still don't understand," the Great Tribal Chief stammered.

Chapter 1964
"What!"
"The Skywheel Serpent King is dead?"
"Heavens …"
Whether it was the Eagle Race, the Peng Race, the Yang Race, or the Black Bird Race, all of them were shocked by Luo Zheng's words.
That damned serpent was the nightmare of these three races!
The reason for the deep conflict between these three races was ultimately because of the Skywheel Serpent King.
At the start, the Black Bird Race, the Peng Race, the Yang Race, and the Eagle Race didn't have much of a conflict.
But every so often, their children had to be sacrificed to the Skywheel Serpent King. No race could accept such a thing.
Thus, these races began to snatch each other's children to be sacrificed to the Skywheel Serpent King. Gradually, fierce conflicts arose between the three races, transforming into irreconcilable hatred.
If the Skywheel Serpent King was truly dead, this hatred would not disappear so quickly, but would slowly fade away with the passage of time.
One could say that the Skywheel Serpent King was the source of the hatred between the three races.
"Are you telling the truth?" the Great Tribal Chief asked.
This was only Luo Zheng's side of the story, and the Great Tribal Chief found it hard to believe.
Luo Zheng faintly smiled. Taking the imperial jade seal in his hand, he poured a thought into it and pointed to a nearby place. "Look over there …"
The Great Tribal Chief and the others looked over with strange gazes.
The place Luo Zheng was pointing at was nothing but a desert.
Just as they were wondering what was going on, they heard rustling sounds coming from the ground. A small sand dune rose up in the desert as if something was about to break through the earth.
"Something's about to come out!"
"Could it be a Skywheel Insect?"
"That's impossible. Insects in the desert don't appear during the day …"
These people stared at the small sand dune.
"Yoo!"
"Chirp chirp chirp …"
"Yoo!"
But the Black Birds, Pengs, and Eagles were extremely wary as they stared at the small sand dune.
Given the keen senses of these feathered beasts, it was most likely a Skywheel Insect.
"Huala …"
Just as they had speculated, more than one hundred Skywheel Insects emerged from the desert in an instant.
But these Sky Wheel Bugs didn't attack. They just stuck a part of their body out of the sand dune and stopped moving.
The Great Tribal Chief's eyes widened as he stared at the bugs and asked, "You're saying that you're controlling those bugs?"
"Well, I will control all the Sky Wheel insects in the desert and the sea of insects." Luo Zheng said and pressed down on the Sky Wheel insects. The Sky Wheel insects that emerged from the small sand dune immediately retreated back into the desert. Then Luo Zheng said, "I don't need your worship anymore. If you want to worship me, then worship me. Hehe."
The Great Tribal Chief and everyone else had a look of disbelief on their faces. After a while, their expressions turned into ecstasy. Then, they opened their mouths and roared, "Wow!"
"Huo!"
"Huo!"
"Huo!"
All the people in front of the altar roared.
They had endured this kind of helplessness for too long. Every once in a while, they would have to give their offspring to the Sky Wheel King Snake to be eaten. For all their clansmen, this was a kind of torture, a nightmare. Now that they had suddenly gotten rid of this nightmare, how could they not be excited?
Almost every clansman's face was full of excitement. This emotion infected the eagles, Golden Pengs, and the numerous Xuan Birds, and they also began to cry out.
Mu Ning held the little boy, her eyes fixed on Luo Zheng.
Before going to the Sky Wheel Temple, she had asked Luo Zheng how to save these children.
At that time, Luo Zheng had only told her to take one step at a time. She hadn't expected that he would be able to solve these problems unknowingly …
At this time, a figure slowly moved forward among the crowd.
That person's pace was very strange. Even if he was walking close to them, he didn't attract anyone's attention. He was like a wandering ghost.
Constantly shuttling behind the crowd, he quickly approached Luo Zheng!
"Whoosh!"
The moment he got close to Luo Zheng, that person's speed reached the extreme.
In a crawl, a transparent monk's blade appeared in his hand.
This transparent monk's blade had strands of purple lightning swimming around it. This monk's blade came from the "Heavenly Works of Hao Yue". Among the first-rate grandmist treasures in the God Realm, it was one of the top!
Although that person couldn't use the divine dao, at this moment, he fully displayed the power of the monk's blade. Combined with his own strength, he accurately and swiftly chopped at Luo Zheng's wrist. This full strength attack was almost unstoppable!
Even Luo Zheng couldn't react in time. He didn't even have time to use the concentric garment!
By the time Luo Zheng noticed this scene, the monk's blade had already chopped off Luo Zheng's hand!
"Puchi!"
In an instant, Luo Zheng's arm flew out along with the imperial jade seal.
"Ah!"
Mu Ning only had time to scream. Only then did she see clearly that the person was Han Jia's Perfection True God Han Tianxiao!
Not all Paragon True Gods would be able to find the correct path after entering this world.
Some Perfection True Gods found the Heavenly Wheel Shrine in advance. For example, the Sword Race's Perfection True God died in the desert.
Han Tianxiao also explored this time and space for a long time. After asking around, he found the Golden Roc Clan and then found clues about the Sky Wheel Temple.
But by the time he arrived, the Perfection True Gods had already entered the Heavenly Wheel Shrine.
Helpless, Han Tianxiao could only lie low.
He never thought that in the end, it would be Luo Zheng who took out the imperial jade seal from the Heavenly Wheel Shrine.
Also …
The Mu Family's little girl was actually hugging this kid, what was going on …
Seeing this confused Han Tianxiao, he decided to make a move.
Han Tianxiao had never seen Luo Zheng as a member of the Han Family. This kid was just an outsider. Whether or not he was Luo Xiao's son, Han Tianxiao couldn't accept him.
Just at the moment when Luo Zheng was the most relaxed, Han Tianxiao finally made a move, and it was a successful strike!
The moment Luo Zheng's arm was cut off, he was exceptionally calm. His body leaned back slightly, and then he immediately rushed forward at an extremely fast speed.
The moment he grabbed the imperial jade seal, Luo Zheng's thoughts were transmitted through the imperial jade seal again!
The giant worms lying on the countless rays of light began to move again … Once again, they swallowed for a breath's time.
Immediately after, Luo Zheng's figure flashed unnaturally.
The arm that was cut off was once again connected into one …
"What!" Han Tianxiao's eyes widened in an instant, staring blankly at this scene.
Luo Zheng grabbed the imperial jade seal with one hand, and at the same time, pulled Mu Ning with the other, and rushed into the sky. He hovered in the air and looked down at Han Tianxiao, his face full of awe.
He never thought that he would be ambushed by a Perfection True God, and by Han Tianxiao at that!
Luo Zheng didn't have much time to study this imperial jade seal, and he didn't know if it could recognize an owner.
If this guy really seized the imperial jade seal, he was afraid that he would lose everything.
Han Tianxiao didn't think that Luo Zheng's reaction would be so fast, and that he would have such a strange ability. His expression changed several times, and then he said, "Luo Tianxing! As a member of my Han Clan, you should give this imperial jade seal to my Han Clan! "
Hearing this, a sneer appeared on Luo Zheng's face. He stared at Han Tianxiao and said coldly, "If you thought that I would obediently hand it over, you wouldn't have ambushed me. Why do you still have to say such nonsense?"
Han Tianxiao narrowed his eyes, and then said, "If you give the imperial jade seal to me, I guarantee that the Mu Clan won't dare to touch you …"
But he was only halfway through his words when Mu Ning made a face at Han Tianxiao. "You're an idiot, so don't think that others are as idiotic as you. Making up such an idiotic reason, to think that you can even say it!"
Luo Zheng and Mu Ning both choked Han Tianxiao, and indeed, Han Tianxiao was choked speechless. The muscles in his eyes kept twitching, and he secretly lamented in his heart that it was a pity. Just now, that was an excellent opportunity, and he actually didn't grasp it …
Han Tianxiao thought for a moment, and wanted to say something, but Luo Zheng had already pulled Mu Ning away.

Chapter 1965
At the edge of this space-time …
A wide waterfall hung from the cliff like a waterfall, splashing down with a splash.
The surroundings were covered by a circle of emerald green jungle, and the edge of the cliff formed a natural semicircular terrace. The scenery was very pleasant.
Luo Zheng found this place under the guidance of Mu Ning.
Some time ago, after Mu Ning borrowed the King Eagle, the sky was high and she could fly freely, traveling to many places.
On the edge of the cliff, an ancient cypress grew diagonally.
Mu Ning sat on the trunk and took off her shoes, revealing a pair of snow-white slender legs.
She gently kicked out her legs and immersed them in the waterfall beside her. Countless crystal clear water droplets washed out from the waterfall, blooming on her well-shaped calves and insteps, as if they were decorated with a layer of pearl-like luster.
She looked at Luo Zheng sitting cross-legged on the terrace, her round eyes staring at Luo Zheng in a daze.
For a moment, she thought that it would be good to stay like this forever.
In this narrow world, there would be no more disputes.
The turbulent flow of the rich and powerful families, the scheming and fighting, everything was incomparably far away from her. She had put down the so-called defense, and her state of mind was incomparably relaxed.
Such days made her reluctant to part with them …
Once she left the world of the imperial jade seal, she was afraid that she would have to face endless troubles.
Luo Zheng separated a wisp of his soul and escaped into the imperial jade seal.
He naturally didn't know Mu Ning's thoughts.
On the contrary, Luo Zheng now needed to make sufficient preparations.
Once he left the imperial jade seal, he would return to the Sea of Time Forbidden Land, and after leaving the Sea of Time Forbidden Land, he would have to face a group of Saints.
Now that his identity had been exposed, how to escape in front of a group of Saints was the first problem he had to consider!
Previously, he was in a hurry to control the imperial jade seal and didn't have time to carefully explore it.
Those huge insects crawling on the light could devour for one to three breaths of time, which was indeed a very special ability.
But thinking about it carefully, this ability wasn't particularly strong …
At this stage, its effect on Luo Zheng was only to prevent him from suffering any harm for a short period of time.
If he used all his strength to urge those insects to devour for three breaths of time, any harm he suffered would be ineffective. Of course, it was the same for the others.
If those Upper True Gods or Perfect True Gods were to continuously attack him for more than three breaths of time, they would still be able to kill him. This was because the time that the Imperial Jade Seal could erase was less than the time that he was injured.
In other words, he tore off three pages of the Book of Time in succession, but he was still killed on the fourth page. In that case, he still couldn't escape death.
Since the imperial jade seal could control such a huge sea of insects, it should have more uses than that …
Luo Zheng was lost in thought with his eyes closed. His soul kept exploring the Imperial Jade Seal.
Previously, Luo Zheng had transformed his soul into the Imperial Jade Seal and quickly spread it out to connect with those insects.
This time, Luo Zheng tried to understand the inside of the imperial jade seal.
Soon, he discovered that there seemed to be a small film of light in the center of the imperial jade seal.
"Charge!"
With a thought, Luo Zheng's wisp of soul slammed into the light barrier.
"Dong …"
After slamming into the light barrier, the soul attack was blocked easily.
After pondering for a while, Luo Zheng changed his method. With the continuous entanglement of his soul, it turned into a sharp awl and shot toward the light barrier again!
"Puff …"
The light barrier was instantly broken.
After opening the layer of light barrier, Luo Zheng discovered three extremely bright spots on the side of the light barrier. Each of the bright spots contained a very special symbol.
The imperial jade seal was originally very small, and the symbols were even smaller, like dust.
After immersing his soul into the bright spots and merging with them, Luo Zheng suddenly had a new feeling.
"Those black spears … They are actually called Spears of Time."
Suddenly, there was a layer of light barrier surrounding the Temple of Sky Wheel in the middle of the desert. Then, all the black spears on the light barrier were revealed.
Following Luo Zheng's thoughts, all the black spears stood up in an instant!
The layer of light barrier looked like a huge round hedgehog!
Han Tianxiao was unable to track Luo Zheng and could only continue to search around the temple. For a moment, he could not find a chance to enter the Temple of Sky Wheel.
When he went around the altar, he suddenly saw a screen of light appear. Black spears were placed vertically in front of each other, and he was immediately startled.
Han Tianxiao had heard of the power of these black spears.
Because he had been to the Roc Clan.
Han Tianxiao was unable to learn the Roc Clan's language in a short period of time. While he was troubled, he found the heavily injured Dongfang Taiqing.
Dongfang Taiqing's strength was unfathomable among the Perfection Paragons. Even Mu Xuerong was wary of him. Who would have thought that he would be injured in this world? Han Tianxiao was shocked.
He saw that the flesh on Dongfang Taiqing's chest was already rotten, showing a grayish-white color. It was as if the wound had been rotten for countless years. This kind of injury was indeed very strange. Even repairing it was very troublesome.
After asking Dongfang Taiqing, he found out that he was injured by an extremely powerful black spear.
However, when he arrived at the Temple of Sky Wheel, he did not see the black spears. Because the Sky Wheel King Snake had already left, the layer of light barrier was also hidden.
He did not expect that at this moment, they would suddenly appear, and there were actually so many of them!
The corner of Luo Zheng's mouth slightly curved. He turned his head again, and one of the black spears began to continuously adjust its angle.
Han Tianxiao was still a bit puzzled. Frowning, his gaze followed the black spear.
In the instant that the black spear was aimed at Laughing in Heaven, it actually shot towards him!
"Xiu!"
"Fuck!"
Seeing the black spear shoot towards him, Han Tianxiao was shocked and hurriedly dodged to the side.
That black spear flew past his body, grazing the brocade robe he was wearing. By the time he crawled up, this brocade robe made of cloud gold thread had already turned to dust …
The spear itself did not have much power.
However, after being hit by the spear, the flow of time instantly underwent the baptism of endless years. This brocade robe could not withstand it and was naturally corroded by time.
"Pu!"
The black spear finally shot into a wall. That wall was instantly covered with cracks and then turned to dust …
Dust to dust, dust to dust. Under the corrosion of time, everything turned to dust.
Seeing this scene, Han Tianxiao's back was also filled with chills.
In theory, the lifespan of a true god was unlimited …
Because the body would not decay with the passage of time, that is to say, the body's metabolism was enough to offset the passage of time. When the two were in balance, a true god could live forever.
However, in an instant, experiencing endless years at such a fast speed, who could withstand it?
No wonder Dongfang Taiqing suffered such a serious injury after being hit by the spear …
It seems that Dongfang Taiqing limited the influence of the spear to a small area. If not, Dongfang Taiqing would not have been able to withstand it.
Just as Han Tianxiao was thinking about this problem, he saw the black spears hanging on the light screen move. They began to continuously adjust their direction and began to aim at him.
"What the hell …"
After cursing, he clenched his teeth and began to madly run towards the desert.
On the other side of this world, Luo Zheng had a faint smile on his face.

Chapter 1966
After fusing with the symbols in the Imperial Jade Seal, Luo Zheng's control over the space-time in the Imperial Jade Seal increased to another level. It was almost equivalent to controlling his own internal world!
At the same time, he discovered that there was a crimson light film in the depths of the Imperial Jade Seal.
On the surface of the light film, there was a small pattern of a black scorpion. Through this translucent crimson light film, Luo Zheng could see that there was a light spot floating inside.
He tried to use his soul to attack this light film. After several attempts, he was unable to break through it. He could only choose to give up …
With his current control over the Imperial Jade Seal, he might not be able to kill those Perfect True Gods, but they would not be able to leave.
The freedom of these Perfect True Gods would become his bargaining chip to safely leave the Sea of Time.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's gaze swept over Mu Ning, who was on the tree trunk. Seeing her pair of slender white legs that were somewhat dazzling to the eye, a trace of restlessness came from his heart.
Mu Ning also noticed Luo Zheng's gaze. She smiled slightly, stretched out her hand, and gently pressed on the tree trunk. Holding her shoes, she jumped up and stood barefoot on the semicircular platform where Luo Zheng was standing. At the same time, a handful of brilliant water droplets splashed towards Luo Zheng.
Facing the series of water droplets, Luo Zheng's thoughts moved slightly. All the water droplets suspended in the air, then gathered together in a spiral shape, turning into a small water knife that he held in his hand.
Seeing this scene, Mu Ning's eyebrows twitched slightly.
This kind of ability could only be completed in his own internal world. It seemed that Luo Zheng's control of space-time was very high through the Imperial Jade Seal.
"We're leaving," Luo Zheng said.
Although the flow of time in this world was much faster than in the Divine Realm, if they stayed for too long, the transit sun would pass.
At that time, the Sea of Time forbidden area would close, and they would have to wait for the next transit sun forbidden area to open. That would probably be eight years later …
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Mu Ning's heart jumped slightly, and she was silent for a while.
She took off her shoes and walked barefoot in front of Luo Zheng.
She was not tall, but her small body was constantly bursting with vitality. After looking up at Luo Zheng, her face was full of seriousness. "I want to ask you again. Is the hatred between our Mu and Luo families really unresolvable?"
Hearing Mu Ning's question, Luo Zheng was slightly surprised. His expression then became cold. "I can let Mu Xuerong's life go, but it doesn't mean I will forgive the Mu family and Mu.."
Mu Ning probably already guessed Luo Zheng's thoughts, so she didn't show any disappointment. She only said in a lonely voice, "Then are we enemies?"
"Maybe, maybe not," Luo Zheng replied.
She opened her mouth slightly, probably wanting to say something, but Luo Zheng had already turned around and waved his hand lightly.
A five-colored halo quickly spread out. That was the passage to leave this world, and only Luo Zheng could construct it now.
. "Let's go," Luo Zheng said in a muffled voice.
Luo Zheng's position was like a boulder. It was impossible to change it in the slightest.
Even so, he was still affected by the fluctuations in his emotions. His mood was somewhat melancholic …
Luo Zheng then stepped into the halo.
Mu Ning watched as Luo Zheng disappeared. The disappointment on her beautiful face became more and more intense. Then she closed her eyes.
After a few breaths, she let out a long breath and opened her eyes. All the emotions in her eyes were swept away.
These days, her understanding of Luo Zheng had undergone a drastic change, but it did not mean that her personality would change.
She was still the same as Mu Xuerong. Once she set her goal, she would not give up. If necessary, she would do anything. She would sacrifice everything except her goal.
"Is it really unresolvable?" Mu Ning pursed her lips and murmured. Her eyes, full of vitality, were dyed with a trace of darkness. Then she jumped into the halo without hesitation.
In the space-time passageway, Luo Zheng and Mu Ning were elongated again. They kept sliding along the light.
Not long after, Luo Zheng found a black bug lying on the light again. The bug crossed several light lines and went straight to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's mind moved slightly. The black bug stopped and crawled in the opposite direction. The bug in this space-time passageway was also under Luo Zheng's control.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng felt his elongated body return to normal. Immediately, he appeared in the canyon.
The statue of the Goddess, which looked exactly like Luo Yan, still stood quietly in the middle of the canyon. Because of the battle between the Perfect Sovereigns and Shi Mi, its surface had been destroyed and mottled.
Outside the Sea of Time Forbidden Area.
The Saints had been paying attention to the small imperial jade seal.
Previously, they had felt the fluctuations produced after time was swallowed. They knew that someone had completely controlled the imperial jade seal.
The Saints were all guessing who controlled the imperial jade seal. At the same time, they were also thinking of various countermeasures.
As the halo fluctuated, all the Saints stared at the imperial jade seal. It was time to reveal the result.
When Luo Zheng appeared on the halo, almost all the Saints were taken aback. Their wise and farsighted eyes were filled with bewilderment.
"How can it be him?" Emperor Hanqing's face revealed a look of surprise. At this moment, he even suspected that he had seen wrongly.
Aunt Hanjiu's face was also full of disbelief, but her eyes could not hide the surprise.
"What about the Perfect True Gods?"
"This kid is the first to come out?"
"The first to come out might not be the one who controls the imperial jade seal."
"That's true. How can a mere lower True God compete with those Perfect Sovereigns?"
The Saints did not know what had happened in the imperial jade seal. Being the first to come out of the imperial jade seal did not mean that he had really controlled the imperial jade seal.
Under the gazes of the Saints, Mu Ning also crawled out of the halo …
After leaving the world of the imperial jade seal, the imperial jade seal in Luo Zheng's hand turned into a ball of illusory light. With a slight thought, the imperial jade seal on the ground trembled slightly, then flew into his hand and merged into one.
Luo Zheng held the imperial jade seal and lightly shook it. The bottom of the imperial jade seal disappeared and turned into a small black snake pattern floating in his palm.
Seeing this scene …
The Saints outside the forbidden area of the sea of time fell silent.
Dongfang Chunjun's face revealed a trace of light as he looked at Luo Zheng.
"How is this possible?" Even the Saints who were usually calm in all situations could not remain calm. One of the Saints was flabbergasted.
"Hahaha," Tang Lun laughed loudly and said to Emperor Hanqing, "Your Han family has produced many geniuses! This child's future is limitless. Emperor Hanqing, you seem to be very willing to give him to Big Brother Dongfang. "
When Emperor Hanqing heard this, the corners of his eyes twitched.
No one would have thought that a group of Perfect Sovereigns would fight over the imperial jade seal, and in the end, it would fall into the hands of a lower True God. This was too unexpected.
Emperor Hanqing's thoughts turned quickly.
Luo Tianxing's identity was too troublesome. Regardless of whether or not he could obtain the imperial jade seal, he could not stay in the Han family.
But Emperor Hanqing was unwilling to give the imperial jade seal to Dongfang Chunjun just like that.
"This Luo Tianxing's future is limitless," Emperor Hanqing said calmly. "It's just that his background is a bit unclear. If he really is Luo Xiao's son, then we'll see how Great Sage Chunjun is willing to deal with him. However, he obtained the imperial jade seal as a member of the Han family, so it should still belong to the Han family, right?" Emperor Hanqing looked at the nearby female leader.
On the surface, he said this to Dongfang Chunjun, but in reality, he said it to the female leader of the Blind Tribe.

Chapter 1967
Causing trouble on the Blind Tribe's island wasn't a good decision, even for a Sage.
The female tribe leader was wearing a mask so her expression couldn't be seen clearly. However, her voice came faintly, "The Blind Tribe opened the Space Festival and invited these little fellows who are fated to enter the forbidden land of the Sea of Time. It is also their own luck and ability that they can obtain the opportunity in the forbidden land of the Sea of Time! That kid can give the imperial jade seal to whoever he wants. "
Dongfang Chunjun chuckled. "That's great. Brother Qing, I'm afraid you'll have to give it up this time!"
Emperor Hanqing's expression darkened.
If Han Tianxiao obtained the imperial jade seal, Dongfang Chunjun wouldn't have the face to say that …
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng's identity was so sensitive that Emperor Hanqing couldn't think of a way to deal with it.
It was like a piece of meat that was supposed to belong to Han Jia, but he could only watch it fly away. Even though Emperor Han Qing was a Saint, he was still very conflicted.
However, his state of mind returned to normal in the blink of an eye. With a slight smile on his handsome face, he said generously, "Supreme Sage Chun Jun's vision is indeed extraordinary. You have the Light of Insight. I'm afraid you already knew that this child could get the Imperial Jade Seal! I'll have to give it up this time. "
As he spoke, he shook his head, revealing a look of admiration for Dongfang Chunjun.
Dongfang Chunjun nodded slightly and looked at Qing Thearch with admiration. "There aren't many people in the divine realm that I admire, but you are definitely one of them, Qing Thearch. Han Jia's floating island will probably rise a few levels in the future!"
With Luo Zheng's help, Han Jia's floating island had risen a level.
If it continued to rise, it wouldn't be able to push the Dongfang Family down. It would only be able to push the Leng Family, Xuanyue Family, and the other families down. Dongfang Chunjun's words were very interesting.
Leng Yao, the Leng Family's Sage, looked at him with disdain. He didn't say anything.
Below the Sages ….
The True Gods who had entered the Sea of Time Forbidden Zone had already left the forbidden zone.
They couldn't see what happened in the forbidden zone, but they could hear the conversation between the Sages.
Dongfang Ning, Zhan Ming, and the other upper true gods were stunned.
"Did Luo Tianxing get the imperial jade seal?"
"He did enter the halo, but so many other Great Perfection deities went in before him!"
"That guy's luck is heaven-defying, right? Otherwise, what right did he have to obtain the imperial jade seal in front of a Great Perfection cultivator? If you show your face, you'll be killed! "
In the eyes of these True Gods, Paragons were simply undefeatable existences.
However, they didn't know that the space-time in the Imperial Jade Seal had isolated all the Taoist Connotation in the Divine Realm. The strength that the Great Fulfilment experts could display was less than one percent of their peak strength. They could only rely on brute force.
Luo Zheng's power was not at an absolute disadvantage when facing a Perfect Real God.
"Sister, Luo Zheng … actually succeeded?" Han Chuyue's delicate little face was also full of surprise.
Han Biluo also had a look of disbelief on her face, "That guy is really …"
She had seen Luo Zheng's extraordinariness in Hantian Mansion, but that kind of extraordinariness was far from being comparable to that of a Wealthy Family's disciple.
However, this guy had created one inconceivable miracle after another in front of her. Her little heart was shocked. She simply couldn't describe Luo Zheng's actions …
Han Liusu stood between the two girls, staring blankly at the exit of the Sea of Time without the slightest trace of joy on her face.
So what if Luo Zheng got the imperial jade seal?
How could he resist Dongfang Chunjun?
Listening to her father's words, he didn't intend to compete with Dongfang Chunjun for the imperial jade seal. He probably wanted to give Luo Zheng and the imperial jade seal to Dongfang Chunjun.
If Dongfang Chunjun had some interest in Luo Zheng before, the most likely possibility now was that Dongfang Chunjun would take away the imperial jade seal and then kill Luo Zheng. This kind of result was almost irresistible.
Han Liusu thought about it, and it seemed that she could only watch this happen without any power to stop it.
However, she still prayed silently in her heart. Luo Zheng was her miracle child. She hoped that Luo Zheng could perform another miracle for her!
…
…
In the forbidden area …
Luo Zheng brought Mu Ning back the way they came.
It was very smooth all the way back.
Those Time Wraiths should have been lured away by the True Gods who returned early.
The road leading to the Temple of Time was empty.
Luo Zheng didn't walk fast on this path …
He was thinking about all kinds of possibilities after leaving the Sea of Time.
Mu Ning followed Luo Zheng without saying anything. She followed Luo Zheng at a steady pace.
Not long after passing by the Temple of Time, Luo Zheng saw a piece of gold scattered on the ground. It was the gold left behind after the Thousand Beast Golden Luan Statue was cut.
Previously, in order to lure away those Time Wraiths, Luo Zheng didn't have a chance to retrieve them. At this moment, with a thought, all the gold pieces merged with each other and quickly turned into the Thousand Beast Golden Luan Statue.
This Thousand Beast Golden Luan Statue was originally given to the Evil Old Man as a physical body, but now was not the time to think about such trivial matters.
Not long after moving forward with the Thousand Beast Golden Luan Statue, they finally encountered a group of Time Wraiths.
After Luo Zheng stopped, he immediately whispered to Mu Ning, "Same old way. I'll lure those Time Wraiths away. You just have to follow …"
Unexpectedly, Mu Ning didn't care about those Time Wraiths at all. Instead, she asked, "Are you nervous?"
"Why should I be nervous?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"But we have to face those Sages," Mu Ning said.
Luo Zheng shrugged and didn't answer.
The Thousand Beast Golden Luan Statue beside him turned into a golden light and shot toward the group of Time Wraiths. When the group of Time Wraiths became restless and followed the Thousand Beast Golden Luan Statue, Luo Zheng shouted coldly, "Run!"
"Whoosh!"
"Whoosh!"
One in front and one behind, Mu Ning ran behind Luo Zheng!
After luring away the group of Time Wraiths, there weren't many obstacles ahead. The two of them took advantage of this opportunity to move forward quickly.
However, they didn't expect that just after they rushed out for a while, a large group of Time Wraiths would appear in front of them again!
"What should we do?"
Mu Ning also saw the group of Time Wraiths blocking the way and asked from not far behind Luo Zheng.
The Thousand Beast Golden Luan Statue had already lured away a group of Time Wraiths. If it turned to Luo Zheng's side, they would be surrounded. However, if they didn't lure away the Time Wraiths in front, they wouldn't be able to rush through at all!
"Charge!" Luo Zheng said calmly.
Mu Ning knew that Luo Zheng used the imperial jade seal and had an incomprehensible ability.
She didn't know how Luo Zheng did it. It seemed that no matter how serious the injury was, he could recover in an instant …
No matter what, she now trusted Luo Zheng's words very much. Even if those Time Wraiths were so terrifying, she didn't hesitate at all.
"Whoosh, whoosh!"
When they approached the Time Wraiths, the large group of Time Wraiths was like a group of headless flies, circling crazily, and then pouncing toward Luo Zheng and Mu Ning.
At this time, the Sages in the Sea of Time Forbidden Area also stretched their necks and stared at the scene in front of them.
They also knew the ability of the imperial jade seal. It seemed to be able to devour a part of time, but after all, they hadn't seen Luo Zheng use it with their own eyes. Naturally, they were very curious.
Seeing the large group of Time Wraiths pouncing toward them …
A thought flashed through Luo Zheng's mind.
"Devouring for three breaths of time …"
The giant worms lying on those rays of light began to wriggle, devouring these three breaths of time.
With a thought, Luo Zheng tore three more pages of time …
Under the gaze of the Sages, Luo Zheng and Mu Ning were almost inevitably possessed by the Time Wraiths, dragged into the cracks of time, and cut into pieces.
However, with a flash of their figures, they inexplicably rushed through the group of Time Wraiths and continued to sprint forward quickly.

Chapter 1968
The moment Luo Zheng activated this ability, Dongfang Chunjun frowned slightly.
There was a helpless look in his eyes, and his figure blurred again, avoiding the devouring of time …
"Sure enough, this kid erased a period of time!"
"The ability of the imperial jade seal …"
"Just now, I sensed the difference between the time in the universe and the divine realm by three breaths! I don't know if that's the limit of the imperial jade seal, but even so, this imperial jade seal is already very scary! "
The time in the universe flowed many times faster than the divine realm, but it was still easy to calculate how long the divine realm had disappeared. This was not difficult for the saints.
These saints looked at Luo Zheng with fiery eyes.
The ability of the imperial jade seal given to Luo Zheng was not very heaven-defying, but the stronger the person, the more powerful it was!
For three breaths of time, he was completely invincible. If the saints obtained it, they would often be able to use it to great effect. The space for imagination was almost infinite. It could be said that it was a treasure that became stronger when the opponent was stronger.
Moreover, this might not be the limit of the imperial jade seal's ability!
Emperor Hanqing forced himself to be calm, but there was still some unwillingness in the depths of his eyes. Who would be willing to give such a treasure of faith to Dongfang Chunjun for nothing?
But now he could only endure it, and there was not much room for choice.
…
The vast number of time wraiths chased Luo Zheng and Mu Ning all the way.
After running like this for dozens of breaths of time, the two finally rushed out of the main road of the Sea of Time Forbidden Land and rushed into the branch road opened by the Aqua Star in one fell swoop.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Those time wraiths did not rush in, but only crazily circled outside the branch road.
After the two rushed to a safe area, they stopped for a moment.
After these Time Wraiths hovered for a while, they swarmed away. Only then did Luo Zheng carefully return. He found the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue that had been cut into pieces along the way. After completely merging with it, he returned to the branch road again.
Following this branch road all the way, they came to the exit of the Aqua Star.
Mu Ning looked deeply at Luo Zheng and said in a low voice, "I wish you good luck …" Then, she took a step forward and left through the exit.
The moment Mu Ning left the forbidden area of the Sea of Time, she immediately felt the powerful aura of the Sages pressing on her. However, Mu Ning had seen many Sages since she was young, so she remained calm. She puffed out her chest and looked straight ahead as she walked toward the place where the True Gods of the Mu family were gathered.
"Little doll of the Mu Clan, why didn't Mu Xuerong come out with you? Why did you and Luo Tianxing leave the Imperial Jade Seal? "Tang Lun was the most impatient among the Sages. He lowered his head and asked.
Mu Ning looked up at Tang Lun and answered truthfully, "Luo Tianxing is willing to let me out."
"Why?!" Tang Lun's eyes burned with flames, as if he could see through Mu Ning's heart.
If an ordinary True God was stared at by a Sage like this, they would have panicked and lost their composure. Mu Ning just smiled gently. "Uncle Saint Emperor, he controls the Imperial Jade Seal, which is equivalent to controlling everyone's life and death. Naturally, it's not up to me to decide who lives or dies. As for why Luo Tianxing let me go, I don't know. Maybe it's because I'm pretty?"
Mu Ning was extremely smart. Although she was only half-joking, she directly blocked Tang Lun's doubts. The key was to tell all the Sages present, including Dongfang Chunjun, that the life and death of all the Great Perfection Stage True Gods in the Imperial Jade Seal was controlled by Luo Zheng!
In this way, the Sages would be afraid to attack the rat for fear of breaking the vases, and they could fight for a chance of survival for Luo Zheng.
"Hmph, the girls of the Mu family are all sharp-tongued. Your sister is the same, and so are you." Tang Lun snorted and stopped talking.
Mu Ning smiled slightly, tilted her head, and bowed to Tang Lun. "Thank you for your praise, Uncle Saint Emperor!"
Then, Mu Ning met up with the True Gods …
Just as Mu Ning turned to look at the exit, she saw a wave of fluctuations at the exit. The "Aqua Star" in the center of the nine towers rippled, and Luo Zheng's figure came out of the passage.
Everyone's eyes gathered on Luo Zheng in an instant.
The gazes of the True Gods were still fine, but the gazes of the Sages were like substance, forming a force of trillions of kilograms on Luo Zheng's body.
If it were in the universe, with just the gazes of these Sages, they could "see" a mountain range that stretched for thousands of miles into powder, could "see" the stars in the sky into fragments, and could even directly "see" an ordinary Lower True God into a pile of meat paste!
With such a large force exerted on Luo Zheng's body, Luo Zheng also felt his whole body become sluggish.
However, the strength of his physical body now was not inferior to that of a Great Perfection Stage True God. Coupled with the protection of the Concentric Garment, it did not cause too much damage to him.
As the nine stars in his Inner World rotated slightly, he actually resisted the pressure and took a few steps forward until a voice came.
"Little guy! Where's Tang Wan? Let him out. If not, I will extract your soul and refine it with True Fire! "Tang Lun stared at Luo Zheng and said coldly.
Previously, Mu Ning had already reminded him that Luo Zheng could dictate the life and death of the Great Perfection True Gods in the imperial jade seal. Tang Lun was impatient and naturally would not allow anyone to hurt Tang Wan. That was his most outstanding son.
Facing Luo Zheng, Tang Lun did not have the slightest scruple. He sat on the wide flame throne, and flames were burning in his eyes. The temperature in his gaze was also extremely hot, as if the flames were burning his body.
Not to mention a Lower True God, even an Upper True God would not be able to resist without using any means. Luo Zheng, on the other hand, remained motionless. This burning sensation was nothing to him …
Luo Zheng's gaze passed through the crowd and landed on Mu Ning. Mu Ning nodded slightly at him.
He knew that Mu Ning had deliberately misled the Sages.
"Saint Emperor, I don't understand what you mean," Luo Zheng said calmly.
"Arrogant! You dare to play dumb in front of me? You don't know what's good for you! "Tang Lun slapped the throne hard, and flames rose from the armrest of the flame throne.
The remaining power of the flames spread out and could easily burn the entire island …
Leng Yao, who was beside him, reminded him, "Brother Tang Lun, don't be impatient!"
As soon as he finished speaking, an invisible chill spread out and surrounded Tang Lun's body.
The chill was colorless and invisible. Everyone only felt a refreshing feeling. The flames on the flame throne instantly shrunk. It was obvious that the flames released by Tang Lun were extinguished by the chill.
"Tang Lun, be careful. You were invited to the Blind Tribe to participate in the Ling Sun Festival!" The Blind Tribe's female tribe leader warned.
"Humph!"
Tang Lun snorted coldly and did not say anything else.
Then, Dongfang Chunjun finally spoke.
Compared to the irritable Tang Lun, Dongfang Chunjun was much more amiable.
He smiled at Luo Zheng and gave people a feeling of being bathed in the spring breeze. He did not have the slightest arrogance.
"Your surname is Luo?" Dongfang Chunjun asked.
"Yes," Luo Zheng replied.
"You're Luo Xiao's son?" Dongfang Chunjun smiled.
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded again.
In the Sea of Time Forbidden Area, Mu Xuerong had almost confirmed his identity.
He could stir up trouble among a group of Great Perfection cultivators, but there was no way he could deceive the Saints here, so Luo Zheng openly admitted it.
Aunt Hanjiu sighed softly and sighed in her heart. This little guy really did have some of his father's upright character. However, in front of so many Saints, even if she wanted to take him away, she might not be able to …
She had already decided in her heart that she would take Luo Zheng away.
After reaching the Great Perfection level, greater teleportation would leave almost no trace.
However, Great Perfection cultivators in the Divine Territory could not teleport very far.
The distance between a quasi-sage and a Saint was very exaggerated. If she was given a chance, she was confident that she could take Luo Zheng away.

Chapter 1969
Luo Zheng openly admitted his identity.
The True Gods were in an uproar …
"He really is Luo Xiao's son!"
"No wonder he has such strength!"
"So what if he's the son of the Sage Emperor? Aren't you? Aren't I … "
At least 30% of the True Gods present were the direct descendants of the Sages.
If it was outside the Sea of Time, it would be rare, but it was nothing in the Sea of Time …
However, in terms of strength, the gap between these direct descendants of the Sage Emperor and Luo Zheng was still relatively large.
Han Liusu clenched her fists tightly, and sweat oozed out of them. She did not know what Luo Zheng would face if he really admitted it.
On the other hand, Mu Ning's face was calm. Her thin lips were gently pursed, and her eyebrows were also furrowed.
"Good, Luo Xiao's son is indeed upright!" Dongfang Chunjun praised.
Luo Zheng stood in place, and his expression did not change.
"If you are willing, I will not care about the conflict between me and Luo Xiao. If you join the Dongfang family, I will accept you as my disciple! How about it? "Dongfang Chunjun smiled.
Hearing this …
Many Sages had a strange look in their eyes. They did not understand what Dongfang Chunjun was thinking.
It was fine that Dongfang Chunjun did not know Luo Zheng's true identity.
Who would be willing to accept such a genius as their disciple?
However, this kid was Luo Xiao's son!
He was the son of Dongfang Chunjun's enemy.
Accepting such a guy as his disciple would be tantamount to raising a tiger and inviting calamity.
The True Gods also whispered among themselves …
"This guy is really lucky!"
"The Dongfang Sage Emperor is willing to let bygones be bygones and accept him as his disciple …"
"I thought he was dead for sure!"
Han Liusu stared at Luo Zheng. She hoped that Luo Zheng would agree. Although Dongfang Chunjun did not have good intentions, this was almost the only path Luo Zheng could choose!
However, Han Liusu also knew that with Luo Zheng's character, he would most likely refuse.
Dongfang Chunjun's words surprised Luo Zheng. He looked up at Dongfang Chunjun and said calmly, "It is a great honor to be accepted as a disciple by the Dongfang Sage Emperor."
Dongfang Chunjun nodded. His aura was still like a spring breeze, making everyone on the island feel warm as if they were bathing in spring.
"Many thanks for Dongfang Shenghuang's good intentions, but I'm not willing." Luo Zheng changed the topic.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, most of the people present stared at Luo Zheng as if they were looking at an idiot …
In their eyes, Luo Zheng was a criminal on death row waiting to be executed. Now that he finally had a chance to be pardoned, which was impossible for ordinary people to get, he refused without any hesitation.
That was simply courting death.
Dongfang Chunjun's face froze for a moment, then his expression returned to normal. He asked calmly, "Why?"
Luo Zheng cracked a smile.
The hatred suppressed in his body was released. He turned his gaze slightly and pointed at Dongfang Chunjun.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a faint murderous intent as he calmly said: "That year, you overthrew my Luo Clan and killed my father. When has there ever been a time limit for this hatred? I, Luo Zheng, as his son, must avenge him! How can I acknowledge a criminal as my master! "
His aura was far from that of a Sage.
Compared to a Sage, it was like the difference between the light of a firefly and the light of the sun and the moon.
However, within his aura, there was a rock-solid state of mind. No matter how insignificant it was, it could not be ignored.
The True Gods were dumbfounded.
[This guy is a little too bold …]
The only person who could be mentioned in the same breath as Luo Zheng was probably Yu Taibai from the Arena of Gods.
However, Yu Taibai was so unyielding because he controlled Mount Buzhou. If he hid in Mount Buzhou, the Sage Emperors really could not do much to him!
This Luo Zheng was a lower-level True God. Running in front of these Sages to provoke them was so arrogant that it made one's hair stand on end.
"Is he courting death?"
"Maybe it's like what Mu Ning said. He thinks that by threatening those Great Perfection stage Sages, the Sages will not dare to do anything!"
"Think about it carefully. If he can instantly kill those Great Perfection stage Sages, it will indeed be an unbearable loss for the wealthy families!"
The Sages of the Sword Tribe, the Xuanyue Clan, and the other wealthy families watched this scene with the mentality of watching a show.
As for the Sages of the Dongfang Clan, the Tang Clan, the Fang Clan, and the other wealthy families, they were slightly stunned for a moment before laughing one after another.
"Hahaha …"
"This little guy …"
"Luo Xiao is quite capable!"
In the eyes of the Sages, Luo Zheng was indeed extremely outstanding. However, his strength was completely negligible.
More importantly, before the Sage Mountain descended, all True Gods could only cultivate to the Great Perfection stage at most, and Great Perfection stage True Gods were almost not qualified to fight against the Sages. From this point alone, no matter how strong Luo Zheng's talent was, there was nothing to be afraid of.
Therefore, in the eyes of this group of Sages, Luo Zheng's words were like the words of an ignorant six-year-old child. It was extremely laughable.
There was no anger on Dongfang Chunjun's face. He still stared at Luo Zheng with a calm and relaxed expression and said, "Do you think you have the chance? Or are you confident that you can leave this place? "
If these Sages could let a mere Lower True God escape, it would really be the biggest joke in the world …
In front of everyone, Luo Zheng slowly raised his right hand. "I have a reason to leave this place!"
There was a small black snake engraved on his hand. That small snake was transformed from the imperial jade seal. "Tang Wan, Mu Xuerong, Fang Henshao, Dongfang Taiqing … The lives of these Great Perfection True Gods are not of any concern to the Sages, right?"
When Luo Zheng said this, the expressions of the Sages present changed slightly.
It was definitely not easy to cultivate a Great Perfection True God. Most wealthy families only had one or two Great Perfection True Gods. Almost all of them were the leaders of these wealthy families and had a very important position in the wealthy families.
"The spacetime in the imperial jade seal is already under my control. It's very easy to kill them," Luo Zheng said coldly.
Although he appeared to be calm and composed, he was still quite nervous.
The lives of those Great Perfection True Gods were practically the only thing Luo Zheng could rely on now.
"Young man, my Sword Tribe has no enmity with you. I don't care what you do, but you have to release Shi Jian first!" a Sword Tribe Sage said as he stared at Luo Zheng indifferently.
Luo Zheng cupped his hands toward that Sage and said, "When I entered the imperial jade seal, Shi Jian had already died. All that's left is a pile of bones."
"What!"
The Sages of the Sword Tribe were all shocked.
"If there's a chance, I'll return his bones," Luo Zheng said calmly.
Luo Zheng did not want to offend the other wealthy families on the floating island.
"Young man, if you dare to hurt Tang Wan in the slightest, I won't let you off," Tang Lun said coldly.
"That will depend on whether the Sage Emperors will let me leave. If I can leave this place safely, I'll naturally ensure the safety of these Great Perfection True Gods," Luo Zheng replied in a tit-for-tat manner.
