The other Sages all fell silent. Some of them looked at Dongfang Chunjun. After this young man gained control of the imperial jade seal, things had indeed become troublesome.
Were they really going to sacrifice the lives of so many Great Perfection True Gods for the sake of a lesser True God?
Seeing the reactions of the Sages, Aunt Hanjiu felt a little more at ease. She had not expected things to develop to this point.
Perhaps Luo Zheng could really rely on the lives of those Great Perfection True Gods to escape?
While Aunt Hanjiu was thinking, Dongfang Chunjun said with a mocking smile, "It's as you said. You do have the lives of more than twenty Great Perfection True Gods in your hands, but to my Dongfang Family, there's only Dongfang Taiqing. I, Dongfang Chunjun, have never been threatened by anyone, so I don't care!"
As soon as he finished speaking, the three Han Family sisters and Mu Ning's expressions changed slightly. Aunt Hanjiu's heart also sank.

Chapter 1970
Aunt Hanjiu's beautiful eyes quietly swept across Dongfang Chunjun's face.
Dongfang Chunjun could attack at any time …
Luo Zheng was able to exchange the lives of more than twenty perfection-stage Saints for his own escape. This move was almost perfect. For a Lower True God, this could be said to be the pinnacle.
Unfortunately, it was far from enough …
Aunt Hanjiu could only let out a long sigh in her heart.
At that moment, the originally dark sky began to light up.
The female clan leader, who had been silent all this time, calmly announced, "The transit is over."
The female clan leader didn't seem to care about Luo Zheng's life or death. Even if Luo Zheng obtained the imperial jade seal and was threatened by the Saints, she didn't say a word.
Whoosh … Whoosh …
The water channel that appeared in the center of the nine towers began to slowly dissipate.
The Saints' attention temporarily shifted from Luo Zheng to the nine towers.
"Now!"
A trace of decisiveness flashed in the depths of her eyes. She couldn't let Luo Xiao's son die here!
She tapped the air with the tip of her foot.
A purple circular mist spread out from the tip of her feet ….
"Great Purple Qi Art, Purple Sky Decimating Earth, Mist of Confusion!"
Before all the True Gods and Sages could react, the purple fog had spread to the entire island in an instant.
The mist transformed into circular spaces, enveloping all the Saints and True Gods within!
All the Saints in the air were suddenly shrouded in circles of purple mist. The moment they were shrouded, it was as if the laws of space around them had completely changed. Each circle of purple mist was an independent space, and all senses connected to this world had completely disappeared.
"Purple Sky Decimating Earth has descended? What is Secondary Sage Han Jia doing? "A Saint frowned slightly.
"Aunt Hanjiu, what are you doing!" Emperor Han Qing's expression changed drastically.
"Heh." A Saint only sneered.
This Mist of Confusion was indeed a miraculous technique that could even trap a Saint. However, the Saints trapped within it didn't panic at all!
Whoosh!
Taking advantage of the purple mist, Aunt Hanjiu appeared by Luo Zheng's side.
She grabbed Luo Zheng's hand and said, "Let's go!"
After saying that, he was about to use Greater Teleportation to leave this place!
If Aunt Hanjiu could bring Luo Zheng out of this place using greater teleportation, they would be able to escape from Dongfang Chunjun.
Success or failure would depend on this move.
Dongfang Chunjun also fell into the fog, but his expression didn't change at all …
Amidst the fog, he slowly raised his hand and closed his eyes …
His hand swayed gently in the rolling mist, as if he was aiming at a fish in the water.
When Aunt Hanjiu grabbed Luo Zheng and used the great teleportation, Dongfang Chunjun felt the fluctuation of space and immediately reached out to grab it!
Regardless of whether it was a Great Perfection or a Sage, there was almost no pause in using greater teleportation in the Divine Territory.
More often than not, the person would disappear with a single thought.
However, the distance that a Saint could traverse was many times greater than that of a Great Perfection Saint.
But the moment Aunt Hanjiu and Luo Zheng teleported, Dongfang Chunjun's grab actually hit Aunt Hanjiu and forcibly pulled them down. This method was very strange!
When Luo Zheng noticed that Dongfang Chunjun was grabbing Aunt Hanjiu, he once again used the ability of the imperial jade seal. With a thought, he urged those insects to swallow up all the time in the span of three breaths!
Dongfang Chunjun's face flashed with a cold smile, and the grab he made in the air became extremely blurry!
The span of three breaths was once again wiped out by Luo Zheng.
According to Luo Zheng's judgment, any damage that Dongfang Chunjun caused in these three breaths would not have any effect!
But this time, Luo Zheng's ability failed …
He urged those insects to tear up all the pages, leaving only a small blind spot compared to the entire Divine Territory. This blind spot was like cast iron and could not be devoured at all …
This blind spot was next to Luo Zheng and Aunt Hanjiu. It was the blurry claw print!
"Ka ka ka ka ka …"
Accompanied by the sound of space being torn apart, the billowing fog instantly dissipated completely!
Everyone immediately saw it clearly.
"Aunt Hanjiu!"
"Aunt Jiu …"
The expressions of Han Cangyan, Han Liusu, and the others changed drastically.
The eyes of the other True Gods were also full of surprise …
A black spatial crack appeared above Luo Zheng's head, and Aunt Hanjiu fell next to Luo Zheng. Her beautiful face was full of pain, and there were three more black claw prints on her waist. The claw prints were emitting wisps of black aura.
There was also a faint black aura emitting from Dongfang Chunjun's hand. He slowly retracted his hand and lightly shook his right hand. He looked indifferently at Aunt Hanjiu and smiled. "The Secondary Saint of the Han Family still hasn't given up on love? You're willing to sacrifice everything for Luo Xiao to save this little guy's life. What a surprise. "
Aunt Hanjiu half sat on the ground and spat out a large amount of blood. Her face was full of unwillingness, but her hand was still tightly gripping Luo Zheng. She smiled bitterly and said to Luo Zheng, "I'm sorry … I failed …"
It was almost impossible to escape from Dongfang Chunjun.
Luo Zheng had even used the imperial jade seal's help, but he still couldn't avoid Dongfang Chunjun's grasp.
Emperor Hanqing's fists were tightly clenched, and he frowned at Dongfang Chunjun. A trace of killing intent leaked out of his heart.
Dongfang Chunjun seemed to have sensed Emperor Han Qing's emotions, so he smiled at him with a relaxed expression. "Brother Emperor Han, what is the meaning of this? The quasi-saint of your Han Family wants to escape with this kid?"
Emperor Hanqing's face froze, and his clenched fists relaxed. He said awkwardly, "I, I don't know why Aunty Hanjiu is like this!"
Dongfang Chunjun laughed and walked slowly towards Aunt Hanjiu. At the same time, he said loudly, "It's not your fault if you don't know. But Brother Hanqing, you know my temper. I hate it when people disturb my business. Don't blame me if the Secondary Saint of the Han Family wants to die!"
Han Liusu and Han Cangyan stared at their father …
Aunt Hanjiu was the Secondary Saint of the Han Family after all!
Father wouldn't leave her in the lurch, would he?
Emperor Hanqing stared at Dongfang Chunjun, his body stiff.
Seeing Dongfang Chunjun walk towards Aunt Hanjiu, he nodded with great difficulty. "Of course, Great Sage Dongfang can do whatever he wants."
Hearing this, Han Liusu's lungs exploded. She couldn't help but shout, "Father! How can you do this! "
Han Chuyue, Han Biluo, and Han Cangyan all looked at Emperor Hanqing in disbelief …
Aunt Hanjiu and Luo Zheng looked at the approaching Dongfang Chunjun, their eyes filled with despair.

Chapter 1971
The Saints also looked at Luo Zheng coldly.
As peak figures in the Divine Realm, they hated being threatened the most. They were also happy to see Dongfang Chunjun deal with the quasi-saints.
Just as Dongfang Chunjun was walking toward Luo Zheng and Aunt Han Jiu, the female chief's body trembled.
The female clan leader's body trembled slightly. Without any movement, she appeared in front of Dongfang Chunjun.
"You promised that you wouldn't cause trouble during the transit," the female chief said in a clear voice.
Dongfang Chunjun stopped stepping in the air. His broad eyebrows furrowed slightly. "This is an exception."
"Every time you want to attack, it's an exception?" the female chief asked.
"Yes, so what? You want to stop me? Or do you think you can stop me? " Dongfang Chunjun stared at the masked female chief. His aura was like a spring breeze, but it faintly revealed a trace of aggressive aggression.
The female chief was silent for a while under the mask.
She controlled the forbidden land of the sea of time, but it didn't mean that she controlled the sea of time.
Of course, Dongfang Chunjun wasn't the master of the sea of time. It was just that she didn't have a good way to deal with Dongfang Chunjun now …
"Get out of the way," Dongfang Chunjun said indifferently.
The female chief could only move to the side silently.
But at the same time …
Somewhere in the Divine Realm …
Thick black clouds that stretched for millions of miles covered the sky.
Even the strongest and sharpest sunlight couldn't penetrate these black clouds.
If it was during the day, these black clouds would gather together and remain still …
At night, the black clouds would come to life.
Thousands of souls would awaken. They screamed, rolled, tangled, and tore apart in the clouds. It was like this for one Divine Era, and it was also like this for 100 Divine Eras.
The stronger souls could devour the smaller souls, while the stronger souls could devour everything.
The most primitive law of the jungle was vividly displayed in these thick clouds.
Occasionally, there would be powerful spirit bodies that would fall from the black clouds after becoming sufficiently strong. However, facing these powerful spirit bodies would be another trial of slaughter. In the Soul Desolate, there was no lack of powerful spirit bodies …
Accompanied by the huge and thick black clouds, it stretched into the depths of the Soul Wilderness.
There was a huge black shadow.
It was the "Wilderness" giant.
It was a form completely constructed by the soul.
They had their own intelligence and abilities that belonged to the soul.
Even in the extremely cruel Soul Desolation, the Desolate Giants could still stand at the top of the food chain.
But at this moment, the Wild Giant was half-kneeling on the ground, carefully lifting its shoulder …
A woman with a graceful figure was lying on his broad shoulders.
She was wearing a light green dress that covered her shoulders all the way to her snow-white thighs. The extremely tight dress accentuated her figure completely. The Desolate Giant's broad shoulders emitted traces of black aura, covering half of her snow-white and slender legs. It was as if she was sleeping in a soft black cloud. She looked lazy and tired, beautiful and enchanting.
That was the controller of the Soul Desolation, Shi Qiaoqiao.
She had been lying on the giant's shoulders for some time.
Through the Desolate Giant's powerful soul, her own soul was continuously nourished. This feeling made her feel peaceful, and she fell into a deep sleep.
At this moment.
Shi Xiaoqiao suddenly opened her eyes.
That extremely mysterious aura was also emitted from her extremely beautiful eyes.
In the depths of those eyes, there was a hint of surprise, and there was even a trace of a bitter smile on her face.
"Cute little guy … his ability to cause trouble is really beyond my imagination!" She sighed.
"Gulp …"
A strange sound came from the Desolate Giant's throat.
Shi Xiaoqiao gently patted the Desolate Giant. "I'm not talking about you!"
She had never expected that not long after she left the Takiguan Forest, Luo Zheng would rush into the sea of time.
With Luo Zheng's talent …
Entering the sea of time was not something that could not be figured out.
But in such a short time, he had provoked Dongfang Chunjun, which inevitably made Shi Xiaoqiao look askance.
She leaned on the Desolate Giant's shoulder and gently raised one of her white arms. A scepter that flickered with orange light appeared in her hand.
That was the "Scepter of Silence of the Gods".
"Wuya –"
"Ga –"
"Wuwuwu –"
The sky of the Soul Desolation was originally extremely noisy.
Those damned souls fought day and night, and the sound they emitted was very ear-piercing, making people irritated.
When the orange scepter appeared in Shi Xiaoqiao's hand.
All the sounds came to an abrupt halt.
Waves of awe, uneasiness, and fear spread out.
Those emotions were emitted from pure soul bodies, and they were not concealed in the slightest.
Shi Xiaoqiao held the scepter in one hand, and at the same time, she placed two white fingers that were like scallions on the side of her sparkling pink lips.
Her pale vermilion lips opened slightly, and then she blew out a breath.
"Shh – don't talk …"
In an instant …
An invisible force spread out at an incredible speed.
The spreading speed was beyond common sense. In less than half a breath, it covered half of the Divine Realm, and after a breath, it covered the entire Divine Realm.
The entire Divine Realm fell silent!
In the entire Divine Realm, all the Gods, all the True Gods, and all the living beings became mute.
The sound of the tide hitting the shore …
The buzzing of insects …
The rustling of the wind blowing through the trees …
The rain splashed down along the eaves, and the dripping sound …
The sound of talking, sighing, exploding …
All the sounds disappeared.
An upper-level True God flew away in the air.
In an instant, he felt that he could no longer use the power in his body, and he was rapidly falling down. His face turned pale with fright, and he cried out in alarm, but he found that he could not make a sound. He thought that he had become deaf!
Not only him, but everyone in the Divine Realm thought that they had gone deaf.
Because all the sounds had disappeared.
All the True Gods were unable to use any abilities.
All the Divine Rune Arrays were also suppressed …
The Soaring Sky Array on the floating island floating on the Sea of Time was also silenced. The energy within it could no longer be used, and it began to rapidly fall down.
On the Blind Tribe's island …
The Sages who were originally floating in mid-air also fell from mid-air.
Most of the Sages maintained their standing posture, and even after being silenced, their landing posture was graceful and stable.
Tang Lun lay lazily on the wide Blazing Flame Throne.
The moment he was silenced, the layer of warm flames surrounding him suddenly disappeared.
His bell-sized eyes suddenly widened, and he could not help but let out an "oh" sound, but he found that no sound came from his throat.
Before he could fully react, his entire body involuntarily fell down. His wide and thick body sprawled out, smashing a large hole in the ground.
Not far away, Mu Ning, Han Liusu, and the group of True Gods saw this scene, and they were so shocked that their eyeballs almost fell out. It was really unexpected that a Sage would fall so miserably. They all wanted to laugh but did not dare to, and could only force themselves to hold it in.
Dongfang Chunjun stood steadily on the ground.
In an instant, he understood what had happened. In the universe he had constructed, nine stars were connected together like candied haws. When the nine stars transformed into this form, they could gather power at the fastest speed!
If the power of faith was silenced, Dongfang Chunjun would charge up and attack with pure power.
Although Dongfang Chunjun's reaction was quite fast, the moment Shi Xiaoqiao raised the Scepter of Silence of the Gods, the small petal between Luo Zheng's eyebrows had already spread out.
The petal formed a unique diamond-shaped pattern that covered a small area, and this area just happened to include Aunt Hanjiu and Luo Zheng.
In the entire Divine Realm, perhaps only this small area was not silenced.
In her despair, Aunt Hanjiu did not even think that Luo Zheng would have such a backup plan!
However, the moment the Sages fell rapidly, Aunt Hanjiu had already reacted. Seeing Dongfang Chunjun erupting with power and rushing over, she once again grabbed Luo Zheng, and did not forget to glance at Tang Lun, who had fallen miserably not far away. She covered her red lips with one hand, and there was a trace of uncontrollable laughter in her voice. "Let's go!"
Whoosh …

Chapter 1972
Dongfang Chunjun's grab was extremely powerful.
However, the heavily injured Aunt Han Jiu still dragged Luo Zheng out of the Sea of Time in an extremely light manner, appearing at a certain seaside in the Sea of Time.
The petal between Luo Zheng's eyebrows was still shining, forming a diamond-shaped pattern that enveloped the two.
"Continue!"
"Hu …"
Without any hesitation, Aunt Han Jiu took Luo Zheng and left again.
It was just one shift, but it was still possible for the Saints' divine sense to lock onto the spatial ripples and deduce the approximate direction and location of the shift.
However, under the silence of the entire divine realm, this possibility was very small.
However, Dongfang Chunjun had countless backup plans, and Aunt Han Jiu grasped this chance to escape, so she naturally wouldn't be careless.
In just that period of "silence," Aunt Han Jiu continuously shifted as many as four times!
Shi Qiao didn't make the entire divine realm fall into silence for long.
In total, it was only one and a half breaths of time.
The floating islands in the Sea of Time all fell in this one and a half breaths of time.
The Luo Family's floating island at the bottom was even close to the surface of the Sea of Time …
However, after the silence passed, the divine patterns hidden inside the floating islands began to operate again, and all the floating islands began to rise slowly. The True Gods on the floating islands also let out a long breath.
Once the floating islands fell into the Sea of Time, the consequences would be unimaginable.
After the silence passed, the vast space of the divine realm returned to normal …
One and a half breaths of time wasn't long, but it still caused quite a bit of chaos.
On the island, Dongfang Chunjun stood in place.
The power drawn from the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Nine Stars kept rolling in his body, and although that power wasn't released, the powerful force had already bent the space around him. Bystanders looked at him and felt that his body was a bit distorted.
Although the silence had passed, the atmosphere on the island was extremely gloomy.
Emperor Han Qing's eyes narrowed slightly, and his expression became relaxed …
As for Tang Lun, Leng Yao, and the other Saints who got up from the ground, their expressions were very ugly.
Not only did that brat run away, but he also took the imperial jade seal and imprisoned a group of consummate True Gods …
Before Luo Zheng left the forbidden area, how could these Saints have imagined that the final result would be like this?
Within Mu Ning's eyes, which were filled with boundless vitality, there was a faint trace of intense excitement.
She saw the flower petal flickering between Luo Zheng's eyebrows. She saw Aunt Hanjiu take Luo Zheng and disappear in a flash. The excitement in her heart was hard to suppress!
In the end, he still left …
But will we have a chance to meet again?
Thinking of this, Mu Ning's heart faintly revealed some sadness. When we meet again, will we be enemies or friends?
"It's definitely not an enemy!"
Mu Ning quietly said, her eyes flashing with a sharp aura like a sword intent. She had inherited the characteristics of Mu Xuerong's personality, but that wasn't necessarily a bad thing. Once she set her sights on a goal, even if the ground was covered with thorns, she would still press forward.
Han Liusu quietly let out a sigh of relief, her hand tightly gripping Han Chu Yue also sweating. Han Bi Luo and Han Chu Yue's taut heartstrings also relaxed.
However, the direct descendants of the Han Clan looked at Emperor Han Qing with disappointment.
Aunt Han Jiu was the secondary saint of the Han Family and the second most important person in the Han Family.
At such a critical moment, not only did their father not stand up for them, but he also completely abandoned them. Emperor Han Qing's originally incomparably lofty image of their father had collapsed in their hearts.
Even Han Cangyan, who usually didn't think much, was full of doubts. This was completely different from the father he had known since he was young …
The female leader of the Blind Clan still didn't move. Her masked face didn't reveal any emotions.
As for the other saints who had nothing to do with this, especially those from the Sword Clan and the Xuanyue Clan, faintly revealed a hint of schadenfreude on their faces.
It was good that the imperial jade seal fell into that kid's hands. At least it was much better than Dongfang Chunjun getting it!
Especially Zhan Wuji of the War God Temple, he didn't hide the smile on his face at all. If it weren't for the fact that he didn't want to go head to head with Dongfang Chunjun, he would probably praise that clever kid and Shi Qiaoqiao!
"Dong!"
Dongfang Chunjun lightly stepped forward.
He couldn't help but release some of the power that had been accumulating in his body for a long time. In an instant, the ground cracked open.
"Shi Qiaoqiao … Hmph!"
He gritted his teeth and said these words. Then, his figure disappeared from the spot.
According to the rules, after the transit, the Blind Clan would hold a bonfire banquet.
The ingredients for this banquet all came from the Sea of Time. Naturally, they were very precious.
However, Dongfang Chunjun naturally wasn't in the mood to participate.
After Dongfang Chunjun left first, Tang Lun, Leng Yao, and the other saints from the League of Wealthy Clans also chose to leave.
Only the True Gods and the saints from different forces remained.
After those saints left, Zhan Wuji of the War God Temple chuckled. "Shi Qiaoqiao's move is amazing! Previously, Shi Qiaoqiao tricked Mu Haiji in the Luo Xiao Universe. This time, she tricked Dongfang Chunjun again! "
The other saints also faintly smiled faintly.
These wealthy clans didn't want to be enemies with the League of Wealthy Clans, but it didn't mean that they liked Dongfang Chunjun. Naturally, the saints were happy to see Dongfang Chunjun fall …
…
…
Aunt Han Jiu and Luo Zheng were still continuously teleporting.
The two of them constantly traveled through space, cruising and passing through many regions of the Realm of the Gods …
Sometimes, she directly appeared with Luo Zheng in the city of a Realm of the Gods. After a round of exclamations, the two of them quickly disappeared, leaving behind a group of dumbfounded True Gods.
Sometimes, they appeared on the peak of a mountain deep in the mountains.
They only stayed for a moment. Luo Zheng didn't even have time to say a word to Aunt Han Jiu before she dragged him away again.
After thirty or forty consecutive teleportations …
The two of them finally appeared at the edge of a lake.
This time, Aunt Han Jiu had just appeared with Luo Zheng when she couldn't help but groan. Her thin eyebrows were slightly furrowed, and the expression on her charming face was very painful.
She had been scratched by Dongfang Chunjun and was quite injured.
However, along the way, she had almost no time to care.
Luo Zheng's gaze focused on Aunt Han Jiu's waist. His brows were also tightly furrowed …
The three bloody marks on Aunt Han Jiu's soft waist had already turned black, and black smoke was rising from them.
As time passed, the black smoke didn't dissipate. Instead, it grew thicker.
The moment Dongfang Chunjun grabbed that attack, Luo Zheng also used the power of the imperial jade seal. However, he still couldn't erase Dongfang Chunjun's attack. It seemed that Dongfang Chunjun used some method to avoid it …
"This injury …"
Luo Zheng had just opened his mouth when Aunt Han Jiu shook her head. Enduring the pain, she stood up and walked towards the lake.
She noticed that Luo Zheng was also following her. She hurriedly stopped him and said, "Don't come over!"
When the clear lake water flowed over her waist and came into contact with her wounds, Luo Zheng heard a "chi" sound.
The originally clear lake water quickly spread out into patches of black water. Not long after, the entire clear lake turned into a lake of black water …
Along with the billowing black smoke floating on the lake's surface, the big fish and small shrimp in the lake died one after another. Even the big fish and water monsters that were more than a hundred feet long were no exception. Their snow-white fish bellies were flipped over, and they all died!
"What a strong poison!" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly.
Aunt Han Jiu soaked in the lake for a long time before walking ashore. Her face was full of weakness. Her pale face faintly glanced at Luo Zheng and then said, "I'll heal first …"
Luo Zheng originally wanted to invite Aunt Han Jiu to Immortal Mansion. There were still many golden pills in Master's medicine room that could help Aunt Han Jiu heal.
However, after Aunt Han Jiu silently sat down cross-legged on the shore, a colorful golden pill appeared in her hand. It seemed to be at least a seventh transformation golden pill.

Chapter 1973
Luo Zheng originally wanted to enter Buzhou Spiritual Mountain after the trip to the Sea of Time.
However, after a few accidents, he could only threaten them with the lives of these Perfected True Gods. He did not expect that the Sages would not be threatened at all.
He had almost caused him to lose everything.
Now, the lives of these Perfected True Gods were of little value!
Aunt Hanjiu swallowed the Golden Elixir in one gulp. Wisps of purple aura, like small snakes, coiled around her body and finally entered the wound on her waist.
During the healing process, Aunt Hanjiu's beautiful eyes also noticed the small black snake in Luo Zheng's palm. After noticing Luo Zheng's gradually unfriendly expression, she said softly, "After I stabilize my injuries, let them go, and then we will leave this place."
"Why do you want to let them go?" Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
Perhaps a few of these Perfected True Gods in the imperial jade seal were innocent, but most of them were hostile toward him and even wanted to kill him, especially Mu Xuerong, Tang Wan, and the others.
As for Dongfang Taiqing, he had helped him block Mu Xuerong's sword in the forbidden land, but his goal was definitely not to let him go. He just wanted to hand him over to Dongfang Chunjun to deal with.
Of course, he was unwilling to let the Perfected True Gods go just like that!
"If you really kill these Perfected True Gods, those Sages will definitely not let you go," Aunt Hanjiu continued.
"Aunt Jiu, are you saying that if I don't kill them, the Sages will let me go?" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed as he asked.
Aunt Hanjiu's beautiful eyes glanced at Luo Zheng and smiled. "You are indeed Luo Xiao's son, but after all, you are only a lower True God. It is not enough for a group of Sages to go to war and kill you. However, if you kill those Perfected True Gods, the meaning will be different …"
Hearing Aunt Hanjiu's words, Luo Zheng fell into deep thought.
She was right …
In the eyes of Dongfang Chunjun and the other Sages, he was still too insignificant. He was like a speck of dust in the air. He was not even qualified for them to take him seriously.
Even if Dongfang Chunjun knew that he was Luo Xiao's son, he was still willing to accept him as a disciple. Perhaps he had other plans, but it also showed that his identity was indeed insignificant.
"If you can help your father fulfill his great wish and revive the Luo family in the future, these Perfect True Gods will be no more than ants in your eyes. It will be easy for you to take their lives," Aunt Hanjiu said.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng nodded silently.
With a gentle wave of his hand, a five-colored halo appeared in front of him. As the halo slowly spread, it formed a passage of time. Then, he stepped into it.
…
Outside the Light Wheel Shrine …
Mu Xuerong, Tang Wan, Fang Henshao, and many other Perfect True Gods had ugly expressions.
The flow of time in this space-time was much faster than in the God Domain.

Mu Xuerong and the others kept exploring the Light Wheel Shrine, but they did not find anything valuable. In the end, they could only return to the Light Wheel Shrine through the underground cave.

Outside the light screen, Dongfang Taiqing, Han Tianxiao, and the other Perfect True Gods rushed over …
After a period of exploration, these Perfect True Gods finally found the Light Wheel Shrine.
Although they were outside the light screen, they still could not leave this space-time. The reason was simple — they could not construct a way out!
"What do we do now?"
Mu Xuerong looked at Dongfang Taiqing through the light screen.
"What else can we do? Let's sleep! "Tang Wan lay on the sand dune casually. She was in a good mood.
Dongfang Taiqing frowned and stared at Mu Xuerong. "You have so many Perfect True Gods. How did that kid steal the Imperial Jade Seal?"
Mu Xuerong snorted coldly. "You have the nerve to say that! If not for you, that kid would have been killed by me in the canyon. How could he have had the chance to steal the Imperial Jade Seal? "
Hearing this, Dongfang Taiqing's expression turned ugly …
The world was unpredictable. How could he have known that his block would lead to this predicament?
"Alright, now is not the time to blame each other." Fang Henshao shook his head. "Even if that kid gets the Imperial Jade Seal, he won't be able to escape. In the end, he'll have to obediently leave the Sea of Time. Do you think the Saints will let him go?"
Tang Wan lazily supported her head with her hand and agreed. "You're right! He's an outsider of the Han Family's floating island. With Emperor Han Qingdi's courage, would he dare to protect that kid? So it's better to sleep! "
Not far away, Han Tianxiao heard this and did not say anything.
"If I have another chance, I'll cut him into pieces!" Mu Xuerong's eyes flashed with a surging killing intent. She fiercely bit her lips and glared fiercely at Tang Wan, who was lying on the ground.
That killing intent made Tang Wan very uncomfortable. She shouted, "Can you not glare at me so fiercely? It's not like I'm the reason you can't leave … "
Before Tang Wan could finish, Luo Zheng's voice was heard.
"I'll give you a chance. Do you have the ability to cut me into pieces?"
Hearing this voice, the faces of all the True Perfection True Gods sank.
A look of panic appeared on Happy Sky's face. Without the slightest hesitation, his figure rapidly retreated to the back.
He knew that Luo Zheng could control these black spears. Everyone was standing so close to each other. It would not be fun to be shot by these spears.
However, the other True Perfection True Gods did not know.
"Hiss …"
The sand on the ground kept moving, making a soft sound.
The sand kept gathering together and quickly formed the shape of a person. In the end, it turned into a sand sculpture that looked exactly like Luo Zheng. It looked very lifelike.
Mu Xuerong was already extremely angry with Luo Zheng. So, when she saw that there was a faint trace of ridicule on Luo Zheng's face, she became even angrier!
"Whoosh!"
She slashed out with her scimitar in an instant.
In the blink of an eye, she slashed out countless times!
After she put away her scimitar, the surface of the sand sculpture showed traces of scimitar marks. Then, the sand sculpture suddenly collapsed.
"Is it fun?"
Luo Zheng's voice was heard again.
In the blink of an eye, another five sand sculptures gathered together. These sand sculptures gathered around Mu Xuerong.
Mu Xuerong could not tolerate this kind of provocation. She slashed out with her scimitar again!
However, just as she destroyed these sand sculptures, more sand sculptures stood up. The sand in this desert was inexhaustible. How could she destroy them?
Mu Xuerong could not help but shout, "If you have the ability, then show your true body. What are you doing sneaking around!"
"So what if I show myself?"
After saying this, Luo Zheng quietly appeared on the other side of the light screen. He stood calmly in midair.
It was impossible to fly in this world …
The only person who had seen Luo Zheng fly was Han Tianxiao. However, he did not tell this to the other True Perfection True Gods.
Thus, when the other True Perfection True Gods saw this, their expressions changed several times.
Dongfang Taiqing's expression also darkened. Just now, the sand had formed naturally. Now, Luo Zheng was floating in midair. This meant that he had a very high degree of control over this world. This also meant that in this space-time, Luo Zheng could dictate the life and death of everyone!
"Shua!"
Mu Xuerong suddenly raised the scimitar in her hand and said sternly, "Hand over the imperial jade seal, and I'll spare your life!"
She was impatient and murderous, so she did not think too much.

Chapter 1974
Those black spears were aimed at everyone.
The expressions of the Paragons present turned ugly. Even Tang Wan, who had been sleeping lazily, stood up and glared at Luo Zheng.
The Paragons had seen the power of these black spears before.
Dongfang Taiqing had been shot by a spear and had been recuperating for a long time. Even now, the injuries caused by the black spears had yet to heal.
As for the Sword Clan's Paragons, they had even fallen to the black spears!
When the densely packed black spears were shot out, how could the Great Perfection Real Gods that were present be lucky enough to escape?
Mu Xuerong didn't speak. She still tightly gripped her scimitar, her face filled with unwillingness …
Her stubbornness far exceeded that of an ordinary person. How could the word "death" make her submit?

"Assassination …"
That bunch of fine sand streaked across Mu Xuerong's wrist, leaving a bloody scar on her white wrist. The curved blade also flew out of her hand under the impact of the sand.

Two black spears shot out and nailed themselves in front of the two of them.

Seeing the scimitar fly out of her hand, Mu Xuerong flipped over and once again exploded with a powerful force. She sent a knee strike straight at Luo Zheng's face!

"Pa!"
Luo Zheng just raised his hand and casually blocked her knee.

"Hiss hiss hiss …"
Strands of sand flowed towards Mu Xuerong's legs and hands.
Even though she tried to get up from the ground with all her might, the strands of sand kept her firmly fixed to the ground.
Although Luo Zheng didn't have complete control over the imperial jade seal, after breaking through the first light membrane, Luo Zheng's control over this world wasn't much different from his own inner world. Since the Divine Dao couldn't be used in this world, the Paragons present had no chance of winning against him.
Mu Xuerong knelt on the ground, unable to even lift her arms.
"If we were outside, you wouldn't even be able to take a single blow from me!" She gritted her teeth and said.
Luo Zheng had a faint smile on his face. He slowly walked towards Mu Xuerong and squatted down. He reached out and grabbed her chin, lifting up her beautiful and angry face. "But here, you can't even take a single blow from me."
Mu Xuerong was forced to look up at Luo Zheng, at this True Divinity.
When had she ever been humiliated like this?
At this moment, her beautiful face was so angry that it was almost distorted. But under this distortion, it revealed a different kind of beauty.
If her shackles were released, she would probably pounce on Luo Zheng and swallow him whole …
"Actually … I'm not willing to let you go like this." Luo Zheng looked down at the face in his hand.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the Paragons' eyes flashed …
They were well aware that their lives were already in Luo Zheng's hands.
Did these words mean that Luo Zheng was planning to let them go?
"As you said, if we were outside of this space-time, I would indeed be far from being your opponent." Luo Zheng stared at Mu Xuerong and continued, "But don't forget, I'm only a lower True Divinity. The future is long, and I believe that we will have a chance to meet again."
After saying this, he took back his hand.
With a gentle wave of his hand, the curtain of light with the black spear hanging outside the Temple of Heavenly Wheels disappeared.
At the same time, he pointed at the ground, and a colorful halo appeared.
"That's the passage to leave this place …"
"Does that mean you're planning to let us go?
The Paragons' faces were filled with surprise.
Before Luo Zheng stepped into the halo, he left behind a sentence. "I will arrange those light wheel insects in this halo. If you enter this passage within three days, you will be completely devoured by those insects. After three days, I will remove all the insects. You can leave, but take care of yourselves! "
After saying this, he stepped into the halo and disappeared in front of everyone's eyes.
In the passage of time, the body would be pulled into an extremely special state. In this state, even Paragons could not fight against those insects.
Boom!
Tang Wan smashed her fist into the ground, creating a huge crater. Then she cursed, "Motherf * cker! Arrogant brat! If you fall into my hands one day, I will definitely make you regret it! "
Huala …
After Luo Zheng left, the sand binding Mu Xuerong slipped away.
She remained in a kneeling position, motionless. Her head was lowered, and her pitch-black hair covered her face, making it impossible to see her expression.
But drop after drop of blood dripped from her lips and merged with the sand …
Fang Henshao shook the fan in his hand and frowned as he looked at the colorful halo. "It seems that brat is under some kind of pressure and does not dare to kill anyone?"
Dongfang Taiqing, who had been silent all this time, nodded. "From the meaning of his words, he does not dare to kill us."
"Then he should have reached an agreement with the Sage Emperor and used our lives as a hostage to escape the sea of time?" Fang Henshao guessed, holding the fan.
Dongfang Taiqing shook his head. "Impossible, Father Emperor would not be threatened by a Lesser True God!"
"Then why did he let us go?" Fang Henshao asked curiously.
"Right now, we are trapped in this world and have received too little information. We do not know what has happened outside. Three days later … Let's talk about it after we get out," Dongfang Taiqing said, shaking his head.
After returning from the imperial jade seal, Luo Zheng said to Aunt Hanjiu, "The arrangements have been made. They will escape from this halo soon. We cannot stay here for long."
Three days in the imperial jade seal was equivalent to less than an hour in the divine realm.
Aunt Hanjiu nodded. She planned to take Luo Zheng and use greater teleportation to leave this place.
Luo Zheng took out a token. "Although the divine realm is vast, there are still some risks. Why don't we hide in the immortal estate first?"
Luo Zheng had been in the divine realm for so long, but he had not returned to the immortal estate.
Aunt Hanjiu looked at the "Gen" token in Luo Zheng's hand. She raised her eyebrows, revealing a trace of surprise. "Gu Bei's immortal estate in Takeshi Xuan Forest?"
Luo Zheng replied, "Hmm."
She thought for a moment, then nodded.
Although Gu Bei's immortal estate was not an unbreakable existence, it was indeed a good place to stay temporarily.
After seeing Aunt Hanjiu nod, he activated the "Gen" token in his hand.
The instant the token was activated, the surrounding space began to slowly vibrate …
The space in the divine realm was countless times stronger than the universe.
In the universe, he could hide in the immortal estate in an instant. It was very convenient. However, the "Gen" token in the divine realm was very difficult to break through space. After a full six or seven breaths of time, the "Gen" token pulled him and Aunt Hanjiu into a spatial tunnel.

Chapter 1975
After some time after Luo Zheng and Aunt Han Jiu left …
Ripples suddenly appeared in the colorful halo at the edge of the lake.
Following the ripples, Dongfang Taiqing leaped out of the ripples and stood firmly to the side.
He looked around with a surprised look in his eyes.
Then, Fang Henshao, Tang Wan, Mu Xuerong, Han Tianxiao, and the other Perfect True Gods also came out of the halo one by one …
"This is not the Sea of Time!" Tang Wan said as she looked at the lake.
Fang Henshao glanced at Tang Wan. "Nonsense."
"That brat should have escaped from the Sea of Time." Dongfang Taiqing frowned. "With his strength, it's impossible for him to escape from the Sea of Time …" Dongfang Taiqing took a few steps towards the lake. This black lake was too eye-catching.
Then, he reached his iron hand into the lake and gently tapped on the water.
The surface of the iron hand that was originally shining with silver light suddenly turned black. His eyes flashed and he said, "The Saint Emperor also attacked and injured someone with the Annihilation Hand. I don't know which Saint it is, but it should be this Saint who escaped with that kid …"
"You're saying that Saint Chun Jun attacked?" Fang Henshao's eyes flashed.
Dongfang Taiqing nodded. "This Saint was also seriously injured. I can't guess who it is."
"We'll know when we get back. We just don't know where we are in the Divine Territory. We have to find out first," Fang Henshao said.
The Divine Territory was too big. A Saint could travel freely in the Divine Territory, but a Perfect True God's teleportation distance was much shorter.
"Why aren't you saying anything?" Tang Wan glanced at Mu Xuerong.
During this time, Mu Xuerong's mood was very low. She didn't say a word …

At this moment, Fu's expression suddenly changed, and a look of joy appeared on his face. "Master is back!"

The Saints had many heirs, so they naturally chose those with the strongest talents.

The female disciples of the Purple Soul Palace all cultivated the Purple Cloud Divine Dao. Overall, their talent was weaker than the average Floating Island disciple. Proving the Divine Dao and becoming a True Divinity …

After so many divine epochs, there were quite a number of golden Sanskrit words circulating in the divine realm. Some of them had been translated, but they didn't have much value.
The truly rare Sanskrit words existed in all the great clans. Especially the rare Sanskrit words that were collected by saints. Not even a copy of them had been leaked …
But those rare Sanskrit words were extremely difficult to translate. So far, the rare Sanskrit words that had been completely translated were extremely rare. To decipher a Sanskrit word, it often required a lot of experience. Even so, sometimes the Sanskrit words translated were completely different. It was very absurd!
Gu Bei liked to travel in the primal chaos. Because of Jiu Sheng, the Sanskrit words he collected were extremely valuable. If one could really translate a passage, it would be an extraordinary existence. This child had actually translated 30% of it? Aunt Hanjiu found this hard to believe.
How could she not be shocked?
And when Aunt Hanjiu looked at the words that Luo Nian had translated, the shock on her face froze.

Chapter 1977

At first, Luo Zheng was still able to read.

By the time he read a hundred words, his tongue felt a little numb. By the time he read two hundred words, his tongue began to tremble as if he were drunk, and his head began to swell. By the time he read five hundred words, he felt his soul shake …
Aunt Hanjiu watched Luo Zheng's reaction with a faint smile.
By the time Luo Zheng read eight hundred words, a faint trace of hallucination appeared.

Every time he took a step forward, he had to go through great pain!
That kind of pain wasn't a specific soul attack or physical injury, but a kind of pressure on the spiritual level.
Luo Zheng clearly felt that the path he walked was very short, very short. He had only taken a small step forward, and the end of this path was boundless. However, the end of this path actually had a great attraction to him. It was as if he would pay any price to traverse this world!
When he gritted his teeth and read the 900th word, he felt a "boom" in his mind. It was as if something had smashed into his soul. He felt dizzy and took a few steps back before he could steady himself. He looked at those words with a face full of astonishment …
"Master, you were able to read nine hundred characters on your first try. That is already very impressive," said the Immortal in the Book with a smile. "However, Little Master is already able to read twelve hundred characters. Furthermore, Little Master is writing as he reads."

The immortal smiled. "To be exact, one thousand two hundred and thirty-six words. That's the record that the little master just set!"
It turned out that Luo Nian had been deciphering those golden Sanskrit for the past few years.
The golden Sanskrit left by Gu Bei was very rich …
According to speculation, the origin of these golden Sanskrit came from creatures of a higher level.
No matter what kind of creature or tribe it was, it must have a complex culture. The golden Sanskrit didn't necessarily record cultivation methods. There were a lot of other things.
For example, the golden Sanskrit that Luo Nian had deciphered earlier recorded a boundless blue lake in the chaos.
The golden Sanskrit that Luo Nian had deciphered later was even more outrageous. There were even some strange recipes and strange adventures.
The value of these golden Sanskrit wasn't very high. It could only help them understand the creatures of a higher level.
But the six golden Sanskrit that hung on the wall of the side hall appeared at the same time. Luo Nian found something strange in the process of deciphering them. The six golden Sanskrit totalled six thousand and four hundred words. The words recorded in them didn't have much meaning individually, but when they were connected together, they were full of a special true meaning.
It was this true meaning that piqued Luo Nian and the others' interest.
In the beginning, Ning Yudie and the other girls also tried to read this paragraph of text, but they couldn't go on after reading sixty or seventy words.
Only Luo Nian could persevere and read all the way to three hundred words. But after three hundred words, the pressure he faced increased sharply. If he continued to comprehend the true meaning of these words, he would directly pass out …
Ning Yudie pitied her son, so there was some dispute between them. She didn't want Luo Nian to comprehend these strange words.
However, Afo and the immortal supported Luo Nian to persevere.
Luo Nian gradually figured out some of the rules after repeated attempts. Now, he could write and read one word at a time. He could already read more than twelve hundred words. His perseverance was amazing!
"How is this possible … I feel that Nian 'er's soul is indeed much stronger than ordinary people's. He can't possibly persevere until this step!"
Luo Zheng couldn't understand.

Sage Jiu had studied the golden Sanskrit characters deeply. His understanding of the Primal Chaos was probably above Aunt Hanjiu's.
Even some of his master, the North Sage's travels had indicated that the entire Chaos was empty.

Chapter 1978
"At the end of the Shinto Dao is a boundless ocean …"

The Saints indeed stood at the peak of the God Domain. But for those who had cultivated to this level, who didn't wish to reach the highest level of enlightenment and take another step forward?

"These six golden Sanskrit tablets form a set of 9,673 words. I have deciphered them one by one. Naturally, this is the complete set." At this moment, a slightly childish voice sounded.

In terms of seniority, Aunt Hanjiu was someone of Luo Nian's "grandfather's generation." In terms of age, she was simply not worth mentioning.

Even if Hanbiluo and Luo Nian were about the same age, she would still call her Aunt Jiu.
