"Don't mind this." Aunt Hanjiu waved her hand. "Just call me Aunt Jiu. Little genius, can you write down the entire text for Aunt Jiu to see?"
At Luo Nian's age, he had always been trapped in Immortal Mansion and rarely had the opportunity to go out. Naturally, he liked to hear others praise him.
Although he could not sense Aunt Hanjiu's cultivation, he could faintly sense that this woman was not an ordinary person. He nodded and walked to the side. Picking up an ink brush, he walked over to the white paper spread out on the ground.
"Nian 'er, do you want to take a break first?" Ning Yudie asked.
Luo Nian had a look of disapproval as he said, "It's not like mother doesn't know these words. As long as I don't read them or think about them, I'll be fine …"
The first time Luo Nian deciphered the golden Sanskrit characters, he had discovered the mysteries of this set of golden Sanskrit characters. If these strange characters were not linked together, reading them together would not be a problem. But once they were linked together, it would make him dizzy.
So every time he wrote, Luo Nian would decipher the golden Sanskrit characters one by one. This way, he had no pressure at all.
Luo Nian held the brush in his hand, his expression very serious. Comparing the six golden Sanskrit characters, he began to write on the white paper like a dragon flying and a phoenix dancing …
"Swish swish swish …"
In the side hall, everyone was silent. They could only watch as the white-clothed youth moved his brush like a knife, moving clouds and flowing water.
About half an hour later, over ten thousand characters were displayed one by one in front of everyone.
Seeing his son like this, Luo Zheng was very pleased.
After Luo Nian finished writing, Aunt Hanjiu finally could not hold back. Looking at Luo Zheng, she said, "Nian 'er, could you let me take a look at this True Meaning Heart Sutra that you wrote?"
Before Luo Zheng could answer, Luo Nian curled his lips in dissatisfaction and said, "This True Meaning Heart Sutra was written by me. Aunt Jiu, you should be asking me!"
Seeing Luo Nian's expression, Aunt Hanjiu smiled faintly. "Alright, may I ask if Nian 'er can let me take a look?"
"Of course I can. I'm not that stingy." Luo Nian shrugged. "But I'll only give you something in return."

Chapter 1979
A sly smile emerged on Luo Nian's face, and there was a hint of pride within it.
Looking at Luo Nian's mischievous expression, Luo Zheng sighed in his mind. When he was 16 years old, his mentality was much heavier than Luo Nian's.
It was all because of the pressure his father gave him, and that was why he was able to carry this pressure all the way.
Luo Nian wasn't part of his father's plan, and if he could grow up happily, that would be one of Luo Zheng's biggest wishes.
"Benefits?" Aunt Han Jiu's smile grew wider, then she asked, "What do you want? If I have it, I'll give it to you!"
Hearing this, Luo Nian quickly thought of something. "I want a spear!"
"A spear?"
Luo Zheng was startled.
Ning Yudie shrugged, then she turned to Luo Zheng and said, "Your son is obsessed with practicing spear skills, and he often goes to the Cultivation Tower to spar with Xun."
"He can spar with Xun?" Luo Zheng's eyes were filled with surprise.
In the past, Luo Zheng didn't notice that Luo Nian had the talent to practice martial arts. Even now, Luo Nian had just broken through to the Divine Core realm, and there was still a long way to go before he could break through the Life and Death realm and expand his Divine Sea.
In the past, Luo Zheng had too little resources, and he had to fight for everything himself.
But before Ning Yudie and the others entered the Immortal Abode, they had made ample preparations, and they could freely use the resources in the Immortal Abode.
Therefore, Luo Nian's cultivation speed wasn't fast, it was actually very slow …
"It's just a spar." Ning Yudie said with a smile on her face.
"Even if it's just a spar, I don't think it's possible." Luo Zheng looked at Luo Nian and said.
Xun's spear skills weren't complicated, but when she used them, they were terrifying. It was impossible for them to spar.
"Dad, you're always looking down on me!" Luo Nian wasn't happy. He stared at Luo Zheng, "If it wasn't for Aunt Xun's amazing spear, I might have been able to beat her! I even asked Shenzi Shenzi to forge a few spears for me, but none of them are as good as Aunt Xun's! "
"Is that true?" Luo Zheng still looked doubtful.
Smoke's Sacred Slaughter Spear was a sacred item of the Yao Night clan. No matter how good a spear Lian Shenzi made, it wouldn't be able to compete with Smoke's Sacred Slaughter Spear.
"Humph!" Luo Nian just snorted, as if it was beneath him to explain to Luo Zheng.
Aunt Hanjiu then said, "I don't use weapons. I have some guns in my hands, but …"
As she spoke, she waved her hand gently, and six or seven spears appeared in a row. At the same time, she flicked her fingertips, and wisps of purple mist coiled around these spears, suspending them in the air.
The Second Saint was extremely wealthy. All of these spears were graded grandmist treasures, and the aura exuding from the tip of the spear was extremely powerful.
Ning Yudie, Granny Yu, and Mu Mingxue didn't use spears, but when they saw these spears, their eyes burned with passion. They naturally hoped that Luo Nian would choose a good spear.
"But, I don't think Nian 'er can handle the weight of these spears." Aunt Hanjiu continued.
The materials in the Divine Realm were many times heavier than the materials in the universe. Even a Divine Realm warrior would find it difficult to handle these spears, let alone Luo Nian who was only a Divine Core warrior.
After that, Aunt Hanjiu waved her hand again, and a crystal blue spear appeared in front of everyone. She gently pushed the spear, and it slowly floated towards Luo Nian. "This Spatial Divine Spear is weightless, and it should be suitable for Nian 'er. But, because it's weightless, it doesn't feel comfortable. It will take some time for him to get used to it!"
Looking at the slowly floating spear, Luo Nian grabbed it with his hand.
"Swoosh!
Swoosh! "He casually swung the light spear like a windmill. The tip of the spear danced in the air, and it seemed like it was going to tear the surrounding space apart.
"Alright! Deal! I'll allow you to see the True Intent cultivation method! "Luo Nian smiled, then he grabbed the spear and dashed out of the side hall." I'm going to find Aunt Xun. "
Since Aunt Hanjiu wanted to see the True Intent cultivation method, everyone else left the side hall.
After closing the side hall's door, Luo Zheng asked, "Oh right, where is Nian 'er going to find Xun to practice with the spear? Why haven't I seen her? "
Xun was the only person Luo Zheng hadn't seen since his return.
"She's cultivating in the Cultivation Tower." Ning Yudie glanced at Luo Zheng, then answered.
No matter if it was Su Lingyun, Ning Yudie, Yang, or Xun, they all had their own personalities. They were waiting for Luo Zheng here, and their relationship was quite harmonious.
But, there was still some friction between them, and it was impossible for them to be as harmonious as best friends.
However, Mu Mingxue was an exception.
Therefore, no matter if it was Mu Mingxue, Granny Yu, Ning Yudie, or Su Lingyun, her relationship with them was very good. Even Luo Nian was the closest to Mu Mingxue.
As for Xun …
Her presence in Immortal Mansion was quite low.
She spent most of her time in the Cultivation Tower. Occasionally, she would come out, but she would only exchange a few simple words, ask if Luo Zheng had returned, and then return to the Cultivation Tower …
Luo Nian was somewhat curious about Xun. Very accidentally, he entered the Cultivation Tower and saw Xun brandishing a double-tipped spear like a dragon emerging from the sea. The curious Luo Nian had a trace of interest in practicing the spear. Therefore, even though he originally had no interest in martial arts, he also began to cultivate.
When Luo Zheng pushed open the Cultivation Tower's door, he heard the sound of something breaking through the air.
"Aunt Xun, I got a good spear today. If I exchange moves with you, it shouldn't be damaged!"
Luo Zheng saw Luo Nian brandishing the Sky Domain Spear vigorously and rushing to the other side.
On the other side, a tall and exquisite figure brandished the Holy Slaughter Spear, and with a light turn, blocked Luo Nian's spear with an extremely straightforward move. The two spears attacked several times in an instant, and a crisp sound constantly reverberated on the first floor of the Cultivation Tower.
"How's Nian 'er's spear skills?"
Ning Yudie's small white hand quietly grabbed Luo Zheng's arm and smiled.
"Her understanding of spear intent is indeed good," Luo Zheng nodded unconsciously.
Xun suppressed her cultivation and could indeed suppress Luo Nian purely by exchanging moves. However, Luo Nian's every move had its merits, far exceeding Luo Zheng's expectations.
"Ding ding dang dang …"
As the spears crossed, Luo Nian was very proud, "This spear won't be damaged so easily! Hehe, this spear should be more powerful than Aunt Xun's spear! "
Although Luo Nian was a child and his cultivation was far from enough, her character was very serious. Concentrating on exchanging moves with Luo Nian, she didn't pay attention to the door. She asked curiously, "That guy Lian Shenzi's refining skills improved so quickly? This spear … can't be refined by him, right? "
Previously, Lian Shenzi had forged seven or eight spears for Luo Nian, and Luo Nian couldn't protect the spear with vital essence. Under the destruction of Xun's Holy Spear of Massacre, it wouldn't take long for the spear to break or for the spear tip to collapse …
Luo Zheng's casual stab with the spear faintly showed signs of piercing through the surrounding space!
This was the Realm of the Gods. Even Xun couldn't do this, but Luo Nian could easily do it. The spear in his hand was no ordinary item. It was even far superior to Xun's Holy Spear of Massacre.
"That guy doesn't have this ability," Luo Nian said, curling his lips. "Aunt Jiu gave it to me! Hehe! "
"Aunt Jiu? Someone from the mansion came? "Xun was slightly surprised.
"Yes, my father brought it back!" Luo Nian continued.
Hearing this, Xun almost subconsciously looked towards the door. How could she still be in the mood to exchange moves with Luo Nian?
With this glance, she saw Luo Zheng at the door, and her body stiffened slightly.
At this time, Luo Nian's spear had already reached its limit and couldn't be pulled back.
Xun pursed her lips and revealed a pure smile. She didn't even look at the spear. With a clever twist of her hand, she grabbed the tip of the spear and pulled it out of Luo Nian's hand. The spear flew to the side and hit the wall with a "ding".
"Aunt Xun, you cheated …" Luo Nian saw this scene and felt wronged.
Xun ignored Luo Nian. She turned into a gust of wind and rushed towards Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1980
She rushed towards Luo Zheng at an extremely fast speed. Her perfect and flawless body was extremely resilient, and she threw Luo Zheng out of the Cultivation Pagoda in one move.
Ning Yudie pulled Luo Zheng's hand away.
Everyone's expressions were different …
Su Lingyun saw how unrestrained Xun was and covered her mouth with her hand in surprise.
Luo Nian's mouth was wide open as he stared blankly at the scene outside.
Ning Yudie's delicate eyebrows were tightly knitted together, and then she showed an angry expression. She rushed into the Cultivation Pagoda and closed the door with a bang. At the same time, she reprimanded, "What are you looking at, little kid!"
"I didn't mean to see it," Luo Nian pouted, unconvinced. "Besides, I'm already sixteen …"
Luo Nian grew up in the Cloud Hall. At that time, he still had some playmates from the Cloud Hall.
Later, Luo Zheng was worried and brought Luo Nian into Immortal Mansion. His days weren't so good.
Although he was obsessed with deciphering Sanskrit and practicing the spear, fifteen or sixteen years old was still a hot-blooded age. He had reached the age of first awakening of love and was full of curiosity about everything in this world. He always hoped to explore outside Immortal Mansion.
He mentioned it a few times, but Ning Yudie refused because he was too young.
In fact, Luo Nian didn't have the ability to leave Immortal Mansion …
If Immortal Mansion's enchantment opened, they would all need to hide in the Cultivation Pagoda. Otherwise, they would disintegrate into true essence and disappear from this world.
This was the limitation of secondary life forms. Without great magical power, it was impossible to get rid of it. Even Luo Zheng had to earnestly comprehend Shinto before sneaking in. Luo Nian couldn't go to the Realm of the Gods.
And returning to Huan Yu wasn't a simple matter.
Now that the node of the immortal estate's teleportation was bound to Luo Zheng's Gen Token, if he wanted to enter the Grand Yan Universe, he would have to cross a vast distance and find the entrance to the Grand Yan Universe.
It could be said that the moment Ning Yudie and the others entered Immortal Mansion, it was equivalent to stepping onto an "isolated island". If they left Immortal Mansion, they wouldn't be able to survive.
… …
The Yao Night clan didn't have as many etiquette and rules as humans, and Xun lacked the restraint of a woman.
She missed Luo Zheng, so naturally, she would find ways to express it.
For example, she was like an octopus wrapped around Luo Zheng, completely ignoring other people's gazes.
When Su Lingyun, Mu Mingxue, and the other girls saw this, they blushed shyly and looked away.
Clang, clang, clang …
For some reason, Refined Shenzi left the forging workshop for the first time. When he saw Luo Zheng return to the immortal abode, his metal head glowed faintly. He had something to say to Luo Zheng, but after noticing this, he silently retreated back into the forging workshop.
Granny Yu, who was not far away, saw this scene and was just as angry as Ning Yudie, carefully muttering to herself, "No matter how thirsty you are, you shouldn't do it in broad daylight. How improper, how improper …"
. "" I don't want this body anymore, "Xun whispered into Luo Zheng's ear as she tightly clung to him.
Luo Zheng paused, then immediately understood what Xun meant. He slowly sat up and hugged Xun, smiling, "What nonsense are you saying?"
"This body is an obstacle to me. It doesn't have any meaning anymore," Xun said.
Xun had been attached to Luo Zheng's body in the form of a remnant soul, attaching herself to his sword spirit body. She had accompanied Luo Zheng for a period of time and experienced countless trials and tribulations.
The first time she was separated from Luo Zheng, she wasn't used to it, and this time, it had been even longer …
In order to obtain a chance to enter Divine Realm.
For the past two years, she had been cultivating like crazy in the Cultivation Pagoda.
She was a Realm Lord, and the martial path she cultivated was originally focused on killing. She wanted to rely on her own comprehension to comprehend the Killing God Dao.
However, she lacked a few prerequisites, so it was difficult for her to truly step onto this Dao.
Moreover, even if she did cultivate a godhead, she wouldn't be able to leave Immortal Mansion. As long as she left Immortal Mansion, she would be punished by the slate. This was because she was only a secondary life form.
This made Xun extremely unwilling!
"How could it not have any meaning, silly," Luo Zheng stretched out a finger and tapped Xun's forehead.
Xun's demonic red eyes were still so serious, "It's only meaningful if I accompany you, so my soul is enough. I have to think of a way to deal with my physical body!"
Seeing that Xun seemed to be speaking the truth, Luo Zheng hurriedly persuaded her, "That won't do. Moreover, your soul is also a secondary life form. Even if it hides in my body, I'm afraid it will still disintegrate …"
"Then teach me the Divine Dao, let me enter the Divine Realm, let me stay by your side, or you don't have to leave, just stay in the Immortal Abode …" Her thoughts were not complicated, and she said whatever she wanted, so she spoke without fear like a child.
Looking at Xun's innocent appearance, a trace of restlessness slowly surged in Luo Zheng's heart.
In terms of mental state, Luo Zheng was naturally able to restrain his desires with ease. However, after returning to Immortal Mansion with great difficulty, Luo Zheng's mental state also completely relaxed.
However, it was broad daylight, and he and Xun weren't the only two people in Immortal Mansion. In the end, there had to be some decorum …
For several days, Aunt Hanjiu didn't leave the side hall. She had been comprehending the True Meaning mental cultivation method that Luo Nian had deciphered.
For a mighty figure like a quasi-sage to comprehend it, perhaps an instant would be enough. Perhaps several years wouldn't be enough. Even seclusion for one or two divine eras was common. Luo Zheng also instructed that no one was to disturb her.
Luo Zheng was also extremely interested in that True Meaning of the Dao.
However, since he had returned this time, he wasn't in a hurry. At most, it wouldn't be a problem to let Luo Nian write another one.
This period of time was also quite fulfilling for Luo Zheng. During the day, he taught Luo Nian martial arts. If Luo Nian had some questions, Luo Zheng would naturally answer them one by one. In addition, Luo Nian also gave Luo Zheng the contents of some golden Sanskrit characters that he had deciphered in the past few years.
The contents of those golden Sanskrit characters were extremely strange and varied. Among them, the most valuable was undoubtedly the mental cultivation method of the True Meaning of the Dao. However, some of the other contents gave Luo Zheng a general understanding of "creatures of a higher level".
In the past, those creatures of a higher level were almost unimaginable. What kind of shape they were, and what kind of state they existed in, were completely unfathomable.
However, according to the contents of those golden Sanskrit characters, Luo Zheng found that these creatures of a higher level weren't as mysterious as he had imagined. In their world, there were also temples and complex social relationships. Similarly, they could also die. They also had experts, a heart of reverence for experts, and so on.
Luo Nian also exchanged some of his views with Luo Zheng. Although the little guy's cultivation wasn't deep, he had benefited a lot from the library. His knowledge was so extensive that even many true gods who had lived for many divine eras couldn't compare to him.
As night fell, Luo Zheng paced back and forth in the land of warmth and tenderness.
On the first day, he had offended Ning Yudie. She had ignored Luo Zheng for an entire day. Her jealousy couldn't be changed. However, Luo Zheng had to coax her for a long time before her tears turned into laughter …
… …
… …
The Far East of the Divine Realm.
In the boundless desert, dozens of people draped in black cloaks were rapidly moving forward.
These people were all middle true gods … …
The person in the lead had a long sword hanging on his back. He was tall and straight, and his steps were steady.
Under the lead of the person in the lead, they discovered a sinkhole that was millions of feet deep in the desert.
After everyone slid down the sinkhole, a series of surprised voices came from the people in the black cloaks.
"It's the Bone Jade Divine City … …"
"The entire Bone Jade Divine City has fallen. What in the world happened?"
After the person in the lead slid down to the bottom of the sinkhole, he lifted the hood on his head, revealing a face full of heroic spirit. He glanced at the fallen Bone Jade Divine City and said indifferently, "Let's find the stone tablet first."

Chapter 1981
In the divine territory, there would occasionally be conflicts between wealthy families and large families, but it was rare for a large-scale war to break out.
The wealthy families in the Sea of Time would maintain the stability of the entire divine territory …
There were many divine cities in the divine territory, and most of them were controlled by second-and third-tier families. These first-tier and second-tier families usually belonged to those wealthy families.
Therefore, the wealthy families in the divine territory controlled 90% of the divine cities through these first-tier and second-tier families.
Bone Jade City, which had fallen into the huge pit, was a medium-sized divine city. It was protected by a Hundred Commandments Slate. Bone Jade City was originally controlled by the second-tier family, He Family.
The He Family had to pay Shenwu Coins to the first-tier family, Lu Family, every year.
As for Lu Family, it was a first-tier family at the foot of the Fusang Divine Tree.
East Pole Fusang.
The sun rose from the east. Every day, the sun would rise above the Fusang Divine Tree as usual.
As one of the four divine trees in the divine territory, this one trillion feet tall divine tree had experienced countless changes over the years.
Now, the Fusang Divine Tree was occupied by the Dongfang Family.
With the Fusang Divine Tree as their backing, the entire Dongfang Family controlled hundreds of regions, tens of thousands of divine cities, countless divine citizens, martial artists who had proven themselves to be gods, and True Gods …
Even some first-tier families with quasi-sages had to submit!
No one dared to offend the Dongfang Family's territory.
The Bone Jade Divine City was nothing, but the fact that the entire Divine City had suddenly disappeared still attracted the attention of the Dongfang Family.
Therefore, this group of people was sent to explore …
"Let's observe first," a middle-level True God in the group said carefully as he observed the surroundings.
"Yes, who knows what kind of power caused this divine city to fall!"
Even if a divine city fell, it was impossible to kill all the True Gods. Although most True Gods could not fly, it was rare for them to be injured in such a collapse.
But now, this Bone Jade City was terrifyingly quiet …
The sunlight refracted from above the pit covered the divine city like a veil. Under the light, the situation inside the divine city could not be seen clearly. Instead, it added a bit of mystery and strangeness.
"There's no need to observe. Let's hurry up." The young man in the lead turned around and smiled brightly.
Someone in the team was unhappy. Another middle-level true god pulled down his hood and said with a sullen face, "Sky Sword, the orders from above cannot be disobeyed. This is your first mission and you are acting on your own. Aren't you afraid of punishment?"
The middle True God called Sky Sword turned his head and smiled. He shook his head and said, "Don't be afraid." After saying that, his figure flashed. He dragged the black cloak and turned into a black shadow. He leaped up and entered the Bone Jade City through a crack in the city wall.
"Should we follow them too?"
"That brat Sky Sword is a little strange …"
"Don't care about him. I'll sue him when I get back!"
The other middle true gods hesitated outside the city wall for a while before they jumped onto the city wall and entered the Bone Jade Divine City. However, they could no longer find Sky Sword.
After the Middle Real God named Sky Sword entered the Bone Jade Holy City, the smile on his face disappeared and it was replaced with vigilance and seriousness.
"Sou!"
With a sudden leap, he jumped to the top of a building, then he raised his index finger and narrowed his eyes.
The effect of the stone slab was circular and spread out in all directions.
Therefore, almost all the stone slabs were placed in the center of the city. Moreover, most of the cities in the Divine Territory were circular. This way, they could maximize the coverage of the stone slabs.
"The distance … is enough."
After he finished measuring, he muttered to himself.
Then, he took out a small fire starter from his hand.
This fire starter's shape was very unique. It did not look like it was made of wood. Instead, it looked like a yellow metal.
"Fuse!"
A flame lit up in Sky Sword's hand and it lit up at the tip of the fire starter.
"Crackle crackle crackle …"
The metal-like fire starter started to burn and it exploded slightly. At the same time, it turned into a strange oily green light …
The moment Sky Sword lit up the fire starter, he turned into a gust of wind and rushed toward the center of the Bone Jade Divine City!
Along the way, he nimbly jumped up and down among the buildings of various shapes.
"Ge …"
"Ge …"
After he rushed out, some of the things hidden in the Bone Jade Divine City began to stir.
If one looked carefully, they would notice that there were countless black and red centipedes in the cracks on the walls.
Even if the weak light refracted on these centipedes, it could still reflect the gorgeous colors on their shells. The poison contained in these centipedes was a fatal threat even to true gods.
It was obvious that these black and red centipedes wanted to swarm Sky Sword and devour him completely.
However, they seemed to be extremely afraid of the fire starter in Sky Sword's hand. Not only did they not charge at him, they all avoided him …
After a few dozen breaths, Sky Sword arrived at the center of the Divine City as he ran at full speed.
With a leap, he stepped into the pavilion in the center of the Divine City. He aimed at one of the walls and smashed it into smithereens.
Behind the wall was a roofless patio. Countless black and red centipedes were gathered in the patio.
Sky Sword tossed the fire starter in his hand into the patio and jumped in after them.
"Sha sha sha sha sha …"
Those black and red centipedes crawled in all directions and disappeared completely in the blink of an eye.
A green stone slab appeared at the bottom of the patio …
"Bang!"
Sky Sword stood firmly at the bottom of the patio and looked at the stone slab with a hint of joy in his eyes.
The Ten Commandments Stone Slab, Hundred Commandments Stone Slab, and Thousand Commandments Stone Slab were all very special weapons. As long as the rules on the stone slab were modified to protect oneself, one could use the power of the stone slab to fight against enemies. However, most people who obtained these stone slabs would hand them over to their family and use them to build a Divine City.
Only a Divine City could continuously earn Shenwu Coins. It was a waste to use these stone slabs as weapons.
After Sky Sword found the stone slab, he did not pull it out from the ground. The joy in his eyes gradually changed and a hint of hunger and greed flashed in the depths of his eyes. He then hugged the stone slab and began to gnaw on it!
The origins of all the stone slabs in the Divine Territory were unknown.
The golden Sanskrit that he had deciphered did not have any clues about these stone slabs.
Moreover, these stone slabs could not be refined or cut.
Over countless Divine Epochs, not even a single Ten Commandments Stone Slab had been destroyed!
However, Sky Sword bit down on the stone slab and actually bit off a piece of it. He put it in his mouth and chewed quickly before forcefully swallowing it.
The fire extinguished and the black and yellow centipedes crawled forward again. Seeing this, Sky Sword lit another fire extinguisher and continued to eat the Hundred Commandments Stone Slab …
"Ah!"
A series of screams rang out in the Bone Jade City.
After those intermediate True Gods entered the city, they were attacked by those black and red centipedes.
The poison of these centipedes was extremely terrifying. Once bitten, even an intermediate True God would not be able to last more than a few breaths …
In the blink of an eye, more than twenty intermediate True Gods were poisoned and died.
Countless centipedes formed a sea of black and red, blocking their path of retreat.
Their only way out was the path that Sky Sword had opened with the fire extinguisher. The remaining True Gods could only mindlessly charge toward the center of the Bone Jade City.
In the end …
Only one intermediate True God managed to shake off the centipedes and charged toward the central pavilion of the Divine City, passing through the wall.
When he lowered his head, he saw an unbelievable scene.
"Sky Sword, you, what are you eating …"
Half of the Hundred Commandments Slate had already been gnawed away by the Sky Sword. He raised his head and smiled, "Slate, do you want some?"
"You, who exactly are you?!" That middle True God seemed to have realized something.
"So what if you know? You can't go back anyway …" Sky Sword shook his head and continued to gnaw on the stone slab.

Chapter 1982
After a short while, Sky Sword finished swallowing the stone slab, dusted his hands, and stood up.
Looking at the corpse of the middle-level True God, his heroic eyes revealed a hint of helplessness …
Everything that had happened before him was contrary to his nature.
But since he'd already stepped onto this path, his conviction had already transformed into iron, and it was impossible for him to be shaken in the slightest. Everything he did was for the sake of his goal, and he would mercilessly eliminate any obstacles along the way!
"From a macro perspective, all of you … can be considered to have sacrificed yourselves for this broken ship," he muttered.
Just as he was about to leave, an extremely soft and weak voice suddenly came from behind him.
"Save, save me …"
Hearing this voice, Sky Sword's eyes flashed, and he frowned.
He suddenly turned around and saw a small hole in the wall, and it was from this small hole that a weak voice came.
"Advanced Breath Concealment Technique? Or a special Breath Concealment Treasure? "
With his keen insight, if there was a living person on the other side of the patio, it would be impossible for him to miss it, unless that person's concealment technique was extremely ingenious.
As he turned around, his eyes revealed a hint of killing intent, and he lightly patted the wall.
The wall collapsed …
In the mezzanine of the wall, there was a space half the height of a person, and it was in this space that a fifteen-year-old girl was curled up.
This girl was wearing a thin veil, and she hugged her legs as she trembled. There were many abrasions on her arms and legs, and there was a lot of dust on them. Her face was extremely beautiful and cute, especially her pair of eyes that were unusually large. However, her pupils were lifeless, as if she had experienced extreme fear, and she looked weak, like an abandoned doll.
"This thin veil …"
He reached out and took off the thin veil covering her body, and gently rubbed it with his hand. "It is indeed a very rare treasure, and even a first-tier family might not have it …"
"Save me, I beg you …"
The girl begged again.
"You saw it?" Sky Sword stared at the girl and asked.
The girl pursed her lips, and she realized something, and quickly shook her head. "I didn't see anything!"
Hearing this, Sky Sword stared at her and smiled, then covered her body with the thin veil. "You're lying, and I can't save you. Goodbye."
Then, Sky Sword gently retreated, leaped down, and climbed up the well, disappearing from her sight …
The girl's body stiffened even more. She wrapped her arms around her knees and pressed her dusty legs tightly together. Her eyes were filled with helplessness. She pursed her lips tightly, afraid that she would cry out loud.
She shouldn't have asked this person for help …
He actually ate the Hundred Commandments Slate. Moreover, he didn't have the heart to save them. He could have saved the cloaked man.
Now that he had taken away the Illusory Feather Muslin, he could no longer conceal his presence. Those terrifying insects …
"Sha sha sha sha sha …"
A sound that made her heart palpitate came from above the patio.
That voice was like the footsteps of the Grim Reaper, approaching her bit by bit.
"Master Huo is dead. Guard Jian is dead. I will die too …"
She was terrified.
Three days ago, they were still reunited, and she was still the apple of their eye, surrounded by everyone. Now, she had to face this kind of fear alone.
"Sha sha sha …"
Those black and red centipedes crawled down the wall again. They slowly crawled toward the girl through the hole created by Sky Sword.
She closed her eyes and comforted herself in her heart. Perhaps death wasn't as scary as she thought …
At this moment.
"Pata …"
Something small fell to the bottom of the well.
"Crackle crackle crackle …"
The girl opened her eyes. She saw a bright spark. Those sparks were like fireworks, constantly emitting small crackling sounds.
"Sha sha sha sha …"
The moment that fire spark was thrown into the well, all the centipedes were like ants on a hot pan. They avoided the fire spark and frantically fled in all directions. In the blink of an eye, the densely packed centipedes in the well were all gone.
The girl was slightly stunned. She stared blankly at the gently crackling fire spark.
"Dong …"
Sky Sword appeared in front of her again. He sighed lightly and extended his hand toward the girl.
"You, you saved me?"
She was very surprised to see him return.
"You don't want to live?" Sky Sword asked.
"But I saw it …" She was very shocked to see Sky Sword swallow the Hundred Commandments Slate. What kind of person was he to be able to eat this indestructible slate?
"Very honest." Sky Sword extended his hand and pulled her up. He gently pulled her over and carried her on his back. Then, he used the tip of his foot to lightly flick the fire spark. The fire spark tumbled and landed in his mouth.
Then, the girl saw the surrounding scenery fly by. She shuttled through the fallen city at an extremely fast speed. Her slender legs swayed with the wind in his arms.
"What's your name?" Sky Sword asked with the fire spark in his mouth.
"I'm Dongfang Yingqing," she said.
Sky Sword was stunned. He slowed down his running speed. "You're from the Dongfang family? Main family or branch family? "
Just like Han Jia, the main family of the Dongfang family migrated to the floating island.
However, the situation of the two families was slightly different.
The Dongfang family relied on the Fusang Divine Tree. The family's roots were very large.
The main family led by Dongfang Chunjun was indeed the largest family. However, after the main family moved to the floating island, the Dongfang family's branch family that stayed on the Fusang Divine Tree was also a large family. There were three Dongfang family Saints in the Fusang Divine Tree. This was also the reason why the Dongfang family's foundation was far superior to the other families.
"Branch family …" Dongfang Yingqing replied.
Sky Sword's eyes narrowed slightly. A trace of regret appeared on his face. The killing intent in his heart surfaced again.
He thought she was a girl from the He family. He didn't expect her to be a direct subordinate of the Dongfang family.
He shouldn't have saved this girl. He had to kill her.
He brought Dongfang Yingqing away from the Bone Jade Divine City. They arrived at the edge of the sinkhole and put her down …
Dongfang Yingqing seemed to have sensed something. Her large eyes blinked. Sorrow appeared once again. She looked weakly at Sky Sword. "You want to kill me, right?"
"Mn," Sky Sword replied expressionlessly.
If it was someone from a small family, Sky Sword might be able to save her life.
However, the situation was completely different for someone from the Dongfang family.
Dongfang Yingqing revealed a miserable expression. She looked at Sky Sword with her expressive eyes. She saw him reach out and place his hand on her forehead. She could feel the coldness from Sky Sword's fingertips.
"I, I swear on my mental demons …"
"I'll always be with you, okay?"
"I won't resist. I won't reveal your secret either."
She kept begging.
He cultivated the Divine Dao of Justice and pursued the supreme belief of justice. However, there was absolute justice and limited justice. Even in the "Dao Battle", there was no correct conclusion.
In terms of limited justice, he had to kill her. Because if he didn't die, he might save even more people.
In terms of absolute justice, he had no reason to kill her …
Sky Sword looked at her eyes. He was a bit confused.
She didn't say anything else. She just looked at him pitifully.
"Sigh …"
Sky Sword sighed. He was a bit distressed that she had given him such a difficult problem. In the end, he said, "Follow me. Swear on your mental demons that I won't kill you."

Chapter 1983
After the transit of the sun, the atmosphere on the floating island became subtle and grim.
There weren't many True Gods who saw Dongfang Chunjun's rage, but the events of the transit festival quickly spread to the various floating islands, turning into all sorts of rumors.
"So the anonymous person's identity was exposed. He's actually Luo Xiao's son!"
"He's really bold. Not only did he sneak around the Jiang family's supervision, but he actually went straight to the divine realm!"
"And he actually stole a jade seal from the hands of so many Paragons. It's said that the jade seal is a level four faith treasure!"
"What's his relationship with Shi Qiaoqiao? She actually silenced the divine realm for him …"
"Don't forget, that kid still controls the lives of all the Paragons. He's just a lower True God. This move is really heaven-defying!"
Most of the time, the relationships between these rich families were relatively stable.
The numerous True Gods were like hardworking ants, shuttling back and forth between the forbidden area and the floating island.
This incident quickly swept across the floating islands on the Sea of Time.
Not only did it involve Luo Zheng, but it also involved Han Jia's secondary sage.
Everyone wanted to see how Dongfang Chunjun would react in the end, how Han Jia would deal with this matter, and whether the Paragons Luo Zheng took away could return safely.
If those Paragons were really killed by Luo Zheng, this incident would probably turn into a huge earthquake.
Not long after, Mu Xuerong, Dongfang Taiqing, Han Tianxiao, and the other Paragons finally returned.
Han Tianxiao had just stepped onto Han Jia's floating island when he was summoned by Han Jia's Sage Emperor.
The next day …
The three Han Jia sisters had already entered the main hall of the Purple Soul Palace.
All the female disciples of the Purple Soul Palace entered the spacious hall one after another.
These days, the disciples of the Purple Soul Palace had also heard all sorts of crazy rumors.
At first, they didn't believe it, but after hearing some solid evidence and seeing no trace of Aunt Han Jiu, they gradually understood that Aunt Han Jiu had indeed escaped with Luo Tianxing …
The Purple Soul Palace existed because of Aunt Han Jiu's existence, and also because of the strength of the secondary sage.
Now that Aunt Han Jiu had offended Dongfang Chunjun, there was probably no possibility of her returning to the floating island. They spent these days in fear and unease.
Han Liusu was draped in a floor-length velvet skirt, expressionlessly gazing at the Purple Soul Palace's entrance.
Han Bi Luo and Han Chu Yue also did not have the slightest trace of happiness on their faces, one on the left and one on the right, tightly grasping Han Liusu's hands.
Not long after, the Purple Soul Hall's disciples gathered.
Soon after, Han Tianxiao appeared on the highest throne in the Purple Soul Palace and announced in a calm voice, "Because the Han Family's second sage betrayed the Han Family, the Purple Soul Palace's 30,000 disciples also view him as a traitor! However, the Purple Soul Palace will be disbanded from today onwards and will be incorporated into the Saint Emperor City. All female disciples of the Purple Soul Palace will be expelled from the floating island within the next few days! "
When the female disciples in the Purple Soul Palace heard Han Tianxiao's words, there was a period of silence.
Smiling in the sky only coldly glanced at them, sneered, and then disappeared from everyone's eyes.
After Heavenly Smile left, a sobbing sound came from the Purple Soul Palace. Immediately after, the other female disciples also began to cry. Soon, tens of thousands of female disciples were crying …
Han Meng stood at the very front of these people, her expression sorrowful and her heart filled with self-blame.
If she hadn't brought Luo Tianxing to the floating island back then, perhaps this wouldn't have happened, and Aunt Han Jiu wouldn't have been implicated.
But thinking about it carefully, if it hadn't been for Luo Tianxing, the Purple Soul Palace probably still wouldn't have been able to escape the fate of disbanding …
She clenched her fists, a nameless fury surging in her heart.
"Master didn't betray the Han Family!"
Granny Yue Ying, who was in the corner, used her ear-piercing voice to shout.
"Right, Master didn't betray the Han Family!"
"How could Master betray the Han Family? She's the Han Family's second sage!"
"Even if you want us to leave, you can't slander Master like this!"
A few female disciples were furious, but they couldn't find a target. In the end, their gazes locked onto Han Liusu.
The three sisters were considered the Han Family's most direct descendants. At this time, they naturally became their target.
Seeing their angry expressions, Han Liusu lightly raised her hand, indicating for everyone to quiet down. But how could they listen to Han Liusu? A few female disciples, in their agitation, even started cursing Han Liusu and the Han Family …
Seeing that the situation was about to spiral out of control, Granny Yue Ying loudly said, "Everyone, quiet down. Let's see what the eldest princess wants to say!"
Although everyone in the Purple Soul Palace was afraid of Granny Yue Ying, everyone knew that she was loyal to the Purple Soul Palace and Aunt Jiu. With her authority, the girls would still listen to her.
When the Purple Soul Palace quieted down, Han Liusu softly said, "You're right. Aunt Jiu didn't betray the Han Family!"
Hearing Han Liusu's words, the female disciples were stunned.
"It's the Han Family that betrayed Aunt Jiu," Han Liusu softly said.
If the Han Family really had such a heroic spirit, they would naturally fight against Dongfang Chunjun.
But father's subservience to the Dongfang family had thoroughly disappointed Han Liusu …
"There's no point in staying with a family like this. Since you've all been expelled from the Han Family today, I'll leave with you!" Han Liusu continued.
When Han Chuyue and Han Biluo heard this, they didn't immediately react.
They were all direct descendants of the Han Family. How could they leave just like that?
The female disciples of the Purple Soul Palace were all moved.
No matter what, Han Liusu was the eldest princess of the Han Family. She was also one of the Han Family's iconic geniuses. She wanted to leave the Han Family too?
Actually, from the moment Aunt Hanjiu escaped with Luo Zheng, she already had this thought in her heart.
She'd held it in until today …
"Big sister …"
"Big sister Liusu …"
Han Biluo and Han Chuyue both looked at her in surprise.
… …
After the Purple Soul Palace was disbanded, all the female disciples packed up and left.
Han Liusu did indeed take action.
Even Han Chuyue and Han Biluo, on a whim, disguised themselves like Han Liusu, wanting to blend in with the other female disciples and quietly leave the floating island.
Unfortunately, before they could leave the floating island, they were stopped by the Han Family.
At the same time, they were told to keep a tight watch and not leave the floating island for a short period of time.
… …
In the side hall of the Immortal Residence …
Aunt Jiu's light purple palace dress moved without wind.
She muttered those words over and over again in her mouth ….
A mere ten thousand words, to a quasi-sage, could be firmly memorized with a single glance. But with a thought, these True Intent mental cultivation methods would disappear without a trace, unable to be memorized.
If you really wanted to memorize this True Intent mental cultivation method, you had to recite it word by word. The number of words you persisted in memorizing would be the number of words you could memorize!
Even with a quasi-sage's talent, wanting to completely memorize this True Intent of Dao in a short period of time was not an easy task.
On the first day, she could only recite up to 1700 words.
On the second day, she could recite up to 3200 words … …
On the third day … …
The more words there were, the harder it was.
The hard part was not reciting them, but comprehending the True Intent of this mental cultivation method.
After the Divine Dao, in the vast sea of nothingness, the True Intent of Dao was like a small boat that could lead her to a deeper place. Comprehending the True Intent of Dao was the process of climbing onto that small boat.
On the fifteenth day, she could already recite 4700 words in one go, which was more or less half of the True Intent mental cultivation method … …
And when she recited to this point, her eyes suddenly flashed with a blazing luster, and a trace of joy appeared on her face.

Chapter 1984
"Three thousand Divine Daos, they all use the laws to enter the Dao. It can be said that they use the laws to the extreme, and the Divine Daos that are derived are all strange …"
"But Dao techniques, in the end, cannot escape the word 'nature' …"
"Dao techniques are natural …"
At this time, Aunt Hanjiu had already comprehended quite a bit.
Her slender fingers gently moved, and the source of power, and strands of purple qi slowly dissipated, filling the side hall.
In the Divine Realm, Aunt Hanjiu had absolute authority in the Purple Qi Divine Dao.
The Purple Qi Divine Dao was naturally suited to set up formations.
When the predecessors cultivated the Purple Qi Divine Dao, they used the Purple Qi to nurture all kinds of creatures, and set up all kinds of formations.
Aunt Hanjiu followed this line of thought, and with Luo Xiao's help, she developed it.
The four sword variations of the God Slaughtering Sword Formation were the foundation, and Luo Xiao laid the foundation.
The 32 sword variations were even more complicated, even sharper …
The 96 sword was the upper limit of the God Slaughtering Sword Formation, and also the most perfect form of the God Slaughtering Sword Formation. The variations and killing intent within were even more terrifying.
Besides the sword formation, Aunt Hanjiu also had the [Thousand Beast Soaring Dragon Formation], [Bird Seizing Dream Formation], [Purple Spirit Strife] and other formations.
In order to display the Purple Qi Divine Dao to the extreme, Aunt Hanjiu painstakingly set up an even bigger formation, wanting to perfectly combine the Purple Qi Divine Dao and the source of power …
This was a huge project.
The variations were even higher by several orders of magnitude.
Even for Aunt Hanjiu, wanting to set up this formation was extremely difficult, so after fumbling for so long, there was not much progress …
"If you persist, it will not be easy, if you want to act, it will be restricted, if you want to think, it will be empty …"
"If you don't persist, you can't, don't read …"
"Comprehend by yourself."
Aunt Hanjiu no longer controlled the Purple Qi, and no longer traced the source of power, but allowed it to float in the side hall …
She muttered to herself as a strange thought popped up in her mind.
The invisible power and the Purple Qi slowly flowed in the side hall, sometimes the Purple Qi would wrap around the power, sometimes the power would push the Purple Qi away.
The Purple Qi was like the clouds in the sky, sometimes it would become ten thousand horses galloping, sometimes it would become gods and evil spirits, all kinds of strange appearances.
"Formations are profound and ingenious, but in the end, they are formed from thoughts."
"In terms of profoundness, what in this world can compare to the profoundness of nature …"
"Hiss hiss hiss …"
At this moment, the purple Qi and the power began to fuse together. It no longer had any shape, and only slowly circled around Aunt Hanjiu.
"This is also a formation …"
Auntie Hanjiu's eyes glowed with a strange light as she watched this scene.
The formation in front of her didn't seem to be in shape, but if she changed it slightly, it would be filled with killing intent. If she brought out an even greater source of power and more purple qi, the power would be even more unimaginable.
"The name of this formation." Aunt Hanjiu thought for a moment, then frowned. The formless formation suddenly lost its killing intent and became a mess, without any natural rhythm.
She patted her forehead and said with a smile, "This is the will of nature. If you forcefully add a name to it, there will be form and restriction, and it will lose nature …"
Afterwards, Aunt Han Jiu dispersed the purple qi and the power source. She gently reached out and carefully put away the true meaning heart mantra that Luo Nian had written. Then she pushed open the door to the side hall.
Half a month had already passed since she began to comprehend …
Opening the door, a ray of morning light shone in. Reflected in her eyes, the world had changed. The trees, the sunlight, the fish in the pond, all of them contained the Dao of nature, the true meaning was infinite.
Only the pavilions, the carved beams and painted rafters, these man-made objects, all of them had no artistic conception …
"Huhuhu …"
On the platform facing the pond, a white robed Luo Nian was dancing with his spear.
Luo Nian and his spear did not have any natural meaning, but when Auntie Hanjiu saw his spear piercing through the air, her face was filled with surprise.
Luo Nian's spear skills were not bad, but in Auntie Hanjiu's eyes, they were nothing. But when the spear pierced out, there was a trace of the true meaning of nature, and it was because of this that Luo Nian's spear skills were extraordinary!
"Yes, he translated the mantra and can recite a thousand words. Of course he has some understanding of the Dao. He's only sixteen years old. Although his cultivation is not high, to be able to comprehend a trace of the true meaning of the Dao, when he grows up he'll be even more powerful than his father …" Auntie Hanjiu's eyes were filled with admiration.
She walked forward, but saw Luo Nian's angry face. "Nian 'er, where's your father?"
"Sleeping!"
Luo Nian spat out three words, then continued to wave the spear in his hand. The spear was like a wheel, whistling through the air.
He'd thought Immortal Mansion would be more lively with his father's return.
He hadn't expected it to be the opposite. Whether it was his mother, Auntie Xun, or Auntie Yun, they all circled around his father, not bothering to pay him any attention.
Especially when Auntie Xun no longer accompanied him in his spear practice …
"Still sleeping?"
Auntie Hanjiu looked up at the sky. Although it was morning, the sun was already high in the sky.
When he thought of Luo Nian's angry look, he understood in his heart. A helpless smile appeared on his face.
It turned out Luo Zheng had just returned to the Immortal Mansion, and his wives were all stunning beauties. That was why he hadn't had a good rest.
But, since they had all cultivated the True Divinity Body, their energy was nearly limitless. Therefore, they shouldn't be unable to get up from their beds …
"Young men really don't know how to control themselves …"
She sighed inwardly, then she leapt onto the stage. With a slight twist of her waist, she said with a smile, "I'll accompany you in your spear practice!"
Luo Nian looked at Auntie Hanjiu with a strange expression, "You don't even have a weapon, how are you going to practice?"
"Just attack me." Auntie Hanjiu said with an indifferent tone.
Without any hesitation, Luo Nian waved his spear and thrust it toward Auntie Hanjiu like a poisonous snake.
With Luo Zheng's current cultivation, the spear skill he displayed was nothing.
A few months ago, Xun Du didn't even bother to spar with Luo Nian. After all, a fight between a World Lord and a Divine Core warrior wasn't like the difference between an adult and a child, it was like the difference between a human and a speck of dust.
Therefore, every time Xun Du casually swung his spear, he would knock Luo Nian's spear away, then lightly knock him down.
But after Luo Nian had deciphered the six golden Sanskrit symbols, the situation had changed!
Without any warning, Luo Nian had actually stabbed Xun with his spear!
Although Xun Du had suppressed his cultivation to the Divine Core realm, and it was an unexpected attack, it was more than enough to shock Xun Du.
Therefore, Xun Du started taking this' little opponent 'seriously, and started sparing with all his heart.
In the beginning, Xun Du was still able to suppress Luo Nian with his attacks.
But as Luo Nian was able to 'read' more and more True Intent cultivation methods, and his understanding of the True Intent was getting deeper and deeper, he was now able to fight evenly with Xun Du.
When Luo Nian thrust his spear forward, Auntie Hanjiu immediately turned around.
Her movement wasn't fast, and she didn't even think about it. Her body moved like a gentle breeze, and she easily dodged the spear.
"Eh …"
Luo Nian could tell that there was a special True Intent of Dao hidden in Auntie Hanjiu's body as well. But, the True Intent she possessed was much more profound than his.
"Again!"
Sparring like this would be of great benefit to Luo Nian.
He already had a basic understanding of the True Intent of Dao, and now, he was able to further comprehend it from Auntie Hanjiu's movement skill.
A mighty Secondary Saint was actually sparring with a Divine Core warrior from a second-ranked creature, this was indeed a marvelous sight.
"The Dao of nature, turning the eternal into the eternal, all laws are natural, not by comprehending them …" Luo Nian kept thrusting his spear forward. As he gained some understanding, he couldn't help but start reciting the True Intent cultivation method.
Hearing Luo Nian reciting the True Intent cultivation method, Auntie Hanjiu smiled. Her lips moved slightly, and she too started reciting the True Intent cultivation method …

Chapter 1985
In the residence's sleeping quarters.
Xun's face was resting on Luo Zheng's chest, breathing evenly and slowly.
On the other side, Ning Yudie was curled up like a baby under his arm, sleeping soundly.
With their cultivation, they didn't need to sleep to replenish their energy. For most of the year, they just needed to sit cross-legged in meditation to keep their spirits high.
But these days, it was different.
Sometimes, even sleeping had a special meaning.
Luo Zheng's arm moved slightly, and Xun immediately opened her eyes. She looked at Luo Zheng with her red eyes, like a vigilant kitten.
On the other side, Ning Yudie also woke up. The moment her soul recovered, she remembered the absurdity of last night. Her face couldn't help but blush. She was extremely embarrassed. "Husband, yesterday I …"

Hearing this, Ning Yudie's expression became very unnatural. She opened her mouth, but didn't know what to say. She could only tightly wrap her body with the blanket.

Xun's face revealed a mischievous expression …

After he tidied up and looked over, he heard the whistling of spears.
After turning the corner, he saw Luo Nian's spear whistling, constantly greeting Aunt Hanjiu.
"Aunt Jiu has come out of seclusion?" Luo Zheng's eyes brightened.

Aunty Hanjiu glanced indifferently at Luo Zheng and shook her head. "It's still early. I've only comprehended half of the true meaning of the Dao … but you'll be free and unfettered once you return to the immortal estate."
Hearing this, Ning Yudie was immediately embarrassed.
Luo Zheng smiled. He probably knew that Aunt Hanjiu was going to use the tone of an elder to teach him a lesson.
At this time, a violent vibration suddenly came from outside the immortal mansion!
"Dong dong dong dong dong …"
It was as if there were countless behemoths running madly outside.
Luo Zheng's face suddenly changed. Aunt Hanjiu's gaze also turned towards the immortal mansion's main entrance.
"Master, it seems that a large number of monsters are rushing over!" Afo immediately informed Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes. "Let's go take a look!"
Luo Zheng was quite confident in the immortal mansion his master had built.
First of all, that layer of enchantment was almost unbreakable. It could even separate the immortal mansion from the realm of the gods, ensuring that the people inside the enchantment would not be reduced to true essence!
Although the monsters in this Takeshi Xuan forest were powerful, they were simply unable to enter the immortal mansion.
Luo Zheng took the lead and rushed out of the immortal mansion. He stood in front of the pale pink enchantment.
The huge trees in the Takeshi Xuan forest were constantly being broken, falling down one by one. Bursts of chaotic footsteps could be heard, as if there were thousands of soldiers and horses galloping …
"What is that!" Ning Yudie followed closely behind Luo Zheng. Her small face was somewhat nervous.
She had stayed in the immortal mansion for so long. Occasionally she would look at the realm of the gods from within the enchantment. Occasionally she would see some terrifying monsters, but those monsters simply did not dare to approach the enchantment. This immortal mansion was like a paradise, maintaining its tranquility.
"I'm not sure. It should be the monsters in the Takeshi Xuan forest rushing over," Luo Zheng said with a frown.
What he was worried about was not the monsters.
Now his and Aunt Hanjiu's identities were extremely sensitive.
If Dongfang Chunjun discovered that they were hiding in this immortal mansion, the situation would be even more troublesome.
"Boom!"
The tree trunk closest to the edge of the enchantment suddenly shattered.
Two people could be seen drilling out of the tree trunk, rushing towards the immortal mansion at an extremely fast speed …
Behind the two of them was a large group of giant apes!
Each of the giant apes was seven to eight hundred meters tall, and they were like moving mountains. Every step they took would create a huge crater, as if there was an earthquake. Such a huge body, but it was still so agile. Using both hands and feet, they madly chased after the two people!
The person in the lead had long flowing hair and a broad face. As he ran, he constantly shouted, "Damn it, we've poked the monkey's nest! I already said to go to the immortal mansion first, but now we've brought a bunch of trouble for no reason! "
Behind this person was a child.
The boy was sitting on the back of a green ox. The green ox was also running wildly with its hooves.
"Those golden-haired creatures have caught up," the child said calmly. At the same time, he stood on the green ox and waved the fishing rod in his hand. The child's young eyes narrowed slightly, flashing with murderous intent.
"Whoosh!"
At this moment, a few golden lights suddenly flashed among the group of giant apes.
Several pocket-sized golden apes jumped down from the backs of the giant apes, pouncing towards the two people.
This so-called "pocket-sized" was only relative to the seven or eight hundred feet tall giant apes. In reality, these four golden apes were more than thirty feet tall, much taller than the child and the long-haired man!
"Hmph!"
The child snorted coldly, then suddenly swung the fishing rod in his hand!
"Swish swish swish swish!"
A faint golden fishing line suddenly shot out!
"Pu pu pu …"
Of the four golden apes that pounced down, three of them had bloody wounds on their bodies. Blood directly exploded from their bodies.
Given the child's strength, even a Paragon True God would be severely injured by his golden fishing line. However, when this fishing line cut through the golden apes, it only left a small wound on their bodies!
Moreover, one of the golden apes relied on its powerful instincts to avoid the child's fishing line and pounced in front of him.
In the instant that the golden ape reached out with its large furry hand to grab the child, the child calmly spat out a single word: "Dodge."
Then, the child and the green ox disappeared from where they were …
"Wait for me!"
When the long-haired man saw the child use spatial teleportation to appear in front of him, his face revealed an indignant expression. As he leapt up, he also mysteriously disappeared from where he was.
As the two of them continuously flashed, they were getting closer and closer to the barrier of the immortal residence!
"This, this is … Second Senior Brother?"
Luo Zheng, who was standing behind the immortal residence, saw the two of them charge over and opened his eyes wide as he spoke.
Luo Zheng didn't recognize Jing Wuhuan.
However, on the Path of True Extinction, Luo Zheng had fought with Yu Shenfeng before, so he had a deep impression of this Second Senior Brother whose nickname was Xian An. Naturally, he recognized him at a glance.
Aunt Hanjiu revealed a faint smile on her face: "It is indeed Jing Wuhuan and Yu Shenfeng."
"Can they come in? Do you need me to open the barrier? "Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng instantly became anxious.

Chapter 1986
Now, looking at their momentum, Luo Zheng was also very conflicted.
When Shi Qiaoqiao left the Immortal Mansion, the barrier couldn't stop her.
But Luo Zheng didn't know whether she broke the barrier or used some special method to get through.
If a Perfect True God could directly tear open this barrier, the divine territory would be connected to the Immortal Mansion. The consequences would be unimaginable.
While he was worrying, Afo, who rushed out with Luo Zheng, said, "Master, don't worry, they can freely enter this barrier."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng calmed down.
His close relatives were all in the Immortal Mansion. They were all the most important to him. Naturally, he wouldn't allow the slightest accident.
The two people were getting closer and closer to the barrier, but they didn't slow down in the slightest.
The second senior brother, who was rushing in front, suddenly flipped his hand and a small token appeared in his hand.
"Xun" token!
Luo Zheng saw the token at a glance.
Kan, Li, Zhen, Xun, Gen, Dui, Kun, Qian. These eight tokens each had different functions. The "Xun" token was the first one that Luo Zheng obtained. The only function of this token seemed to be to avoid the two huge puppets in the Immortal Mansion and safely pass through the Immortal Mansion.
After Luo Zheng obtained Afo's recognition, the "Xun" token didn't have much effect on Luo Zheng. Moreover, he entered the Immortal Mansion several times and directly passed through the spatial node in the valley in the eastern region. Later, after obtaining the "Gen" token, he could directly return to the Immortal Mansion.
He naturally didn't think that the "Xun" token had such an ability.
When Second Senior Brother took out the token, he directly crashed into the enchantment. This indestructible enchantment fluctuated slightly, and his entire body was sucked in!
At the same time, the boy riding the green ox also raised a "Xun" token and entered the barrier.
"Dong dong dong dong …"
The apes chasing after them arrived in the blink of an eye, stomping out large pits on the ground outside the barrier.
The golden apes seemed quite unwilling to give up. They pounded their chests madly and bared their fangs at the barrier, provoking the people inside ….
"Phew … These damned apes are really hard to deal with." The long-haired man panted a few times.
"Xian An, Ping Huan, long time no see." Fu went up to greet them, smiling at the long-haired man and the child.
Xian An was the nickname of Second Senior Brother Yu Shen Feng, and Ping Huan was the nickname of Eldest Senior Brother Jing Wu Huan. Afo was very familiar with them, and always called them by their nicknames.
Yu Shen Feng and Jing Wu Huan also greeted Afo. Their eyes were focused on Luo Zheng. Then they looked at Aunt Hanjiu, Ning Yudie, Xun, and the other girls behind Luo Zheng. Mu Mingxue and Su Lingyun also rushed over.
"This, isn't this Little Junior Brother? Shouldn't you be at Buzhou Spirit Mountain? How could he be in the Immortal Mansion! This … this is Han Jia's secondary saint! "Yu Shenfeng's eyes widened. He naturally recognized Luo Zheng.
Jing Wu Huan was also a bit stunned.
The two of them had started to act after hearing Yu Taibai's words on the floating island.
After leaving Gu Family's floating island, they didn't know what happened in the Sea of Time. Naturally, they didn't expect to meet Luo Zheng in the Immortal Mansion.
"Long time no see," Aunt Hanjiu nodded slightly.
Aunt Hanjiu, Jing Wu Huan, and Yu Shen Feng also knew each other.
"Eldest Senior Brother, Second Senior Brother, nice to meet you!" Luo Zheng cupped his hands in greeting.
Although he already knew of his two senior brothers' great reputations, this was the first time they met in the truest sense.
After greeting each other, Luo Zheng naturally invited his two senior brothers into the mansion for a chat.

Many Divine Cities in the Divine Dominion forbade fighting. If one fought, they would be punished by the stone slab. This wasn't a rare occurrence.
Jing Wuhuan smiled. "It's not like what you think. You can't fight in a non-offensive region. If you enter, you will be affected by a strange concept. You won't be able to fight others."
"Even Saints are no exception?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes, even Saints are no exception," Jing Wuhuan added. "You can't imagine that feeling now, but you'll know when you get there."
"A non-offensive region is quite unique …" Luo Zheng muttered.
Just as Jing Wuhuan had analyzed, the timing of his entry into the Realm of the Gods was very important. His father would definitely have made some calculations about it. If he calculated according to the timeline, his next step would be to participate in the Dao Struggle.
He was only worried that Yu Taibai and Luo Yan would think of this step.

…
The forbidden area of Buzhou Spirit Mountain was a vast area.
The tens of thousands of meters tall mountain range extended for hundreds of thousands of miles, circling around an unimaginably huge mountain.
That huge mountain was the famous Buzhou Spirit Mountain.
Most of the time, lower True Gods could only explore the surrounding mountain ranges.
In the middle of one of the mountain ranges …
In a lush ancient forest, there was a sudden violent shaking.
"Swish, swish, swish …"
Three figures shot out from the ancient forest and rushed down the mountain.
"Big Brother, let's go! I'll bring up the rear!"
One of the middle True Gods wielded a large saber. His entire body was riddled with wounds, and his snow-white robes were dyed red with blood. He looked extremely exhausted, but he was holding on by sheer willpower.
"Go! If we're leaving, we're leaving together! "
The other middle True God ordered.
The three middle True Gods followed the mountain ridge and charged down.
The ancient forest swayed again, and seven or eight bulky bulls drilled out of it.
These Barbarian Bulls had solid muscles all over their bodies. Their eyes were crimson red, and there were traces of black flames circulating around their sharp horns.
Although they were huge, they were abnormally agile on this mountain path. After they came out of the forest, they charged at the three middle True Gods.
These bulls were the third-level vicious beasts of Buzhou Spirit Mountain — Collapsing Bulls. They were widely distributed in Buzhou Spirit Mountain. They had violent temperaments, thick skin, and extremely strong hearts for grudges. Few True Gods were willing to provoke them.
The three middle True Gods were True Gods of the wealthy Bai family.
When they were exploring Buzhou Spirit Mountain, they had accidentally gotten separated from the main group. They had bumped into many obstacles along the way, and were in a very sorry state.
They had never imagined that they would provoke this herd of Collapsing Bulls. Unable to defeat them, they could only flee frantically.
After running for dozens of miles on the uneven mountain ridge, the middle True God in the lead suddenly stopped in his tracks.
"Second Brother, what are you doing!" The True God behind him asked.
"No, there's no way out …" The middle True God in the lead replied.
In front of them was a huge canyon …
If it was a canyon in an ordinary mountain range, it was nothing to a middle True God.
Although middle True Gods could not fly, these True Gods from wealthy families had some tricks up their sleeves. A height of tens of thousands of feet was not enough to make them afraid.
However, the canyon in Buzhou Spirit Mountain's forbidden area was a river without a source. If they fell into it, they could forget about climbing back up. If they jumped down, they would definitely die!
"Gulp, gulp …"
Those Collapsing Bulls quickly caught up.
They left behind footprints with traces of black flames as they closed in on the three middle True Gods of the Bai family.
"Big Brother, what should we do?"
"Jump down?"
"No, we'll definitely die if we jump down. Let's fight it out with these Collapsing Bulls. Maybe one of us can escape!"
As they spoke, the three of them took out their own life-saving treasures. Their eyes were red as they really planned to fight with these Collapsing Bulls.
However, after the three middle True Gods prepared for a while, they saw the herd of Collapsing Bulls walking in front of them. As they walked past, they would occasionally glare at them with their fiery red eyes. They did not attack …
The blood of the three True Gods slowly cooled down. They stared at each other, completely unable to understand what was happening in front of them.
"What's going on?"
"They're not attacking?"
"Collapsing Bulls don't have any spirituality or intelligence. This is not right …"
Just as the Bai family's True Gods were baffled, a clear and lively voice rang out.
"You … Which family's True Gods are you?"
A light pink cloth shoe lightly tapped on the mountain path. As the sleeves of her clothes fluttered, an agile figure instantly appeared. It was actually a young girl who looked to be seventeen or eighteen years old. She used the tip of her foot to tap on the back of the bull and used a graceful posture to look down on the three.
"You, who are you!"
The three middle True Gods of the Zhao family stared at the young girl on the bull's back. Their faces were filled with vigilance.
This young girl was admittedly a peerless beauty. Her pure face was brimming with a harmless and amiable smile. However, she suddenly appeared in such a situation. Furthermore, from the looks of it, these Collapsing Bulls seemed to be obeying her orders. No matter how one looked at it, it was extremely strange …
"Answer me. Which wealthy family are you from?" The young girl stared at the three and asked indifferently.
The situation before them was no longer up to the three middle True Gods to choose. They would die either way. No matter how strange it was, they would still die. The middle True God in the lead gritted his teeth and said, "We're from the Bai family! The wealthy Bai family! I'm Bai Xin. This is my second brother Bai Jin. That's my third brother … "
"I didn't ask for your names …"
The young girl did not expect this middle True God to open his mouth and introduce a whole bunch of people. Her face revealed a trace of helplessness. Her small eyebrows twitched slightly and she also emitted an enchanting aura. Her beauty was unparalleled.
"I just want to ask you a few things. If you can answer truthfully, I can guarantee that you can safely leave Buzhou Spiritual Mountain," the young girl added.
Hearing the young girl's words, the eyes of the three middle True Gods of the Bai family instantly lit up.
Although they did not know who this young girl was, since she could calm these rampaging Collapsing Bulls, she indeed had the ability to guarantee their safety!
"What is it? If this Bai knows, he will definitely tell you!" Bai Xin cupped his hands and said to the young girl.

Chapter 1991
"Something big happened on the floating island?"
Bai Xin was slightly taken aback.
Those who could enter the forbidden area were almost all true gods on the floating island.
From the way the girl asked, she didn't seem to know much about the floating island.
In other words, this girl had been staying on Buzhou Spiritual Mountain all this time?
Someone who could stay on Buzhou Spiritual Mountain all this time … The answer was already clear in his mind.
Buzhou Spiritual Mountain was now firmly in Yu Taibai's hands, which meant this girl should be on Yu Taibai's side!
The girl saw Bai Xin's flickering expression and knew what he was thinking.
The tip of her foot lightly stepped on Beng Niu's back, and her voice slowly turned cold. "Don't guess my identity. Tell me, what big thing happened?"
"Something big did happen on the floating island!" Bai Xin hurriedly said. "And, and it's related to the Luo family!"
Bai Xin already knew this girl was from the Luo family, and naturally understood what she was asking about. Moreover, there was only one big thing that happened on the floating island recently, so it wasn't hard to guess. Thus, his heart calmed down a little.
Their Bai family and the Luo family didn't have any grudges. As long as he answered this girl's question honestly, their lives would be guaranteed this time.
"The Luo family!"
Just as Bai Xin expected, the girl's eyes revealed a trace of anxiety. It seemed like she was very concerned about the Luo family.
"Rumor has it that one of Luo Xiao's sons snuck in from the universe and entered the floating island. He became an anonymous person and obtained terrifying battle results in the Arena of the Gods …" Bai Xin said.
After the Tower of Dusk Pageant, this matter had been privately spread by the true gods who had participated in the pageant. It wasn't really a secret.
When the girl heard this, her face was filled with unconcealable concern.
She had been waiting for her brother …
The hint she had asked Yu Taibai to pass to Luo Zheng was that she hoped Luo Zheng would come to Buzhou Spiritual Mountain.
Logically speaking, after the floating island's ranking, Luo Zheng should be able to enter the Cloud Dragon Stream with his ability. However, almost a month had passed, and she still hadn't seen him!
[Something must have happened …]
She might not be able to find out about this kind of thing.
For example, once Luo Zheng's identity was exposed, he would most likely be dealt with by the Mu family or the Dongfang family in private. Ordinary True Gods would not be able to find out.
However, her heart was filled with worry, and she couldn't help it. Thus, she used a scheme to trap the Bai Family's True Gods here.
She had never imagined that the Bai Family's True Divinity would clearly reveal what she wanted to hear. How could she remain calm?
"How is he now?" the girl asked again.
"Later, he was invited to participate in the High Sun Festival and was almost killed by Mu Xuerong," Bai Xin added.
Hearing this, the girl's heart trembled slightly.
"However, I heard that he was stopped by a Perfected Real God from the Dongfang Family. Then, he somehow snatched a treasure from a group of Perfected Real Gods and left the forbidden land of Sea of Time …" Bai Xin said with a puzzled expression.
Many true gods on the floating island felt that this rumor was nonsense when they heard it. Many true gods even raised their doubts.
For a mere lower True Divinity to snatch away a supreme treasure from the hands of a group of True Divinities was simply unimaginable. As for what happened in the imperial jade seal, only those True Divinities knew. Since those True Divinities wouldn't speak of the process, it was naturally impossible for ordinary True Divinities to know.
"And then?" the girl asked again.
She knew very well what it would mean if her brother's identity was exposed.
The Saints of the Grand Alliance would never let her brother go.
"Then, the controller of Soul Desolation, Shi Xiaoqiao, went silent. The Han Family's Secondary Sage carried her and used Space Travel to escape from the Dongfang Shenghuang." Bai Xin shrugged.
Many people had seen this scene, and no one doubted it.
"Escaped …"
Hearing this news, the young girl's ever-changing face finally settled down. She gently exhaled and a serene smile appeared on her face.
"Can you let us go now?" Bai Xin asked.
The girl nodded. Then, three Collapsing Bulls slowly walked toward the three True Gods.
The three Bai Family's Intermediate True Gods looked at the three Collapsing Bulls and became nervous. They were afraid that the girl would silence them.
They heard the girl say softly, "These Collapsing Bulls can carry you to the entrance …"
As soon as she finished speaking, she leaped into the air like a fairy dancing in the air before landing in the canyon behind the three True Gods. Then, she landed freely on the ground, her clothes fluttering in the wind …
When she was a hundred feet away from the bottomless water at the bottom of the canyon, her falling momentum suddenly stopped. Then, like a nightingale, she flew towards the direction of Buzhou Spirit Mountain.

On the south side of Buzhou Spiritual Mountain.
Someone had built a series of spacious stone houses here.

The two uncles in the stone house were Second Uncle Luo Bingquan and Third Uncle Luo Junyi.
They looked no different from Second Uncle and Third Uncle of the Luo Family in the Eastern Region's Chongyang County.
Luo Yan was shocked when she was brought to Buzhou Spiritual Mountain by Taibai Yu for the first time. When she met these two uncles, she didn't look happy for a long time.
It turned out that when her father was setting up the Eastern Region, he had created two "substitutes" for her uncles, borrowing their names and appearances.
As for what happened in the Eastern Region's Luo Family, the two uncles in Buzhou Spiritual Mountain naturally didn't know anything …
It could be said that the two of them were inexplicably blamed by their big brother. So when they first met their little niece, Luo Yan was puzzled for a long time about the hostility they faintly showed. After Luo Yan explained, the two couldn't help but laugh, saying that Luo Xiao's move was really unkind.
"Luo Yan's talent and ability are not inferior to Luo Zheng's … the only thing she lacks is experience," Taibai Yu said indifferently. "Some things are better for her to do by herself."
"Yes!" Luo Yan said to her two uncles with slight dissatisfaction. "Uncles, you can't worry too much about Luo Yan. Like this, Luo Yan will never be able to grow!"
Luo Bingquan and Luo Junyi could only shake their heads and smile. Then, Second Uncle asked, "Then what did you find out?"
"Big Brother had an accident on the floating island," Luo Yan said calmly. "His identity was seen through by Mu Xuerong."
"What?"

Second Uncle and Third Uncle's faces changed.
Taibai Yu also showed a trace of concern. He could see that Luo Yan didn't have any sad emotions. It seemed that the situation wasn't too bad.
"Later, he was saved by Aunt Hanjiu," Luo Yan replied.
Hearing this, the others let out a sigh of relief.
Then, Luo Yan recounted the news he had heard.
After speaking, Luo Yan said with a worried face, "I don't know where Aunt Hanjiu took Big Brother …"

Chapter 1992
Luo Zheng's failure to enter Buzhou Spirit Mountain caused the situation to become complicated. Yu Taibai needed to redo his calculations.
Then, he had Luo Yan repeat the information he'd gathered.
"The treasure that Paragons fought over in the forbidden area must be a supreme treasure of faith!"
