When Second Uncle and Third Uncle heard that Luo Zheng had snatched a treasure from a group of Paragons, their faces were filled with shock.
Yu Taibai's eyes also revealed astonishment. "With Luo Zheng's strength, it's almost impossible for him to snatch a treasure from a Paragon. He must have encountered some other great opportunity …"
Luo Yan's face revealed a noncommittal expression. His lips curled up slightly as he raised his head and said to Yu Taibai, "Big brother has always been the most powerful!"
Seeing Luo Yan's cute and playful appearance, Luo Bingquan smiled faintly. "Good, good. In Yan 'er's eyes, big brother is indeed the most powerful …"
Yu Taibai also laughed involuntarily. Then, he sat cross-legged on the spot and began to move his fingers.
Wisps of gray aura revolved around his right hand. This gray aura was the "power of faith" activated by the Great Yan Divine Dao.
The Great Yan Divine Dao's calculations differed greatly from ordinary divination techniques.
All kinds of divination techniques were futile in attempting to see through the mysteries and seek the truth.
This move was still relatively useful in the universe because the universe's fate did not have many variables. The Saints who built the universe would more or less leave some flaws, and even deliberately release some hints, allowing others to deduce the truth of the future to a certain extent.
However, the divine domain was much more tightly constructed than the universe. It was almost impossible to deduce the future through some flaws in the divine domain.
However, the Great Yan Divine Dao's divination technique didn't rely on the divine domain.
At the same time as the divination began, Yu Taibai would completely integrate all possible changes into the Great Yan Divine Dao.
Relying on the Great Yan Divine Dao to create a temporary world, then observe the changes in this world, and finally investigate the results.
For example, if a bird was incubating its egg in this world, then the egg would eventually hatch, and a little bird would definitely be born from the egg. This was a predetermined cause, and then a result would be obtained.
As Yu Taibai and Mu Xuerong battled, he would take into consideration Mu Xuerong's personality and the Killing God Dao that she cultivated, as well as the fact that a Great Perfection Saint could instantly teleport away. He would begin to calculate the possible directions of her attacks …
In this process, the more variables there were and the more people involved, the more difficult it would be to calculate.
"Luo Zheng, Aunt Han Jiu, Dongfang Chunjun, Mu Xuerong, Faith Treasure …"
Yu Taibai's fingers danced, and the strands of gray aura began to transform.
In the eyes of Luo Yan, Second Uncle, and Third Uncle, the gray aura was transforming into human figures, but they couldn't see their shapes clearly …
"Immortal mansion …"
"Phew …"
The grey aura instantly transformed into a mini immortal abode that was constantly flowing. This immortal abode was exactly the same as the one Gu Bei had built.
"Royal God Blade …"
"Jing Wuhuan!"
Hearing Yu Taibai's muttering, Third Uncle Luo Junyi's face revealed a trace of confusion, "How is it related to the Gu brothers?"

"Hiss …"
With a flip of his hand, Yu Taibai crushed the immortal mansion formed by the mist, a faint smile appearing on his face, "Second Uncle is right, Aunt Han Jiu is an outsider, she doesn't know our plans. Royal God Blade … although he's powerful, there's something wrong with his head. Jing Wuhuan knows our plans, and with his ability, it's not hard to predict our next move. The Dao Battle … Luo Zheng should be heading to a place that isn't an attack."
"Doesn't the Dao Battle have to wait two or three years?" Luo Yan said with dissatisfaction.
Yu Taibai smiled, "Yan 'er, you can't wait?"
"Three years and three years, I've already waited for so many years," Luo Yan said angrily.
Although the flow of time in the universe was much faster than in the realm of the gods, Luo Yan had left Luo Zheng when he was in the Central Domain.
Calculating these decades, according to the flow of time in the realm of the gods, it was still six years …
"Two years, there's still two years, we'll definitely see him," Yu Taibai smiled faintly, "But … I'm afraid he won't have time to comprehend the portion that master gave to Luo Zheng."

Seeing Luo Yan's face full of confidence, the others shook their heads and smiled.
They didn't doubt Luo Yan's talent in the slightest. This Dao Battle would probably be the most exciting one in history.

…
Within the Immortal Mansion …
Luo Zheng, Jing Wuhuan, and Yu Shenfeng sat in a side hall, silently reciting the True Meaning of Dao Technique written by Luo Nian.
The True Meaning of Dao wasn't that easy to comprehend.
With Aunt Hanjiu's talent, she could only recite 2500 words in the first ten days, almost unable to recite more than 2501 words!
But on the eleventh day, her thoughts suddenly became clear. In just four days, she comprehended nearly half of the True Meaning of Dao.
At this time, Aunt Hanjiu no longer hoped to break through the shackles, as it would be difficult for her to continue.
"With one thought, with ten thousand thoughts, with desire …"
In this period of time, Luo Zheng could already recite the True Meaning of Dao Technique to 2700 words.
Jing Wuhuan and Yu Shenfeng weren't far behind. Jing Wuhuan broke through the 3000 word barrier in just seven days, his speed actually slightly faster than Aunt Hanjiu's.
But he was a very rational person, and whenever he felt tired, he would immediately stop.
Then he pushed open the door to the side hall, took out his fishing rod, and sat by the pond, fishing silently. This would often take several hours, his entire person in a state of deep meditation.
As for Yu Shenfeng …
"Plop!"
A moment ago, Yu Shenfeng was still gnashing his teeth and silently chanting. At this moment, he had already fallen to the ground and fainted.
"Idiot." Jing Wuhuan glared at Yu Shenfeng, then continued reciting.
Yu Shen Feng's talent was slightly higher than Jing Wu Huan's, but he was like a madman who risked his life, chasing after Yu Shen Feng relentlessly. In just three days, he broke through the barrier of 2500 words, his performance was even more stunning than Aunt Hanjiu's!
But the True Meaning of Dao Technique pursued the natural path, and when the conditions were right, rushing for success wouldn't be good.
Thus, after two thousand five hundred words, it was already difficult for him to move forward. Just like that, after two thousand six hundred words, he had already reached his limit. It was very common for him to faint at the slightest provocation …
However, after Yu Shenfeng repeatedly fainted over a hundred times, it was as if he had been injected with chicken blood. Word after word came out of his mouth clearly. His comprehension speed actually began to increase explosively. In just a few days, he actually broke through the 3,500 word barrier.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng and Jing Wuhuan were also very surprised.
"This guy, maybe being straightforward is also a kind of nature …" Jing Wuhuan helplessly curled his lips. He was used to Yu Shenfeng being like this, his talent was originally higher than his.
"Hehe, what's so hard about reciting these stupid words?" Yu Shenfeng smacked his lips, then continued to recite.
He, who was far ahead, was full of disdain towards the progress of Eldest Senior Brother and Youngest Junior Brother.
"What's with your expression?" Jing Wuhuan unhappily said.
Luo Zheng looked at Yu Shenfeng's complacent face and shook his head helplessly. He could only concentrate and silently recite.
However, after breaking through two thousand eight hundred words, he seemed to have reached a bottleneck. His soul trembled more and more violently, and it became more and more difficult …
"Since the True Meaning of Dao is comprehended naturally, this True Meaning is not created for the later generations. Rather, it is the True Meaning of an innate existence …"
When he memorized 2,850 words, a thought suddenly popped up in his mind.
As he thought of this, he suddenly felt that he had grasped an important opportunity.
The true meaning of nature that they cultivated was based on the human heart. However, this true meaning was the true meaning of nature. It could be imitated by all things!

Chapter 1993
A strange light seemed to shoot into Luo Zheng's mind.
The true meaning of the Dao was originally extremely complicated.
As one recited it, the true meaning contained within could touch one's soul and firmly engrave those words into one's soul.
If one couldn't bear the true meaning of the Dao, they naturally wouldn't be able to recite it. Thus, it was impossible to forcibly memorize these tens of thousands of words. One could only recite it, comprehend it, and memorize it.
The more words there were, the more the true meaning of the Dao increased, and the greater the effect on one's soul!
If one forcibly endured it, even someone as strong as Yu Shenfeng, who was an outstanding Perfection Paragon, wouldn't be able to bear the pressure of the true meaning in the end. Thus, Yu Shenfeng frequently fainted.
After Luo Zheng was enlightened in an instant, he discovered that all the pressure had completely disappeared.
Then, he recited the true meaning of the Dao technique again, and it became abnormally easy. As he continued to recite it, Luo Zheng felt that his soul had been upgraded to a strange world.
It was a desolate ocean …
Bits of faint crystal light scattered in the ocean. This ocean seemed to extend endlessly, and the other side couldn't be seen clearly.
As he took a step forward, an illusory boat appeared under his feet. At this moment, Luo Zheng faintly felt a sense of security in his heart. Only by relying on this boat could he possibly cross this boundless ocean.
In the side hall …
Yu Shenfeng, who originally seemed to have been injected with chicken blood, and Jing Wuhuan, who was constantly calming down, stared blankly at their little junior brother.

After breaking through his shackles, he could already recite three thousand five hundred words. In terms of comprehending the true meaning of the Dao technique, he was far ahead of eldest senior brother and little junior brother.
"No, no, I can't let this guy surpass me. He's just a mere Lower True God …" Yu Shenfeng also began to recite loudly.
Comprehending the true meaning of the Dao required both body and mind. How could one tolerate the slightest bit of distraction?
But the more anxious he was, the harder it was for him to recite …
Not long after, he felt dizzy, and the pressure on his soul doubled again. He had no choice but to stop!
On the other hand, Luo Zheng...
It was as if he had forgotten everything in this world.
All that was left was him and the heart sutra of the True Meaning of Nature.
As he chanted silently, the small boat beneath his feet grew larger and more stable!
"Four thousand words …" Jing Wu Huan's eyebrows jumped.
Yu Shenfeng was even more speechless.
His greatest pride was his unparalleled innate talent. This was probably the only thing he could use to cover up his shame as Gu Bei's disciple!

Now, Yu Shenfeng finally understood that his Junior Brother's talent was above his!
Another incense stick of time passed …
"Four thousand five hundred words," Jing Wu Huan sighed, "I don't know where his limit is."
"Aunty Han Jiu said that the limit of her cultivation is 4,900 words, which is half of the True Meaning of the Dao. This guy's limit is probably around there as well!" Yu Shenfeng muttered. He was planning in his heart when he could catch up.
However, Luo Zheng continued to recite in an unhurried manner …
He saw an illusion in his eyes.
The small boat beneath his feet slowly grew larger. Gradually, a mast, side of the boat, and large oars appeared. They naturally merged into one whole …
The True Meaning boat became more and more complete!
"Four thousand seven hundred words," Jing Wu Huan smiled. He was very happy to see his Junior Brother's depressed expression. This guy should be dealt a heavy blow, and let him know that there was always someone stronger.
"What's so great about that? He probably won't be able to reach four thousand nine hundred words. I'll catch up soon!" Yu Shenfeng said disdainfully. He forced himself to remain calm.
"Four thousand eight hundred words …"
"Four thousand nine hundred words …"
Jing Wu Huan agreed with Yu Shenfeng's words.
Four thousand nine hundred words was originally a bottleneck. Most people who cultivated the True Meaning of Dao would be stuck at this bottleneck. If they wanted to break through, they would need a considerable amount of accumulation.
At this time, he and Yu Shenfeng stared at Luo Zheng with a trace of nervousness in their eyes. They pricked up their ears to listen to Luo Zheng's chant. The two Senior Brothers wanted to know if their guesses were correct.
However, Luo Zheng continued to chant at a steady pace …
"Five, five thousand words …" Jing Wu Huan muttered.
"This guy, ahhhhhhh …" Yu Shenfeng cried out sadly. He found it hard to accept.
He was the number one genius under his master. His master had said that among all the Perfection Sovereigns on the floating islands, his talent was at least in the top three. His talent was quite rare in the divine realm.
Yu Shenfeng still took good care of this junior brother of his. However, the talent that Ye Xiwen displayed made him lose his balance.
Not long after …
"Six thousand words." Jing Wu Huan didn't feel any burden in his heart. His eyes grew brighter and brighter. "Master's foresight is indeed not something we can compare to. I wonder what he will think if he knows Junior Brother is like this!"
Yu Shenfeng stared blankly at Luo Zheng. He was already completely numb. He didn't make a sound, nor did he react.
"Seven thousand words …"
"Eight thousand words …"
When Luo Zheng recited eight thousand words, Luo Zheng's eyes lit up.
A layer of pale white light emanated from his body.
This light was so pure that it seemed to be able to merge with all things.
After 8,000 words, Luo Zheng finally stopped. His vacant eyes had already slowly closed, and he sat cross-legged on the spot without moving at all. He'd already entered into a meditative state.
"Looks like it won't be long before little junior brother will be able to completely comprehend the True Meaning of Nature … He's only a Lower True God, but his talent is already terrifying." Jing Wu Huan sighed.
Yu Shenfeng looked at the meditating Luo Zheng. After a while, he said, "Junior Brother is simply cheating! His son is cheating. He's also cheating! "
He saw Junior Brother Luo Nian decipher a complete True Meaning Mental Cultivation Method. It was already quite ridiculous. One had to know that the golden Sanskrit was left behind by Master. This Luo Nian was only a teenager, yet he could completely decipher it. It was simply incomprehensible!
Now, Luo Zheng comprehended the True Meaning of Dao to such an extent right in front of him. It was even more difficult for him to accept …
At this moment, rumbling sounds came from outside the side hall. It was Luo Nian bringing the puppet that Yu Shenfeng had given him into the side hall.
Ever since he had this puppet, Luo Nian had been very happy. He brought it around the Immortal Mansion all day long.
"Shh." Jing Wu Huan asked Luo Zheng to lower his voice. Now that Luo Zheng had entered a meditative state, he shouldn't be disturbed.
"Yi, where's my father?" Luo Nian's gaze swept around the side hall. He actually couldn't find Luo Zheng's figure!
Yu Shenfeng's face was filled with confusion. He pointed at Luo Zheng. "Isn't that him?"
Luo Zheng sat upright on the floor of the side hall. Under the influence of the True Meaning of Dao, he was a chair, a table, or a pillar in this room.
As long as one didn't look carefully, it would be difficult for others to detect his existence!

Chapter 1994
Luo Nian turned his head and stared at Luo Zheng for a long time before separating him from the surrounding scenery.
This was a very magical feeling.
Because Luo Zheng wasn't in a hidden state. He was standing in the same place without any concealment.
"Father, how did you turn into a pillar …"

The purpose of the Dao Battle was to solve the problems that had been passed down from countless divine epochs.

The faster he ran, the more blood he would lose.
After witnessing Aunt Hanjiu's strange method of killing, he didn't dare to stop at all. He still shot out at an extremely high speed and at the same time tried to stop the blood from gushing out …
But the small wound on his leg couldn't heal at all. This wound was like a demon, desperately sucking the blood out of his body and then spitting it out!
At this moment, he finally understood what this wound meant to him.
Seeing that he was losing more and more blood, he made up his mind and chopped down!
"Puchi!"
This chop directly chopped off his injured left leg.
This move was effective. If Luo Zheng had bitten his head with the Bloody Desolate Bite, he probably wouldn't have cut it off …
Rein was still flying away with half of his leg.
"Should we chase after him?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Don't waste time," Aunt Hanjiu shook her head and slightly focused her eyes. Strands of purple mist slowly condensed and turned into a monster covered with sharp thorns.
After this monster was formed, it waved its sharp front claws and crazily dug into the snow, quickly diving into the snow.
A slight bulge appeared in the snow, and this bulge spread towards the direction that Rein was escaping at an extremely fast speed!
"Let's go," after Aunt Hanjiu finished arranging this, she took a step forward and left a small and exquisite footprint in the snow.
Luo Zheng didn't even look back as he followed behind …
Xiao Ye and the group of lower true gods stood motionless in the same place. They were already dumbfounded.
Two upper true gods who had experienced hundreds of battles couldn't fight back at all in front of these two people. Especially that cloaked beauty, the monster she created with her purple mist divine path was many times more terrifying than an upper true god …
And she herself was merely a lower True God!
The scene in front of them was completely beyond their scope of understanding.

Chapter 1998
After the two of them left, the respectful expressions on the faces of Xiao Ye and the other True Gods slowly faded away.
The few of them glanced at the corpses of the two True Gods, then looked at each other and rushed over with a "swoosh".
Just now, they had given all their wealth to the two upper True Gods. Now, they naturally had to get it back!
These two upper True Deities were quite wealthy. They were likely going to strike it rich after looting it once.
Very quickly, the black and white upper True God's belongings, as well as their spatial rings, were all stripped clean. Finally, the middle True God Xiao Ye was the leader of this group of people, and he was the leader of this group.
"Where did those two lower True Gods come from? They didn't even care about upper True God's belongings? "One of the lower True Gods said with a strange expression.
"Sigh, how could a guy who could easily kill these two big brothers be interested in trash like this? Didn't you see that the youth was too lazy to do anything and casually took out a few hundred Shenwu coins? "Xiao Ye sighed," Those two big brothers must be blind. I think those two most likely came from a floating island … "
"You mean those rich and powerful people?" Another lower True God asked with wide eyes.
"Even a lower True God from a rich and powerful family wouldn't be able to take care of two upper True Gods so easily, right?" Another slightly older True God shook his head.
To most True Gods in the Divine Domain, the rich and powerful families in the Sea of Time were legendary existences. The True Gods that came from there were the elites of the elites.
It wasn't strange for a lower True God to challenge a middle True God outside of a rich and powerful family. However, to be able to easily kill a middle True God who was two levels higher … That was too unbelievable.
…
At the border of the non-attacking area …
"The non-attacking area is suppressed by the true meaning of the path of that skeleton. The stronger the cultivation, the stronger the suppression. If I go in, the Sword Race will find out," Aunt Hanjiu said softly, "I can only send you here."
The difficulty of suppressing a Sage and suppressing a True God was naturally different.
Luo Zheng smiled, "Aunt Jiu can also participate in the Dao Battle …"
Aunt Hanjiu slightly shook her head, "I still have to assist Gu Bei's two apprentices. Who let me be schemed against by your father again?"
Luo Zheng originally thought that Auntie Hanjiu had no choice but to be dragged into this mess. Now that he thought about it, Luo Zheng entering the floating island was already very dangerous. The only person who could bring Luo Zheng away was Auntie Hanjiu. Her being dragged into this mess might have been part of Luo Zheng's calculations.
Of course, Aunt Hanjiu was willing to do so.
Now that Jing Wu Huan was trying to control the Waterfall Spiral Forest, he was afraid that he would alert those Saints. Aunt Han Jiu would return to the immortal estate and temporarily take Ning Yudie and the others into her Purple Extreme Realm, then continue to assist Jing Wu Huan and Yu Shen Feng in their actions …
"Okay." Luo Zheng nodded. Then, he turned around and stepped into the border of the non-offensive land.
The moment he entered the border, an invisible aura enveloped his body.
After reaching the God Realm, most of the auras that True Gods emitted were ancient and ancient. After all, the lifespan of many True Gods was several God Eras, or even dozens of God Eras. This kind of time span was several orders of magnitude longer than the time that secondary life forms experienced.
However, Luo Zheng truly felt what eternity was from this aura in the non-offensive land.
This aura contained a sense of vicissitudes. It was like an invisible arrow that pointed from the beginning of time all the way to the end of time. It was as if all the destruction of this world was unable to eliminate this aura!
"This is the aura of that skeleton? Truly astonishing … "
Like all the other True Gods who had stepped into this land, they were all very shocked when they first felt this aura.
Seeing Luo Zheng standing in place and feeling that aura, Aunt Hanjiu smiled faintly. She gathered her cloak and disappeared from where she was …
The "Ruomu Domain" covered by the non-offensive land was considered a large domain in the God Realm. For True Gods, it was also quite large.
After getting used to that aura, Luo Zheng put on the cloak and continued to move forward.
The path that Aunt Hanjiu had helped him choose was quite remote. There were few people on the way. Occasionally, he would meet some True Gods who were moving forward with their heads lowered. They even faintly revealed a look of alertness and did not have the slightest intention of chatting.
After walking like this for more than half a month, Luo Zheng saw a huge shadow faintly appear in the distant blue sky.
That shadow was like a boundless giant beast, sitting extremely far away …
"Ruo Mu?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly.
He had heard that Ruo Mu was not tall. It was the only one of the four sacred trees that grew horizontally. Like the banyan tree, it took root when it came into contact with water. It grew wildly in the Ruomu Domain's sea until it completely covered the sea. Only then did it stop expanding.
As he continued to move forward, he got closer and closer to Ruo Mu. The huge shadow also became clearer and clearer.
The number of True Gods he met along the way also increased.
Most of these True Gods were in groups of three to five and were moving together.
Among them, there were even hundreds of people who wore the same uniform and were moving forward in a line. It was obvious that they were well-trained disciples of certain clans …
After walking for more than half a month, Luo Zheng heard the sound of waves.
"Hua la la …"
He saw the thick roots of Ruo Mu in the distance. They were like the feet of a giant beast lying on the surface of the sea. They formed an extremely wide road that stretched to the shore.
On the shore, there was a towering sacred city. From the interior of this sacred city, there was a slender building that soared to the sky, like a huge sword pointing to the sky! Other than this huge sword, the intersecting buildings inside the sacred city were all of the same style.
Hence, from afar, there seemed to be countless swords erected inside the sacred city. It gave people a different feeling.
"As expected of the Sword Clan." Luo Zheng looked at the sacred city and smiled slightly. The culture of the sword had been completely imprinted in this clan.
"Plop!"
"Plop!"
At this moment, not far away, a few True Gods carrying longswords suddenly knelt down toward the sacred city. After bowing in the snow, they wiped off the snow on their bodies and continued moving forward …
Not far away, they knelt down again, bowed, and continued moving forward …
To these True Gods who cultivated the sword, the Ruo Mu Region was their pilgrimage path. The reverence on their faces was not directed at Ruo Mu, but toward the Sword Clan.
When they reached the sacred city, Luo Zheng saw a row of guards dressed in black and white robes standing in front of the city gate.
They were lined up in a row, their expressions solemn. Waves of awe-inspiring righteousness emanated from them.
Those with evil natures would feel extremely uneasy in front of this awe-inspiring aura!
Most sacred cities in the Divine Territory required one to pay Shenwu Coins to enter. However, this sacred city did not have such a tradition. All the True Gods who came to participate in the Dao Battle only needed to show a Sanskrit token and they could enter freely.
It wasn't difficult to obtain a Sanskrit Inscription.
"Hey hey hey, young man, do you have Sanskrit? You can't enter without Sanskrit! " By the roadside of the city gate, a lower True God with a face full of stubble waved at Luo Zheng. With a 'whoosh', he took out a stack of white paper. The Sanskrit words on the white paper were naturally copied. There was even a layer of gold powder on the paper that flickered with a golden radiance. "One Shenwu coin per piece …"
Luo Zheng glanced at it and shook his head slightly.
The structure of the Sanskrit was rather simple. The information contained within was not very large. It was obvious that it was common goods.

Chapter 1999
Luo Zheng couldn't be bothered with this person.
He didn't expect this lower True God to pester him and block Luo Zheng's path, not allowing him to leave.
They couldn't fight in a non-offensive area, so no one was afraid of the other.
This lower True God saw this and pestered Luo Zheng at the city gates. Although many True Gods were participating in the Dao Struggle and had prepared some Sanskrit tablets, they were always able to fool some True Gods …
"You can't miss this opportunity! If you can decipher this Sanskrit tablet, you'll be able to shine in this Dao Struggle! " This lower True God observed Luo Zheng's expression as he walked. Seeing that Luo Zheng didn't respond, he took out another stack of papers with his other hand and said, "You don't like those. How about these? These purple-gold Sanskrit tablets are even rarer and cost one Shenwu coin each. It's a losing deal … "
Hearing the purple-gold Sanskrit tablets, Luo Zheng was slightly puzzled. His gaze fell on the stack of papers, and he didn't know whether to laugh or cry.
It turned out that the purple-gold Sanskrit tablets were just a rubbing of Sanskrit tablets with a layer of purple-gold powder …
Many True Gods who were about to enter the city burst into laughter when they saw the purple-gold Sanskrit tablets.
This lower True God didn't seem to care at all and continued to pester Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng had no choice but to stop and look at this True God indifferently. He then snatched the golden Sanskrit tablet from his hand and said indifferently, "This Sanskrit tablet's structure is extremely simple. There are only forty-six words in it. The general idea is that it describes a kind of plant in the chaos. The fruit of that plant is very sweet and can quench one's thirst after eating it!"
After saying this, he stuffed the golden Sanskrit tablet back into the lower True God's hand and snatched the purple-gold Sanskrit tablet. "This Sanskrit tablet is even more boring. It's probably a poem from some race … These two Sanskrit tablets are meaningless. Stop pestering me!"
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, the Lower Real God who was listening was stunned. The other Real Gods were also skeptical of what Luo Zheng had said.
Even simple Sanskrit tablets weren't easy to decipher.
In God's Domain, there were probably thousands of different interpretations of a single Sanskrit character. This was also because the deconstruction of Sanskrit characters differed from one another. There had never been a unified understanding of Sanskrit characters. Many True Gods had their own interpretations.
As for some of the almighty figures in the Divine Realm, their gazes were even sharper and their interpretations were even more accurate. However, those comprehensions and understandings naturally wouldn't be spread among ordinary True Gods.
Luo Zheng explained the meaning of the two Sanskrit tablets so confidently, which of course attracted a lot of attention.
However, most of the True Gods thought that Luo Zheng was talking nonsense …
"Eh?"
At this moment, a white-clothed old man standing on top of the city wall frowned slightly. His sharp, sword-like eyes stared fixedly at Lu Zheng.
"This kid's explanation … is actually exactly the same as Great Master Qing Mu's understanding!" the white-clothed old man said with a face full of surprise.
Beside the white-clothed old man, there was a dignified young man. He nodded and said, "I remember that Great Master Qing Mu deciphered that divine pattern. According to Great Master Qing Mu's speculation, that divine pattern indeed recorded a tree. However, Great Master Qing Mu only said that there was a magical fruit on that tree. He didn't know what the fruit was used for. That kid actually said that the fruit could be used to quench his thirst …"
In the minds of the True Gods, gods of a higher level were creatures beyond their imagination. Why would they need to quench their thirst?
"But the first half of his explanation does match Great Master Qing Mu's," the old man said. "And the second Sanskrit tablet, he actually said that it was a poem. I remember that Great Master Qing Mu said that it was a kind of cultivation method."
The young man laughed. "That's right. Because of Great Master Qing Mu's words, many people desperately tried to comprehend some great magic from it, but in the end, they came up empty-handed. I kind of agree with this guy. Maybe it's just a lousy poem, but everyone treats it like a treasure. Let's find a chance to get in touch with this person."
"Let's wait first." The white-clothed old man shook his head and said, "If we get in touch too early, we won't be able to see the real thing. If he really has something, he'll definitely stand out. It won't be too late to get in touch with him then."
Luo Zheng had forced Luo Nian to decipher a lot of Sanskrit in Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion.
For example, the two Sanskrit tablets in the young man's hands were of the most inconspicuous type. However, for the sake of fighting for the Dao, Luo Zheng had almost memorized every single word.
To be honest, Luo Zheng was a little confused.
Luo Nian came from the Vast Thousand Worlds and knew almost nothing about the universe and the Realm of the Gods.
These Sanskrit words were so complex that even a Saint would find it difficult to comprehend their meaning. However, when Luo Nian came into contact with them, it was as simple as eating and drinking. It was a piece of cake …
For a period of time, Luo Zheng wanted to release a wisp of divine sense and drill into Luo Nian's mind to take a look. There seemed to be a secret hidden in his soul that even he didn't know.
However, Luo Nian swore to die and even complained to Ning Yudie that Luo Zheng wanted to invade his privacy. In the end, Luo Zheng could only give up.
Looking at Luo Nian's drooping head and the faint sly look in his eyes, he really felt that Luo Nian was hiding something …
That lower True God seemed to be frightened by Luo Zheng's explanation. Holding the two stacks of Sanskrit rubbed, he stared at Luo Zheng and said in a daze, "You, are you telling the truth?"
Luo Zheng rolled his eyes and walked toward the city gate …
The True Gods at the city gate lined up in an orderly manner and showed their Sanskrit to the guards in order.
After waiting for a while, it was Luo Zheng's turn. He shook his finger lightly, and a piece of emerald green paper appeared in his hand. There was a faint Sanskrit inscribed on the paper, and the Sanskrit inscribed on it emitted a faint aura …
"The original!"
"This boy actually has an original Sanskrit!"
"Where did he get it!?"
The eyes of the guards and the surrounding True Gods brightened when they sensed this Sanskrit.
There were countless Sanskrit words circulating in the Realm of the Gods …
This was because when a Sanskrit word appeared, there would often be millions or even tens of millions of copies. These copies circulated at a very fast speed.
However, there was often only one original copy.
There were even some Sanskrit words that didn't have the original copy and only existed in copies …
The vast majority of True Gods might have never seen real Sanskrit in their entire lives. The real Sanskrit here naturally referred to the original Sanskrit.
Some Sanskrit words were engraved on stone tablets, some were engraved on walls, and some Sanskrit words occasionally floated in the air.
There were also some Sanskrit words, such as the "Supreme Equipment Refining Technique" that Luo Zheng practiced, which was directly engraved on his own body …
Luo Zheng naturally made ample preparations before coming to participate in the Dao Battle. This Sanskrit word wasn't particularly important. He naturally didn't expect that a Sanskrit word that he casually took out would attract such astonished gazes from these people. Both inside and outside the city wall suddenly became quiet. Everyone's eyes were firmly fixed on the green piece of paper in his hand.
The inferior True God who had previously sold Luo Zheng the Sanskrit word was looking at the original copy in Luo Zheng's hand with his mouth agape.
On the city wall, the two True Gods, one old and one young, were also dumbfounded.
"This boy … Where did he come from? How could he have an original copy?" the young man said in a daze.
The expression of the white-clothed old man was also very interesting. Back then, he had begged and begged Great Master Qing Mu to allow him to see an original copy of the Sanskrit word. Moreover, Great Master Qing Mu was very careful and only allowed him to see it once!
No matter who it was, if they had an original copy of the Sanskrit word, they would most likely keep it a secret and carefully store it away like a treasure.
Copies could be modified, and the original Sanskrit word was the most accurate. This was another reason why original copies of the Sanskrit word were precious.

Chapter 2000
The guards stared blankly at the original Sanskrit in Luo Zheng's hand for a while.
The first guard to react looked at Luo Zheng with respect. He pointed at Luo Zheng's green paper and asked cautiously, "Can I, can I make a copy?"
There was no important information recorded on the Sanskrit on the green paper. Luo Zheng didn't mind and nodded in agreement.
The guard didn't expect Luo Zheng to agree. He was overjoyed. He thanked Luo Zheng repeatedly and quickly took out a light yellow stone.
There was a simple divine pattern on the back of the stone, and there was nothing on the front.
If the side of the stone was pointed at someone and the divine pattern was activated, the person's appearance could be copied.
It wasn't just a person's face that could be copied. If it was pointed at a mountain, the mountain's appearance could be copied. If it was pointed at a river, the river could be copied. It was very convenient to use.
Therefore, many True Gods had prepared a few pieces to use to copy some good golden Sanskrit characters.
The guard pointed the front of the stone plate at the green paper in Luo Zheng's hand. After a wave of energy came from his hand, the runes on the back of the stone plate glowed. At the same time, an identical Sanskrit character appeared on the front of the stone plate.
"Hey, what is this? It's very interesting." Luo Zheng looked at the stone with curiosity. He had never seen this kind of thing before.
"This is a Rubbing Stone." The guard smiled. "It's a common thing in our Ruomu Territory. I can give you some."
After saying that, the guard stretched out his hand and a stack of light yellow stone appeared in his hand. It was the Rubbing Stone he spoke of.
The origin of this kind of stone was the sea below Ruomu Territory. True Gods cut the Rubbing Stone from the bottom of the sea and fished it up. They then cut it into thin stone pieces and added divine patterns to it to obtain this strange ability.
However, the Rubbing Stone had a certain time limit. After a Rubbing Stone left the sea of Ruomu Territory for about two years, it would completely lose its effect.
So the Rubbing Stone wasn't widely circulated in the God Territory. Luo Zheng naturally hadn't seen it before.
Luo Zheng smiled and took the stack of stone pieces. After thanking the guard, he prepared to enter the city …
Who knew that before he could take a step, he would see the guards and dozens of True Gods looking at him.
They all looked at Luo Zheng with a pitiful expression. Luo Zheng didn't need them to say anything. They all wanted to copy his original Sanskrit.
Most True Gods had never seen the original Sanskrit in their lives.
They had encountered such a rare opportunity.. Moreover, Luo Zheng was willing to let others copy it. Who would be willing to let go of such an opportunity?
Not to mention them, even the white-robed old man and the young man on the city wall had the impulse to put aside their pride and run down to make Skill Copying!
"You … can copy his Sanskrit," Luo Zheng curled his lips and said. He then lowered his head and walked away.
In such a short period of time, he had already attracted so many people. If he really allowed these people to copy one by one, he probably wouldn't be able to enter the city!
The atmosphere in the Sword Territory's divine city was very peaceful.
Perhaps it was because it wasn't a place to attack, but the True Gods here didn't have much hostility. They wouldn't casually break out into conflicts. Even if there was a conflict, they could only glare at each other. At most, they could only teach each other a lesson.
In this way, if the True Gods of the Wood Territory could spend more time on cultivation and comprehension, they would be more at peace with the rest of the territory.
Because of the Dao War, the streets on both sides of the divine city were filled with True Gods from all over the God Territory.
Sanskrit was a commodity that was widely circulated in the God Territory. People in all the major divine cities interpreted it and even traded it with each other.
Luo Zheng had seen it in the Han Family's ancestral land.
Now, almost all of these people had come to the Wood Territory. Naturally, it was very lively.
Along the way, True Gods placed stacks of golden Sanskrit in different shapes on both sides of the road to trade. Some common goods were placed casually on the ground, while some rare golden Sanskrit was placed on delicate transparent jade pieces. In order to prevent people from casually copying, the surface of the jade pieces had been blurred. At a glance, one could only see a rough shape of the golden Sanskrit. Naturally, this was to prevent people from casually copying.
Seeing the dazzling array of divine patterns, Luo Zheng was instantly interested.
Most of the divine patterns were worthless. After countless years of deciphering, it would only be a waste of time. Thus, many True Gods wouldn't spend much effort to decipher some rare Sanskrit.
But this wasn't a problem for Luo Nian.
Anyway, deciphering a Sanskrit was as easy as eating and drinking for him. People who didn't know him would think that he was a lifeform that surpassed his level. It would be a waste if he didn't use such a heaven-defying ability.
If some Sanskrit contained the true meaning of Dao, or even some unique and precious content, then he would make a killing …
Thus, Luo Zheng's eyes swept across the road like a knife as he thought.
Behind Luo Zheng, the old man in white and the young man had quietly followed Luo Zheng.
"He seems to want to buy some Sanskrit?" The young man had a strange look on his face.
In their eyes, Luo Zheng wasn't an ordinary person. Why would he be interested in these common goods?
The old man in white also shook his head. "I can't see through this person either. Maybe he wants to pick up some rare Sanskrit?"
"Pick up some rare Sanskrit? Old man, are you kidding? You know what good things these guys have. Their rare Sanskrit is almost impossible to decipher. They don't have much value at all! "The young man laughed disdainfully.
The old man in white nodded slightly. Looking at Luo Zheng's back, he said faintly, "For you and me, it's indeed impossible to decipher, but maybe he can …"
Soon, Luo Zheng's actions were predicted by the two.
Luo Zheng's footsteps suddenly stopped in front of a stall.
The stall owner was a middle-aged man with dark hair all over his body. His face was covered in a layer of fine hair.
Apart from humans, there were many other races in the Realm of the Gods. However, after these races became true gods, they were no different from ordinary people unless they deliberately preserved their race's characteristics.
This middle-aged man's head was drooping, and his eyes were half-closed. He looked lifeless, but his narrowed eyes hid a shrewd luster.
In front of Luo Zheng was a row of exquisite brocade boxes. Blurry golden Sanskrit characters appeared on the surface of the brocade boxes. There were as many as seven of them.
These golden Sanskrit characters …
When Luo Zheng noticed the first golden Sanskrit character, his heart skipped a beat.
Luo Zheng had seen this golden Sanskrit character before.
To be precise, he had seen half of this golden Sanskrit character in the Immortal Mansion!
At that time, Luo Nian had told Luo Zheng that this golden Sanskrit character should be a kind of True Meaning mantra …
However, because there was only half of it, Luo Nian's decipherment was only intermittent. Thus, the True Meaning of Dao within was also intermittent. Thus, there was no value in deciphering it.
Now, he had actually seen this complete golden Sanskrit character!
More importantly, the True Meaning of Dao that Luo Zheng cultivated was a set of six Sanskrit characters. And the seven golden Sanskrit characters in front of this middle-aged man also seemed to be a set!
"Could this also be a complete set of True Meaning mantras?" Luo Zheng's heart madly jumped.
Not far away, the old man in white and the young man in white looked at Luo Zheng's uncontrollable excitement. Their faces revealed strange expressions.
"I don't understand this guy …"
"This guy has studied Sanskrit so thoroughly. How come he doesn't even know the Seven Sanskrit characters of Luo Shui!"
The Seven Sanskrit characters of Luo Shui referred to this set of seven Sanskrit characters.
